## src/client/Main.client.luau

```lua
--[[
	DEAD AIR: CHANNEL ZERO — client bootstrap.

	The client owns input, UI, camera and presentation. It never decides
	outcomes: every interaction, edit, purchase and reward is validated on the
	server, and the HUD only reflects replicated state.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Net = require(Shared.Net)

local State = require(script.Parent.State)
local Controllers = script.Parent.Controllers

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local remotes = Net.wait(ReplicatedStorage)
State.init(remotes)

local AudioController = require(Controllers.AudioController)
local HUDController = require(Controllers.HUDController)
local CameraController = require(Controllers.CameraController)
local InputController = require(Controllers.InputController)
local DeckController = require(Controllers.DeckController)
local MenuController = require(Controllers.MenuController)
local ReportController = require(Controllers.ReportController)
local SpectateController = require(Controllers.SpectateController)
local KeypadController = require(Controllers.KeypadController)
local EffectsController = require(Controllers.EffectsController)
local MonitorController = require(Controllers.MonitorController)
local ToolController = require(Controllers.ToolController)
local PromptController = require(Controllers.PromptController)
local TutorialController = require(Controllers.TutorialController)

local function safeInit(name, fn, ...)
	local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
	if not ok then
		warn(string.format("[DeadAir] %s failed to start: %s", name, tostring(err)))
	end
end

safeInit("Audio", AudioController.init)
safeInit("HUD", HUDController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Camera", CameraController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Input", InputController.init)
safeInit("Deck", DeckController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Menu", MenuController.init, playerGui, DeckController)
safeInit("Report", ReportController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Spectate", SpectateController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Keypad", KeypadController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Effects", EffectsController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Monitors", MonitorController.init)
safeInit("Tools", ToolController.init, playerGui, DeckController)
safeInit("Prompts", PromptController.init, playerGui)
safeInit("Tutorial", TutorialController.init, playerGui)

InputController.onTool = ToolController.toggle
-- Your footsteps land in time with the head bob.
CameraController.onFootstep = AudioController.footstep

InputController.onDeck = function()
	if DeckController.isOpen() then
		DeckController.close()
		return
	end
	if State.anyModal() then
		return
	end
	if State.inMission() then
		if State.isAlive() then
			local deckId = DeckController.nearbyDeck()
			local caseDef = State.caseDef()
			local deck = deckId and caseDef and caseDef.decks[deckId]
			local powered = State.mission.flags and State.mission.flags.generator_restored
			if deck and not deck.battery and not powered then
				State.notify:Fire(deck.label .. " has no power. Restore the generator first.")
			elseif deckId then
				DeckController.open({ mode = "mission", deckId = deckId })
			elseif State.profile and State.profile.equipment == "Monitor" then
				DeckController.open({ mode = "handheld" })
			else
				State.notify:Fire("Stand at a tape deck to open it.")
			end
		else
			DeckController.open({ mode = "spectate" })
		end
	end
end

InputController.onMenu = function()
	if DeckController.isOpen() then
		DeckController.close()
		return
	end
	MenuController.toggle()
end

-- Text size setting: every label keeps its designed size times the scale.
local function scaleText(gui)
	local function apply(obj)
		-- Deferred: the object may already have been removed by a rebuild.
		if obj.Parent == nil then
			return
		end
		if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
			local base = obj:GetAttribute("BaseTextSize")
			if not base then
				base = obj.TextSize
				obj:SetAttribute("BaseTextSize", base)
			end
			obj.TextSize = math.floor(base * State.settings.textScale + 0.5)
		end
	end
	for _, d in gui:GetDescendants() do
		apply(d)
	end
	gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
		task.defer(apply, d)
	end)
	State.settingsChanged:Connect(function()
		for _, d in gui:GetDescendants() do
			apply(d)
		end
	end)
end

for _, gui in playerGui:GetChildren() do
	if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and string.sub(gui.Name, 1, 7) == "DeadAir" then
		scaleText(gui)
	end
end

-- Ask for our profile in case it arrived before the listeners were ready.
task.spawn(function()
	State.request("GetProfile", {})
end)
```

## src/client/State.luau

```lua
--[[
	Client state store. Everything the UI shows comes from server snapshots; the
	client never decides game outcomes.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Signal = require(Shared.Util.Signal)
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local ProfileSchema = require(Shared.Core.ProfileSchema)
local TimelineCore = require(Shared.Core.TimelineCore)

local State = {}

State.player = Players.LocalPlayer
State.remotes = nil
State.mission = nil
State.profile = nil
State.party = nil
State.report = nil
State.settings = table.clone(ProfileSchema.DefaultSettings)

State.missionChanged = Signal.new()
State.profileChanged = Signal.new()
State.partyChanged = Signal.new()
State.settingsChanged = Signal.new()
State.cue = Signal.new()
State.subtitle = Signal.new()
State.notify = Signal.new()
State.reportReceived = Signal.new()
State.ghosts = Signal.new()
State.uiChanged = Signal.new()

-- Which modal screens are open (input is suspended while any is).
State.ui = {}

function State.setUI(name, open)
	if (State.ui[name] == true) ~= (open == true) then
		State.ui[name] = open or nil
		State.uiChanged:Fire(name, open)
	end
end

function State.anyModal()
	return next(State.ui) ~= nil
end

function State.now()
	return Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
end

function State.action(kind, payload)
	payload = payload or {}
	payload.type = kind
	State.remotes.Action:FireServer(payload)
end

function State.request(kind, payload)
	payload = payload or {}
	payload.type = kind
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return State.remotes.Request:InvokeServer(payload)
	end)
	if not ok then
		return { ok = false, message = "Connection problem." }
	end
	return result or { ok = false }
end

function State.inMission()
	local m = State.mission
	if not m or m.phase == "None" then
		return false
	end
	local me = m.participants and m.participants[tostring(State.player.UserId)]
	return me ~= nil and me.status ~= "Disconnected"
end

function State.me()
	local m = State.mission
	return m and m.participants and m.participants[tostring(State.player.UserId)]
end

function State.isAlive()
	local me = State.me()
	return me ~= nil and me.status == "Alive"
end

function State.caseDef()
	local m = State.mission
	return m and m.caseId and CaseRegistry.get(m.caseId) or nil
end

-- Event definitions after seeded variants (for footage), cached per run.
local defsCache = { key = nil, defs = nil }
function State.eventDefs(caseDef, patrolFlip)
	caseDef = caseDef or State.caseDef()
	if not caseDef then
		return nil
	end
	local key = caseDef.id .. ":" .. tostring(patrolFlip == true)
	if defsCache.key ~= key then
		defsCache.key = key
		defsCache.defs = TimelineCore.new(caseDef, { patrolFlip = patrolFlip == true }):eventDefinitions()
	end
	return defsCache.defs
end

function State.broadcastClock()
	local m = State.mission
	if not m or not m.broadcastStart then
		return 0
	end
	return State.now() - m.broadcastStart
end

function State.hasTape(tapeId)
	local m = State.mission
	if not m or not m.inventory then
		return false
	end
	local caseDef = State.caseDef()
	if not caseDef then
		return false
	end
	for itemId, item in caseDef.items do
		if item.kind == "Tape" and item.tape == tapeId and m.inventory[itemId] then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function State.applySettings(settings)
	State.settings = ProfileSchema.sanitizeSettings(State.settings, settings)
	State.settingsChanged:Fire(State.settings)
end

-- Saves settings to the server shortly after the player stops changing them.
local saveToken = 0
function State.saveSettingsSoon()
	saveToken += 1
	local token = saveToken
	task.delay(1.5, function()
		if token == saveToken then
			State.request("SaveSettings", { settings = State.settings })
		end
	end)
end

-- Effective intensity multiplier for distortion-type effects.
function State.intensity(kind)
	local s = State.settings
	local base = s[kind] or 1
	if s.lowIntensity then
		base *= 0.25
	end
	return base
end

function State.init(remotes)
	State.remotes = remotes
	remotes.Mission.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(snapshot)
		if snapshot.phase == "None" then
			State.mission = nil
		else
			State.mission = snapshot
		end
		State.missionChanged:Fire(State.mission)
	end)
	remotes.Profile.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(profile)
		local first = State.profile == nil
		State.profile = profile
		if first and profile.settings then
			State.applySettings(profile.settings)
		end
		State.profileChanged:Fire(profile)
	end)
	remotes.Party.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(party)
		State.party = party
		State.partyChanged:Fire(party)
	end)
	remotes.Cue.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(cue)
		State.cue:Fire(cue)
	end)
	remotes.Subtitle.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(line)
		State.subtitle:Fire(line)
	end)
	remotes.Notify.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(n)
		State.notify:Fire(n.text)
	end)
	remotes.Report.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(payload)
		State.report = payload
		State.reportReceived:Fire(payload)
	end)
	remotes.Ghosts.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(payload)
		State.ghosts:Fire(payload)
	end)
end

return State
```

## src/client/Controllers/AudioController.luau

```lua
--[[
	AudioController — everything the player hears.

	  Mixer       Sound groups for Music, Ambience, Effects, Interface and
	              Cinematic under a master, following the Settings sliders, with
	              ducking (radio lines, the deck) and the false-safety silence.
	  Music       A director picks the track from the situation - main menu,
	              field office, briefing, exploring (with a tension layer that
	              swells with the camera's tension value), the hunt, extraction,
	              the case report - and crossfades between them.
	  Ambience    Room tone that follows where you are and whether the lights
	              are on, fluorescent buzz, wind at the dock, the office hum.
	  Machines    Positional loops on the generator, the transmitter and the van
	              once they are running, and the Presenter's static face.
	  Footsteps   Yours, in time with the head bob, and everyone else's, chosen
	              by the floor surface; the Presenter's heavy tread.
	  Events      Every server cue, door, pickup, relay, radio line, subtitle
	              sound description and interface action has a sound.

	All sounds are slices of four original audio banks (Config/AudioBank) whose
	uploaded ids live in Config/AudioAssets. Until the banks are uploaded, every
	call is a silent no-op; subtitles and visuals carry all information anyway.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Audio = require(Shared.Config.Audio)
local AudioAssets = require(Shared.Config.AudioAssets)
local AudioBank = require(Shared.Config.AudioBank)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Sound)

local AudioController = {}

local STEP_LENGTH = 3.1 -- studs per footstep, matches the head bob
local HISTORY = 400

-- Recently requested sounds, newest last (for tests and debugging).
AudioController.history = {}
AudioController.track = nil -- current music track key ("Explore", "Hunt"...)

local groups = {}
local master
local emitterFolder
local recent = {} -- [name] = os.clock() of the last play (dedupe)
local layers = {} -- [name] = { sound, level, target, up, down }
local emitters = {} -- [name] = { sound, part }
local silencedUntil = 0
local radioUntil = 0
local rng = Random.new()

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Sounds
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function record(name)
	local history = AudioController.history
	table.insert(history, name)
	if #history > HISTORY then
		table.remove(history, 1)
	end
end

function AudioController.enabled()
	for _, id in AudioAssets.Banks do
		if id ~= "" then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

-- A Sound playing exactly one sound's slice of its bank, or nil if that bank
-- has not been uploaded yet.
local function newSound(name, parent)
	local region = AudioBank.Sounds[name]
	if not region then
		return nil
	end
	local id = AudioAssets.Banks[region.bank]
	if not id or id == "" then
		return nil
	end
	local def = Audio.def(name)
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
	sound.Name = name
	sound.SoundId = id
	sound.PlaybackRegionsEnabled = true
	local range = NumberRange.new(region.start, region.start + region.length)
	sound.PlaybackRegion = range
	if region.loop then
		sound.LoopRegion = range
		sound.Looped = true
	end
	sound.Volume = def.volume or 0.7
	sound.SoundGroup = groups[def.group or "Effects"] or groups.Effects
	if parent and parent ~= SoundService then
		local spatial = def.spatial or { 6, 80 }
		sound.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.InverseTapered
		sound.RollOffMinDistance = spatial[1]
		sound.RollOffMaxDistance = spatial[2]
	end
	sound.Parent = parent or SoundService
	return sound, region, def
end

local function emitterAt(position)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = "DeadAirSound"
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.Transparency = 1
	part.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
	part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
	part.Parent = emitterFolder
	return part
end

--[[
	opts: at (Vector3, plays there), parent (BasePart/Attachment), volume (x),
	      dedupe (seconds: skip if the same sound played that recently)
]]
function AudioController.play(name, opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local now = os.clock()
	if opts.dedupe and recent[name] and now - recent[name] < opts.dedupe then
		return nil
	end
	recent[name] = now
	record(name)
	local temporary = opts.at and emitterAt(opts.at) or nil
	local sound, region, def = newSound(name, temporary or opts.parent)
	if not sound then
		if temporary then
			temporary:Destroy()
		end
		return nil
	end
	local pitch = def.pitch
	if pitch then
		sound.PlaybackSpeed = pitch[1] + (pitch[2] - pitch[1]) * rng:NextNumber()
	end
	sound.Volume *= opts.volume or 1
	sound:Play()
	task.delay(region.length / math.max(sound.PlaybackSpeed, 0.1) + 0.3, function()
		sound:Destroy()
		if temporary then
			temporary:Destroy()
		end
	end)
	return sound
end

-- Plays `name` `count` times, `gap` seconds apart.
local function repeatPlay(name, count, gap, opts)
	for i = 0, count - 1 do
		task.delay(i * gap, AudioController.play, name, opts)
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Layers: looping beds and music that fade toward a target level
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function setLayer(name, target, up, down)
	local layer = layers[name]
	if not layer then
		if target <= 0 then
			return
		end
		layer = { sound = nil, level = 0, target = 0, up = 0.6, down = 0.4 }
		layers[name] = layer
	end
	layer.target = target
	layer.up = up or layer.up
	layer.down = down or layer.down
end

local function stepLayers(dt)
	for name, layer in layers do
		local rate = layer.target > layer.level and layer.up or layer.down
		local delta = layer.target - layer.level
		layer.level += math.sign(delta) * math.min(math.abs(delta), rate * dt)
		if layer.level > 0.001 and not layer.sound then
			layer.sound = newSound(name)
			if layer.sound then
				layer.sound.Volume = 0
				layer.sound:Play()
			end
		end
		if layer.sound then
			layer.sound.Volume = (Audio.def(name).volume or 0.7) * layer.level
		end
		if layer.level <= 0.001 and layer.target <= 0 then
			if layer.sound then
				layer.sound:Destroy()
			end
			layers[name] = nil
		end
	end
end

function AudioController.layerLevel(name)
	local layer = layers[name]
	return layer and layer.target or 0
end

-- A positional loop attached to a part (nil part removes it).
local function setEmitter(name, part)
	local entry = emitters[name]
	if entry and (part == nil or entry.part ~= part or entry.sound.Parent == nil) then
		entry.sound:Destroy()
		emitters[name] = nil
		entry = nil
	end
	if part and not entry then
		local sound = newSound(name, part)
		record(name)
		if sound then
			sound:Play()
			emitters[name] = { sound = sound, part = part }
		else
			-- Remember the request so tests and debugging can see it.
			emitters[name] = { sound = { Destroy = function() end, Parent = part }, part = part }
		end
	end
end

function AudioController.emitterActive(name)
	return emitters[name] ~= nil
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- World lookups
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function station()
	return Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
end

local function propPart(roomId, modelName, partName)
	local s = station()
	local rooms = s and s:FindFirstChild("Rooms")
	local room = rooms and rooms:FindFirstChild(roomId)
	local props = room and room:FindFirstChild("Props")
	local model = props and props:FindFirstChild(modelName)
	return model and model:FindFirstChild(partName)
end

local function presenter()
	local model = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	if model and model:IsA("Model") and model.PrimaryPart then
		return model
	end
	return nil
end

local function myRoot()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function listenerPosition()
	local root = myRoot()
	if root and State.inMission() and State.isAlive() then
		return root.Position
	end
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	return camera and camera.CFrame.Position or Vector3.zero
end

local function toV3(p)
	return Vector3.new(p.x, p.y or 0, p.z)
end

local function generatorPosition()
	local body = propPart("Generator", "Generator", "Body")
	return body and body.Position
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Footsteps
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function surfaceFor(material)
	return Audio.StepSurface[material and material.Name or ""] or "concrete"
end

local function stepName(kind)
	return string.format("Step_%s_%d", kind, rng:NextInteger(1, Audio.StepVariants))
end

-- The local player's step (called by the camera in time with the head bob).
function AudioController.footstep(sprinting)
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then
		return
	end
	AudioController.play(stepName(surfaceFor(humanoid.FloorMaterial)), { volume = sprinting and 1.25 or 0.9 })
end

local floorParams = RaycastParams.new()
floorParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

local function floorKind(root, character)
	floorParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character }
	local hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, -6, 0), floorParams)
	return surfaceFor(hit and hit.Material)
end

-- Other investigators: a step every STEP_LENGTH studs they move.
local others = {} -- [player] = { last, travelled }
local function stepOthers()
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
			local character = player.Character
			local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local track = others[player]
			if root then
				local pos = root.Position
				if not track or track.root ~= root then
					track = { root = root, last = pos, travelled = 0 }
					others[player] = track
				end
				local moved = Vector3.new(pos.X - track.last.X, 0, pos.Z - track.last.Z).Magnitude
				track.last = pos
				if moved < 4 then -- ignore teleports
					track.travelled += moved
				end
				if track.travelled >= STEP_LENGTH then
					track.travelled = 0
					AudioController.play(stepName(floorKind(root, character)), { parent = root })
				end
			else
				others[player] = nil
			end
		end
	end
	for player in others do
		if player.Parent == nil then
			others[player] = nil
		end
	end
end

-- Roblox's default running sound is replaced by the surface footsteps above.
local function muteDefaultSteps(character)
	local root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
	if not root then
		return
	end
	local function mute(child)
		if child:IsA("Sound") and child.Name == "Running" then
			child.Volume = 0
		end
	end
	for _, child in root:GetChildren() do
		mute(child)
	end
	root.ChildAdded:Connect(mute)
end

local presenterTrack = { last = nil, travelled = 0 }
local function stepPresenter()
	local model = presenter()
	if not model then
		presenterTrack.last = nil
		return
	end
	local root = model.PrimaryPart
	local pos = root.Position
	if presenterTrack.last then
		local moved = Vector3.new(pos.X - presenterTrack.last.X, 0, pos.Z - presenterTrack.last.Z).Magnitude
		if moved < 6 then
			presenterTrack.travelled += moved
		end
	end
	presenterTrack.last = pos
	-- Its stride is long: a step every 4.6 studs.
	if presenterTrack.travelled >= 4.6 then
		presenterTrack.travelled = 0
		AudioController.play("PresenterStep", { parent = root })
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Directors (run ten times a second)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function chooseMusic()
	if State.ui.report then
		return "Report"
	end
	local m = State.mission
	if m and State.inMission() then
		if m.phase == "Briefing" then
			return "Briefing"
		end
		if m.phase == "Completed" or m.phase == "Failed" then
			return "Report"
		end
		local model = presenter()
		if model and model:GetAttribute("State") == "Hunting" then
			return "Hunt"
		end
		if m.phase == "ExtractionAvailable" then
			return "Extract"
		end
		return "Explore"
	end
	if State.ui.menu then
		return "Menu"
	end
	return "Lobby"
end

local function directMusic()
	local key = chooseMusic()
	AudioController.track = key
	for trackKey, name in Audio.Music do
		if trackKey ~= "Tension" then
			local on = trackKey == key
			if trackKey == "Hunt" then
				setLayer(name, on and 1 or 0, 3, 0.35) -- slams in, lingers out
			else
				setLayer(name, on and 1 or 0, 0.5, 0.5)
			end
		end
	end
	-- The tension layer swells over the exploration score.
	local tension = State.tension or 0
	local swell = key == "Explore" and math.clamp((tension - 0.3) / 0.45, 0, 1) or 0
	setLayer(Audio.Music.Tension, swell, 0.8, 0.25)
end

local function directAmbience()
	local beds = { AmbStation = 0, AmbDark = 0, AmbHQ = 0, AmbOutdoor = 0, Fluorescent = 0 }
	if State.inMission() then
		local pos = listenerPosition()
		local roomId = MapLayout.roomAt(pos.X, pos.Z)
		local room = roomId and MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
		local s = station()
		local folder = s and roomId and s:FindFirstChild("Rooms") and s.Rooms:FindFirstChild(roomId)
		local mode = folder and folder:GetAttribute("LightMode") or "off"
		if room and room.outdoor then
			beds.AmbOutdoor = 1
			beds.AmbDark = 0.3
		elseif mode == "on" then
			beds.AmbStation = 1
			beds.Fluorescent = 1
		elseif mode == "emergency" then
			beds.AmbStation = 0.6
			beds.AmbDark = 0.6
		else
			beds.AmbDark = 1
			beds.AmbStation = 0.2
		end
	else
		beds.AmbHQ = 1
		beds.Fluorescent = 0.45
	end
	for name, target in beds do
		setLayer(name, target, 0.5, 0.35)
	end
end

local function directMachines()
	local m = State.mission
	local flags = m and m.flags or {}
	local inMission = m ~= nil and State.inMission()
	setEmitter("GeneratorLoop", inMission and flags.generator_restored and propPart("Generator", "Generator", "Body") or nil)
	setEmitter("TransmitterLoop", inMission and flags.transmitter_armed and propPart("Transmitter", "Transmitter", "Cabinet") or nil)
	local vanRunning = inMission and (m.phase == "ExtractionAvailable" or flags.extraction_open)
	setEmitter("VanIdle", vanRunning and propPart("LoadingDock", "ExtractionVan", "Body") or nil)

	local model = presenter()
	local near = 0
	local face = nil
	if model and inMission then
		local d = (model.PrimaryPart.Position - listenerPosition()).Magnitude
		near = math.clamp(1 - d / 45, 0, 1)
		if d < 90 then
			face = model:FindFirstChild("Head") or model.PrimaryPart
		end
	end
	setEmitter("PresenterStatic", face)
	setLayer("PresenterNear", near, 1.5, 0.6)
end

-- Heartbeat when tension is high.
local nextBeat = 0
local function stepHeart()
	local tension = State.tension or 0
	if tension < 0.55 or not State.inMission() or not State.isAlive() then
		return
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	if now >= nextBeat then
		nextBeat = now + 60 / (64 + tension * 70)
		AudioController.play("Heartbeat", { volume = math.clamp((tension - 0.55) / 0.45, 0.2, 1) })
	end
end

-- Mixer: settings, ducking and the false-safety silence.
local function mixGroups(dt)
	local settings = State.settings
	local now = os.clock()
	local silent = now < silencedUntil and 0 or 1
	local duckMusic, duckAmbience = 1, 1
	if now < radioUntil then
		duckMusic, duckAmbience = 0.55, 0.75
	end
	if State.ui.deck then
		duckMusic, duckAmbience = math.min(duckMusic, 0.6), math.min(duckAmbience, 0.7)
	end
	local targets = {
		Music = settings.music * duckMusic * silent,
		Ambience = settings.ambience * duckAmbience * silent,
		Effects = settings.effects * silent,
		Interface = settings.effects * silent,
		Cinematic = settings.effects,
	}
	local k = math.min(1, dt * 4)
	for name, target in targets do
		local g = groups[name]
		if g then
			g.Volume += (target - g.Volume) * k
		end
	end
	master.Volume = settings.master
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Events
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function doorSound(cue)
	local door = MapLayout.doorById(cue.door)
	local at = door and toV3(MapLayout.doorPosition(door)) + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0) or nil
	if cue.cue == "DoorForced" then
		AudioController.play("DoorForced", { at = at })
	elseif cue.kind == "freight" then
		AudioController.play("FreightDoor", { at = at })
	else
		AudioController.play(cue.open and "DoorOpen" or "DoorClose", { at = at })
	end
end

local function onCue(cue)
	local name = cue.cue
	local me = Players.LocalPlayer.UserId
	if name == "DoorMoved" or name == "DoorForced" then
		doorSound(cue)
		return
	elseif name == "Eliminated" then
		AudioController.play(cue.userId == me and "Eliminated" or "StaticShort")
		return
	elseif name == "Extracted" then
		AudioController.play("VanDoorSlam")
		return
	elseif name == "LocalSilence" then
		silencedUntil = os.clock() + (cue.duration or 5)
		return
	elseif name == "EditorShadow" and cue.userId ~= me then
		return
	elseif name == "GeneratorPrime" then
		AudioController.play("GeneratorPrime", { at = generatorPosition() })
		return
	elseif name == "PowerOn" then
		AudioController.play("GeneratorStart", { at = generatorPosition() })
	end
	local sound = Audio.Cues[name]
	if sound then
		-- The editor hears their own commit at once; the server's echo is skipped.
		AudioController.play(sound, { dedupe = 1.5 })
	end
end

local function onSubtitle(line)
	if line.kind == "radio" then
		if line.caller then
			AudioController.play("PhoneRing", { dedupe = 20 })
		end
		AudioController.play("RadioIn", { dedupe = 0.3 })
		local duration = 2.2 + #(line.text or "") * 0.045
		radioUntil = os.clock() + duration
		setLayer("RadioBed", 1, 6, 6)
		task.delay(duration, function()
			if os.clock() >= radioUntil - 0.05 then
				setLayer("RadioBed", 0, 6, 6)
				AudioController.play("RadioOut", { dedupe = 0.3 })
			end
		end)
	elseif line.kind == "cue" then
		local text = string.upper(line.text or "")
		for _, entry in Audio.SubtitleSounds do
			if string.find(text, entry[1], 1, true) then
				local model = presenter()
				local opts = model and { parent = model.PrimaryPart } or { volume = 0.6 }
				repeatPlay(entry[2], entry[3], 0.75, opts)
				break
			end
		end
	end
end

-- What changed between two mission snapshots.
local lastMission = nil
local tapesFound = 0
local function onMission(m)
	local before = lastMission
	local wasIn = before ~= nil
	local nowIn = m ~= nil and State.inMission()
	if nowIn and not wasIn then
		tapesFound = 0
		AudioController.play("Deploy")
	end
	if m and before and nowIn then
		local caseDef = State.caseDef()
		for itemId in m.inventory or {} do
			if not (before.inventory and before.inventory[itemId]) then
				local item = caseDef and caseDef.items[itemId]
				local kind = item and item.kind
				if kind == "Tape" then
					AudioController.play("PickupTape")
					tapesFound += 1
					if tapesFound == 1 then
						task.delay(0.35, AudioController.play, "StingDiscovery")
					end
				elseif kind == "Key" then
					AudioController.play("PickupKey")
				else
					AudioController.play("PickupMetal")
				end
			end
		end
		for relayId, on in m.relays or {} do
			if before.relays and before.relays[relayId] ~= on then
				local relay = MapLayout.Relays[relayId]
				AudioController.play("Relay", { at = relay and toV3(relay.pos) + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0) or nil })
			end
		end
	end
	lastMission = nowIn and m or nil
end

local function onReport(payload)
	local report = payload and payload.report
	if not report then
		return
	end
	local success = report.result == "Success" or report.result == "Partial"
	AudioController.play(success and "StingSuccess" or "StingFail")
	local personal = payload.personal
	if personal and (personal.master or personal.clearance or #(personal.evidence or {}) > 0 or #(personal.challenges or {}) > 0) then
		task.delay(2.2, AudioController.play, "Unlock")
	end
end

local function onUI(name, open)
	if name == "menu" then
		AudioController.play(open and "MenuOpen" or "MenuClose", { dedupe = 0.2 })
	elseif name == "deck" then
		AudioController.play(open and "DeckOpen" or "DeckClose", { dedupe = 0.2 })
		setLayer("DeckHum", open and 1 or 0, 2, 3)
	elseif name == "tutorial" and open then
		AudioController.play("TutorialPage")
	elseif name == "report" and open then
		AudioController.play("ReportStamp")
		repeatPlay("ReportKey", 8, 0.07, nil)
	end
end

-- Lobby: a stamp when you ready up, a tick each second of the countdown.
local wasReady = false
local lastCount = nil
local function onParty(party)
	local me = nil
	for _, member in (party and party.members) or {} do
		if member.userId == Players.LocalPlayer.UserId then
			me = member
		end
	end
	local ready = me ~= nil and me.ready == true
	if ready and not wasReady then
		AudioController.play("Ready")
	end
	wasReady = ready
end

local function stepCountdown()
	local party = State.party
	if not party or not party.countdownEnd or State.inMission() then
		lastCount = nil
		return
	end
	local remaining = math.ceil(party.countdownEnd - State.now())
	if remaining > 0 and remaining ~= lastCount then
		lastCount = remaining
		AudioController.play("Countdown")
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Init
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function AudioController.init()
	master = Instance.new("SoundGroup")
	master.Name = "DeadAirMaster"
	master.Parent = SoundService
	for _, name in Audio.Groups do
		local g = Instance.new("SoundGroup")
		g.Name = name
		g.Volume = 0
		g.Parent = master
		groups[name] = g
	end
	emitterFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	emitterFolder.Name = "DeadAirSounds"
	emitterFolder.Parent = Workspace

	-- Every controller plays through the Sound bridge.
	Sound.play = AudioController.play
	Sound.loop = function(name, on)
		setLayer(name, on and 1 or 0, 8, 4)
	end

	State.cue:Connect(onCue)
	State.subtitle:Connect(onSubtitle)
	State.notify:Connect(function()
		AudioController.play("UINotify", { dedupe = 0.15 })
	end)
	State.missionChanged:Connect(onMission)
	State.reportReceived:Connect(onReport)
	State.uiChanged:Connect(onUI)
	State.partyChanged:Connect(onParty)

	local function watchCharacter(character)
		task.spawn(muteDefaultSteps, character)
	end
	local function watchPlayer(player)
		if player.Character then
			watchCharacter(player.Character)
		end
		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchCharacter)
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		watchPlayer(player)
	end
	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayer)

	local accum = 0
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		mixGroups(dt)
		stepLayers(dt)
		stepPresenter()
		stepOthers()
		accum += dt
		if accum >= 0.1 then
			accum = 0
			directMusic()
			directAmbience()
			directMachines()
			stepHeart()
			stepCountdown()
		end
	end)
end

return AudioController
```

## src/client/Controllers/CameraController.luau

```lua
--[[
	CameraController — how the first-person view feels. Presentation only.

	  Head bob     The view rises and falls with each step (up, down, up, down),
	               with a small sideways sway and roll. Sprinting bobs harder;
	               stopping settles the camera back smoothly.
	  Tension      One 0..1 value that builds while the Presenter is close,
	               hunting or in view, while the lights are out, while the Loop
	               runs and as the broadcast clock runs down. It rises fast and
	               drains slowly, so dread lingers after a close call.
	  From tension The vignette closes in and pulses like a heartbeat, the field
	               of view narrows a few degrees, the head drifts with uneven
	               breathing, and turning the camera smears with motion blur.
	  Shake        Short bursts from cues (hunts, lights failing).

	Every effect has its own setting (head bob, motion blur, vignette, camera
	shake, field of view) and low intensity mode scales them all down. The
	camera's own scripts own position and rotation; this controller only adds a
	local offset and roll after them each frame, and roll never changes the
	look direction, so nothing drifts.
]]

local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)

local CameraController = {}

local INSPECT_FOV = 40
local WALK_REFERENCE = 12 -- studs/s: the walk speed the bob is tuned for
local BOB_HEIGHT = 0.11 -- studs, at walking pace
local BOB_SWAY = 0.06
local BOB_ROLL = math.rad(0.6)
local STEP_LENGTH = 3.1 -- studs per footstep

CameraController.tension = 0
CameraController.inspecting = false
CameraController.onFootstep = nil -- optional callback(sprinting)

local gui, vignetteEdges, pulse
local blur, focus
local phase = 0
local bobAmount = 0
local lastStepSign = 1
local shake = 0
local breathT = 0
local lastLook = nil
local turnBlur = 0
local tensionTarget = 0
local sightCheckAt = 0
local inSight = false
local heartbeat = 0

local function myHumanoid()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function presenter()
	local model = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	if model and model:IsA("Model") and model.PrimaryPart then
		return model
	end
	return nil
end

function CameraController.shake(amount)
	shake = math.max(shake, amount * State.intensity("cameraShake"))
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Tension
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function roomLightMode(position)
	local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
	local roomId = MapLayout.roomAt(position.X, position.Z)
	local rooms = station and station:FindFirstChild("Rooms")
	local folder = roomId and rooms and rooms:FindFirstChild(roomId)
	return folder and folder:GetAttribute("LightMode")
end

local function computeTension(camera, root)
	if not State.inMission() or not State.isAlive() or not root then
		return 0
	end
	local t = 0
	local model = presenter()
	if model then
		local offset = model.PrimaryPart.Position - root.Position
		local distance = offset.Magnitude
		t = math.max(t, math.clamp(1 - distance / 60, 0, 1) * 0.85)
		if model:GetAttribute("State") == "Hunting" then
			t = math.max(t, 0.55 + math.clamp(1 - distance / 40, 0, 1) * 0.45)
		end
		-- Seeing it on screen spikes tension (checked a few times a second).
		local now = os.clock()
		if now >= sightCheckAt then
			sightCheckAt = now + 0.25
			inSight = false
			if distance < 70 and offset.Magnitude > 0.1 and camera.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(offset.Unit) > 0.55 then
				local params = RaycastParams.new()
				params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Players.LocalPlayer.Character, model }
				inSight = Workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, model.PrimaryPart.Position - camera.CFrame.Position, params) == nil
			end
		end
		if inSight then
			t = math.max(t, 0.5) + 0.25
		end
	end
	local light = roomLightMode(root.Position)
	if light == "off" then
		t += 0.22
	elseif light == "emergency" then
		t += 0.1
	end
	local m = State.mission
	if m and m.loop and m.loop.active then
		t += 0.15
	end
	if m and m.timeLimit and m.broadcastStart then
		local progress = math.clamp(State.broadcastClock() / m.timeLimit, 0, 1)
		t += progress * progress * 0.18
	end
	return math.clamp(t, 0, 1)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Vignette: four soft edges (no image assets); corners darken where they meet
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function buildVignette(playerGui)
	-- Below every other screen so it never darkens the HUD text.
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirVignette", 1, playerGui)
	vignetteEdges = {}
	local function edge(rotation, size, position, anchor)
		local frame = UI.new("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AnchorPoint = anchor,
			Position = position,
			Size = size,
			Parent = gui,
		})
		local gradient = UI.new("UIGradient", { Rotation = rotation, Parent = frame })
		table.insert(vignetteEdges, { frame = frame, gradient = gradient })
	end
	-- Each gradient runs from the screen edge (dark) towards the centre (clear).
	edge(90, UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.42), UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), Vector2.new(0, 0)) -- top
	edge(-90, UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.42), UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Vector2.new(0, 1)) -- bottom
	edge(0, UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 1), UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), Vector2.new(0, 0)) -- left
	edge(180, UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 1), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), Vector2.new(1, 0)) -- right
end

local function setVignette(strength)
	-- strength 0..1: how dark the very edge gets.
	local edgeAlpha = 1 - math.clamp(strength, 0, 0.95)
	local sequence = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, edgeAlpha),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 1 - (1 - edgeAlpha) * 0.35),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
	})
	for _, e in vignetteEdges do
		e.gradient.Transparency = sequence
		e.frame.Visible = strength > 0.01
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Per frame
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function ownView(camera, humanoid)
	return camera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom and humanoid ~= nil and camera.CameraSubject == humanoid
end

local function step(dt)
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then
		return
	end
	dt = math.min(dt, 0.1)
	local humanoid = myHumanoid()
	local root = humanoid and humanoid.Parent and humanoid.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local own = ownView(camera, humanoid)
	local settings = State.settings

	-- Tension rises quickly and drains slowly.
	tensionTarget = own and computeTension(camera, root) or 0
	local rate = tensionTarget > CameraController.tension and 1.6 or 0.22
	CameraController.tension += (tensionTarget - CameraController.tension) * math.min(1, dt * rate)
	local tension = CameraController.tension
	State.tension = tension

	-- Field of view: the setting, narrowed slightly by tension; inspect zooms.
	if camera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
		local base = settings.fieldOfView or 70
		local fov = CameraController.inspecting and INSPECT_FOV or (base - tension * 5 * State.intensity("distortion"))
		if math.abs(camera.FieldOfView - fov) > 0.05 then
			camera.FieldOfView += (fov - camera.FieldOfView) * math.min(1, dt * 8)
		end
	end

	-- Motion blur from how fast the view turns, stronger under tension.
	local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local turnRate = 0
	if lastLook then
		local cos = math.clamp(lastLook:Dot(look), -1, 1)
		turnRate = math.acos(cos) / math.max(dt, 1 / 240) -- radians per second
	end
	lastLook = look
	local blurSetting = State.intensity("motionBlur")
	local wanted = 0
	if own and blurSetting > 0 then
		wanted = math.clamp((turnRate - 1.5) * 1.2, 0, 6) * (0.6 + tension * 0.8) * blurSetting
		wanted += tension * tension * 1.5 * State.intensity("distortion")
	end
	turnBlur += (wanted - turnBlur) * math.min(1, dt * (wanted > turnBlur and 14 or 6))
	blur.Size = turnBlur
	-- Under tension the far end of the room goes soft: the world closes in.
	focus.FarIntensity = own and math.clamp(tension * 0.35 * State.intensity("distortion"), 0, 0.35) or 0

	-- Vignette: always slightly on, darker and pulsing as tension builds.
	local vig = State.intensity("vignette")
	heartbeat += dt * (1.1 + tension * 1.4)
	local beat = math.max(0, math.sin(heartbeat * math.pi * 2)) ^ 6
	local pulseAmount = tension > 0.45 and beat * (tension - 0.45) * 0.5 or 0
	pulse = (0.28 + tension * 0.42 + pulseAmount) * vig
	setVignette(own and pulse or 0.25 * vig)

	if not own then
		bobAmount = 0
		return
	end

	-- Head bob, phase-locked to distance travelled so it matches the steps.
	local velocity = root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
	local speed = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude
	local grounded = humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
	local moving = grounded and speed > 1 and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05
	local bobSetting = State.intensity("headBob")
	local targetAmount = moving and math.clamp(speed / WALK_REFERENCE, 0.4, 1.7) or 0
	bobAmount += (targetAmount - bobAmount) * math.min(1, dt * (moving and 6 or 4))
	if moving then
		phase += (speed * dt) / STEP_LENGTH * math.pi
		local sign = math.sin(phase) >= 0 and 1 or -1
		if sign ~= lastStepSign then
			lastStepSign = sign
			if CameraController.onFootstep then
				CameraController.onFootstep(speed > WALK_REFERENCE + 2)
			end
		end
	end
	local amount = bobAmount * bobSetting
	-- |sin| gives one dip per footstep: up, down, up, down.
	local bobY = (math.abs(math.sin(phase)) - 0.5) * 2 * BOB_HEIGHT * amount
	local swayX = math.sin(phase) * BOB_SWAY * amount
	local roll = math.sin(phase) * BOB_ROLL * amount

	-- Uneven breathing drift, mostly felt when standing still and tense.
	breathT += dt
	local breath = (0.25 + tension * 0.9) * bobSetting
	local breathY = (math.sin(breathT * 1.7) + math.sin(breathT * 2.9) * 0.35) * 0.018 * breath
	local breathRoll = math.sin(breathT * 0.9) * math.rad(0.25) * breath

	local shakeX, shakeY = 0, 0
	if shake > 0.001 then
		shakeX = (math.random() - 0.5) * shake
		shakeY = (math.random() - 0.5) * shake
		shake = math.max(0, shake - dt * 1.5)
	end
	-- High tension adds a faint tremor.
	local tremor = tension > 0.7 and (tension - 0.7) * 0.03 * State.intensity("cameraShake") or 0
	shakeX += (math.random() - 0.5) * tremor
	shakeY += (math.random() - 0.5) * tremor

	camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(swayX + shakeX, bobY + breathY + shakeY, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, roll + breathRoll)
end

function CameraController.init(playerGui)
	State.tension = 0
	buildVignette(playerGui)
	setVignette(0.25)
	blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
	blur.Name = "DeadAirMotionBlur"
	blur.Size = 0
	blur.Parent = Lighting
	focus = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
	focus.Name = "DeadAirTensionFocus"
	focus.FarIntensity = 0
	focus.NearIntensity = 0
	focus.FocusDistance = 18
	focus.InFocusRadius = 22
	focus.Parent = Lighting
	-- After the default camera scripts have placed the camera this frame.
	RunService:BindToRenderStep("DeadAirCameraFeel", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, step)
end

return CameraController
```

## src/client/Controllers/DeckController.luau

```lua
--[[
	DeckController — the Time-Shift Deck screen.

	Modes:
	  mission   at a physical deck: inspect tapes and commit edits
	  spectate  eliminated/extracted players: watch the team's tapes, no edits
	  handheld  the handheld monitor: live cameras only, no tapes, no edits
	  archive   field office replay of recovered tapes (original predictions),
	            hidden frames can be marked

	The client only proposes edits. The server validates and commits them; the
	new timeline arrives in the next mission snapshot and the footage updates.
]]

local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local Footage = require(Shared.Core.Footage)
local SignalModel = require(Shared.Core.SignalModel)
local Format = require(Shared.Util.Format)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)
local FootageView = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.FootageView)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Sound)

local DeckController = {}

local SPEEDS = { 1, 2, 4 }
local FRAME = 1 / Format.FRAMES_PER_SECOND
local WALK_AWAY = 16

local gui, root
local view
local widgets = {}
local session = nil -- { mode, deckId, caseDef, tapeId, cameraId, t, playing, speed, selected, proposed }
local renderConn = nil
local lastMarkerKey = nil

local function toV3(p)
	return Vector3.new(p.x, p.y or 0, p.z)
end

local function presenterPosition()
	for _, model in Workspace:GetChildren() do
		if model.Name == "Presenter" and model:IsA("Model") and model.PrimaryPart then
			return model.PrimaryPart.Position
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function snapshotTimeline()
	if session.mode == "archive" then
		return nil
	end
	local m = State.mission
	return m and m.timeline or nil
end

local function tapesAvailable()
	local list = {}
	local caseDef = session.caseDef
	if not caseDef then
		return list
	end
	for _, tapeId in caseDef.tapeOrder do
		local has
		if session.mode == "archive" then
			has = State.profile and State.profile.tapes and State.profile.tapes[tapeId]
		else
			has = State.hasTape(tapeId)
		end
		if has then
			table.insert(list, tapeId)
		end
	end
	return list
end

local function camerasForSession()
	if session.mode == "handheld" then
		return MapLayout.CameraOrder
	end
	local tape = session.tapeId and session.caseDef.tapes[session.tapeId]
	return tape and tape.cameras or {}
end

local function cameraQuality(cameraId)
	if session.mode == "archive" then
		return 85, nil
	end
	local m = State.mission
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	if not m or not camera then
		return 0, "NO CAMERA"
	end
	local presenter = presenterPosition()
	local deck = session.deckId and session.caseDef.decks[session.deckId]
	local relayOn = true
	if camera.relay == "WEST" or camera.relay == "EAST" then
		relayOn = m.relays and m.relays[camera.relay] ~= false
	end
	return SignalModel.cameraQuality({
		relay = camera.relay,
		powerOn = m.flags and m.flags.generator_restored == true,
		relayOn = relayOn,
		deckBattery = session.mode ~= "mission" or (deck and deck.battery == true),
		presenterDistance = presenter and (presenter - toV3(camera.pos)).Magnitude or nil,
		anomaly = m.loop and m.loop.active and table.find(camera.rooms, session.caseDef.loop.room) ~= nil,
	})
end

local function garble(text, quality)
	if quality >= SignalModel.CLEAR then
		return text
	end
	local out = {}
	for i = 1, #text do
		local ch = string.sub(text, i, i)
		if ch ~= " " and (i * 7 + #text) % 3 == 0 then
			table.insert(out, "#")
		else
			table.insert(out, ch)
		end
	end
	return table.concat(out)
end

local function tapeClock(tapeId, t)
	local tape = session.caseDef.tapes[tapeId]
	local m = State.mission
	local tl = snapshotTimeline()
	local anchor = tl and tl.anchors and tl.anchors[tape.anchor]
	if session.mode ~= "archive" and anchor and m and m.broadcastStart then
		return Format.timecode(anchor - m.broadcastStart + t), anchor
	end
	return "T+" .. Format.timecode(t), nil
end

local function eventById(eventId)
	local defs = State.eventDefs(session.caseDef, State.mission and State.mission.patrolFlip)
	return defs and defs[eventId]
end

local function eventRecord(eventId)
	local tl = snapshotTimeline()
	return tl and tl.events and tl.events[eventId]
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Building the screen
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function build(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirDeck", 10, playerGui)
	gui.Enabled = false
	root = UI.new("Frame", {
		Name = "Deck",
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.04,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Parent = gui,
	})
	UI.modalUnlock(root)
	UI.padding(root, 14, 12)

	-- Header
	local header = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), Parent = root })
	widgets.title = UI.text(header, "TIME-SHIFT DECK", {
		FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
		TextSize = Theme.LargeText,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
		Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0),
	})
	widgets.charges = UI.text(header, "", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.35, 0, 1, 0),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Cream,
	})
	widgets.close = UI.button(header, "X", function()
		DeckController.close()
	end, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 36) })

	local body = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 44), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -44), Parent = root })

	-- Left: tapes and cameras
	local left = UI.panel(body, { Size = UDim2.new(0.2, -8, 1, 0) })
	UI.padding(left, 8)
	UI.text(left, "TAPES", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18) })
	widgets.tapes = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.42, -22), Parent = left })
	UI.list(widgets.tapes, 4)
	UI.text(left, "CAMERAS", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.44, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18) })
	widgets.cameras = UI.scroll(left, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.44, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.56, -22) })
	UI.list(widgets.cameras, 4)

	-- Centre: footage, scrubber, transport
	local centre = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.52, -8, 1, 0), Parent = body })
	local screenHolder = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -104), Parent = centre })
	local screen = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Parent = screenHolder,
	})
	UI.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint", { AspectRatio = 4 / 3, Parent = screen })
	UI.stroke(screen, Theme.Line, 2)
	view = FootageView.new(screen)

	widgets.scrubber = UI.new("TextButton", {
		Text = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelLight,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -98),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
		Selectable = false,
		Parent = centre,
	})
	UI.stroke(widgets.scrubber, Theme.Line)
	widgets.markers = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = widgets.scrubber })
	widgets.nowMarker = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Cream,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = widgets.scrubber,
	})
	widgets.playhead = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 8),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -4),
		ZIndex = 4,
		Parent = widgets.scrubber,
	})
	widgets.scrubLabel = UI.text(centre, "", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -62),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
		TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim,
		TextSize = Theme.SmallText,
	})

	local transport = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -44), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), Parent = centre })
	UI.list(transport, 6, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center)
	local function tbtn(text, fn, width)
		return UI.button(transport, text, fn, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width or 56, 44) })
	end
	tbtn("-1s", function()
		DeckController.step(-1)
	end)
	tbtn("<|", function()
		DeckController.step(-FRAME)
	end)
	widgets.play = tbtn("PLAY", function()
		DeckController.setPlaying(not session.playing)
	end, 76)
	tbtn("|>", function()
		DeckController.step(FRAME)
	end)
	tbtn("+1s", function()
		DeckController.step(1)
	end)
	widgets.speed = tbtn("1x", function()
		local i = table.find(SPEEDS, session.speed) or 1
		session.speed = SPEEDS[(i % #SPEEDS) + 1]
	end, 48)
	widgets.mark = tbtn("MARK FRAME", function()
		DeckController.markFrame()
	end, 120)

	-- Right: event panel
	local right = UI.panel(body, { Position = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.28, 0, 1, 0) })
	UI.padding(right, 10)
	local scroll = UI.scroll(right, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -112) })
	UI.list(scroll, 6)
	widgets.eventTitle = UI.text(scroll, "", { FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextColor3 = Theme.Accent, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 1 })
	widgets.eventMeta = UI.text(scroll, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2 })
	widgets.eventState = UI.text(scroll, "", { AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 3 })
	widgets.alternatives = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 4, Parent = scroll })
	UI.list(widgets.alternatives, 4)
	widgets.proposed = UI.text(scroll, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.Cream, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 5 })
	widgets.warning = UI.text(scroll, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.Amber, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 6 })
	widgets.clue = UI.text(scroll, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.Text, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 7 })
	widgets.reason = UI.text(scroll, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.Danger, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 8 })

	local actions = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -104), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 104), Parent = right })
	widgets.commit = UI.button(actions, "COMMIT EDIT", function()
		DeckController.commit()
	end, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48), TextColor3 = Theme.Accent })
	widgets.cancel = UI.button(actions, "CANCEL", function()
		session.proposed = nil
		DeckController.refreshPanel()
	end, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 56), Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 0, 44) })
	UI.button(actions, "CLOSE", function()
		DeckController.close()
	end, { Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 0, 0, 56), Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 0, 44) })

	-- Scrubbing
	local dragging = false
	local scrubToken = 0
	local function scrubTo(x)
		local tape = session and session.tapeId and session.caseDef.tapes[session.tapeId]
		if not tape then
			return
		end
		local alpha = math.clamp((x - widgets.scrubber.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, widgets.scrubber.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
		DeckController.seek(alpha * tape.duration)
		-- The tape whines while it is dragged; it stops shortly after the drag does.
		Sound.loop("TapeScrub", true)
		scrubToken += 1
		local token = scrubToken
		task.delay(0.2, function()
			if token == scrubToken then
				Sound.loop("TapeScrub", false)
			end
		end)
	end
	widgets.scrubber.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			scrubTo(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	widgets.scrubber.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			scrubTo(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	widgets.scrubber.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = false
		end
	end)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Lists
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local lastTapesKey = nil

local function refreshTapes(force)
	local key = session.mode .. ":" .. tostring(session.tapeId) .. ":" .. table.concat(tapesAvailable(), ",")
	if not force and key == lastTapesKey then
		return
	end
	lastTapesKey = key
	UI.clear(widgets.tapes)
	if session.mode == "handheld" then
		UI.text(widgets.tapes, "LIVE FEEDS", { TextColor3 = Theme.Accent })
		UI.text(widgets.tapes, "Handheld monitors cannot play tapes or edit.", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40) })
		return
	end
	local tapes = tapesAvailable()
	if #tapes == 0 then
		UI.text(widgets.tapes, "No tapes recovered yet.", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40) })
	end
	for _, tapeId in tapes do
		local tape = session.caseDef.tapes[tapeId]
		local b = UI.button(widgets.tapes, tape.label .. "  " .. tape.title, function()
			DeckController.selectTape(tapeId)
		end, { TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left })
		UI.padding(b, 8, 0)
		if tapeId == session.tapeId then
			b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
		end
	end
end

local function refreshCameras()
	UI.clear(widgets.cameras)
	for _, cameraId in camerasForSession() do
		local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
		local quality = cameraQuality(cameraId)
		local b = UI.button(widgets.cameras, string.format("%s  %d%%", camera.label, quality), function()
			DeckController.selectCamera(cameraId)
		end, { TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 40) })
		UI.padding(b, 8, 0)
		b.Name = cameraId
		if cameraId == session.cameraId then
			b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
		end
	end
end

local function updateCameraLabels()
	for _, b in widgets.cameras:GetChildren() do
		if b:IsA("TextButton") then
			local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[b.Name]
			if camera then
				b.Text = string.format("%s  %d%%", camera.label, cameraQuality(b.Name))
			end
		end
	end
end

local function markerColour(marker)
	if marker.fired then
		return Theme.TextDim
	elseif marker.committed then
		return Theme.Cream
	elseif marker.editable then
		return Theme.Accent
	end
	return Theme.Amber
end

local function refreshMarkers(force)
	if session.mode == "handheld" or not session.tapeId then
		UI.clear(widgets.markers)
		lastMarkerKey = nil
		return
	end
	local tape = session.caseDef.tapes[session.tapeId]
	local quality = cameraQuality(session.cameraId)
	local markers = Footage.markers(session.caseDef, State.eventDefs(session.caseDef, State.mission and State.mission.patrolFlip), snapshotTimeline(), session.tapeId)
	local parts = { session.tapeId, tostring(quality >= SignalModel.WEAK) }
	for _, mk in markers do
		table.insert(parts, mk.eventId .. mk.state .. tostring(mk.fired) .. tostring(mk.committed ~= nil))
	end
	local key = table.concat(parts, "|")
	if not force and key == lastMarkerKey then
		return
	end
	lastMarkerKey = key
	UI.clear(widgets.markers)
	if quality < SignalModel.WEAK then
		return
	end
	for _, mk in markers do
		-- Diamond = editable event, circle = consequence.
		local b = UI.new("TextButton", {
			Text = "",
			AutoButtonColor = false,
			BackgroundColor3 = markerColour(mk),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(mk.t / tape.duration, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
			Rotation = mk.editable and 45 or 0,
			ZIndex = 5,
			Selectable = true,
			Parent = widgets.markers,
		})
		if not mk.editable then
			UI.new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = b })
		end
		if session.selected == mk.eventId then
			UI.stroke(b, Theme.Text, 2)
		end
		b.Activated:Connect(function()
			DeckController.selectEvent(mk.eventId)
		end)
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Event panel
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function commitBlockReason(ev, rec)
	local m = State.mission
	if session.mode ~= "mission" then
		return session.mode == "archive" and "Archive copies are read-only." or "Edits are only possible at a tape deck."
	end
	if not State.isAlive() then
		return "Spectators can watch the timeline but cannot edit it."
	end
	if not ev.isEditable then
		local source = ev.derivedFrom and eventById(ev.derivedFrom)
		return "This follows from " .. (source and source.label or "another event") .. ". Edit that instead."
	end
	if rec and rec.fired then
		return "This event has already happened."
	end
	if rec and rec.edits >= (ev.maxEdits or 1) then
		return "Already edited" .. (rec.committed and rec.committed.name and (" by " .. rec.committed.name) or "") .. "."
	end
	if rec and rec.locked then
		return "Locked — the event is about to play out."
	end
	for _, flag in ev.prerequisites or {} do
		if not (m.flags and m.flags[flag]) then
			return "Requires: " .. ((session.caseDef.flagLabels and session.caseDef.flagLabels[flag]) or flag) .. "."
		end
	end
	if (m.charges or 0) < (ev.editCost or 1) then
		return "No edit charges left."
	end
	if cameraQuality(session.cameraId) < SignalModel.WEAK then
		local any = false
		for _, cam in ev.cameras do
			if cameraQuality(cam) >= SignalModel.WEAK then
				any = true
			end
		end
		if not any then
			return "Signal too weak to edit. Reset the relay or try another camera."
		end
	end
	if not session.proposed then
		return "Choose an alternative state above."
	end
	return nil
end

function DeckController.refreshPanel()
	if not session then
		return
	end
	local m = State.mission
	if widgets.charges then
		if m and session.mode ~= "archive" then
			widgets.charges.Text = string.format("EDIT CHARGES %d / %d", m.charges or 0, m.maxCharges or 0)
		else
			widgets.charges.Text = session.mode == "archive" and "ARCHIVE COPY" or ""
		end
	end
	UI.clear(widgets.alternatives)
	local eventId = session.selected
	local ev = eventId and eventById(eventId)
	if not ev then
		widgets.eventTitle.Text = session.mode == "handheld" and "LIVE MONITOR" or "NO EVENT SELECTED"
		widgets.eventMeta.Text = session.mode == "handheld" and "Watching raises its awareness of you." or "Select a marker on the timeline. Diamond = editable · circle = consequence."
		widgets.eventState.Text = ""
		widgets.proposed.Text = ""
		widgets.warning.Text = ""
		widgets.clue.Text = ""
		widgets.reason.Text = ""
		widgets.commit.Visible = session.mode == "mission"
		UI.setEnabled(widgets.commit, false)
		widgets.cancel.Visible = false
		return
	end
	local rec = eventRecord(eventId)
	local current = (rec and rec.state) or ev.originalState
	local tape = session.caseDef.tapes[ev.tape]
	local clockText = tapeClock(ev.tape, ev.offset)
	widgets.eventTitle.Text = string.upper(ev.label)
	widgets.eventMeta.Text = string.format("%s · %s · %s · %s", tape.label, Format.relative(ev.offset), clockText, MapLayout.Rooms[ev.locationId] and MapLayout.Rooms[ev.locationId].label or ev.locationId)
	local lines = {
		"ORIGINAL: " .. (ev.stateLabels[ev.originalState] or ev.originalState),
		"PREDICTED: " .. (ev.stateLabels[current] or current),
	}
	if rec and rec.committed then
		table.insert(lines, string.format("EDITED BY %s", string.upper(rec.committed.name or "?")))
	end
	if rec and rec.fired then
		table.insert(lines, "STATUS: OCCURRED")
	elseif rec and rec.locked then
		table.insert(lines, "STATUS: LOCKED — IMMINENT")
	end
	if not ev.isEditable then
		table.insert(lines, "CONSEQUENCE — NOT DIRECTLY EDITABLE")
	end
	widgets.eventState.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")

	local reason = commitBlockReason(ev, rec)
	if ev.isEditable and session.mode == "mission" then
		for _, state in ev.allowedStates do
			if state ~= current then
				local b = UI.button(widgets.alternatives, "> " .. (ev.stateLabels[state] or state), function()
					session.proposed = state
					DeckController.refreshPanel()
				end, { TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
				if session.proposed == state then
					b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
				end
			end
		end
	end
	widgets.proposed.Text = session.proposed and ("PROPOSED: " .. (ev.stateLabels[session.proposed] or session.proposed)) or ""
	local quality = cameraQuality(session.cameraId)
	if ev.warning and #(ev.consequenceEventIds or {}) > 0 then
		widgets.warning.Text = ev.warning
		local clue = ev.clue or ""
		if State.mission and State.mission.mode == "Restricted" then
			clue = ""
		end
		widgets.clue.Text = clue ~= "" and ("CLUE: " .. garble(clue, quality)) or ""
	else
		widgets.warning.Text = ""
		widgets.clue.Text = ""
	end
	widgets.commit.Visible = session.mode == "mission" and ev.isEditable
	widgets.commit.Text = string.format("COMMIT EDIT (%d CHARGE%s)", ev.editCost or 1, (ev.editCost or 1) == 1 and "" or "S")
	UI.setEnabled(widgets.commit, reason == nil)
	widgets.cancel.Visible = session.proposed ~= nil
	widgets.reason.Text = reason or ""
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Rendering
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function liveActors(cameraId)
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	local actors = {}
	local presenter = presenterPosition()
	if presenter then
		local pos = { x = presenter.X, y = 0, z = presenter.Z }
		if MapLayout.inRooms(pos, camera.rooms, 1) then
			table.insert(actors, { kind = "Presenter", pos = pos, look = { x = camera.pos.x, y = 0, z = camera.pos.z } })
		end
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local character = player.Character
		local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hrp then
			local pos = { x = hrp.Position.X, y = 0, z = hrp.Position.Z }
			if MapLayout.inRooms(pos, camera.rooms, 0.5) then
				local look = hrp.Position + hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 4
				table.insert(actors, { kind = "Investigator", pos = pos, look = { x = look.X, y = 0, z = look.Z } })
			end
		end
	end
	return actors
end

local function liveLights(cameraId)
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
	local folder = station and station:FindFirstChild("Rooms") and station.Rooms:FindFirstChild(camera.rooms[1])
	return folder and folder:GetAttribute("LightMode") or "off"
end

local function render(dt)
	if not session then
		return
	end
	local intensity = State.intensity("distortion")
	local cameraId = session.cameraId
	if not cameraId then
		view:setTexts({ message = "SELECT A TAPE", timestamp = "", status = "" })
		view:step(dt)
		return
	end
	local quality, reason = cameraQuality(cameraId)
	view:setSignal(quality, intensity)
	if session.mode == "handheld" then
		if quality <= 0 then
			view:setActors({})
			view:setLights("off")
			view:setTexts({ message = reason or "NO CARRIER", timestamp = "LIVE", status = "" })
		else
			view:setActors(liveActors(cameraId))
			view:setLights(liveLights(cameraId))
			view:setTexts({
				message = quality < SignalModel.WEAK and (reason or "SIGNAL LOST") or "",
				timestamp = "LIVE " .. Format.clock(State.broadcastClock()),
				status = string.format("SIGNAL %d%% · %s", quality, SignalModel.band(quality)),
				hidden = "",
			})
		end
		view:step(dt)
		return
	end

	local tape = session.caseDef.tapes[session.tapeId]
	if session.playing then
		session.t = math.min(tape.duration, session.t + dt * session.speed)
		if session.t >= tape.duration then
			DeckController.setPlaying(false)
		end
	end
	widgets.play.Text = session.playing and "PAUSE" or "PLAY"
	widgets.speed.Text = session.speed .. "x"
	widgets.playhead.Position = UDim2.new(session.t / tape.duration, 0, 0, -4)

	local clock, anchor = tapeClock(session.tapeId, session.t)
	local nowRel = anchor and (State.now() - anchor) or nil
	if nowRel and nowRel >= 0 and nowRel <= tape.duration then
		widgets.nowMarker.Visible = true
		widgets.nowMarker.Position = UDim2.fromScale(nowRel / tape.duration, 0)
	else
		widgets.nowMarker.Visible = false
	end
	local tag
	if session.mode == "archive" then
		tag = "ARCHIVE"
	elseif not anchor then
		local labels = session.caseDef.flagLabels or {}
		tag = "AWAITING " .. string.upper(labels[tape.anchor] or string.gsub(tape.anchor, "_", " "))
	elseif nowRel and session.t <= nowRel then
		tag = "RECORDED"
	else
		tag = "PREDICTED"
	end
	widgets.scrubLabel.Text = string.format("%s · %s · FRAME %06d · %s", tape.label, Format.relative(session.t), Format.frameNumber(session.t), tag)

	if quality <= 0 then
		view:setActors({})
		view:setLights("off")
		view:setTexts({ message = reason or "NO CARRIER", timestamp = "PLAY " .. clock, status = "", hidden = "" })
	else
		local frame = Footage.frame(session.caseDef, State.eventDefs(session.caseDef, State.mission and State.mission.patrolFlip), snapshotTimeline(), session.tapeId, cameraId, session.t)
		view:setActors(frame.actors)
		view:setLights(frame.lights)
		view:setTexts({
			message = quality < SignalModel.WEAK and ((reason or "SIGNAL LOST") .. "\nMARKERS UNAVAILABLE") or "",
			timestamp = (session.playing and "PLAY " or "PAUSE ") .. clock,
			status = string.format("SIGNAL %d%% · %s%s", quality, SignalModel.band(quality), reason and (" · " .. reason) or ""),
			hidden = (frame.hiddenFrame and quality >= SignalModel.WEAK) and frame.hiddenFrame.text or "",
		})
	end
	view:step(dt)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Public API
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function DeckController.seek(t)
	if not session or not session.tapeId then
		return
	end
	local tape = session.caseDef.tapes[session.tapeId]
	session.t = math.clamp(t, 0, tape.duration)
end

-- Transport with its sounds: a frame (or second) step, and play/pause.
function DeckController.step(delta)
	if not session or not session.tapeId then
		return
	end
	DeckController.seek(session.t + delta)
	Sound.play("FrameStep")
end

function DeckController.setPlaying(on)
	if not session or session.playing == on then
		return
	end
	session.playing = on
	Sound.play(on and "DeckPlay" or "DeckPause")
end

function DeckController.selectTape(tapeId)
	if session.tapeId and session.tapeId ~= tapeId then
		Sound.play("TapeEject")
		task.delay(0.6, Sound.play, "TapeInsert")
	elseif session.tapeId ~= tapeId then
		Sound.play("TapeInsert")
	end
	session.tapeId = tapeId
	session.t = 0
	session.selected = nil
	session.proposed = nil
	local tape = session.caseDef.tapes[tapeId]
	session.cameraId = tape.cameras[1]
	view:setCamera(session.cameraId)
	if session.mode == "mission" then
		State.action("DeckOpen", { deck = session.deckId, tape = tapeId })
	end
	refreshTapes(true)
	refreshCameras()
	refreshMarkers(true)
	DeckController.refreshPanel()
end

function DeckController.selectCamera(cameraId)
	if session.cameraId ~= cameraId then
		Sound.play("ChannelSwitch")
	end
	session.cameraId = cameraId
	view:setCamera(cameraId)
	refreshCameras()
	refreshMarkers(true)
end

function DeckController.selectEvent(eventId)
	session.selected = eventId
	session.proposed = nil
	local ev = eventById(eventId)
	if ev then
		session.t = ev.offset
		if not table.find(ev.cameras, session.cameraId) and ev.cameras[1] then
			DeckController.selectCamera(ev.cameras[1])
		end
	end
	if session.mode ~= "archive" and session.mode ~= "handheld" then
		State.action("InspectEvent", { event = eventId })
	end
	DeckController.refreshPanel()
end

function DeckController.commit()
	if not session or not session.selected or not session.proposed then
		return
	end
	local ev = eventById(session.selected)
	local rec = eventRecord(session.selected)
	local result = State.request("CommitEdit", {
		event = session.selected,
		state = session.proposed,
		deck = session.deckId,
		expected = (rec and rec.state) or (ev and ev.originalState),
	})
	if result.ok then
		State.notify:Fire("Edit committed. The tape is updating.")
		session.proposed = nil
		State.cue:Fire({ cue = "LocalEditCommitted" })
	else
		widgets.reason.Text = result.message or "Edit refused."
		Sound.play("EditRejected")
	end
	DeckController.refreshPanel()
end

function DeckController.markFrame()
	if not session or not session.tapeId then
		return
	end
	Sound.play("MarkFrame")
	local result = State.request("MarkFrame", { tape = session.tapeId, camera = session.cameraId, t = session.t })
	State.notify:Fire(result.ok and ("Hidden frame marked: " .. (result.message or result.frame or "")) or (result.message or "Nothing unusual."))
end

--[[
	opts = { mode = "mission" | "spectate" | "handheld" | "archive", deckId, caseId, tapeId }
]]
function DeckController.open(opts)
	local caseDef = opts.caseId and CaseRegistry.get(opts.caseId) or State.caseDef() or CaseRegistry.get("case01")
	session = {
		mode = opts.mode,
		deckId = opts.deckId,
		caseDef = caseDef,
		tapeId = nil,
		cameraId = nil,
		t = 0,
		playing = false,
		speed = 1,
		selected = nil,
		proposed = nil,
	}
	local deck = opts.deckId and caseDef.decks[opts.deckId]
	if opts.mode == "handheld" then
		widgets.title.Text = "HANDHELD MONITOR · LIVE"
	elseif opts.mode == "archive" then
		widgets.title.Text = "EVIDENCE ARCHIVE · TAPE REPLAY"
	elseif opts.mode == "spectate" then
		widgets.title.Text = "TIME-SHIFT DECK · OBSERVER"
	else
		widgets.title.Text = "TIME-SHIFT DECK · " .. (deck and deck.label or "")
	end
	widgets.mark.Visible = opts.mode ~= "handheld"
	widgets.scrubber.Visible = opts.mode ~= "handheld"
	gui.Enabled = true
	State.setUI("deck", true)
	view:powerOn()
	if opts.mode == "mission" then
		State.action("DeckOpen", { deck = opts.deckId })
	elseif opts.mode == "handheld" then
		State.action("DeckOpen", { deck = "HANDHELD" })
	end
	if opts.mode == "handheld" then
		session.cameraId = MapLayout.CameraOrder[1]
		view:setCamera(session.cameraId)
		refreshTapes()
		refreshCameras()
		refreshMarkers(true)
	else
		local tapes = tapesAvailable()
		local first = opts.tapeId or tapes[#tapes]
		refreshTapes()
		if first then
			DeckController.selectTape(first)
		else
			refreshCameras()
			refreshMarkers(true)
		end
	end
	DeckController.refreshPanel()
	if GuiService:IsTenFootInterface() or #game:GetService("UserInputService"):GetConnectedGamepads() > 0 then
		GuiService.SelectedObject = widgets.close
	end
	if renderConn then
		renderConn:Disconnect()
	end
	local accum = 0
	renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		render(dt)
		accum += dt
		if accum > 0.5 then
			accum = 0
			updateCameraLabels()
			refreshMarkers(false)
			DeckController.refreshPanel()
			-- Walking away (or being taken off-air) closes the deck.
			if session and session.mode == "mission" then
				local deckDef = session.caseDef.decks[session.deckId]
				local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
				local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not State.isAlive() or not hrp or (deckDef and (hrp.Position - toV3(deckDef.pos)).Magnitude > WALK_AWAY) then
					DeckController.close()
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority("DeckKeys", function(_, inputState, input)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin or not session then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end
		local key = input.KeyCode
		if key == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
			DeckController.setPlaying(not session.playing)
		elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.Left or key == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft then
			DeckController.step(-FRAME)
		elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.Right or key == Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight then
			DeckController.step(FRAME)
		elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB or key == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace then
			DeckController.close()
		else
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end, false, 3000, Enum.KeyCode.Space, Enum.KeyCode.Left, Enum.KeyCode.Right, Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft, Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB, Enum.KeyCode.Backspace)
end

function DeckController.close()
	if not session then
		return
	end
	local mode = session.mode
	session = nil
	gui.Enabled = false
	State.setUI("deck", false)
	if renderConn then
		renderConn:Disconnect()
		renderConn = nil
	end
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction("DeckKeys")
	GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
	if mode == "mission" or mode == "handheld" then
		State.action("DeckClose")
	end
end

function DeckController.isOpen()
	return session ~= nil
end

-- Nearest physical deck within reach, or nil.
function DeckController.nearbyDeck()
	local caseDef = State.caseDef()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not caseDef or not hrp then
		return nil
	end
	local best, bestDist = nil, 10
	for deckId, deck in caseDef.decks do
		local d = (hrp.Position - toV3(deck.pos)).Magnitude
		if d < bestDist then
			best, bestDist = deckId, d
		end
	end
	return best
end

function DeckController.init(playerGui)
	build(playerGui)
	State.cue:Connect(function(cue)
		if cue.cue == "OpenDeck" and not session then
			DeckController.open({ mode = "mission", deckId = cue.deck })
		end
	end)
	State.missionChanged:Connect(function(m)
		if not session then
			return
		end
		if not m and session.mode ~= "archive" then
			DeckController.close()
			return
		end
		refreshTapes()
		refreshMarkers(false)
		DeckController.refreshPanel()
	end)
end

return DeckController
```

## src/client/Controllers/EffectsController.luau

```lua
--[[
	EffectsController — presentation only. Nothing here changes gameplay; every
	effect scales with the distortion / flicker / camera-shake / low-intensity
	settings so players can tone it down without losing information.
]]

local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
```
