```lua
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local Figures = require(Shared.World.Figures)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)
local FootageView = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.FootageView)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Sound)

local EffectsController = {}

local rng = Random.new()
local gui, staticFrame, staticDots, staticText
local grade
local staticUntil, staticStrength = 0, 0
local ghostFolder
local loopLastCycle = nil

local function presenter()
	local model = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	if model and model:IsA("Model") and model.PrimaryPart then
		return model
	end
	return nil
end

local function myRoot()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Static bursts
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function EffectsController.static(duration, strength, text)
	local scale = State.intensity("distortion")
	if scale <= 0 and not text then
		return
	end
	staticUntil = math.max(staticUntil, os.clock() + duration)
	staticStrength = math.max(staticStrength, strength * math.max(scale, text and 0.3 or 0))
	staticText.Text = text or ""
end

local function stepStatic()
	local now = os.clock()
	local active = now < staticUntil
	staticFrame.Visible = active
	if not active then
		staticStrength = 0
		staticText.Text = ""
		return
	end
	staticFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - staticStrength * 0.35
	for _, dot in staticDots do
		if rng:NextNumber() < staticStrength then
			dot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.6
			dot.Size = UDim2.new(rng:NextNumber() * 0.3, 0, 0, rng:NextInteger(1, 4))
			dot.Position = UDim2.fromScale(rng:NextNumber(), rng:NextNumber())
		else
			dot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Presenter face static
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local faceCells = {}

local function stepPresenterFace()
	local model = presenter()
	if not model then
		return
	end
	local gui3d = model:FindFirstChild("Static", true)
	if not gui3d or not gui3d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
		return
	end
	local cells = faceCells[gui3d]
	if not cells then
		cells = {}
		for y = 0, 5 do
			for x = 0, 7 do
				table.insert(cells, UI.new("Frame", {
					BorderSizePixel = 0,
					Position = UDim2.fromScale(x / 8, y / 6),
					Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 / 8, 1 / 6),
					Parent = gui3d,
				}))
			end
		end
		faceCells = { [gui3d] = cells }
	end
	local root = myRoot()
	if root and (root.Position - model.PrimaryPart.Position).Magnitude > 90 then
		return
	end
	local hunting = model:GetAttribute("State") == "Hunting"
	for _, cell in cells do
		local v = rng:NextNumber()
		local bright = hunting and (0.5 + v * 0.5) or (0.25 + v * 0.6)
		cell.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(bright, bright, bright * 1.02)
		cell.BackgroundTransparency = rng:NextNumber() * 0.3
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Fluorescent flicker (local only; the server owns whether a light is on)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local flickering = {}

local function stepFlicker()
	local flicker = State.intensity("flicker")
	local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
	local root = myRoot()
	if flicker <= 0 or not station or not root then
		return
	end
	local roomId = MapLayout.roomAt(root.Position.X, root.Position.Z)
	local roomFolder = roomId and station.Rooms:FindFirstChild(roomId)
	if not roomFolder or roomFolder:GetAttribute("LightMode") ~= "on" then
		return
	end
	local model = presenter()
	local near = model and (model.PrimaryPart.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 28
	local chance = (near and 0.25 or 0.012) * flicker
	if rng:NextNumber() > chance then
		return
	end
	local lights = roomFolder:FindFirstChild("Lights")
	if not lights then
		return
	end
	local fixtures = {}
	for _, p in lights:GetChildren() do
		if p:GetAttribute("LightKind") == "Fluorescent" then
			table.insert(fixtures, p)
		end
	end
	if #fixtures == 0 then
		return
	end
	local fixture = fixtures[rng:NextInteger(1, #fixtures)]
	if flickering[fixture] then
		return
	end
	flickering[fixture] = true
	local light = fixture:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Light")
	task.spawn(function()
		for _ = 1, rng:NextInteger(2, 5) do
			if light then
				light.Enabled = false
			end
			fixture.Transparency = 0.6
			task.wait(0.04 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.08)
			if roomFolder:GetAttribute("LightMode") == "on" then
				if light then
					light.Enabled = true
				end
			end
			fixture.Transparency = 0
			task.wait(0.05 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.12)
		end
		flickering[fixture] = nil
	end)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Hunt grading
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function stepGrade(dt)
	-- Driven by the camera's tension value so every effect agrees on it.
	local danger = State.tension or 0
	local model = presenter()
	if model and model:GetAttribute("State") == "Hunting" and State.inMission() then
		danger = math.max(danger, 0.5)
	end
	local scale = State.intensity("distortion")
	local targetSat = -danger * 0.45 * scale
	grade.Saturation += (targetSat - grade.Saturation) * math.min(1, dt * 3)
	grade.Contrast = danger * 0.15 * scale
	-- A cold shift as dread builds.
	grade.TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(214, 226, 236), danger * 0.6 * scale)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- The Loop's props
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function setVariant(container, visible)
	for _, d in container:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			d.Transparency = visible and (d:GetAttribute("BaseTransparency") or 0) or 1
		elseif d:IsA("SurfaceGui") or d:IsA("Light") then
			d.Enabled = visible
		end
	end
end

local function stepLoop()
	local m = State.mission
	local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
	local props = station and station:FindFirstChild("LoopProps")
	if not props then
		return
	end
	local loop = m and m.loop
	local odd = false
	local cycle = nil
	if loop and loop.active then
		local elapsed = State.now() - loop.cycleStart
		cycle = math.floor(elapsed / loop.cycleLength) + 1
		odd = cycle % 2 == 0
		if cycle ~= loopLastCycle then
			loopLastCycle = cycle
			EffectsController.static(0.35, 0.8)
			local caseDef = State.caseDef()
			for _, beat in (caseDef and caseDef.loop.beats) or {} do
				task.delay(beat.at, function()
					local still = State.mission and State.mission.loop and State.mission.loop.active
					if still and loopLastCycle == cycle then
						State.subtitle:Fire({ kind = "cue", text = beat.text })
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	else
		loopLastCycle = nil
	end
	for _, model in props:GetChildren() do
		local showOdd = odd and loop and loop.odd == model.Name
		local normal = model:FindFirstChild("Normal")
		local oddModel = model:FindFirstChild("Odd")
		if normal then
			setVariant(normal, not showOdd)
		end
		if oddModel then
			setVariant(oddModel, showOdd)
		end
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Rewind ghosts
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function playGhosts(payload)
	if not ghostFolder then
		ghostFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
		ghostFolder.Name = "DeadAirGhosts"
		ghostFolder.Parent = Workspace
	end
	for _, track in payload.tracks or {} do
		local model = Figures.investigator(ghostFolder, Color3.fromRGB(150, 170, 160))
		for _, d in model:GetDescendants() do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then
				d.Transparency = 0.65
				d.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
			end
		end
		local samples = track.samples
		local start = os.clock()
		local conn
		conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			local t = os.clock() - start
			if t > (payload.duration or 30) or model.Parent == nil then
				conn:Disconnect()
				model:Destroy()
				return
			end
			local a, b = samples[1], samples[#samples]
			for i = 1, #samples - 1 do
				if samples[i][1] <= t and samples[i + 1][1] > t then
					a, b = samples[i], samples[i + 1]
					break
				end
			end
			local span = math.max(0.001, b[1] - a[1])
			local alpha = math.clamp((t - a[1]) / span, 0, 1)
			local x = a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * alpha
			local z = a[3] + (b[3] - a[3]) * alpha
			local look = Vector3.new(a[4], 0, a[5])
			if look.Magnitude < 0.1 then
				look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
			end
			local at = Vector3.new(x, Figures.rootHeight(model), z)
			model:PivotTo(CFrame.lookAt(at, at + look))
		end)
	end
	EffectsController.static(0.6, 0.7)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- False safety (extraction) and going off-air
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local falseSafety

local function playFalseSafety()
	local root = myRoot()
	local lastPos = root and root.Position or Vector3.new(0, 0, -100)
	local overlay = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 20, Parent = gui })
	falseSafety = overlay
	TweenService:Create(overlay, TweenInfo.new(1.2), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
	State.cue:Fire({ cue = "LocalSilence", duration = 6 })
	task.delay(1.6, function()
		if overlay.Parent == nil then
			return
		end
		local holder = UI.new("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.45),
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.42, 0.42),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			ZIndex = 21,
			Parent = overlay,
		})
		UI.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint", { AspectRatio = 4 / 3, Parent = holder })
		local view = FootageView.new(holder)
		view:setCamera("CAM11")
		view:setLights("on")
		-- It shows you still inside.
		local inside = MapLayout.point("Transmitter", 0.45, 0.8)
		view:setActors({ { kind = "Investigator", pos = inside, look = { x = lastPos.X, y = 0, z = lastPos.Z } } })
		view:setTexts({ timestamp = "REC", status = "CAM 11 · LIVE", message = "" })
		local conn
		conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			view:setSignal(70, math.max(0.3, State.intensity("distortion")))
			view:step(dt)
		end)
		task.delay(3.4, function()
			conn:Disconnect()
			view:setTexts({ message = "END OF TRANSMISSION" })
			Sound.play("EndOfTransmission")
			view:setActors({})
			task.delay(1.5, function()
				TweenService:Create(overlay, TweenInfo.new(1), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
				task.delay(1.1, function()
					overlay:Destroy()
				end)
			end)
		end)
	end)
end

local function onCue(cue)
	local name = cue.cue
	local me = Players.LocalPlayer.UserId
	if name == "Eliminated" then
		if cue.userId == me then
			EffectsController.static(3, 1, "SIGNAL LOST")
		else
			EffectsController.static(0.4, 0.5)
		end
	elseif name == "LightsFail" then
		EffectsController.static(0.25, 0.5)
	elseif name == "LoopStart" or name == "LoopBreak" or name == "LoopReset" then
		EffectsController.static(0.5, 0.9)
	elseif name == "PresenterAppear" then
		local model = presenter()
		local root = myRoot()
		if model and root and (model.PrimaryPart.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 45 then
			EffectsController.static(0.25, 0.6)
		end
	elseif name == "HuntStart" then
		EffectsController.static(0.35, 0.7)
	elseif name == "EditCommitted" or name == "LocalEditCommitted" then
		EffectsController.static(0.3, 0.5)
	elseif name == "Extracted" then
		playFalseSafety()
	elseif name == "Revived" then
		EffectsController.static(1, 0.8, "RE-ENTRY · SIGNAL RESTORED")
	elseif name == "ReplayBriefing" then
		local caseDef = State.caseDef() or CaseRegistry.get("case01")
		for i, line in caseDef.briefing.lines do
			task.delay((i - 1) * 4, function()
				State.subtitle:Fire({ kind = "radio", stamp = "REPLAY", sourceId = "NBRD-HQ", channel = 2, text = line.text })
			end)
		end
	elseif name == "EditorShadow" and cue.userId == me then
		State.subtitle:Fire({ kind = "cue", text = "[ON THE RECEPTION MONITOR, SOMETHING STANDS BEHIND YOU]" })
	elseif name == "BroadcastStart" then
		EffectsController.static(0.6, 0.6)
	end
end

function EffectsController.init(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirOverlay", 40, playerGui)
	staticFrame = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 34, 30), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false, Parent = gui })
	staticDots = {}
	for i = 1, 60 do
		staticDots[i] = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = staticFrame })
	end
	staticText = UI.text(staticFrame, "", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		FontFace = Theme.FontTitle,
		TextSize = 40,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Cream,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4,
		ZIndex = 3,
	})

	grade = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
	grade.Name = "DeadAirHuntGrade"
	grade.Parent = Lighting

	State.cue:Connect(onCue)
	State.ghosts:Connect(playGhosts)
	State.missionChanged:Connect(function(m)
		if not m and ghostFolder then
			ghostFolder:ClearAllChildren()
		end
	end)

	local accum = 0
	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		stepStatic()
		stepGrade(dt)
		accum += dt
		if accum >= 0.08 then
			accum = 0
			stepPresenterFace()
			stepFlicker()
			stepLoop()
		end
	end)
	EffectsController.falseSafety = function()
		return falseSafety
	end
end

return EffectsController
```

## src/client/Controllers/HUDController.luau

```lua
--[[
	HUDController — the in-case heads-up display (layout from the brief):
	  top left      case name, objective, optional clue
	  top right     signal quality 0-100%
	  centre        crosshair
	  bottom left   edit charges
	  bottom centre active tool and control hint
	  bottom right  REC indicator, tape time, recording state
	Plus subtitles, the radio log, toasts and the team recovery vote.

	Danger is shown with signal flicker, timestamp jumps and an unexpected REC
	blink - never with red screen flashes alone - and scales with the player's
	distortion / low-intensity settings.
]]

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local ProfileSchema = require(Shared.Core.ProfileSchema)
local SignalModel = require(Shared.Core.SignalModel)
local Format = require(Shared.Util.Format)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)
local Controls = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Controls)

local HUDController = {}

local gui
local w = {}
local subtitles = {}
local toasts = {}
local voteVisible = false
local rng = Random.new()

HUDController.tool = nil -- set by ToolController
HUDController.stamina = 1

local function presenter()
	local model = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	if model and model:IsA("Model") and model.PrimaryPart then
		return model
	end
	return nil
end

local function corner(parent, anchor, position, size)
	local frame = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = anchor,
		Position = position,
		Size = size,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	return frame
end

local function shadowText(parent, text, props)
	local label = UI.text(parent, text, props)
	label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.45
	label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	return label
end

local function build(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirHUD", 5, playerGui)
	local safe = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = gui })
	UI.padding(safe, 18, 14)

	-- Top left: case and objective
	local tl = corner(safe, Vector2.new(0, 0), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30), UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0, 160))
	UI.list(tl, 2)
	w.caseName = shadowText(tl, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, LayoutOrder = 1 })
	w.objective = shadowText(tl, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.Text, TextSize = 18, FontFace = Theme.FontBold, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2 })
	w.clue = shadowText(tl, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.AccentDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 3 })
	w.optional = shadowText(tl, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 4 })

	-- Top right: signal
	local tr = corner(safe, Vector2.new(1, 0), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), UDim2.fromOffset(220, 60))
	w.signal = shadowText(tr, "SIGNAL 100%", { TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextSize = 20, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24) })
	local bar = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelLight, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 28), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6), Parent = tr })
	w.signalFill = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = bar })
	w.signalBand = shadowText(tr, "", { TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 36), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18) })

	-- Radio log (right, below signal)
	w.radio = corner(safe, Vector2.new(1, 0), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 110), UDim2.new(0.36, 0, 0, 170))
	UI.list(w.radio, 2)

	-- Centre crosshair
	w.crosshair = UI.new("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = gui,
	})
	UI.corner(w.crosshair, 2)

	-- Bottom left: charges
	local bl = corner(safe, Vector2.new(0, 1), UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(240, 56))
	w.chargesLabel = shadowText(bl, "EDIT CHARGES", { TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18) })
	w.chargeRow = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), Parent = bl })
	UI.list(w.chargeRow, 6, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal)

	-- Bottom centre: tool, hint, stamina, subtitles
	local bc = corner(safe, Vector2.new(0.5, 1), UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 220))
	w.subtitles = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140), Parent = bc })
	UI.list(w.subtitles, 4, Enum.FillDirection.Vertical, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center).VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
	w.tool = shadowText(bc, "", { TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, FontFace = Theme.FontBold, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -50), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20) })
	w.hint = shadowText(bc, "", { TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -28), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18) })
	local stamina = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelLight, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 3), Parent = bc })
	w.staminaFill = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Cream, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = stamina })
	w.staminaBar = stamina

	-- Bottom right: REC and tape time
	local br = corner(safe, Vector2.new(1, 1), UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(260, 56))
	w.recDot = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Danger, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -150, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), Parent = br })
	UI.corner(w.recDot, 6)
	w.rec = shadowText(br, "REC", { TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextSize = 20, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24) })
	w.recState = shadowText(br, "", { TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 28), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18) })

	-- Toasts (top centre)
	w.toasts = corner(gui, Vector2.new(0.5, 0), UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 70), UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 120))
	UI.list(w.toasts, 4, Enum.FillDirection.Vertical, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center)

	-- Centre banner (phase changes)
	w.banner = shadowText(gui, "", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.32),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 40),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		FontFace = Theme.FontTitle,
		TextSize = 30,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Cream,
		TextTransparency = 1,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 1,
	})

	-- Team recovery vote
	w.vote = UI.panel(gui, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.2, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(380, 130), Visible = false })
	UI.padding(w.vote, 10)
	w.voteText = UI.text(w.vote, "", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60), TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top })
	UI.button(w.vote, "AGREE (Y)", function()
		HUDController.answerVote(true)
	end, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -44), Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 0, 44) })
	UI.button(w.vote, "DECLINE (N)", function()
		HUDController.answerVote(false)
	end, { Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 0, 1, -44), Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 0, 44) })
	w.voteModal = UI.modalUnlock(w.vote)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Subtitles, radio and toasts
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function formatRadio(line)
	return string.format("[%s] %s · CH%d: %s", line.stamp or "--:--:--", line.sourceId or "?", line.channel or 0, line.text)
end

function HUDController.subtitle(line)
	if not State.settings.subtitles and line.kind ~= "radio" then
		return
	end
	if not State.settings.subtitles and line.kind == "radio" and State.settings.radioLog then
		-- Radio is still readable in the log.
		return
	end
	local text = line.kind == "radio" and formatRadio(line) or line.text
	local label = UI.text(w.subtitles, text, {
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextColor3 = line.kind == "radio" and Theme.Cream or Theme.Text,
		TextSize = 17,
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		LayoutOrder = math.floor(os.clock() * 100),
	})
	UI.padding(label, 8, 3)
	table.insert(subtitles, label)
	while #subtitles > 3 do
		table.remove(subtitles, 1):Destroy()
	end
	task.delay(line.kind == "radio" and 8 or 5, function()
		local index = table.find(subtitles, label)
		if index then
			table.remove(subtitles, index)
			label:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end

function HUDController.toast(text)
	local label = UI.text(w.toasts, text, {
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.25,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
		LayoutOrder = math.floor(os.clock() * 100),
	})
	UI.padding(label, 10, 4)
	UI.stroke(label, Theme.Line)
	table.insert(toasts, label)
	while #toasts > 3 do
		table.remove(toasts, 1):Destroy()
	end
	task.delay(4.5, function()
		local index = table.find(toasts, label)
		if index then
			table.remove(toasts, index)
			label:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end

function HUDController.banner(text, seconds)
	w.banner.Text = text
	w.banner.TextTransparency = 0
	w.banner.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
	task.delay(seconds or 3, function()
		if w.banner.Text == text then
			w.banner.TextTransparency = 1
			w.banner.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
		end
	end)
end

local function refreshRadio(m)
	UI.clear(w.radio)
	if not m or not State.settings.radioLog then
		return
	end
	local log = m.radioLog or {}
	local first = math.max(1, #log - 3)
	for i = first, #log do
		local entry = log[i]
		UI.text(w.radio, formatRadio(entry), {
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
			TextSize = Theme.SmallText,
			TextColor3 = Theme.Cream,
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5,
			LayoutOrder = i,
		})
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Vote
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function HUDController.answerVote(yes)
	State.action("TeamVoteReply", { yes = yes })
	voteVisible = false
	w.vote.Visible = false
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Per-frame update
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function controlHint()
	if not State.settings.tips then
		return ""
	end
	local device = Controls.device()
	if device == "touch" then
		return "Tap prompts to interact · buttons on the right for tools"
	end
	local parts = {}
	for _, action in { "Interact", "Deck", "Tool", "Flashlight", "Sprint", "Inspect", "Menu" } do
		table.insert(parts, Controls.tag(action, device) .. " " .. action)
	end
	return table.concat(parts, " · ")
end

local function setCharges(m)
	UI.clear(w.chargeRow)
	for i = 1, m.maxCharges or 0 do
		local full = i <= (m.charges or 0)
		local box = UI.new("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = full and Theme.Accent or Theme.PanelLight,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22),
			LayoutOrder = i,
			Parent = w.chargeRow,
		})
		UI.stroke(box, Theme.Accent, 1, full and 0 or 0.4)
	end
	UI.text(w.chargeRow, string.format("%d / %d", m.charges or 0, m.maxCharges or 0), {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 22),
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5,
		LayoutOrder = 99,
	})
end

local lastCharges = nil

local function onMission(m)
	if not m then
		w.caseName.Text = "NATIONAL BROADCAST RECOVERY DIVISION · FIELD OFFICE"
		w.objective.Text = "Walk to a terminal, or press [Tab] for the menu."
		w.clue.Text = ""
		w.optional.Text = ""
		UI.clear(w.chargeRow)
		w.chargesLabel.Visible = false
		lastCharges = nil
		refreshRadio(nil)
		return
	end
	local caseDef = CaseRegistry.get(m.caseId)
	w.chargesLabel.Visible = true
	w.caseName.Text = string.format("CASE %02d · %s · %s", caseDef.number, caseDef.title, m.modeLabel or "")
	if m.phase == "Briefing" then
		w.objective.Text = "Listen to the briefing. Take the tape from the reception desk."
	elseif m.objective then
		w.objective.Text = m.objective.text
	else
		w.objective.Text = ""
	end
	w.clue.Text = m.objective and m.objective.clue or ""
	w.optional.Text = table.concat(m.optional or {}, "\n")
	local key = tostring(m.charges) .. "/" .. tostring(m.maxCharges)
	if key ~= lastCharges then
		lastCharges = key
		setCharges(m)
	end
	refreshRadio(m)
	if m.vote and State.isAlive() and not voteVisible then
		voteVisible = true
		w.vote.Visible = true
		w.voteText.Text = string.format("%s asked Division for a TEAM RECOVERY.\nEveryone still inside must agree. (%d/%d)", m.vote.proposer, m.vote.yes, m.vote.needed)
	elseif not m.vote and voteVisible then
		voteVisible = false
		w.vote.Visible = false
	end
end

local function update()
	local m = State.mission
	local inCase = m ~= nil and State.inMission()
	gui.Enabled = not State.ui.menu and not State.ui.deck and not State.ui.report
	w.crosshair.Visible = inCase and State.isAlive() and State.settings.crosshair ~= false
	w.staminaBar.Visible = HUDController.stamina < 0.99
	w.staminaFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(HUDController.stamina, 1)

	-- Signal meter and danger cues.
	local signal = State.player:GetAttribute("Signal") or 100
	local model = presenter()
	local hunting = model and model:GetAttribute("State") == "Hunting"
	local distortion = State.intensity("distortion")
	local flicker = State.intensity("flicker")
	local shown = signal
	if inCase and signal < 60 and flicker > 0 and rng:NextNumber() < 0.25 * flicker then
		shown = math.clamp(signal + rng:NextInteger(-12, 12), 0, 100)
	end
	w.signal.Text = string.format("SIGNAL %d%%", inCase and shown or 100)
	w.signalFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale((inCase and signal or 100) / 100, 1)
	local band = SignalModel.band(signal)
	w.signalFill.BackgroundColor3 = band == "CLEAR" and Theme.Accent or (band == "WEAK" and Theme.Amber or Theme.Danger)
	w.signalBand.Text = inCase and band or ""

	-- REC and tape clock.
	if inCase then
		local clock = State.broadcastClock()
		if hunting and distortion > 0 and rng:NextNumber() < 0.15 * distortion then
			clock += rng:NextInteger(-90, 90) -- timestamp jump
		end
		if m.phase == "Briefing" then
			w.rec.Text = "STBY 00:00:00"
			w.recState.Text = "PRE-BROADCAST"
		else
			w.rec.Text = "REC " .. Format.clock(clock)
			w.recState.Text = m.phase == "ExtractionAvailable" and "BROADCAST ENDING" or "BROADCAST LIVE"
		end
		local blink = (os.clock() % 1) < 0.5
		if model and model.PrimaryPart and distortion > 0 then
			local character = State.player.Character
			local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if hrp and (hrp.Position - model.PrimaryPart.Position).Magnitude < 30 then
				blink = rng:NextNumber() < 0.5 -- unexpected REC flicker
			end
		end
		w.recDot.Visible = blink
	else
		w.rec.Text = os.date("!%H:%M:%S")
		w.recState.Text = "FIELD OFFICE"
		w.recDot.Visible = false
	end

	local tool = HUDController.tool
	local toolLabel = tool and ProfileSchema.Equipment[tool] and ProfileSchema.Equipment[tool].label or ""
	w.tool.Text = toolLabel ~= "" and ("TOOL: " .. toolLabel) or ""
	w.hint.Text = controlHint()
end

function HUDController.init(playerGui)
	build(playerGui)
	onMission(State.mission)
	State.missionChanged:Connect(onMission)
	State.settingsChanged:Connect(function()
		onMission(State.mission)
	end)
	State.subtitle:Connect(HUDController.subtitle)
	State.notify:Connect(HUDController.toast)
	State.cue:Connect(function(cue)
		if cue.cue == "BroadcastStart" then
			HUDController.banner("THE BROADCAST HAS BEGUN", 3.5)
		elseif cue.cue == "PowerOn" then
			HUDController.banner("STATION POWER RESTORED", 2.5)
		elseif cue.cue == "TransmitterArmed" then
			HUDController.banner("TRANSMITTER ARMED", 2.5)
		elseif cue.cue == "TeamVote" then
			onMission(State.mission)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if processed or not voteVisible then
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Y then
			HUDController.answerVote(true)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.N then
			HUDController.answerVote(false)
		end
	end)
	local accum = 0
	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		accum += dt
		if accum >= 0.1 then
			accum = 0
			update()
		end
	end)
end

return HUDController
```

## src/client/Controllers/InputController.luau

```lua
--[[
	InputController — controls from the brief, on every device.

	Action        PC              Mobile             Controller
	Interact      E               tap prompt         X (ProximityPrompt)
	Equip tool    1               Tool button        D-pad up
	Open deck     R               Deck button        Y
	Sprint        Shift (hold)    Sprint button      Left-stick click
	Inspect       hold RMB        hold Inspect       hold L2
	Flashlight    F               Light button       D-pad down
	Menu          Tab / P         Menu button        Select
	(Esc stays the Roblox menu; Roblox reserves it.)
]]

local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local HUDController = require(script.Parent.HUDController)
local CameraController = require(script.Parent.CameraController)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Sound)

local InputController = {}

local WALK_SPEED = 12
local SPRINT_SPEED = 19
local STAMINA_SECONDS = 6
local STAMINA_REGEN_DELAY = 1

local sprinting = false
local stamina = 1
local lastSprintAt = 0
local flashlight = false
local inspecting = false

InputController.onTool = nil -- set by ToolController
InputController.onDeck = nil -- set by Main
InputController.onMenu = nil -- set by Main

local function humanoid()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function blocked()
	return State.anyModal()
end

-- Local beam that follows the camera (so it tilts with the view in first
-- person). Other players see the server's head-mounted light instead.
local beamPart, beam

local function ensureBeam()
	if beamPart then
		return
	end
	beamPart = Instance.new("Part")
	beamPart.Name = "DeadAirFlashlight"
	beamPart.Anchored = true
	beamPart.CanCollide = false
	beamPart.CanQuery = false
	beamPart.CanTouch = false
	beamPart.Transparency = 1
	beamPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
	beamPart.Parent = Workspace
	beam = Instance.new("SpotLight")
	beam.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
	beam.Angle = 55
	beam.Range = 38
	beam.Brightness = 2.2
	beam.Color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 230, 210)
	beam.Shadows = true
	beam.Enabled = false
	beam.Parent = beamPart
end

function InputController.setFlashlight(on)
	if on ~= flashlight then
		Sound.play(on and "FlashlightOn" or "FlashlightOff")
	end
	flashlight = on
	ensureBeam()
	beam.Enabled = on
	State.action("Flashlight", { on = on })
end

-- Keep our own copy of the server's head light off locally (no double beam).
local function muteOwnHeadLight(character)
	local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 10)
	local light = head and head:WaitForChild("Flashlight", 10)
	if light then
		light.Enabled = false
		light:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
			if light.Enabled then
				light.Enabled = false
			end
		end)
	end
end

function InputController.shake(amount)
	CameraController.shake(amount)
end

function InputController.isSprinting()
	return sprinting
end

local function bind(name, fn, touchTitle, ...)
	ContextActionService:BindAction(name, function(_, inputState, input)
		return fn(inputState, input)
	end, touchTitle ~= nil, ...)
	if touchTitle then
		ContextActionService:SetTitle(name, touchTitle)
	end
end

local function setupBindings()
	bind("DA_Sprint", function(inputState)
		-- Hold to sprint, or press once to toggle (Settings > Toggle sprint).
		if State.settings.toggleSprint then
			if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
				sprinting = not sprinting
			end
		elseif inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			sprinting = true
		elseif inputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
			sprinting = false
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end, "Sprint", Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, Enum.KeyCode.RightShift, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL3)

	bind("DA_Flashlight", function(inputState)
		if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and not blocked() then
			InputController.setFlashlight(not flashlight)
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end, "Light", Enum.KeyCode.F, Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown)

	bind("DA_Tool", function(inputState)
		if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and not blocked() and InputController.onTool then
			InputController.onTool()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end, "Tool", Enum.KeyCode.One, Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp)

	bind("DA_Deck", function(inputState)
		if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and InputController.onDeck then
			InputController.onDeck()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end, "Deck", Enum.KeyCode.R, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonY)

	bind("DA_Inspect", function(inputState)
		if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and not blocked() then
			inspecting = true
			Sound.play("Inspect")
		elseif inputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
			inspecting = false
		end
		CameraController.inspecting = inspecting
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end, "Inspect", Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2)

	bind("DA_Menu", function(inputState)
		if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and InputController.onMenu then
			InputController.onMenu()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end, "Menu", Enum.KeyCode.Tab, Enum.KeyCode.P, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect)

	-- Touch button layout (right side, large targets).
	local positions = {
		DA_Sprint = UDim2.new(1, -170, 1, -230),
		DA_Inspect = UDim2.new(1, -95, 1, -230),
		DA_Flashlight = UDim2.new(1, -245, 1, -160),
		DA_Tool = UDim2.new(1, -245, 1, -230),
		DA_Deck = UDim2.new(1, -170, 1, -300),
		DA_Menu = UDim2.new(1, -95, 1, -300),
	}
	for name, pos in positions do
		pcall(function()
			ContextActionService:SetPosition(name, pos)
		end)
	end
end

local function step(dt)
	local h = humanoid()
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if blocked() and inspecting then
		inspecting = false
		CameraController.inspecting = false
	end
	if h then
		local moving = h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1
		-- A toggled sprint ends when you stop moving or run out of stamina.
		if State.settings.toggleSprint and sprinting and (not moving or stamina <= 0) then
			sprinting = false
		end
		local canSprint = sprinting and moving and stamina > 0 and not blocked() and (not State.inMission() or State.isAlive())
		if canSprint then
			local before = stamina
			stamina = math.max(0, stamina - dt / STAMINA_SECONDS)
			lastSprintAt = os.clock()
			if before > 0 and stamina <= 0 then
				Sound.play("BreathOut") -- winded
			end
		elseif os.clock() - lastSprintAt > STAMINA_REGEN_DELAY then
			stamina = math.min(1, stamina + dt / (STAMINA_SECONDS * 1.3))
		end
		local target = canSprint and SPRINT_SPEED or WALK_SPEED
		if h.WalkSpeed > 0 and h.WalkSpeed ~= target then
			h.WalkSpeed = target
		end
	end
	HUDController.stamina = stamina
	if beamPart and camera then
		beamPart.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0.4, -0.4, 0)
		beam.Enabled = flashlight and State.inMission() and State.isAlive() or (flashlight and not State.inMission())
	end
end

function InputController.isFlashlightOn()
	return flashlight
end

function InputController.init()
	pcall(function()
		StarterGui:SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", false)
	end)
	setupBindings()
	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(step)
	State.settingsChanged:Connect(function(settings)
		UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity = settings.sensitivity
	end)
	UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity = State.settings.sensitivity
	State.cue:Connect(function(cue)
		if cue.cue == "HuntStart" then
			InputController.shake(0.25)
		elseif cue.cue == "LightsFail" or cue.cue == "LoopStart" then
			InputController.shake(0.12)
		end
	end)
	if Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
		task.spawn(muteOwnHeadLight, Players.LocalPlayer.Character)
	end
	Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		task.spawn(muteOwnHeadLight, character)
		stamina = 1
		if flashlight then
			task.delay(1, function()
				State.action("Flashlight", { on = flashlight })
			end)
		end
	end)
end

return InputController
```

## src/client/Controllers/KeypadController.luau

```lua
-- KeypadController — the parts cage keypad (generator room).

local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Sound)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)

local KeypadController = {}

local DIGITS = 4

local gui, display, message
local entry = ""

local function render()
	local shown = entry .. string.rep("_", DIGITS - #entry)
	display.Text = string.gsub(shown, ".", "%0 ")
end

local function close()
	gui.Enabled = false
	State.setUI("keypad", false)
	GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
end

local function submit()
	if #entry < DIGITS then
		message.Text = "Enter all four digits."
		return
	end
	local code = entry
	entry = ""
	render()
	message.Text = "..."
	local result = State.request("SubmitCode", { code = code })
	if result.ok then
		Sound.play("KeypadRight")
		close()
	else
		Sound.play("KeypadWrong")
		message.Text = result.message or "Rejected."
	end
end

local function press(key)
	Sound.play("KeypadPress")
	if key == "C" then
		entry = ""
	elseif key == "OK" then
		submit()
		return
	elseif #entry < DIGITS then
		entry ..= key
	end
	message.Text = ""
	render()
end

function KeypadController.open()
	entry = ""
	render()
	message.Text = "Division's code comes over Channel 2 with the live clock."
	gui.Enabled = true
	State.setUI("keypad", true)
	if #UserInputService:GetConnectedGamepads() > 0 then
		GuiService.SelectedObject = gui:FindFirstChild("Key5", true)
	end
end

function KeypadController.init(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirKeypad", 25, playerGui)
	gui.Enabled = false
	local panel = UI.panel(gui, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 470) })
	UI.modalUnlock(panel)
	UI.padding(panel, 14)
	UI.text(panel, "PARTS CAGE · KEYPAD", { FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextColor3 = Theme.Accent, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24) })
	display = UI.text(panel, "", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
		TextSize = 34,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
	})
	local grid = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 90), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 280), Parent = panel })
	UI.new("UIGridLayout", { CellSize = UDim2.new(0.31, 0, 0, 62), CellPadding = UDim2.new(0.035, 0, 0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = grid })
	for i, key in { "1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "6", "7", "8", "9", "C", "0", "OK" } do
		local b = UI.button(grid, key, function()
			press(key)
		end, { LayoutOrder = i, TextSize = 24 })
		b.Name = "Key" .. key
	end
	message = UI.text(panel, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 376), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), TextColor3 = Theme.Amber, TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
	UI.button(panel, "CLOSE", close, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -44), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44) })

	State.cue:Connect(function(cue)
		if cue.cue == "OpenKeypad" then
			KeypadController.open()
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if not gui.Enabled or processed then
			return
		end
		local map = {
			[Enum.KeyCode.Zero] = "0", [Enum.KeyCode.One] = "1", [Enum.KeyCode.Two] = "2", [Enum.KeyCode.Three] = "3",
			[Enum.KeyCode.Four] = "4", [Enum.KeyCode.Five] = "5", [Enum.KeyCode.Six] = "6", [Enum.KeyCode.Seven] = "7",
			[Enum.KeyCode.Eight] = "8", [Enum.KeyCode.Nine] = "9",
			[Enum.KeyCode.KeypadZero] = "0", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadOne] = "1", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadTwo] = "2",
			[Enum.KeyCode.KeypadThree] = "3", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadFour] = "4", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadFive] = "5",
			[Enum.KeyCode.KeypadSix] = "6", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadSeven] = "7", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEight] = "8",
			[Enum.KeyCode.KeypadNine] = "9",
		}
		if map[input.KeyCode] then
			press(map[input.KeyCode])
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter then
			submit()
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace then
			press("C")
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB then
			close()
		end
	end)
	State.missionChanged:Connect(function(m)
		if gui.Enabled and (not m or not State.isAlive()) then
			close()
		end
	end)
end

return KeypadController
```

## src/client/Controllers/MenuController.luau

```lua
--[[
	MenuController — main menu, field office pages and the in-case pause menu.

	Main menu: a slow camera pan across the dead control room, the Division
	header, DEAD AIR / CHANNEL ZERO // SIGNAL DETECTED, and Case Select,
	Evidence Archive, Equipment, Settings, Credits (plus Store and "Enter the
	field office"). All buttons are large, controller-selectable and touch-sized.
]]

local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local Cosmetics = require(Shared.Config.Cosmetics)
local Evidence = require(Shared.Config.Evidence)
local ProfileSchema = require(Shared.Core.ProfileSchema)
local Format = require(Shared.Util.Format)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)
local Controls = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Controls)

local MenuController = {}

local gui, root, content, nav, statusLine, title
local page = nil
local panConn = nil
local DeckController -- set in init (avoids a require cycle)

local function gamepadMode()
	return #UserInputService:GetConnectedGamepads() > 0
end

local function heading(parent, text, order)
	return UI.text(parent, text, {
		FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
		TextSize = Theme.LargeText,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),
		LayoutOrder = order or 0,
	})
end

local function para(parent, text, order, colour)
	return UI.text(parent, text, {
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		TextColor3 = colour or Theme.Text,
		LayoutOrder = order or 0,
	})
end

local function row(parent, order, height)
	local f = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height or 44), LayoutOrder = order or 0, Parent = parent })
	UI.list(f, 6, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal)
	return f
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Camera pan across the dead control room
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function startPan()
	if panConn then
		return
	end
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
	local t0 = os.clock()
	panConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local t = (os.clock() - t0) * 0.05
		local x = math.sin(t) * 12
		local pos = Vector3.new(x, 8.5, 15)
		local look = Vector3.new(x * 0.4, 4.5, -18)
		camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, look)
		camera.FieldOfView = 60
	end)
end

local function stopPan()
	if panConn then
		panConn:Disconnect()
		panConn = nil
	end
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
	camera.FieldOfView = State.settings.fieldOfView or 70
	local character = State.player.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if humanoid then
		camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Pages
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local Pages = {}

function Pages.Home(c)
	heading(c, "WELCOME, INVESTIGATOR", 1)
	local p = State.profile
	if p then
		para(c, string.format("CLEARANCE %d · RECOVERY CREDITS %d · RESEARCH %d", p.clearance, p.credits, p.research), 2, Theme.Cream)
		if p.offline then
			para(c, "Progress will not be saved this session.", 3, Theme.Amber)
		end
		local completed = p.cases and p.cases.case01 and p.cases.case01.completed or 0
		if completed == 0 then
			para(c, "Start with CASE SELECT. Case 01 teaches everything: tapes, edits, the Presenter and extraction.", 4, Theme.TextDim)
		end
	else
		para(c, "Connecting to Division...", 2, Theme.TextDim)
	end
	local party = State.party
	if party and party.missionActive and not State.inMission() then
		para(c, "A case is in progress on this server.", 5, Theme.Amber)
	end
end

function Pages.Cases(c)
	heading(c, "CASE SELECT", 1)
	local party = State.party or {}
	local selection = party.selection or { caseId = "case01", mode = "Standard" }
	local profile = State.profile or {}
	for i, entry in CaseRegistry.Order do
		local case = CaseRegistry.get(entry.id)
		local stats = profile.cases and profile.cases[entry.id]
		local label
		if entry.playable then
			label = string.format("CASE %02d · %s · %s", entry.number, entry.title, entry.location)
			if stats then
				label ..= string.format("   BEST %s · RUNS %d", stats.bestGrade or "-", stats.runs or 0)
			end
		else
			label = string.format("CASE %02d · CLASSIFIED · %s · FUTURE UPDATE", entry.number, entry.location)
		end
		local b = UI.button(c, label, function()
			if entry.playable then
				local result = State.request("SelectCase", { case = entry.id, mode = selection.mode })
				if not result.ok then
					State.notify:Fire(result.message or "Unavailable.")
				end
			end
		end, { LayoutOrder = 10 + i * 2, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
		UI.padding(b, 10, 0)
		if not entry.playable then
			UI.setEnabled(b, false)
		elseif selection.caseId == entry.id then
			b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
		end
		if case and selection.caseId == entry.id then
			para(c, case.summary, 10 + i * 2 + 1, Theme.TextDim)
		end
	end

	local selected = CaseRegistry.get(selection.caseId)
	if selected then
		heading(c, "MODE", 30)
		local modes = row(c, 31)
		for _, modeId in { "Standard", "Restricted", "ArchiveChallenge" } do
			local def = selected.modes[modeId]
			local locked = def.requiresClearance and (profile.clearance or 1) < def.requiresClearance
			local b = UI.button(modes, def.label .. (locked and " (LOCKED)" or ""), function()
				local result = State.request("SelectCase", { case = selection.caseId, mode = modeId })
				if not result.ok then
					State.notify:Fire(result.message or "Unavailable.")
				end
			end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.32, 0, 1, 0), TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
			if selection.mode == modeId then
				b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
			end
			if locked then
				UI.setEnabled(b, false)
			end
		end
		local descriptions = {
			Standard = "Three edit charges. Clues shown. The intended way to play.",
			Restricted = "Two charges, no clue lines, a faster and more alert Presenter. Unlocked at clearance 2.",
			ArchiveChallenge = "This week's fixed seed for everyone. Scores go on the weekly leaderboard. Clearance 2.",
		}
		para(c, descriptions[selection.mode] or "", 32, Theme.TextDim)
	end

	heading(c, "TEAM", 40)
	for i, member in party.members or {} do
		local status = member.inMission and "IN CASE" or (member.ready and "READY" or "NOT READY")
		para(c, string.format("%s · CLEARANCE %d · %s", member.name, member.clearance or 1, status), 40 + i, member.ready and Theme.Accent or Theme.Text)
	end
	if party.countdownEnd then
		local remaining = math.max(0, party.countdownEnd - State.now())
		para(c, string.format("DEPLOYING IN %d...", math.ceil(remaining)), 60, Theme.Amber)
	end
	local me
	for _, member in party.members or {} do
		if member.userId == State.player.UserId then
			me = member
		end
	end
	local actions = row(c, 61)
	if party.missionActive then
		local join = UI.button(actions, "JOIN THE CASE IN PROGRESS", function()
			local result = State.request("JoinMission", {})
			if result.ok then
				MenuController.close()
			else
				State.notify:Fire(result.message or "Can't join.")
			end
		end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0) })
		if not party.joinable then
			UI.setEnabled(join, false)
			para(c, "The case in progress is too far along or full. Wait for the team to return.", 62, Theme.TextDim)
		end
	else
		UI.button(actions, (me and me.ready) and "CANCEL READY" or "READY", function()
			State.action("SetReady", { ready = not (me and me.ready) })
		end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0), TextColor3 = Theme.Accent })
		para(c, "When everyone in the field office is ready, the team deploys. You can play alone.", 62, Theme.TextDim)
	end

	if selection.mode == "ArchiveChallenge" then
		heading(c, "THIS WEEK'S ARCHIVE CHALLENGE", 70)
		local holder = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 71, Parent = c })
		UI.list(holder, 2)
		para(holder, "Loading leaderboard...", 1, Theme.TextDim)
		task.spawn(function()
			local result = State.request("GetLeaderboard", {})
			if holder.Parent then
				UI.clear(holder)
				if not result.rows or #result.rows == 0 then
					para(holder, "No scores yet this week.", 1, Theme.TextDim)
				end
				for i, r in result.rows or {} do
					para(holder, string.format("%d. %s · %d", r.rank, r.name, r.score), i)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function transcriptView(parent, entry, order)
	local lines = {}
	for _, line in entry.transcript or {} do
		if line.frame then
			table.insert(lines, string.format("[%s] (%s)", Format.short(line.t), line.text))
		else
			table.insert(lines, string.format("[%s] %s: %s", Format.short(line.t), line.speaker, line.text))
		end
	end
	return para(parent, table.concat(lines, "\n"), order, Theme.Cream)
end

local archiveSelection = nil

function Pages.Archive(c)
	heading(c, "EVIDENCE ARCHIVE", 1)
	local p = State.profile or {}
	para(c, "Recovered tapes stay unlocked. Replay case tapes to find hidden frames.", 2, Theme.TextDim)

	heading(c, "MASTER RECORDINGS", 10)
	for i, master in Evidence.MasterRecordings do
		local unlocked = p.masters and p.masters[master.id]
		local b = UI.button(c, string.format("%s%s", master.title, unlocked and "" or (master.available and "  [NOT RECOVERED]" or "  [FUTURE CASE]")), function()
			archiveSelection = unlocked and master or nil
			MenuController.show("Archive")
		end, { LayoutOrder = 10 + i, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
		UI.padding(b, 10, 0)
		if not unlocked then
			UI.setEnabled(b, false)
		end
	end

	heading(c, "EVIDENCE TAPES", 30)
	local ids = {}
	for id in Evidence.Tapes do
		table.insert(ids, id)
	end
	table.sort(ids)
	for i, id in ids do
		local tape = Evidence.Tapes[id]
		local unlocked = p.evidence and p.evidence[id]
		local b = UI.button(c, unlocked and tape.title or "UNRECOVERED TAPE", function()
			archiveSelection = unlocked and tape or nil
			MenuController.show("Archive")
		end, { LayoutOrder = 30 + i, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
		UI.padding(b, 10, 0)
		if not unlocked then
			UI.setEnabled(b, false)
		end
	end

	heading(c, "CASE TAPES", 50)
	local case = CaseRegistry.get("case01")
	local any = false
	for i, tapeId in case.tapeOrder do
		if p.tapes and p.tapes[tapeId] then
			any = true
			local tape = case.tapes[tapeId]
			UI.button(c, string.format("REPLAY %s · %s", tape.label, tape.title), function()
				MenuController.close()
				DeckController.open({ mode = "archive", caseId = "case01", tapeId = tapeId })
			end, { LayoutOrder = 50 + i, TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
		end
	end
	if not any then
		para(c, "Recover tapes during a case to replay them here.", 51, Theme.TextDim)
	end
	local frames = 0
	for _ in p.frames or {} do
		frames += 1
	end
	para(c, string.format("HIDDEN FRAMES FOUND: %d / %d", frames, #case.hiddenFrames), 60, Theme.Cream)

	heading(c, "INVESTIGATOR CHALLENGES", 70)
	for i, ch in case.challenges do
		local done = p.challenges and p.challenges[ch.id]
		para(c, string.format("%s %s — %s", done and "[X]" or "[ ]", ch.label, ch.text), 70 + i, done and Theme.Accent or Theme.Text)
	end

	if archiveSelection then
		heading(c, archiveSelection.title, -2)
		transcriptView(c, archiveSelection, -1)
	end
end

function Pages.Equipment(c)
	heading(c, "EQUIPMENT", 1)
	local p = State.profile
	if not p then
		para(c, "Connecting...", 2)
		return
	end
	para(c, "Equipment changes how you gather evidence, never whether you can win.", 2, Theme.TextDim)
	for i, id in ProfileSchema.EquipmentOrder do
		local def = ProfileSchema.Equipment[id]
		local b = UI.button(c, string.format("%s%s — %s", def.label, p.equipment == id and " (EQUIPPED)" or "", def.description), function()
			local result = State.request("SetEquipment", { equipment = id })
			if not result.ok then
				State.notify:Fire(result.message or "Unavailable.")
			end
		end, { LayoutOrder = 2 + i, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextWrapped = true, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52) })
		UI.padding(b, 10, 0)
		if p.equipment == id then
			b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
		end
	end

	heading(c, string.format("COSMETICS · %d RECOVERY CREDITS", p.credits), 20)
	local order = 21
	for _, slot in Cosmetics.Slots do
		para(c, Cosmetics.SlotLabels[slot], order, Theme.TextDim)
		order += 1
		local ids = {}
		for id, item in Cosmetics.Items do
			if item.slot == slot then
				table.insert(ids, id)
			end
		end
		table.sort(ids)
		for _, id in ids do
			local item = Cosmetics.Items[id]
			local owned = p.owned and p.owned[id]
			local equipped = p.equipped and p.equipped[slot] == id
			local suffix
			if equipped then
				suffix = "EQUIPPED"
			elseif owned then
				suffix = "EQUIP"
			elseif item.source == "credits" then
				suffix = string.format("BUY · %d CREDITS", item.price)
			elseif item.source == "pass" then
				suffix = "PASS"
			elseif item.source == "challenge" then
				suffix = "CHALLENGE"
			else
				suffix = "BUNDLE"
			end
			local b = UI.button(c, string.format("%s · %s", item.label, suffix), function()
				local result
				if owned then
					result = State.request("SetCosmetic", { item = id })
				elseif item.source == "credits" then
					result = State.request("BuyCosmetic", { item = id })
				else
					result = { ok = false, message = "Available from the store or a challenge." }
				end
				if not result.ok then
					State.notify:Fire(result.message or "Unavailable.")
				end
			end, { LayoutOrder = order, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40) })
			UI.padding(b, 10, 0)
			if equipped then
				b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
			end
			order += 1
		end
	end

	heading(c, string.format("LOADOUT PRESETS (%d SLOT%s)", p.presetSlots or 1, (p.presetSlots or 1) == 1 and "" or "S"), order)
	order += 1
	for slot = 1, p.presetSlots or 1 do
		local r = row(c, order)
		local saved = p.presets and p.presets[tostring(slot)]
		UI.button(r, string.format("SAVE TO SLOT %d", slot), function()
			State.request("SavePreset", { slot = slot })
		end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0), TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
		local load = UI.button(r, saved and string.format("LOAD SLOT %d", slot) or "EMPTY", function()
			State.request("LoadPreset", { slot = slot })
		end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0), TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
		if not saved then
			UI.setEnabled(load, false)
		end
		order += 1
	end

	heading(c, "DESK DECORATIONS", order)
	order += 1
	local anyDecor = false
	local decorIds = {}
	for id in Cosmetics.Decor do
		table.insert(decorIds, id)
	end
	table.sort(decorIds)
	for _, id in decorIds do
		if p.owned and p.owned[id] then
			anyDecor = true
			local on = p.decor and p.decor[id]
			UI.button(c, string.format("%s: %s", Cosmetics.Decor[id].label, on and "SHOWN" or "HIDDEN"), function()
				State.request("SetDecor", { decor = id, on = not on })
			end, { LayoutOrder = order, TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
			order += 1
		end
	end
	if not anyDecor then
		para(c, "Personal Quarters and the Office Decoration Bundle add items to your desk.", order, Theme.TextDim)
	end
end

function Pages.Settings(c)
	heading(c, "SETTINGS", 1)
	local s = State.settings
	local function changed(key)
		return function(value)
			local copy = table.clone(State.settings)
			copy[key] = value
			State.applySettings(copy)
			State.saveSettingsSoon()
		end
	end
	local function section(title, order)
		local holder = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = order, Parent = c })
		UI.list(holder, 4)
		UI.text(holder, title, { FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.Amber, LayoutOrder = -1 })
		return holder
	end

	local controls = section("CONTROLS", 2)
	UI.slider(controls, "MOUSE / LOOK SENSITIVITY", s.sensitivity, changed("sensitivity"), {
		min = 0.2,
		max = 3,
		step = 0.1,
		format = function(v)
			return string.format("%.1fx", v)
		end,
	})
	UI.toggle(controls, "TOGGLE SPRINT (press once instead of holding)", s.toggleSprint, changed("toggleSprint"))

	local camera = section("CAMERA AND COMFORT", 3)
	UI.slider(camera, "FIELD OF VIEW", s.fieldOfView, changed("fieldOfView"), {
		min = 60,
		max = 90,
		step = 1,
		format = function(v)
			return string.format("%d°", v)
		end,
	})
	UI.slider(camera, "HEAD BOB", s.headBob, changed("headBob"))
	UI.slider(camera, "MOTION BLUR", s.motionBlur, changed("motionBlur"))
	UI.slider(camera, "VIGNETTE", s.vignette, changed("vignette"))
	UI.slider(camera, "CAMERA SHAKE", s.cameraShake, changed("cameraShake"))
	UI.slider(camera, "DISTORTION", s.distortion, changed("distortion"))
	UI.slider(camera, "FLICKER", s.flicker, changed("flicker"))
	UI.toggle(camera, "CROSSHAIR", s.crosshair, changed("crosshair"))
	UI.toggle(camera, "LOW INTENSITY MODE (keeps all information)", s.lowIntensity, changed("lowIntensity"))
	para(camera, "Low intensity mode cuts flicker, distortion, shake, blur, bob and static to a minimum without hiding anything you need.", 99, Theme.TextDim)

	local audio = section("AUDIO", 4)
	UI.slider(audio, "MASTER VOLUME", s.master, changed("master"))
	UI.slider(audio, "MUSIC", s.music, changed("music"))
	UI.slider(audio, "AMBIENCE", s.ambience, changed("ambience"))
	UI.slider(audio, "EFFECTS", s.effects, changed("effects"))

	local display = section("TEXT AND HELP", 5)
	UI.slider(display, "TEXT SIZE", s.textScale, changed("textScale"), {
		min = 0.8,
		max = 1.4,
		step = 0.05,
		format = function(v)
			return string.format("%d%%", math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5))
		end,
	})
	UI.toggle(display, "SUBTITLES", s.subtitles, changed("subtitles"))
	UI.toggle(display, "RADIO LOG ON HUD", s.radioLog, changed("radioLog"))
	UI.toggle(display, "TIPS AND CONTROL HINTS", s.tips, changed("tips"))
	UI.button(display, "REPLAY TUTORIAL", function()
		MenuController.close()
		State.cue:Fire({ cue = "OpenTutorial" })
	end, { LayoutOrder = 98 })

	local keysSection = section("KEY BINDINGS (" .. string.upper(Controls.device()) .. ")", 6)
	for i, action in Controls.Order do
		para(keysSection, string.format("%-16s %s", Controls.key(action), Controls.Labels[action]), i, Theme.Text)
	end
	para(keysSection, "Esc stays the Roblox menu.", 50, Theme.TextDim)

	UI.button(c, "RESET ALL SETTINGS TO DEFAULTS", function()
		State.applySettings(table.clone(ProfileSchema.DefaultSettings))
		State.saveSettingsSoon()
		MenuController.show("Settings")
	end, { LayoutOrder = 20, TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim })
end

function Pages.Store(c)
	heading(c, "DIVISION STORE", 1)
	para(c, "Everything here is optional. The whole story can be finished without spending anything. Nothing sold here makes you safer or the Presenter weaker.", 2, Theme.TextDim)
	local p = State.profile or {}
	para(c, string.format("STORED RE-ENTRIES: %d · STORED TEAM RECOVERIES: %d", p.storedRevives or 0, p.storedTeamTokens or 0), 3, Theme.Cream)
	local holder = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 4, Parent = c })
	UI.list(holder, 6)
	para(holder, "Loading...", 1, Theme.TextDim)
	task.spawn(function()
		local store = State.request("GetStore", {})
		if not holder.Parent then
			return
		end
		UI.clear(holder)
		local passes = store.passes or {}
		local products = store.products or {}
		if #passes == 0 and #products == 0 then
			para(holder, "The store is not open yet.", 1, Theme.TextDim)
			return
		end
		for i, pass in passes do
			para(holder, string.format("%s — %s", pass.name, pass.description), i * 2, Theme.Text)
			local b = UI.button(holder, pass.owned and "OWNED" or "VIEW PURCHASE", function()
				State.request("PromptPass", { pass = pass.key })
			end, { LayoutOrder = i * 2 + 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 44) })
			if pass.owned then
				UI.setEnabled(b, false)
			end
		end
		for i, product in products do
			local order = 100 + i * 2
			para(holder, string.format("%s — %s", product.name, product.description), order, Theme.Text)
			if product.key == "EvidenceBundle" or product.key == "LobbyBundle" then
				UI.button(holder, "VIEW PURCHASE", function()
					State.request("PromptBundle", { product = product.key })
				end, { LayoutOrder = order + 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 44) })
			else
				para(holder, "Offered during a case only when it applies. Never required.", order + 1, Theme.TextDim)
			end
		end
	end)
end

function Pages.Credits(c)
	heading(c, "CREDITS", 1)
	para(c, "DEAD AIR: CHANNEL ZERO", 2, Theme.Cream)
	para(c, "An original analog-horror mystery. Inspired by the broad genre appeal of Roblox analog horror; no maps, characters, names, UI, story or assets are copied from any other game.", 3)
	para(c, "Design brief: the eXodus team. Built with Rojo, Luau and Roblox Studio.", 4, Theme.TextDim)
	para(c, "All models in this build are original geometry made in code. Audio cues are placeholders until original or licensed sounds are added.", 5, Theme.TextDim)
end

function Pages.Pause(c)
	heading(c, "PAUSED", 1)
	para(c, "The broadcast does not stop while you are here.", 2, Theme.TextDim)
	UI.button(c, "RESUME", function()
		MenuController.close()
	end, { LayoutOrder = 3 })
	UI.button(c, "SETTINGS", function()
		MenuController.show("Settings")
	end, { LayoutOrder = 4 })
	local m = State.mission
	local someoneDown = false
	for _, p in (m and m.participants) or {} do
		if p.status == "Eliminated" then
			someoneDown = true
		end
	end
	if someoneDown and State.isAlive() and m and not m.teamTokenUsed then
		UI.button(c, "CALL A TEAM RECOVERY (TEAM VOTE)", function()
			local result = State.request("TeamRecovery", {})
			State.notify:Fire(result.ok and "Vote started." or (result.message or "Unavailable."))
			MenuController.close()
		end, { LayoutOrder = 5 })
	end
	if m and State.inMission() then
		local equipment = State.profile and State.profile.equipment
		if equipment == "Recorder" then
			heading(c, "AUDIO RECORDER LOG", 6)
			local lines = {}
			for _, entry in m.radioLog or {} do
				table.insert(lines, string.format("[%s] %s · CH%d: %s", entry.stamp, entry.sourceId, entry.channel, entry.text))
			end
			para(c, #lines > 0 and table.concat(lines, "\n") or "Nothing recorded yet.", 7, Theme.Cream)
			para(c, "LIVE CLOCK " .. Format.clock(State.broadcastClock()), 8, Theme.TextDim)
		end
		UI.button(c, "LEAVE THE CASE (NO REWARDS)", function()
			State.action("LeaveCase")
			MenuController.close()
		end, { LayoutOrder = 20, TextColor3 = Theme.Danger })
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Shell
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local NAV = {
	{ id = "Cases", label = "CASE SELECT" },
	{ id = "Archive", label = "EVIDENCE ARCHIVE" },
	{ id = "Equipment", label = "EQUIPMENT" },
	{ id = "Settings", label = "SETTINGS" },
	{ id = "Store", label = "STORE" },
	{ id = "Credits", label = "CREDITS" },
}

local function build(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirMenu", 20, playerGui)
	gui.Enabled = false
	root = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.25,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Parent = gui,
	})
	UI.modalUnlock(root)
	UI.new("UIGradient", {
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.35),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.85),
		}),
		Parent = root,
	})
	local left = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40), Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 1, -80), Parent = root })
	UI.list(left, 8)
	UI.text(left, "NATIONAL BROADCAST RECOVERY DIVISION", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, LayoutOrder = 1 })
	title = UI.text(left, "DEAD AIR", { FontFace = Theme.FontTitle, TextSize = 64, TextColor3 = Theme.Cream, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 70), LayoutOrder = 2 })
	UI.text(left, "CHANNEL ZERO // SIGNAL DETECTED", { TextColor3 = Theme.Accent, LayoutOrder = 3 })
	statusLine = UI.text(left, "SIGNAL RECEIVED: 00:00:00", { TextColor3 = Theme.Amber, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, LayoutOrder = 4 })
	UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), LayoutOrder = 5, Parent = left })
	nav = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 6, Parent = left })
	UI.list(nav, 6)

	local panel = UI.panel(root, { Position = UDim2.new(0.34, 0, 0, 40), Size = UDim2.new(0.63, 0, 1, -80) })
	UI.padding(panel, 16)
	content = UI.scroll(panel, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
	UI.list(content, 8)
end

local function rebuildNav()
	UI.clear(nav)
	if State.inMission() then
		UI.button(nav, "RESUME", function()
			MenuController.close()
		end, { LayoutOrder = 1 })
		UI.button(nav, "PAUSE MENU", function()
			MenuController.show("Pause")
		end, { LayoutOrder = 2 })
		UI.button(nav, "SETTINGS", function()
			MenuController.show("Settings")
		end, { LayoutOrder = 3 })
		return
	end
	for i, item in NAV do
		local b = UI.button(nav, item.label, function()
			MenuController.show(item.id)
		end, { LayoutOrder = i })
		if page == item.id then
			b.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
		end
	end
	UI.button(nav, "ENTER THE FIELD OFFICE", function()
		MenuController.close()
	end, { LayoutOrder = 50, TextColor3 = Theme.Cream })
end

function MenuController.show(pageId)
	page = pageId
	UI.clear(content)
	content.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
	local builder = Pages[pageId] or Pages.Home
	local ok, err = pcall(builder, content)
	if not ok then
		warn("[Menu] " .. tostring(err))
	end
	rebuildNav()
end

function MenuController.open(pageId)
	gui.Enabled = true
	State.setUI("menu", true)
	if not State.inMission() then
		startPan()
	end
	MenuController.show(pageId or (State.inMission() and "Pause" or "Home"))
	if gamepadMode() then
		local first = nav:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextButton")
		GuiService.SelectedObject = first
	end
end

function MenuController.close()
	gui.Enabled = false
	State.setUI("menu", false)
	GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
	stopPan()
end

function MenuController.toggle()
	if gui.Enabled then
		MenuController.close()
	else
		MenuController.open()
	end
end

function MenuController.isOpen()
	return gui.Enabled
end

function MenuController.init(playerGui, deckController)
	DeckController = deckController
	build(playerGui)
	MenuController.open("Home")
	local function refreshIfOpen()
		if gui.Enabled and page and page ~= "Settings" then
			MenuController.show(page)
		end
	end
	State.profileChanged:Connect(function(profile)
		local menuTheme = profile.equipped and Cosmetics.Items[profile.equipped.menu]
		if menuTheme and menuTheme.colour then
			Theme.applyAccent(Color3.fromRGB(menuTheme.colour[1], menuTheme.colour[2], menuTheme.colour[3]))
		end
		refreshIfOpen()
	end)
	State.partyChanged:Connect(function()
		if page == "Cases" or page == "Home" then
			refreshIfOpen()
		end
	end)
	State.cue:Connect(function(cue)
		if cue.cue == "OpenMenu" then
			MenuController.open(cue.page)
		end
	end)
	local wasInMission = false
	State.missionChanged:Connect(function(m)
		-- Deploying closes the menu; returning to the field office reopens it.
		local inMission = m ~= nil and State.inMission()
		if inMission and gui.Enabled and page ~= "Pause" and page ~= "Settings" then
			MenuController.close()
		elseif wasInMission and not inMission then
			task.delay(1, function()
				if not State.inMission() and not gui.Enabled then
					MenuController.open("Cases")
				end
			end)
		end
		wasInMission = inMission
	end)
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if gui.Enabled then
			statusLine.Text = "SIGNAL RECEIVED: " .. os.date("!%H:%M:%S", math.floor(State.now()))
			local flicker = State.intensity("flicker")
			title.TextTransparency = (flicker > 0 and math.random() < 0.02 * flicker) and 0.6 or 0
		end
	end)
	-- Countdown text refresh.
	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(1)
			if gui.Enabled and page == "Cases" and State.party and State.party.countdownEnd then
				MenuController.show("Cases")
			end
		end
	end)
end

return MenuController
```

## src/client/Controllers/MonitorController.luau

```lua
--[[
	MonitorController — live CCTV on the station's CRT monitors.

	Only monitors near the player are active (performance). Feeds show the real
	room, the real Presenter if it is in frame, and investigators. Two scares live
	here, both presentation only:
	  * phantom frames: a figure appears on a feed of an empty room for a moment
	  * the editor's shadow: after the reception edit, the reception TV shows a
	    figure standing right behind the person who made it
]]

local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local FootageView = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.FootageView)

local MonitorController = {}

local ACTIVE_RADIUS = 34
local MAX_ACTIVE = 5
local UPDATE_INTERVAL = 0.2

local monitors = {} -- [screenPart] = { gui, view, cameraId, role }
local phantom = nil -- { cameraId, until, pos }
local shadow = nil -- { userId, until }
local nextPhantomAt = os.clock() + 70
local rng = Random.new()

local function myRoot()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function presenterPos()
	local model = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	if model and model:IsA("Model") and model.PrimaryPart then
		return model.PrimaryPart.Position
	end
	return nil
end

local function ensure(screen)
	local entry = monitors[screen]
	if entry then
		return entry
	end
	local gui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
	gui.Name = "CCTV"
	gui.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
	gui.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.PixelsPerStud
	gui.PixelsPerStud = 60
	gui.LightInfluence = 0
	gui.Brightness = 1.4
	gui.Parent = screen
	local view = FootageView.new(gui, { compact = true })
	entry = { gui = gui, view = view, cameraId = screen:GetAttribute("CameraId"), role = screen:GetAttribute("MonitorRole"), enabled = false }
	view:setCamera(entry.cameraId)
	monitors[screen] = entry
	return entry
end

local function setEnabled(entry, on)
	if entry.enabled ~= on then
		entry.enabled = on
		entry.gui.Enabled = on
		if on then
			entry.view:powerOn()
		end
	end
end

local function liveActors(cameraId, t)
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	local actors = {}
	local p = presenterPos()
	if p then
		local pos = { x = p.X, y = 0, z = p.Z }
		if MapLayout.inRooms(pos, camera.rooms, 1) then
			table.insert(actors, { kind = "Presenter", pos = pos, look = { x = camera.pos.x, y = 0, z = camera.pos.z } })
		end
	end
	if phantom and phantom.cameraId == cameraId and t < phantom.untilTime then
		table.insert(actors, { kind = "Presenter", pos = phantom.pos, look = { x = camera.pos.x, y = 0, z = camera.pos.z } })
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local character = player.Character
		local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hrp then
			local pos = { x = hrp.Position.X, y = 0, z = hrp.Position.Z }
			if MapLayout.inRooms(pos, camera.rooms, 0.5) then
				local look = hrp.Position + hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 4
				table.insert(actors, { kind = "Investigator", pos = pos, look = { x = look.X, y = 0, z = look.Z } })
				if shadow and shadow.userId == player.UserId and t < shadow.untilTime then
					local behind = hrp.Position - hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 2.6
					table.insert(actors, { kind = "Presenter", pos = { x = behind.X, y = 0, z = behind.Z }, look = { x = hrp.Position.X, y = 0, z = hrp.Position.Z } })
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return actors
end

local function lightMode(cameraId)
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
	local folder = station and station:FindFirstChild("Rooms") and station.Rooms:FindFirstChild(camera.rooms[1])
	return folder and folder:GetAttribute("LightMode") or "off"
end

local function maybePhantom(t)
	if t < nextPhantomAt or not State.inMission() or presenterPos() then
		return
	end
	nextPhantomAt = t + rng:NextInteger(60, 120)
	local root = myRoot()
	if not root then
		return
	end
	-- Pick an active monitor showing a room the player is not in.
	local here = MapLayout.roomAt(root.Position.X, root.Position.Z)
	local candidates = {}
	for _, entry in monitors do
		local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[entry.cameraId]
		if entry.enabled and camera and not table.find(camera.rooms, here) then
			table.insert(candidates, entry)
		end
	end
	if #candidates == 0 then
		return
	end
	local entry = candidates[rng:NextInteger(1, #candidates)]
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[entry.cameraId]
	local roomId = camera.rooms[1]
	phantom = {
		cameraId = entry.cameraId,
		untilTime = t + 1.6,
		pos = MapLayout.point(roomId, 0.3 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.4, 0.3 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.4),
	}
end

local function update()
	local t = os.clock()
	local root = myRoot()
	local inStation = State.inMission()
	maybePhantom(t)
	local nearby = {}
	for _, screen in CollectionService:GetTagged("CCTVMonitor") do
		if screen:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) and screen:GetAttribute("CameraId") and MapLayout.Cameras[screen:GetAttribute("CameraId")] then
			local d = root and (screen.Position - root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
			if inStation and d <= ACTIVE_RADIUS and screen:GetAttribute("MonitorRole") ~= "Van" then
				table.insert(nearby, { screen = screen, d = d })
			elseif monitors[screen] then
				setEnabled(monitors[screen], false)
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(nearby, function(a, b)
		return a.d < b.d
	end)
	local m = State.mission
	local powered = m and m.flags and m.flags.generator_restored
	for i = MAX_ACTIVE + 1, #nearby do
		local far = monitors[nearby[i].screen]
		if far then
			setEnabled(far, false)
		end
	end
	for i = 1, math.min(#nearby, MAX_ACTIVE) do
		local item = nearby[i]
		local entry = ensure(item.screen)
		local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[entry.cameraId]
		local live = camera.relay == "LOCAL" or powered
		setEnabled(entry, true)
		if live then
			entry.view:setCamera(entry.cameraId)
			entry.view:setLights(lightMode(entry.cameraId))
			entry.view:setActors(liveActors(entry.cameraId, t))
			local quality = 90
			local p = presenterPos()
			if p then
				quality -= math.clamp(40 - (p - Vector3.new(camera.pos.x, camera.pos.y, camera.pos.z)).Magnitude, 0, 40)
			end
			entry.view:setSignal(quality, State.intensity("distortion"))
			entry.view:setTexts({ message = "" })
		else
			entry.view:setActors({})
			entry.view:setLights("off")
			entry.view:setSignal(5, State.intensity("distortion"))
			entry.view:setTexts({ message = "NO SIGNAL" })
		end
		entry.view:step(UPDATE_INTERVAL)
	end
end

function MonitorController.init()
	State.cue:Connect(function(cue)
		if cue.cue == "EditorShadow" and cue.userId then
			shadow = { userId = cue.userId, untilTime = os.clock() + 4.5 }
		end
	end)
	-- Station resets replace every monitor part.
```
