```lua
	Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "Station04" then
			for screen, entry in monitors do
				entry.view:destroy()
				entry.gui:Destroy()
				monitors[screen] = nil
			end
		end
	end)
	local accum = 0
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		accum += dt
		if accum >= UPDATE_INTERVAL then
			accum = 0
			local ok, err = pcall(update)
			if not ok then
				warn("[MonitorController] " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end)
end

return MonitorController
```

## src/client/Controllers/PromptController.luau

```lua
--[[
	PromptController — draws every interaction prompt in the game's typeface
	and colours instead of Roblox's default style.

	A prompt shows the key for the current device (E, X or TAP), what it does
	and what it is for, and a fill bar while a hold prompt is held. On touch the
	whole card is a button: press and hold it like the key.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ProximityPromptService = game:GetService("ProximityPromptService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)
local Controls = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Controls)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Sound)

local PromptController = {}

local shown = {} -- [prompt] = { gui, fill, conns }

local function hide(prompt)
	local entry = shown[prompt]
	if not entry then
		return
	end
	shown[prompt] = nil
	for _, conn in entry.conns do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	entry.gui:Destroy()
end

local function show(prompt, playerGui)
	hide(prompt)
	local adornee = prompt.Parent
	if not adornee or not (adornee:IsA("BasePart") or adornee:IsA("Attachment") or adornee:IsA("Model")) then
		return
	end
	local device = Controls.device()
	local gui = UI.new("BillboardGui", {
		Name = "DeadAirPrompt",
		Adornee = adornee,
		AlwaysOnTop = true,
		LightInfluence = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(250, 58),
		StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0),
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		Active = device == "touch",
	})
	local card = UI.new("TextButton", {
		Text = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Selectable = false,
		Parent = gui,
	})
	UI.stroke(card, Theme.Line, 1)
	UI.corner(card, 3)
	local key = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelLight,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 42),
		Parent = card,
	})
	UI.stroke(key, Theme.Accent, 1)
	UI.corner(key, 3)
	local keyText = Controls.promptKey(prompt, device)
	UI.text(key, keyText, {
		FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
		TextSize = #keyText > 2 and 12 or 18,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
	})
	local fill = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.55,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		Parent = key,
	})
	UI.text(card, string.upper(prompt.ActionText), {
		FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
		TextSize = 16,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
		TextWrapped = false,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(60, prompt.ObjectText ~= "" and 8 or 18),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 20),
	})
	if prompt.ObjectText ~= "" then
		UI.text(card, string.upper(prompt.ObjectText), {
			TextSize = 12,
			TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim,
			TextWrapped = false,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 30),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 18),
		})
	end
	if prompt.HoldDuration > 0 then
		UI.text(card, "HOLD", {
			TextSize = 10,
			TextColor3 = Theme.AccentDim,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 3),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 12),
		})
	end

	local entry = { gui = gui, fill = fill, conns = {} }
	shown[prompt] = entry
	-- The server rewrites prompts while they are on screen (a door's "Open"
	-- becomes "Close", the generator moves to its next stage): redraw.
	for _, prop in { "ActionText", "ObjectText", "HoldDuration", "KeyboardKeyCode", "GamepadKeyCode" } do
		table.insert(entry.conns, prompt:GetPropertyChangedSignal(prop):Connect(function()
			if shown[prompt] == entry then
				task.defer(function()
					if shown[prompt] == entry then
						show(prompt, playerGui)
					end
				end)
			end
		end))
	end
	local tween
	table.insert(entry.conns, prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan:Connect(function()
		if tween then
			tween:Cancel()
		end
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
		Sound.play("PromptHold")
		tween = TweenService:Create(fill, TweenInfo.new(prompt.HoldDuration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
		tween:Play()
	end))
	table.insert(entry.conns, prompt.PromptButtonHoldEnded:Connect(function()
		if tween then
			tween:Cancel()
			tween = nil
		end
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
	end))
	table.insert(entry.conns, prompt.Triggered:Connect(function()
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		fill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
	end))
	-- Touch: hold the card like a key.
	table.insert(entry.conns, card.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			prompt:InputHoldBegin()
		end
	end))
	table.insert(entry.conns, card.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			prompt:InputHoldEnd()
		end
	end))
	gui.Parent = playerGui
end

function PromptController.init(playerGui)
	ProximityPromptService.PromptShown:Connect(function(prompt)
		if prompt.Style == Enum.ProximityPromptStyle.Custom then
			show(prompt, playerGui)
		end
	end)
	ProximityPromptService.PromptHidden:Connect(hide)
	Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
		for prompt in shown do
			hide(prompt)
		end
	end)
end

return PromptController
```

## src/client/Controllers/ReportController.luau

```lua
--[[
	ReportController — the case report: result, components, tapes, predictions
	interpreted, edits, team survival, grade D-S, rewards and new unlocks.
]]

local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local Evidence = require(Shared.Config.Evidence)
local Format = require(Shared.Util.Format)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)

local ReportController = {}

local gui, body

local RESULT_TEXT = {
	Success = "SUCCESSFUL EXTRACTION",
	Partial = "PARTIAL EXTRACTION",
	Failed = "FAILED CASE",
}

local function line(text, colour, order, size)
	return UI.text(body, text, {
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		TextColor3 = colour or Theme.Text,
		TextSize = size or Theme.TextSize,
		LayoutOrder = order,
	})
end

local function show(payload)
	local report, personal = payload.report, payload.personal
	UI.clear(body)
	local caseDef = CaseRegistry.get(report.caseId)
	local order = 0
	local function nextOrder()
		order += 1
		return order
	end
	line("NATIONAL BROADCAST RECOVERY DIVISION · CASE REPORT", Theme.TextDim, nextOrder(), Theme.SmallText)
	line(string.format("CASE %02d · %s · %s", caseDef and caseDef.number or 0, report.title, report.modeLabel or report.mode), Theme.Cream, nextOrder())
	line(RESULT_TEXT[report.result] or report.result, report.result == "Failed" and Theme.Danger or Theme.Accent, nextOrder(), 28)
	if report.reason then
		line(report.reason, Theme.TextDim, nextOrder())
	end
	line(string.format("GRADE %s  ·  SCORE %d / 100  ·  BROADCAST TIME %s", report.grade, report.score, Format.clock(report.duration or 0)), Theme.Cream, nextOrder(), 22)

	local comps = {}
	for _, id in caseDef.components do
		table.insert(comps, string.format("%s %s", report.components[id] and "[X]" or "[ ]", caseDef.items[id].label))
	end
	line("COMPONENTS: " .. table.concat(comps, "   ") .. (report.componentsInstalled and "   · INSTALLED" or ""), Theme.Text, nextOrder())
	line(string.format("CASE TAPES RECOVERED: %d / %d   ·   EVIDENCE: %d / %d%s", report.tapesFound, report.tapesTotal, report.evidenceFound, report.evidenceTotal, report.bonusTape and "   · TAPE 07 EXTRACTED" or ""), Theme.Text, nextOrder())
	line(string.format("PREDICTIONS INTERPRETED: %d / %d", report.predictionsInterpreted, report.predictionsTotal), Theme.Text, nextOrder())
	line(string.format("EDITS MADE: %d / %d", report.editsMade, report.maxCharges), Theme.Text, nextOrder())
	for _, edit in report.edits or {} do
		line(string.format("    %s: %s -> %s (%s)", edit.label, edit.from, edit.to, edit.by), Theme.TextDim, nextOrder(), Theme.SmallText)
	end
	local anomalies = {}
	table.insert(anomalies, report.loopSolved and (report.loopFirstTry and "LOOP BROKEN FIRST TRY" or "LOOP BROKEN") or "LOOP UNSOLVED")
	table.insert(anomalies, report.callerFooled and "CALLER'S CODE USED" or "CALLER IGNORED")
	line("ANOMALIES: " .. table.concat(anomalies, " · "), Theme.Text, nextOrder())

	line("TEAM", Theme.Accent, nextOrder())
	for _, p in report.participants or {} do
		local status = ({ Extracted = "EXTRACTED", Eliminated = "OFF-AIR", Disconnected = "LEFT", Alive = "LEFT BEHIND" })[p.status] or p.status
		line(string.format("    %s · %s · items %d · evidence %d · frames %d", p.name, status, p.items, p.evidence, p.frames), Theme.Text, nextOrder(), Theme.SmallText)
	end

	if personal then
		line("REWARDS", Theme.Accent, nextOrder())
		line(string.format("    +%d RECOVERY CREDITS · +%d RESEARCH", personal.credits or 0, personal.research or 0), Theme.Cream, nextOrder())
		if personal.clearance then
			line(string.format("    CLEARANCE RAISED TO %d — Restricted mode and the Archive Challenge are open.", personal.clearance), Theme.Cream, nextOrder())
		end
		if personal.master then
			local master = Evidence.master(personal.master)
			line("    NEW MASTER RECORDING: " .. (master and master.title or personal.master), Theme.Cream, nextOrder())
		end
		for _, id in personal.evidence or {} do
			local tape = Evidence.Tapes[id]
			line("    NEW EVIDENCE: " .. (tape and tape.title or id), Theme.Text, nextOrder(), Theme.SmallText)
		end
		for _, id in personal.challenges or {} do
			for _, ch in caseDef.challenges do
				if ch.id == id then
					line("    CHALLENGE COMPLETE: " .. ch.label, Theme.Text, nextOrder(), Theme.SmallText)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	line("Returning to the field office shortly.", Theme.TextDim, nextOrder(), Theme.SmallText)
	local button = UI.button(body, "RETURN TO THE FIELD OFFICE", function()
		State.action("ReturnToHQ")
		ReportController.close()
	end, { LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 48), TextColor3 = Theme.Accent })

	gui.Enabled = true
	State.setUI("report", true)
	if #UserInputService:GetConnectedGamepads() > 0 then
		GuiService.SelectedObject = button
	end
end

function ReportController.close()
	gui.Enabled = false
	State.setUI("report", false)
	GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
end

function ReportController.init(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirReport", 30, playerGui)
	gui.Enabled = false
	local root = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background, BackgroundTransparency = 0.05, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = gui })
	UI.modalUnlock(root)
	local panel = UI.panel(root, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.72, 0.86) })
	UI.padding(panel, 20)
	body = UI.scroll(panel, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
	UI.list(body, 6)
	State.reportReceived:Connect(show)
	State.missionChanged:Connect(function(m)
		if not m and gui.Enabled then
			ReportController.close()
		end
	end)
end

return ReportController
```

## src/client/Controllers/SpectateController.luau

```lua
--[[
	SpectateController — for eliminated or extracted investigators.

	Follows a living teammate with an over-the-shoulder camera. Offers (never
	pushes) an optional re-entry when one is possible; otherwise the player can
	keep watching until extraction or return to the field office.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Monetisation = require(Shared.Config.Monetisation)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)

local SpectateController = {}

local gui, panel, statusText, watchingText, reviveButton, reviveInfo, modal
local targetIndex = 1
local active = false
local renderConn = nil
local smoothed = nil

local function aliveTargets()
	local list = {}
	local m = State.mission
	if not m then
		return list
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local p = m.participants[tostring(player.UserId)]
		if p and p.status == "Alive" and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
			table.insert(list, player)
		end
	end
	return list
end

local function cycle(delta)
	local list = aliveTargets()
	if #list == 0 then
		return
	end
	targetIndex = ((targetIndex - 1 + delta) % #list) + 1
end

local function reviveAvailable()
	local m = State.mission
	local me = State.me()
	if not m or not me or me.status ~= "Eliminated" then
		return false, nil
	end
	if m.teamTokenUsed then
		return false, "A team recovery was used. No more re-entries this case."
	end
	if (me.revivesUsed or 0) >= 1 then
		return false, "You have already re-entered once this case."
	end
	local stored = State.profile and State.profile.storedRevives or 0
	if stored > 0 then
		return true, string.format("You have %d stored re-entr%s.", stored, stored == 1 and "y" or "ies")
	end
	if Monetisation.Products.Revive.id ~= 0 then
		return true, Monetisation.Products.Revive.description
	end
	return false, "Keep watching — your team can still finish the case."
end

local function refresh()
	local me = State.me()
	local shouldShow = State.mission ~= nil and me ~= nil and (me.status == "Eliminated" or me.status == "Extracted") and State.mission.phase ~= "Completed" and State.mission.phase ~= "Failed"
	if shouldShow ~= active then
		active = shouldShow
		gui.Enabled = shouldShow
		if shouldShow then
			smoothed = nil
			renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
				local list = aliveTargets()
				local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
				local target = list[math.clamp(targetIndex, 1, math.max(1, #list))]
				if target and target.Character then
					local head = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
					local root = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if head and root then
						local desired = CFrame.lookAt(head.Position - root.CFrame.LookVector * 8 + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), head.Position + root.CFrame.LookVector * 6)
						smoothed = smoothed and smoothed:Lerp(desired, math.min(1, dt * 6)) or desired
						camera.CFrame = smoothed
					end
					watchingText.Text = "WATCHING: " .. target.DisplayName .. "   [Q / E or the arrows to switch]"
				else
					watchingText.Text = "NO LIVE FEEDS"
				end
			end)
		else
			if renderConn then
				renderConn:Disconnect()
				renderConn = nil
			end
			local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		end
	end
	if not active then
		return
	end
	if me.status == "Extracted" then
		statusText.Text = "EXTRACTED · WAITING FOR THE TEAM"
	else
		statusText.Text = "SIGNAL LOST · YOU ARE OFF-AIR"
		local m = State.mission
		if m.failPendingUntil then
			statusText.Text ..= string.format(" · CASE ENDS IN %ds", math.max(0, math.ceil(m.failPendingUntil - State.now())))
		end
	end
	local canRevive, info = reviveAvailable()
	reviveButton.Visible = canRevive
	reviveInfo.Text = info or ""
end

function SpectateController.init(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirSpectate", 15, playerGui)
	gui.Enabled = false
	panel = UI.panel(gui, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -20), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(560, 210) })
	UI.padding(panel, 12)
	UI.list(panel, 6)
	modal = UI.modalUnlock(panel)
	statusText = UI.text(panel, "", { FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextColor3 = Theme.Cream, LayoutOrder = 1 })
	watchingText = UI.text(panel, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, LayoutOrder = 2 })
	local row = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), LayoutOrder = 3, Parent = panel })
	UI.list(row, 6, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal)
	UI.button(row, "< PREV", function()
		cycle(-1)
	end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.24, 0, 1, 0) })
	UI.button(row, "NEXT >", function()
		cycle(1)
	end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.24, 0, 1, 0) })
	UI.button(row, "TAPES", function()
		local DeckController = require(script.Parent.DeckController)
		DeckController.open({ mode = "spectate" })
	end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.24, 0, 1, 0) })
	UI.button(row, "LEAVE CASE", function()
		State.action("LeaveCase")
	end, { Size = UDim2.new(0.24, 0, 1, 0), TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim })
	reviveButton = UI.button(panel, "EMERGENCY RE-ENTRY (OPTIONAL)", function()
		local result = State.request("Revive", {})
		if result.message then
			State.notify:Fire(result.message)
		end
	end, { LayoutOrder = 4, TextColor3 = Theme.Accent })
	reviveInfo = UI.text(panel, "", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 5 })
	UI.text(panel, "A teammate still inside can call a team recovery from their pause menu.", { TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 6 })

	State.missionChanged:Connect(refresh)
	State.profileChanged:Connect(refresh)
	game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if processed or not active then
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft then
			cycle(-1)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight then
			cycle(1)
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(1)
			refresh()
		end
	end)
	SpectateController.modal = modal
end

return SpectateController
```

## src/client/Controllers/ToolController.luau

```lua
--[[
	ToolController — the equipped tool (chosen in the field office):

	Camcorder  [1] night-vision viewfinder with REC overlay; filming the Presenter
	           sends a "RecordAnomaly" the server validates for research.
	Analyser   [1] large signal readout with the bearing of the strongest
	           interference.
	Recorder   [1] the full radio log with channel and timestamp, beside the live
	           clock - for telling Division from the Caller.
	Monitor    [1] opens the handheld live CCTV monitor (view-only).

	Tools change how evidence is gathered, never whether a case can be won.
]]

local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Cosmetics = require(Shared.Config.Cosmetics)
local Format = require(Shared.Util.Format)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Sound)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)
local HUDController = require(script.Parent.HUDController)

local ToolController = {}

local gui, viewfinder, vfLabel, vfFrame, analyser, analyserText, analyserArrow, recorder, recorderText
local nightVision
local active = false
local lastRecord = 0
local DeckController

local function equipment()
	return State.profile and State.profile.equipment or "Camcorder"
end

local function presenterRoot()
	local model = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	return model and model:IsA("Model") and model.PrimaryPart or nil
end

local function setActive(on)
	if on ~= active then
		Sound.play(on and "ToolEquip" or "ToolUnequip")
	end
	active = on
	local kind = equipment()
	viewfinder.Visible = on and kind == "Camcorder"
	analyser.Visible = on and kind == "Analyser"
	recorder.Visible = on and kind == "Recorder"
	nightVision.Enabled = on and kind == "Camcorder"
end

function ToolController.toggle()
	local kind = equipment()
	if kind == "Monitor" then
		if DeckController.isOpen() then
			DeckController.close()
		elseif State.inMission() and State.isAlive() then
			DeckController.open({ mode = "handheld" })
		end
		return
	end
	setActive(not active)
end

local function stepCamcorder()
	local skin = State.profile and State.profile.equipped and Cosmetics.Items[State.profile.equipped.camcorder]
	local recStyle = State.profile and State.profile.equipped and Cosmetics.Items[State.profile.equipped.rec]
	if skin and skin.colour then
		vfFrame.Color = Color3.fromRGB(skin.colour[1], skin.colour[2], skin.colour[3])
	end
	local style = recStyle and recStyle.style or "standard"
	local clock = Format.clock(State.broadcastClock())
	if style == "minimal" then
		vfLabel.Text = "REC " .. clock
	elseif style == "classic" then
		vfLabel.Text = "REC  SP  " .. clock .. "  CH-0"
	else
		vfLabel.Text = "REC  " .. clock .. "  BATT |||"
	end
	-- Evidence: the Presenter on screen and close enough to matter.
	local root = presenterRoot()
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if root and camera and State.inMission() and State.isAlive() and os.clock() - lastRecord > 5 then
		local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
		local distance = (camera.CFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
		if onScreen and screenPos.Z > 0 and distance < 55 then
			lastRecord = os.clock()
			State.action("RecordAnomaly")
			Sound.play("RecBeep")
		end
	end
end

local function stepAnalyser()
	local signal = State.player:GetAttribute("Signal") or 100
	local root = presenterRoot()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local bearing = "NO INTERFERENCE"
	analyserArrow.Visible = false
	if root and hrp then
		local delta = root.Position - hrp.Position
		if delta.Magnitude < 80 then
			local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
			local angle = math.deg(math.atan2(delta.X, delta.Z) - math.atan2(look.X, look.Z))
			angle = (angle + 540) % 360 - 180
			analyserArrow.Visible = true
			analyserArrow.Rotation = -angle
			bearing = string.format("INTERFERENCE · %d STUDS", math.floor(delta.Magnitude))
		end
	end
	analyserText.Text = string.format("SIGNAL %d%%\n%s", signal, bearing)
end

local function stepRecorder()
	local m = State.mission
	local lines = { "LIVE CLOCK " .. Format.clock(State.broadcastClock()), "" }
	for _, entry in (m and m.radioLog) or {} do
		table.insert(lines, string.format("[%s] %s · CH%d", entry.stamp, entry.sourceId, entry.channel))
		table.insert(lines, "   " .. entry.text)
	end
	recorderText.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

function ToolController.init(playerGui, deckController)
	DeckController = deckController
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirTool", 4, playerGui)

	viewfinder = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false, Parent = gui })
	local inner = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.86, 0.8), Parent = viewfinder })
	vfFrame = UI.stroke(inner, Theme.Cream, 2, 0.2)
	vfLabel = UI.text(inner, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22), TextColor3 = Theme.Text, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4, FontFace = Theme.FontBold })

	analyser = UI.panel(gui, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.55, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 160), Visible = false })
	UI.padding(analyser, 10)
	analyserText = UI.text(analyser, "", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50), FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextColor3 = Theme.Accent })
	analyserArrow = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Amber, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -10), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 60), Parent = analyser })

	recorder = UI.panel(gui, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.55, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 300), Visible = false })
	UI.padding(recorder, 10)
	recorderText = UI.text(recorder, "", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.Cream })

	nightVision = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
	nightVision.Name = "DeadAirNightVision"
	nightVision.Brightness = 0.18
	nightVision.Contrast = 0.15
	nightVision.Saturation = -0.6
	nightVision.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(170, 235, 175)
	nightVision.Enabled = false
	nightVision.Parent = Lighting

	State.profileChanged:Connect(function()
		HUDController.tool = equipment()
		if active then
			setActive(true)
		end
	end)
	State.missionChanged:Connect(function()
		if active and State.inMission() and not State.isAlive() then
			setActive(false)
		end
	end)
	HUDController.tool = equipment()

	local accum = 0
	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		accum += dt
		if not active or accum < 0.1 then
			return
		end
		accum = 0
		local kind = equipment()
		gui.Enabled = not State.ui.deck and not State.ui.menu
		if kind == "Camcorder" then
			stepCamcorder()
		elseif kind == "Analyser" then
			stepAnalyser()
		elseif kind == "Recorder" then
			stepRecorder()
		end
	end)
end

return ToolController
```

## src/client/Controllers/TutorialController.luau

```lua
--[[
	TutorialController — a short walkthrough the first time someone plays, and a
	handful of one-time tips during their first case.

	The walkthrough is a stack of Division briefing cards over the main menu:
	BACK / NEXT / SKIP, readable with mouse, controller or touch, with the right
	keys for the device in use. Finishing or skipping it saves `tutorialSeen` on
	the profile; Settings > Replay tutorial shows it again.

	Tips appear once per session at the moment they matter (first tape, first
	dark room, first hunt) and can be turned off in Settings.
]]

local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)

local State = require(script.Parent.Parent.State)
local UI = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Theme)
local Controls = require(script.Parent.Parent.UI.Controls)

local TutorialController = {}

local function keys(...)
	local out = {}
	for _, action in { ... } do
		table.insert(out, action)
	end
	return out
end

-- Each card: title, body, and the actions whose keys it shows.
TutorialController.Steps = {
	{
		title = "WELCOME, INVESTIGATOR",
		body = "You work for the National Broadcast Recovery Division. Broadcast Station 04 has been empty for nineteen years. Tonight it started transmitting again.\n\nThis takes a minute. You can skip it and replay it from Settings.",
	},
	{
		title = "MOVING AROUND",
		body = "You see everything through your own eyes. Sprinting drains stamina (the thin bar at the bottom). The station is dark: keep your flashlight handy, and hold inspect to look closely at something.",
		keys = keys("Move", "Look", "Sprint", "Flashlight", "Inspect"),
	},
	{
		title = "THE FIELD OFFICE",
		body = "This is the lobby. Choose a case from CASE SELECT or the case board, then press READY. Play alone or with up to three others; when everyone here is ready, the team deploys to the station.",
		keys = keys("Interact", "Menu"),
	},
	{
		title = "TAPES AND THE DECK",
		body = "VHS tapes are hidden around the station. Take one to a Time-Shift Deck and it shows tonight's CCTV footage - footage of things that have not happened yet.",
		keys = keys("Interact", "Deck"),
	},
	{
		title = "EDITING THE FUTURE",
		body = "Diamonds on the tape are events you can change; circles are their consequences. Amber means one change will set off another. Every edit costs a charge, so think before you commit.",
	},
	{
		title = "THE PRESENTER",
		body = "Something walks the station. If it sees you, it hunts. Break its line of sight, put doors between you, and do not stare at the monitors for too long - it notices who is watching.",
	},
	{
		title = "THE CAMERAS",
		body = "Old closed-circuit sets show the station's cameras live. A weak signal means a rolling, snowy picture. What you see on them is information: use it.",
	},
	{
		title = "GETTING OUT",
		body = "Your objective is always at the top left. Recover the components, stop tonight's broadcast, and get to the van at the loading dock. If you are caught, your team can still finish without you.",
	},
	{
		title = "COMFORT SETTINGS",
		body = "The menu has head bob, motion blur, vignette, field of view, camera shake, text size and a low intensity mode that keeps every piece of information. Good luck.",
		keys = keys("Menu"),
	},
}

local gui, panel, titleText, bodyText, keyRow, counter, backButton, nextButton
local index = 1
local open = false

local tipGui, tipText
local tipsShown = {}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Walkthrough
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function render()
	local step = TutorialController.Steps[index]
	titleText.Text = step.title
	bodyText.Text = step.body
	counter.Text = string.format("BRIEFING %d / %d", index, #TutorialController.Steps)
	UI.clear(keyRow)
	local device = Controls.device()
	for i, action in step.keys or {} do
		local chip = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 30), LayoutOrder = i, Parent = keyRow })
		UI.list(chip, 6, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left).VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
		local cap = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelLight, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 26), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = chip })
		UI.stroke(cap, Theme.Accent, 1)
		UI.corner(cap, 3)
		UI.padding(cap, 8, 0)
		UI.text(cap, Controls.key(action, device), {
			FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
			TextSize = Theme.SmallText,
			TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
			TextWrapped = false,
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
		})
		UI.text(chip, Controls.Labels[action], {
			TextSize = Theme.SmallText,
			TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim,
			TextWrapped = false,
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 26),
			LayoutOrder = 2,
		})
	end
	UI.setEnabled(backButton, index > 1)
	nextButton.Text = index == #TutorialController.Steps and "FINISH" or "NEXT"
end

local function finish()
	if not open then
		return
	end
	open = false
	gui.Enabled = false
	State.setUI("tutorial", false)
	GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
	if State.profile then
		State.profile.tutorialSeen = true
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		State.request("SetTutorialSeen", {})
	end)
end

function TutorialController.start()
	index = 1
	open = true
	gui.Enabled = true
	State.setUI("tutorial", true)
	render()
	panel.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.53)
	TweenService:Create(panel, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) }):Play()
	if #UserInputService:GetConnectedGamepads() > 0 then
		GuiService.SelectedObject = nextButton
	end
end

function TutorialController.isOpen()
	return open
end

local function step(delta)
	local nextIndex = index + delta
	if nextIndex > #TutorialController.Steps then
		finish()
	elseif nextIndex >= 1 then
		index = nextIndex
		render()
	end
end

local function build(playerGui)
	gui = UI.screen("DeadAirTutorial", 60, playerGui)
	gui.Enabled = false
	-- Active: the shade swallows clicks so the menu behind it can't be used.
	local shade = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Black,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.35,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Active = true,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Parent = gui,
	})
	UI.modalUnlock(shade)
	panel = UI.panel(shade, {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 360),
	})
	UI.new("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(640, 420), Parent = panel })
	UI.padding(panel, 22, 18)
	counter = UI.text(panel, "", { TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.Amber, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18) })
	UI.text(panel, "NBRD · FIELD BRIEFING", {
		TextSize = Theme.SmallText,
		TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
	})
	UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Line, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 24), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), Parent = panel })
	titleText = UI.text(panel, "", {
		FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
		TextSize = 26,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Cream,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 36),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
	})
	bodyText = UI.text(panel, "", {
		TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 80),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -190),
	})
	keyRow = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -104), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60), Parent = panel })
	local layout = UI.list(keyRow, 14, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal)
	layout.Wraps = true
	local buttons = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -44), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), Parent = panel })
	UI.button(buttons, "SKIP TUTORIAL", finish, { Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 1, 0), TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim, TextSize = Theme.SmallText })
	backButton = UI.button(buttons, "BACK", function()
		step(-1)
	end, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 1, 0) })
	nextButton = UI.button(buttons, "NEXT", function()
		step(1)
	end, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 1, 0), TextColor3 = Theme.Accent })
	nextButton:SetAttribute("ClickSound", "TutorialPage")
	backButton:SetAttribute("ClickSound", "TutorialPage")

	-- Tip card for in-case hints.
	tipGui = UI.screen("DeadAirTips", 15, playerGui)
	local card = UI.panel(tipGui, {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 200),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(440, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Visible = false,
	})
	UI.padding(card, 12, 8)
	UI.list(card, 2)
	UI.text(card, "TIP", { FontFace = Theme.FontBold, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, TextColor3 = Theme.Amber, LayoutOrder = 1 })
	tipText = UI.text(card, "", { AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextSize = Theme.SmallText, LayoutOrder = 2 })
	TutorialController._tipCard = card
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- One-time tips during a case
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local tipToken = 0

function TutorialController.tip(id, text)
	if tipsShown[id] or not State.settings.tips then
		return false
	end
	tipsShown[id] = true
	tipToken += 1
	local token = tipToken
	tipText.Text = text
	TutorialController._tipCard.Visible = true
	task.delay(7, function()
		if token == tipToken then
			TutorialController._tipCard.Visible = false
		end
	end)
	return true
end

local function firstCase()
	local p = State.profile
	local stats = p and p.cases and p.cases.case01
	return not stats or (stats.runs or 0) < 2
end

local function checkTips()
	if not State.inMission() then
		TutorialController._tipCard.Visible = false
		return
	end
	if not State.isAlive() or not firstCase() then
		return
	end
	local m = State.mission
	if m.phase == "Briefing" or m.phase == "Active" then
		TutorialController.tip("objective", "Your objective is at the top left. Listen to the radio: it tells you where to look.")
	end
	for itemId in m.inventory or {} do
		local caseDef = State.caseDef()
		local item = caseDef and caseDef.items[itemId]
		if item and item.kind == "Tape" then
			TutorialController.tip("tape", string.format("Tape recovered. Stand at a tape deck and press %s to see what happens next.", Controls.tag("Deck")))
		end
	end
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if root then
		local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
		local roomId = MapLayout.roomAt(root.Position.X, root.Position.Z)
		local folder = station and roomId and station.Rooms:FindFirstChild(roomId)
		local mode = folder and folder:GetAttribute("LightMode")
		if mode == "off" or mode == "emergency" then
			TutorialController.tip("dark", string.format("It is dark in here. Press %s for your flashlight.", Controls.tag("Flashlight")))
		end
	end
	local presenter = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	if presenter and presenter:GetAttribute("State") == "Hunting" then
		TutorialController.tip("hunt", "IT IS HUNTING. Break line of sight and keep moving. Doors slow it down.")
	end
end

function TutorialController.init(playerGui)
	build(playerGui)
	local offered = false
	local function maybeStart(profile)
		if offered or not profile then
			return
		end
		offered = true
		if not profile.tutorialSeen and not State.inMission() then
			task.delay(0.5, TutorialController.start)
		end
	end
	State.profileChanged:Connect(maybeStart)
	maybeStart(State.profile)
	State.cue:Connect(function(cue)
		if cue.cue == "OpenTutorial" then
			TutorialController.start()
		end
	end)
	-- Deploying into a case closes the walkthrough (it can be replayed later).
	State.missionChanged:Connect(function()
		if open and State.inMission() then
			finish()
		end
	end)
	local accum = 0
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		accum += dt
		if accum >= 0.5 then
			accum = 0
			checkTips()
		end
	end)
end

return TutorialController
```

## src/client/UI/Controls.luau

```lua
--[[
	Controls — the key, button or touch label for each action on the device the
	player is using right now. The HUD hint, the tutorial, the prompts and the
	controls list in Settings all read from here so they always agree with the
	bindings in InputController.
]]

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local Controls = {}

-- Action order for the controls list.
Controls.Order = { "Move", "Look", "Interact", "Sprint", "Flashlight", "Inspect", "Tool", "Deck", "Menu" }

Controls.Labels = {
	Move = "Move",
	Look = "Look around",
	Interact = "Interact / pick up",
	Sprint = "Sprint",
	Flashlight = "Flashlight",
	Inspect = "Inspect (zoom)",
	Tool = "Equip tool",
	Deck = "Open tape deck",
	Menu = "Menu / pause",
}

local KEYS = {
	keyboard = {
		Move = "W A S D",
		Look = "MOUSE",
		Interact = "E",
		Sprint = "SHIFT",
		Flashlight = "F",
		Inspect = "HOLD RMB",
		Tool = "1",
		Deck = "R",
		Menu = "TAB",
	},
	gamepad = {
		Move = "L-STICK",
		Look = "R-STICK",
		Interact = "X",
		Sprint = "L3",
		Flashlight = "D-DOWN",
		Inspect = "HOLD L2",
		Tool = "D-UP",
		Deck = "Y",
		Menu = "SELECT",
	},
	touch = {
		Move = "LEFT THUMB",
		Look = "DRAG",
		Interact = "TAP PROMPT",
		Sprint = "SPRINT",
		Flashlight = "LIGHT",
		Inspect = "INSPECT",
		Tool = "TOOL",
		Deck = "DECK",
		Menu = "MENU",
	},
}

local GAMEPAD_NAMES = {
	ButtonA = "A",
	ButtonB = "B",
	ButtonX = "X",
	ButtonY = "Y",
	ButtonL1 = "L1",
	ButtonR1 = "R1",
	ButtonL2 = "L2",
	ButtonR2 = "R2",
	ButtonL3 = "L3",
	ButtonR3 = "R3",
	DPadUp = "D-UP",
	DPadDown = "D-DOWN",
	DPadLeft = "D-LEFT",
	DPadRight = "D-RIGHT",
}

local lastDevice = nil

function Controls.device()
	local ok, last = pcall(function()
		return UserInputService:GetLastInputType()
	end)
	if ok and last then
		if last == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			lastDevice = "touch"
		elseif string.sub(last.Name, 1, 7) == "Gamepad" then
			lastDevice = "gamepad"
		elseif last == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or string.sub(last.Name, 1, 5) == "Mouse" then
			lastDevice = "keyboard"
		end
	end
	if lastDevice then
		return lastDevice
	end
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled then
		return "touch"
	elseif UserInputService.GamepadEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled then
		return "gamepad"
	end
	return "keyboard"
end

function Controls.key(action, device)
	local keys = KEYS[device or Controls.device()] or KEYS.keyboard
	return keys[action] or "?"
end

-- "[E]" style, as shown inline in hints.
function Controls.tag(action, device)
	return "[" .. Controls.key(action, device) .. "]"
end

-- Label for a ProximityPrompt's key on the current device.
function Controls.promptKey(prompt, device)
	device = device or Controls.device()
	if device == "touch" then
		return "TAP"
	elseif device == "gamepad" then
		local name = prompt.GamepadKeyCode.Name
		return GAMEPAD_NAMES[name] or name
	end
	local name = prompt.KeyboardKeyCode.Name
	return string.upper(name)
end

return Controls
```

## src/client/UI/FootageView.luau

```lua
--[[
	FootageView — renders one station camera as an old black-and-white tube
	monitor, the kind of closed-circuit set a 1960s building would have had.

	The scene is a static clone of the camera's rooms inside a ViewportFrame,
	converted to greyscale when it is built. Figures (the Presenter,
	investigators) are posed where the footage model says they are. On top sit
	the tube effects: a cool phosphor tint, interlace scanlines, film-like grain
	and dust, a slow dark hum bar rolling up the picture, brightness flicker,
	vertical-hold slips, a faint horizontal wobble, curved-glass falloff at the
	edges, rounded tube corners with a black bezel, and a glass reflection.
	Changing channel rolls the picture; powering on opens from a bright line.
	Noise and slips scale with signal quality and the distortion setting.

	Used by the Time-Shift Deck, the live monitors in the station, the handheld
	monitor and the van monitor.
]]

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local Figures = require(Shared.World.Figures)

local UI = require(script.Parent.UI)
local Theme = require(script.Parent.Theme)

local FootageView = {}
FootageView.__index = FootageView

-- Monochrome: emergency lighting simply reads as a dimmer picture.
local LIGHT_MODES = {
	on = { ambient = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150), light = Color3.fromRGB(226, 226, 226) },
	emergency = { ambient = Color3.fromRGB(78, 78, 78), light = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150) },
	off = { ambient = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 14), light = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 24) },
}

local PHOSPHOR = Color3.fromRGB(218, 226, 232) -- cool white of an old tube
local OSD_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(236, 238, 232)

local NOISE_COUNT = 64
local NOISE_COUNT_COMPACT = 22
local SCANLINES = 60
local SCANLINES_COMPACT = 30

-- Luminance with a little extra contrast, as a tube set renders it.
local function mono(colour)
	local l = 0.299 * colour.R + 0.587 * colour.G + 0.114 * colour.B
	l = math.clamp((l - 0.5) * 1.18 + 0.5, 0, 1)
	return Color3.new(l, l, l)
end

local function toMonochrome(root)
	for _, d in root:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			d.Color = mono(d.Color)
		end
	end
end

local function v3(p, y)
	return Vector3.new(p.x, y or p.y or 0, p.z)
end

local function roomsTouch(doorRooms, rooms)
	for _, id in doorRooms do
		if table.find(rooms, id) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

--[[
	opts = { compact = bool (no labels, for small monitors), zIndex = number }
]]
function FootageView.new(parent, opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local self = setmetatable({}, FootageView)
	self.compact = opts.compact == true
	self.scenes = {}
	self.cameraId = nil
	self.station = nil
	self.quality = 100
	self.intensity = 1
	self.rng = Random.new()

	self.root = UI.new("Frame", {
		Name = "Footage",
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		Size = opts.size or UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Position = opts.position or UDim2.new(),
		Parent = parent,
	})
	self.t = 0
	self.rollOffset = 0
	self.switchUntil = 0
	self.powerT = 1
	self.flicker = 0
	self.viewport = UI.new("ViewportFrame", {
		Name = "Viewport",
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		ImageColor3 = PHOSPHOR,
		Ambient = LIGHT_MODES.on.ambient,
		LightColor = LIGHT_MODES.on.light,
		LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.3, -1, -0.2),
		Parent = self.root,
	})
	UI.new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.06, 0), Parent = self.viewport })
	self.camera = Instance.new("Camera")
	self.camera.FieldOfView = 68
	self.camera.Parent = self.viewport
	self.viewport.CurrentCamera = self.camera

	self.figureRoot = Instance.new("Model")
	self.figureRoot.Name = "Figures"
	self.figureRoot.Parent = self.viewport
	self.presenterPool = {}
	self.investigatorPool = {}

	-- Overlays
	self.overlay = UI.new("Frame", { Name = "Overlay", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 5, Parent = self.root })
	local lines = self.compact and SCANLINES_COMPACT or SCANLINES
	for i = 0, lines - 1 do
		UI.new("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.8,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, i / lines),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
			ZIndex = 5,
			Parent = self.overlay,
		})
	end
	-- Grain: bright specks and dark dust.
	self.noise = {}
	for i = 1, self.compact and NOISE_COUNT_COMPACT or NOISE_COUNT do
		self.noise[i] = UI.new("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = i % 3 == 0 and Color3.new(0, 0, 0) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = 6,
			Parent = self.overlay,
		})
	end
	-- The hum bar: a soft dark band that crawls up the picture.
	self.band = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.22, 0),
		ZIndex = 6,
		Parent = self.overlay,
	})
	self.bandGradient = UI.new("UIGradient", {
		Rotation = 90,
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.7),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
		}),
		Parent = self.band,
	})
	-- A thin bright tracking line that sometimes rides with the bar.
	self.trackLine = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
		ZIndex = 6,
		Parent = self.overlay,
	})
	-- Whole-picture brightness flicker.
	self.flickerFrame = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		ZIndex = 7,
		Parent = self.overlay,
	})
	-- Curved glass: the picture falls off towards the edges.
	local function falloff(rotation, size, position, anchor)
		local f = UI.new("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AnchorPoint = anchor,
			Position = position,
			Size = size,
			ZIndex = 7,
			Parent = self.overlay,
		})
		UI.new("UIGradient", {
			Rotation = rotation,
			Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.82),
				NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
			}),
			Parent = f,
		})
	end
	falloff(90, UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.24), UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), Vector2.new(0, 0))
	falloff(-90, UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.24), UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Vector2.new(0, 1))
	falloff(0, UDim2.fromScale(0.18, 1), UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), Vector2.new(0, 0))
	falloff(180, UDim2.fromScale(0.18, 1), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), Vector2.new(1, 0))
	-- Reflection on the glass, top left.
	local glare = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.55, 0.45),
		ZIndex = 7,
		Parent = self.overlay,
	})
	UI.new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.3, 0), Parent = glare })
	UI.new("UIGradient", {
		Rotation = 35,
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.9),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.965),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
		}),
		Parent = glare,
	})
	-- Power-on: the picture opens out of a bright horizontal line.
	self.powerTop = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), ZIndex = 9, Parent = self.overlay })
	self.powerBottom = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		ZIndex = 9,
		Parent = self.overlay,
	})
	self.powerLine = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = PHOSPHOR,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3),
		ZIndex = 9,
		Parent = self.overlay,
	})
	-- Rounded tube mask and bezel over everything.
	local bezel = UI.new("Frame", { Name = "Bezel", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 10, Parent = self.root })
	UI.new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.06, 0), Parent = bezel })
	UI.new("UIStroke", {
		Color = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 12),
		Thickness = self.compact and 4 or 10,
		ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
		Parent = bezel,
	})
	self.message = UI.text(self.overlay, "", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.9, 0.2),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextColor3 = OSD_TEXT,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4,
		TextSize = self.compact and 14 or 22,
		ZIndex = 8,
	})
	self.hidden = UI.text(self.overlay, "", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.12),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.8, 0.1),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextColor3 = OSD_TEXT,
		TextTransparency = 0.55,
		TextSize = 14,
		ZIndex = 8,
	})
	if not self.compact then
		self.label = UI.text(self.overlay, "", {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6),
			Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 20),
			TextColor3 = OSD_TEXT,
			TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5,
			ZIndex = 8,
		})
		self.timestamp = UI.text(self.overlay, "", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 6),
			Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 20),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
			TextColor3 = OSD_TEXT,
			TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5,
			ZIndex = 8,
		})
		self.status = UI.text(self.overlay, "", {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -26),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20),
			TextColor3 = Theme.Amber,
			TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5,
			ZIndex = 8,
		})
	end
	return self
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Scene
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function FootageView:_station()
	local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
	if station ~= self.station then
		for _, scene in self.scenes do
			scene:Destroy()
		end
		self.scenes = {}
		self.station = station
	end
	return station
end

function FootageView:_buildScene(cameraId)
	local station = self:_station()
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	if not station or not camera then
		return nil
	end
	local scene = Instance.new("Model")
	scene.Name = cameraId
	local rooms = station:FindFirstChild("Rooms")
	for _, roomId in camera.rooms do
		local source = rooms and rooms:FindFirstChild(roomId)
		if source then
			local copy = source:Clone()
			-- Strip anything a viewport can't render. This matters: rooms hold the
			-- CCTV monitors, whose SurfaceGuis contain their own cloned scenes -
			-- keeping them would nest copies inside copies on every monitor.
			for _, d in copy:GetDescendants() do
				if d:IsA("LayerCollector") or d:IsA("Light") or d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or d:IsA("Sound") then
					d:Destroy()
				end
			end
			copy.Parent = scene
		end
	end
	local doors = station:FindFirstChild("Doors")
	if doors then
		for _, door in doors:GetChildren() do
			local layout = MapLayout.doorById(door.Name)
			if layout and roomsTouch(layout.rooms, camera.rooms) then
				door:Clone().Parent = scene
			end
		end
	end
	local windows = station:FindFirstChild("Windows")
	if windows then
		for _, glass in windows:GetChildren() do
			local id = glass:GetAttribute("WindowId")
			for _, win in MapLayout.Windows do
				if win.id == id and roomsTouch(win.rooms, camera.rooms) then
					glass:Clone().Parent = scene
				end
			end
		end
	end
	toMonochrome(scene)
	return scene
end

function FootageView:setCamera(cameraId)
	self:_station()
	if self.cameraId == cameraId and self.scenes[cameraId] and self.scenes[cameraId].Parent == self.viewport then
		return
	end
	for _, scene in self.scenes do
		scene.Parent = nil
	end
	if self.cameraId ~= nil and self.cameraId ~= cameraId then
		-- Changing channel: the picture rolls and breaks up for a moment.
		self.switchUntil = self.t + 0.35
		self.rollOffset = 0.6
	end
	self.cameraId = cameraId
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	if not camera then
		return
	end
	local scene = self.scenes[cameraId]
	if not scene then
		scene = self:_buildScene(cameraId)
		self.scenes[cameraId] = scene
	end
	if scene then
		scene.Parent = self.viewport
	end
	self.camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(v3(camera.pos), v3(camera.look))
	if self.label then
		self.label.Text = camera.label
	end
end

function FootageView:setLights(mode)
	local look = LIGHT_MODES[mode] or LIGHT_MODES.on
	self.viewport.Ambient = look.ambient
	self.viewport.LightColor = look.light
end

local function pool(self, list, build, count)
	while #list < count do
		table.insert(list, build(self.figureRoot))
	end
	for i, model in list do
		if i > count then
			model.Parent = nil
		elseif model.Parent ~= self.figureRoot then
			model.Parent = self.figureRoot
		end
	end
end

local function pose(model, pos, look)
	local height = Figures.rootHeight(model)
	local at = Vector3.new(pos.x, (pos.y or 0) + height, pos.z)
	local target = look and Vector3.new(look.x, at.Y, look.z) or (at + Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
	if (target - at).Magnitude < 0.1 then
		target = at + Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
	end
	model:PivotTo(CFrame.lookAt(at, target))
end

-- actors: { { kind = "Presenter" | "Investigator", pos, look } }
function FootageView:setActors(actors)
	local presenters, people = {}, {}
	for _, a in actors or {} do
		if a.kind == "Presenter" then
			table.insert(presenters, a)
		else
			table.insert(people, a)
		end
	end
	pool(self, self.presenterPool, function(parent)
		local model = Figures.presenter(parent, { ghost = true })
		toMonochrome(model)
		return model
	end, #presenters)
	pool(self, self.investigatorPool, function(parent)
		local model = Figures.investigator(parent)
		toMonochrome(model)
		return model
	end, #people)
	for i, a in presenters do
		pose(self.presenterPool[i], a.pos, a.look)
	end
	for i, a in people do
		pose(self.investigatorPool[i], a.pos, a.look)
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Overlays
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function FootageView:setSignal(quality, intensity)
	self.quality = quality
	self.intensity = intensity or 1
end

function FootageView:setTexts(texts)
	if texts.message ~= nil then
		self.message.Text = texts.message
	end
	if texts.hidden ~= nil then
		self.hidden.Text = texts.hidden
	end
	if self.timestamp and texts.timestamp ~= nil then
		self.timestamp.Text = texts.timestamp
	end
	if self.status and texts.status ~= nil then
		self.status.Text = texts.status
	end
	if self.label and texts.label ~= nil then
		self.label.Text = texts.label
	end
end

-- Plays the tube warming up: the picture opens out of a bright line.
function FootageView:powerOn()
	self.powerT = 0
end

-- Animates the tube; call every frame (or a few times a second for monitors).
function FootageView:step(dt)
	self.t += dt
	local t = self.t
	local rng = self.rng
	local switching = t < self.switchUntil
	local loss = math.clamp((100 - self.quality) / 100, 0, 1)
	if switching then
		loss = math.max(loss, 0.75)
	end
	local intensity = self.intensity

	-- Grain is always there on an old set; signal loss thickens it.
	local amount = math.clamp(0.3 + loss * 0.7, 0, 1) * math.max(intensity, 0.35)
	local active = math.floor(#self.noise * amount + 0.5)
	for i, dot in self.noise do
		if i <= active then
			local long = rng:NextNumber() < loss * 0.5
			dot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.55
			dot.Size = long and UDim2.new(rng:NextNumber() * 0.25, 2, 0, rng:NextInteger(1, 2)) or UDim2.fromOffset(rng:NextInteger(1, 3), rng:NextInteger(1, 3))
			dot.Position = UDim2.fromScale(rng:NextNumber(), rng:NextNumber())
		else
			dot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end
	end

	-- Hum bar crawling upwards, about one pass every seven seconds.
	local barY = 1.1 - ((t * 0.15) % 1.35)
	self.band.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, barY - 0.11)
	self.bandGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, math.clamp(0.8 - loss * 0.3 * intensity, 0.4, 0.9)),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
	})
	local tracking = loss > 0.3 or rng:NextNumber() < 0.04
	self.trackLine.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, barY + (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 0.04)
	self.trackLine.BackgroundTransparency = tracking and (0.55 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.35) or 1

	-- Brightness flicker, with the odd bright pulse.
	local target = 0.9 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.08
	if rng:NextNumber() < 0.03 * math.max(intensity, 0.3) then
		target = 0.75
	end
	self.flicker += (target - self.flicker) * 0.5
	self.flickerFrame.BackgroundTransparency = self.flicker + (1 - self.flicker) * (1 - math.min(1, intensity + 0.3))

	-- Vertical hold slips: the frame rolls and snaps back.
	if self.rollOffset <= 0 and rng:NextNumber() < loss * loss * 0.04 * intensity then
		self.rollOffset = 0.25 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.5
	end
	local rollY = 0
	if self.rollOffset > 0 then
		rollY = self.rollOffset
		self.rollOffset = math.max(0, self.rollOffset - dt * 2.4)
	end
	-- Horizontal wobble from an unsteady time base, worse on a weak signal.
	local wobble = math.sin(t * 7.3) * (0.6 + loss * 3) + math.sin(t * 23.1) * 0.4
	local jitter = loss > 0.45 and (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 10 * loss * intensity or 0
	self.viewport.Position = UDim2.new(0, wobble * intensity + jitter, -rollY, 0)
	self.viewport.ImageTransparency = math.clamp(loss * 0.55 + (switching and 0.3 or 0), 0, 0.75)

	-- Power-on animation.
	if self.powerT < 1 then
		self.powerT = math.min(1, self.powerT + dt / 0.45)
		local open = self.powerT < 0.35 and 0 or (self.powerT - 0.35) / 0.65
		local closed = (1 - open) / 2
		self.powerTop.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, closed)
		self.powerBottom.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, closed)
		self.powerLine.BackgroundTransparency = self.powerT < 0.5 and 0 or (self.powerT - 0.5) * 2
		self.powerLine.Size = UDim2.new(math.min(1, self.powerT / 0.35), 0, 0, 3)
	elseif self.powerTop.Size.Y.Scale > 0 or self.powerLine.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then
		self.powerTop.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
		self.powerBottom.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
		self.powerLine.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end
end

function FootageView:destroy()
	self.root:Destroy()
end

return FootageView
```

```lua
--[[
	Sound — the one call every controller uses to make a noise:

	    Sound.play("TapeInsert")
	    Sound.play("DoorOpen", { at = Vector3 })        -- positional
	    Sound.loop("TapeScrub", true) / Sound.loop("TapeScrub", false)

	AudioController installs the real implementation at start-up. Until then
	(and in any code path that runs without it) these are silent no-ops, so
	nothing depends on audio being available.
]]

local Sound = {}

function Sound.play(_name, _opts) end

function Sound.loop(_name, _on) end

return Sound
```

## src/client/UI/Theme.luau

```lua
--[[
	Visual language for every screen: dark green-black panels, phosphor text,
	cream labels, amber for linked-event warnings, red only for real danger.
	Menu colour themes (cosmetics) only change the accent - all information stays
	equally readable.
]]

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local Typeface = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared").Config.Typeface)

local Theme = {}

Theme.Background = Color3.fromRGB(10, 14, 12)
Theme.Panel = Color3.fromRGB(16, 22, 18)
Theme.PanelLight = Color3.fromRGB(26, 34, 28)
Theme.Line = Color3.fromRGB(60, 84, 66)
Theme.Accent = Color3.fromRGB(150, 210, 160)
Theme.AccentDim = Color3.fromRGB(90, 130, 100)
Theme.Text = Color3.fromRGB(214, 224, 210)
Theme.TextDim = Color3.fromRGB(130, 146, 128)
Theme.Cream = Color3.fromRGB(196, 186, 158)
Theme.Amber = Color3.fromRGB(226, 164, 76)
Theme.Danger = Color3.fromRGB(206, 76, 60)
Theme.Good = Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 120)
Theme.Black = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

-- One family everywhere (see Shared.Config.Typeface); weights set hierarchy.
Theme.Font = Typeface.regular()
Theme.FontBold = Typeface.bold()
Theme.FontTitle = Typeface.bold()

Theme.TextSize = 16
Theme.SmallText = 13
Theme.LargeText = 22
Theme.TouchTarget = 44

function Theme.applyAccent(colour)
	if typeof(colour) == "Color3" then
		Theme.Accent = colour
		Theme.AccentDim = colour:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.4)
	end
end

return Theme
```

## src/client/UI/UI.luau

```lua
--[[
	Tiny UI construction helpers. Everything is built in code so the whole
	interface lives in the repository.
]]

local Theme = require(script.Parent.Theme)
local Sound = require(script.Parent.Sound)

local UI = {}

-- UI.new("Frame", { Size = ..., Parent = ... }, { children })
function UI.new(className, props, children)
	local inst = Instance.new(className)
	-- Every text object starts in the game's one typeface.
	if className == "TextLabel" or className == "TextButton" or className == "TextBox" then
		inst.FontFace = Theme.Font
	end
	local parent = nil
	for k, v in props or {} do
		if k == "Parent" then
			parent = v
		else
			inst[k] = v
		end
	end
	for _, child in children or {} do
		child.Parent = inst
	end
	if parent then
		inst.Parent = parent
	end
	return inst
end

function UI.screen(name, order, playerGui)
	return UI.new("ScreenGui", {
		Name = name,
		DisplayOrder = order,
		IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
		Parent = playerGui,
	})
end

function UI.stroke(parent, colour, thickness, transparency)
	return UI.new("UIStroke", {
		Color = colour or Theme.Line,
		Thickness = thickness or 1,
		Transparency = transparency or 0,
		ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

function UI.corner(parent, radius)
	return UI.new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 2), Parent = parent })
end

function UI.padding(parent, px, py)
	py = py or px
	return UI.new("UIPadding", {
		PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, px),
		PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, px),
		PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, py),
		PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, py),
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

function UI.list(parent, padding, direction, align)
	return UI.new("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, padding or 6),
		FillDirection = direction or Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		HorizontalAlignment = align or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

function UI.panel(parent, props)
	local frame = UI.new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	for k, v in props or {} do
		frame[k] = v
	end
	UI.stroke(frame, Theme.Line, 1)
	return frame
end

function UI.text(parent, text, props)
	local label = UI.new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text or "",
		FontFace = Theme.Font,
		TextSize = Theme.TextSize,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
		TextWrapped = true,
		RichText = false,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, Theme.TextSize + 6),
		Parent = parent,
	})
	for k, v in props or {} do
		label[k] = v
	end
	return label
end

-- Large, controller-selectable button with a visible focus state.
function UI.button(parent, text, onClick, props)
	local button = UI.new("TextButton", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelLight,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Text = text or "",
		FontFace = Theme.FontBold,
		TextSize = Theme.TextSize,
		TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, Theme.TouchTarget),
		Selectable = true,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	for k, v in props or {} do
		button[k] = v
	end
	local stroke = UI.stroke(button, Theme.Line, 1)
	local function refresh(highlight)
		stroke.Color = highlight and Theme.Accent or Theme.Line
		stroke.Thickness = highlight and 2 or 1
		button.BackgroundColor3 = highlight and Theme.PanelLight:Lerp(Theme.Accent, 0.15) or Theme.PanelLight
	end
	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		refresh(true)
		if not button:GetAttribute("Disabled") then
			Sound.play("UIHover")
		end
	end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		refresh(false)
	end)
	button.SelectionGained:Connect(function()
		refresh(true)
		Sound.play("UIHover")
	end)
	button.SelectionLost:Connect(function()
		refresh(false)
	end)
	if onClick then
		button.Activated:Connect(function()
			if button:GetAttribute("Disabled") then
				Sound.play("UIError")
				return
			end
			Sound.play(button:GetAttribute("ClickSound") or "UIClick")
			onClick(button)
		end)
	end
	return button
end

function UI.setEnabled(button, enabled)
	button:SetAttribute("Disabled", not enabled)
	button.TextTransparency = enabled and 0 or 0.55
	button.BackgroundTransparency = enabled and 0.05 or 0.4
end

-- Removes every child except layout objects.
function UI.clear(container)
	for _, child in container:GetChildren() do
		if not child:IsA("UIComponent") then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

-- A scrolling column that grows with its content.
function UI.scroll(parent, props)
	local frame = UI.new("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 6,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.AccentDim,
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
		Selectable = false,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	for k, v in props or {} do
		frame[k] = v
	end
	return frame
end

-- Horizontal slider 0..1. onChange(value) fires while dragging.
function UI.slider(parent, label, value, onChange, opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local holder = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), Parent = parent })
	local caption = UI.text(holder, "", { Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0) })
	local track = UI.new("TextButton", {
		Text = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelLight,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0.47, 0, 0.5, -8),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 16),
		Selectable = true,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	UI.stroke(track, Theme.Line)
	local fill = UI.new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.AccentDim, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Parent = track })
	local min, max = opts.min or 0, opts.max or 1
	local step = opts.step or 0.05
	local current = value
	local function render()
		local alpha = (current - min) / (max - min)
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(alpha, 0, 1), 1)
		caption.Text = string.format("%s  %s", label, opts.format and opts.format(current) or string.format("%d%%", math.floor(alpha * 100 + 0.5)))
	end
	local function setFromX(x)
		local alpha = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
		local raw = min + (max - min) * alpha
		local snapped = math.clamp(math.floor(raw / step + 0.5) * step, min, max)
		if snapped ~= current then
			Sound.play("UISlider")
		end
		current = snapped
		render()
		onChange(current)
	end
	local dragging = false
	track.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Right then
			current = math.clamp(current + step, min, max)
			Sound.play("UISlider")
			render()
			onChange(current)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Left then
			current = math.clamp(current - step, min, max)
			Sound.play("UISlider")
			render()
			onChange(current)
		end
	end)
	track.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	track.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = false
		end
	end)
	render()
	return holder
end

function UI.toggle(parent, label, value, onChange)
	local current = value
	local button
	button = UI.button(parent, "", function()
		current = not current
		button.Text = string.format("%s: %s", label, current and "ON" or "OFF")
		onChange(current)
	end)
	button.Text = string.format("%s: %s", label, current and "ON" or "OFF")
	button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	button:SetAttribute("ClickSound", "UIToggle")
	UI.padding(button, 10, 0)
	return button
end

-- An invisible modal button: while visible it frees the mouse in first person.
function UI.modalUnlock(parent)
	return UI.new("TextButton", {
		Name = "ModalUnlock",
		Text = "",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		Modal = true,
		Selectable = false,
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

return UI
```

## src/server/Main.server.luau

```lua
--[[
	DEAD AIR: CHANNEL ZERO — server bootstrap.

	Service responsibilities (from the design brief):
	  MissionService      starts cases, tracks objectives, extraction/failure, cleanup
	  TimelineService     edit requests from the Time-Shift Deck, hidden frames
	  EntityService       the Presenter's state machine, pathfinding and targeting
	  EvidenceService     tape discovery, evidence and permanent unlocks
	  SignalService       deterministic signal quality
	  InteractionService  ProximityPrompts, range checks
	  DataService         progression with DataStoreService
	  MarketService       gamepasses and developer products
	  LobbyService        the field office: party, deploy, equipment, cosmetics
	  WorldService        the physical station: map template, doors, lights, items
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Net = require(Shared.Net)
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local Surfaces = require(Shared.Config.Surfaces)
local TimelineCore = require(Shared.Core.TimelineCore)
local MapBuilder = require(Shared.World.MapBuilder)

local Services = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Services")
local Router = require(Services.Router)

local deps = {
	DataService = require(Services.DataService),
	MarketService = require(Services.MarketService),
	WorldService = require(Services.WorldService),
	InteractionService = require(Services.InteractionService),
	EntityService = require(Services.EntityService),
	SignalService = require(Services.SignalService),
	TimelineService = require(Services.TimelineService),
	EvidenceService = require(Services.EvidenceService),
	MissionService = require(Services.MissionService),
	LobbyService = require(Services.LobbyService),
}

-- Refuse to run a case whose event graph is inconsistent.
for caseId, caseDef in CaseRegistry.Cases do
	local errors = TimelineCore.validateCase(caseDef, MapLayout)
	if #errors > 0 then
		error(string.format("Case %s failed validation:\n%s", caseId, table.concat(errors, "\n")))
	end
end

-- Optional photographic textures (see Shared.Config.Surfaces).
Surfaces.install(game:GetService("MaterialService"))

local remotes = Net.create(ReplicatedStorage)
Router.init(remotes, Players)

deps.DataService.init()
deps.WorldService.init()
deps.MarketService.init(deps)
deps.InteractionService.init(deps)
deps.EntityService.init(deps)
deps.SignalService.init(deps)
deps.TimelineService.init(deps)
deps.EvidenceService.init(deps)
deps.MissionService.init(deps, remotes)
deps.LobbyService.init(deps, remotes)

deps.InteractionService.bindStation(deps.WorldService.station)
deps.InteractionService.bindHQ(deps.WorldService.hq, MapBuilder.HQTerminals)

-- The station sits idle (lights off) until a case starts.
deps.WorldService.setIdleLights()

print("[DeadAir] Server ready.")
```

## src/server/Services/DataService.luau

```lua
--[[
	DataService — loads and saves player progression with DataStoreService.

	* UpdateAsync everywhere, with a lightweight session lock so two servers never
	  overwrite each other's progress.
	* Retries with back-off; if DataStores are unavailable (for example in Studio
	  without "Enable Studio Access to API Services"), the player keeps a
	  session-only profile and is told progress will not be saved.
	* Autosaves dirty profiles every two minutes, on leave and on shutdown - never
	  after every tiny interaction.
]]

local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local ProfileSchema = require(Shared.Core.ProfileSchema)

local DataService = {}

local STORE_NAME = "DeadAir_Profiles_v1"
local LEADERBOARD_PREFIX = "DeadAir_ArchiveChallenge_w"
local LOCK_TIMEOUT = 300
local AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL = 120
local MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5

DataService.profiles = {} -- [player] = { data, dirty, offline, loaded }
DataService.loadedCallbacks = {}

local store = nil
local storeOk = pcall(function()
	store = DataStoreService:GetDataStore(STORE_NAME)
end)

local function keyFor(userId)
	return "u_" .. tostring(userId)
end

local function retry(fn)
	local lastErr
	for attempt = 1, MAX_ATTEMPTS do
		local ok, result = pcall(fn)
		if ok then
			return true, result
		end
		lastErr = result
		task.wait(math.min(2 ^ attempt * 0.5, 8))
	end
	return false, lastErr
end

-- Strips runtime-only fields before writing.
local function serialise(data)
	local copy = table.clone(data)
	copy.__lock = nil
	return copy
end

function DataService.onLoaded(callback)
	table.insert(DataService.loadedCallbacks, callback)
end

function DataService.get(player)
	local entry = DataService.profiles[player]
	return entry and entry.loaded and entry.data or nil
end

function DataService.isOffline(player)
	local entry = DataService.profiles[player]
	return entry == nil or entry.offline == true
end

function DataService.markDirty(player)
	local entry = DataService.profiles[player]
	if entry then
		entry.dirty = true
	end
end

function DataService.load(player)
	local entry = { data = nil, dirty = false, offline = false, loaded = false }
	DataService.profiles[player] = entry
	local data = nil
	if storeOk and store then
		local key = keyFor(player.UserId)
		local jobId = game.JobId
		for attempt = 1, MAX_ATTEMPTS do
			local blocked = false
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return store:UpdateAsync(key, function(old)
					local profile = ProfileSchema.migrate(old)
					local lock = type(old) == "table" and old.__lock or nil
					local stale = not lock or os.time() - (lock.time or 0) > LOCK_TIMEOUT
					if lock and lock.jobId ~= jobId and not stale and attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS then
						blocked = true
						return nil -- another server still owns it; retry shortly
					end
					local saved = serialise(profile)
					saved.__lock = { jobId = jobId, time = os.time() }
					return saved
				end)
			end)
			if ok and not blocked then
				data = ProfileSchema.migrate(result)
				break
			end
			if not ok then
				warn(string.format("[DataService] load attempt %d for %s failed: %s", attempt, player.Name, tostring(result)))
			end
			task.wait(blocked and 3 or 2 ^ attempt * 0.5)
		end
	end
	if not data then
		entry.offline = true
		data = ProfileSchema.default()
		if not RunService:IsStudio() then
			warn("[DataService] Using a session-only profile for " .. player.Name)
		end
	end
	data.__lock = nil
	entry.data = data
	entry.loaded = true
	if player.Parent ~= Players then
		return nil
	end
	for _, callback in DataService.loadedCallbacks do
		task.spawn(callback, player, data, entry.offline)
	end
	return data
end

function DataService.save(player, release)
	local entry = DataService.profiles[player]
	if not entry or not entry.loaded or entry.offline or not store then
		return false
	end
	local jobId = game.JobId
	local snapshot = serialise(entry.data)
	local ok, err = retry(function()
		store:UpdateAsync(keyFor(player.UserId), function(old)
			local lock = type(old) == "table" and old.__lock or nil
			if lock and lock.jobId ~= jobId and os.time() - (lock.time or 0) <= LOCK_TIMEOUT then
				-- Another server took over this profile; don't clobber it.
				return nil
			end
			local out = table.clone(snapshot)
			out.__lock = not release and { jobId = jobId, time = os.time() } or nil
			return out
		end)
	end)
	if ok then
		entry.dirty = false
	else
		warn(string.format("[DataService] save for %s failed: %s", player.Name, tostring(err)))
	end
	return ok
end

-- Saves now (used after purchases). Returns success.
function DataService.saveNow(player)
	local entry = DataService.profiles[player]
	if entry and entry.offline then
		-- Nothing to persist to; the grant still applies for this session.
		return true
	end
	return DataService.save(player, false)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Archive Challenge leaderboard (weekly OrderedDataStore).
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function DataService.submitScore(week, userId, score)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		local board = DataStoreService:GetOrderedDataStore(LEADERBOARD_PREFIX .. tostring(week))
		board:UpdateAsync(tostring(userId), function(old)
			if type(old) == "number" and old >= score then
				return nil
			end
			return math.floor(score)
		end)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[DataService] leaderboard submit failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
	return ok
end

local leaderboardCache = { week = nil, at = 0, rows = {} }

function DataService.topScores(week)
	if leaderboardCache.week == week and os.clock() - leaderboardCache.at < 60 then
		return leaderboardCache.rows
	end
	local rows = {}
	local ok = pcall(function()
		local board = DataStoreService:GetOrderedDataStore(LEADERBOARD_PREFIX .. tostring(week))
		local page = board:GetSortedAsync(false, 10):GetCurrentPage()
		for rank, entry in page do
			local name = "Investigator"
			pcall(function()
				name = Players:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(tonumber(entry.key))
			end)
			table.insert(rows, { rank = rank, name = name, score = entry.value })
		end
	end)
	if ok then
```
