```lua
		leaderboardCache = { week = week, at = os.clock(), rows = rows }
	end
	return rows
end

function DataService.init()
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		DataService.save(player, true)
		DataService.profiles[player] = nil
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL)
			for player, entry in DataService.profiles do
				if entry.dirty and player.Parent == Players then
					task.spawn(DataService.save, player, false)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	game:BindToClose(function()
		local pending = 0
		for player in DataService.profiles do
			pending += 1
			task.spawn(function()
				DataService.save(player, true)
				pending -= 1
			end)
		end
		local started = os.clock()
		while pending > 0 and os.clock() - started < 25 do
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
	end)
end

return DataService
```

## src/server/Services/EntityService.luau

```lua
--[[
	EntityService — the Presenter in the world.

	PresenterBrain makes every decision; this service gathers the senses it needs
	(line of sight, light, flashlights, refuges, revive protection), then moves the
	rig with Humanoid:MoveTo / PathfindingService. Movement and targeting are
	server-authoritative; clients only add presentation (static, sway, audio).
]]

local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local PresenterBrain = require(Shared.Core.PresenterBrain)
local Figures = require(Shared.World.Figures)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)

local EntityService = {}

local TICK = 0.1
local TELEPORT_DRIFT = 14
local DOOR_OPEN_RADIUS = 5
local STUCK_TIME = 2.5

EntityService.model = nil
EntityService.brain = nil
EntityService.state = "Dormant"
EntityService.activeEventId = nil

local deps
local parked
local path = nil
local pathWaypoints = nil
local pathIndex = 0
local pathTarget = nil
local pathBusy = false
local pathFailedAt = -math.huge
local lastProgress = { pos = nil, at = 0 }
local stuckCount = 0

local function root()
	return EntityService.model and EntityService.model.PrimaryPart
end

local function humanoid()
	return EntityService.model and EntityService.model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function toV3(p)
	return Vector3.new(p.x, p.y or 0, p.z)
end

local function toVec(v)
	return { x = v.X, y = v.Y, z = v.Z }
end

function EntityService.position()
	local r = root()
	if r and EntityService.model.Parent == Workspace then
		return r.Position
	end
	return nil
end

function EntityService.isIdle()
	return EntityService.brain == nil or EntityService.brain:isIdle()
end

local function park()
	local model = EntityService.model
	if model and model.Parent ~= parked then
		model.Parent = parked
	end
	path, pathWaypoints, pathTarget = nil, nil, nil
end

local function place(pos, facePos)
	local model = EntityService.model
	local height = Figures.rootHeight(model)
	local at = Vector3.new(pos.x, (pos.y or 0) + height, pos.z)
	local cf
	if facePos then
		local look = Vector3.new(facePos.x, at.Y, facePos.z)
		if (look - at).Magnitude > 0.1 then
			cf = CFrame.lookAt(at, look)
		end
	end
	model:PivotTo(cf or CFrame.new(at))
	if model.Parent ~= Workspace then
		model.Parent = Workspace
		local r = root()
		pcall(function()
			r:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
		end)
	end
	local h = humanoid()
	if h then
		h:MoveTo(at)
	end
end

local function face(target)
	local r = root()
	if not r or not target then
		return
	end
	local pos = r.Position
	local look = Vector3.new(target.x, pos.Y, target.z)
	if (look - pos).Magnitude > 0.5 then
		r.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, look)
	end
end

function EntityService.reset(modeDef)
	if EntityService.model then
		EntityService.model:Destroy()
	end
	EntityService.model = Figures.presenter(parked)
	EntityService.model:SetAttribute("State", "Dormant")
	local h = humanoid()
	if h then
		-- A welded (non-jointed) rig: keep the Humanoid upright and walking only.
		for _, state in {
			Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown,
			Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll,
			Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing,
			Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming,
			Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,
			Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping,
			Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead,
		} do
			h:SetStateEnabled(state, false)
		end
	end
	EntityService.brain = PresenterBrain.new({ huntSpeed = (modeDef and modeDef.huntSpeed) or 14.5 })
	EntityService.state = "Dormant"
	EntityService.activeEventId = nil
	park()
end

function EntityService.command(command, startTime, meta)
	if not EntityService.brain then
		return
	end
	EntityService.activeEventId = meta and meta.eventId
	EntityService.brain:setCommand(command, startTime, meta)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Observing spots: somewhere in the player's room, at a distance, in view.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
rayParams.RespectCanCollide = true

local function refreshRayFilter()
	local list = {}
	if EntityService.model then
		table.insert(list, EntityService.model)
	end
	for _, p in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if p.Character then
			table.insert(list, p.Character)
		end
	end
	for _, g in deps.WorldService.glass do
		table.insert(list, g)
	end
	if deps.WorldService.itemsFolder then
		table.insert(list, deps.WorldService.itemsFolder)
	end
	rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
end

local function clearLine(from, to)
	local result = Workspace:Raycast(from, to - from, rayParams)
	return result == nil
end

function EntityService.observeNear(userId, startTime)
	if not EntityService.brain or not EntityService.brain:isIdle() then
		return
	end
	local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
	local character = player and player.Character
	local r = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not r then
		return
	end
	refreshRayFilter()
	local roomId = MapLayout.roomAt(r.Position.X, r.Position.Z)
	local room = roomId and MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
	if not room or room.hq then
		return
	end
	local candidates = {}
	for _, u in { 0.12, 0.5, 0.88 } do
		for _, v in { 0.12, 0.5, 0.88 } do
			table.insert(candidates, MapLayout.point(roomId, u, v, 0))
		end
	end
	local best, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
	local eye = r.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
	for _, c in candidates do
		local spot = toV3(c)
		local dist = (Vector3.new(spot.X, eye.Y, spot.Z) - eye).Magnitude
		if dist >= 16 and dist <= 48 and clearLine(eye, spot + Vector3.new(0, 7.5, 0)) then
			local score = -math.abs(dist - 28)
			if score > bestScore then
				best, bestScore = c, score
			end
		end
	end
	if not best then
		return
	end
	EntityService.command({
		kind = "Observe",
		pos = best,
		look = toVec(r.Position),
		duration = 8,
		triggerRadius = 12,
	}, startTime, { scripted = false, eventId = "awareness" })
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Sensing
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function sense(mission, now)
	local r = root()
	local selfPos = r and EntityService.model.Parent == Workspace and r.Position or nil
	local list = {}
	if not selfPos then
		-- Not in the world: distances are irrelevant but the brain may need
		-- positions for an ambush that is about to appear.
		local anchorPos = EntityService.brain.anchorPos
		if anchorPos then
			selfPos = toV3(anchorPos) + Vector3.new(0, Figures.rootHeight(EntityService.model), 0)
		end
	end
	refreshRayFilter()
	local eye = selfPos and (selfPos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
	local lookVector = r and r.CFrame.LookVector or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local p = mission:participant(player.UserId)
		local character = player.Character
		local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if p and p.status == "Alive" and hrp and eye then
			local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local target = (head and head.Position) or (hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
			local flat = Vector3.new(target.X - eye.X, 0, target.Z - eye.Z)
			local dist = flat.Magnitude
			local angle = 0
			if dist > 0.01 then
				local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
				if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.01 then
					local dot = math.clamp(flatLook.Unit:Dot(flat.Unit), -1, 1)
					angle = math.deg(math.acos(dot))
				end
			end
			local roomId = MapLayout.roomAt(hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Z)
			local lit = roomId ~= nil and (mission.lights[roomId] or "off") ~= "off"
			local room = roomId and MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
			local hidden = room ~= nil and room.refuge == true and deps.WorldService.refugeSealed(roomId)
			table.insert(list, {
				id = player.UserId,
				pos = toVec(hrp.Position),
				dist = dist,
				los = dist < 120 and clearLine(eye, target),
				angle = angle,
				lit = lit,
				flashlight = player:GetAttribute("Flashlight") == true,
				hidden = hidden,
				invulnerable = mission:isInvulnerable(player.UserId, now),
			})
		end
	end
	return { pos = selfPos and toVec(selfPos) or nil, players = list }
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Movement
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function computePath(from, to)
	if pathBusy then
		return
	end
	pathBusy = true
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok = pcall(function()
			path:ComputeAsync(from, to)
		end)
		if ok and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
			pathWaypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
			pathIndex = math.min(2, #pathWaypoints)
		else
			pathWaypoints = nil
			pathFailedAt = os.clock()
		end
		pathTarget = to
		pathBusy = false
	end)
end

local function followPath(goal, speed)
	local h = humanoid()
	local r = root()
	if not h or not r then
		return
	end
	h.WalkSpeed = speed
	if not path then
		path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
			AgentRadius = 1.6,
			AgentHeight = 9,
			AgentCanJump = false,
			WaypointSpacing = 4,
			Costs = { Door = 2, LockedDoor = math.huge },
		})
	end
	local moved = not pathTarget or (pathTarget - goal).Magnitude > 5
	-- After a failed search, wait a second before trying the same goal again.
	if moved or (not pathWaypoints and os.clock() - pathFailedAt > 1) then
		computePath(r.Position, goal)
	end
	if pathWaypoints and pathWaypoints[pathIndex] then
		local wp = pathWaypoints[pathIndex].Position
		local flat = Vector3.new(wp.X - r.Position.X, 0, wp.Z - r.Position.Z)
		if flat.Magnitude < 2.5 and pathIndex < #pathWaypoints then
			pathIndex += 1
			wp = pathWaypoints[pathIndex].Position
		end
		h:MoveTo(wp)
	else
		-- No path (yet): head straight for it.
		h:MoveTo(goal)
	end
end

local function openDoorsAhead()
	local r = root()
	if not r then
		return
	end
	for _, doorId in deps.WorldService.closedDoorsNear(r.Position, DOOR_OPEN_RADIUS) do
		local entry = deps.WorldService.door(doorId)
		-- It never opens a refuge door: the soundproof edit bay is a safe room.
		if entry and not entry.refuge then
			deps.WorldService.openDoor(doorId)
			deps.MissionService.cue({ cue = "DoorForced", door = doorId })
		end
	end
end

local function checkStuck(now, moving)
	local r = root()
	if not r or not moving then
		lastProgress = { pos = r and r.Position, at = now }
		stuckCount = 0
		return false
	end
	if not lastProgress.pos or (r.Position - lastProgress.pos).Magnitude > 1 then
		lastProgress = { pos = r.Position, at = now }
		return false
	end
	if now - lastProgress.at > STUCK_TIME then
		lastProgress = { pos = r.Position, at = now }
		stuckCount += 1
		pathTarget = nil
		return stuckCount >= 3
	end
	return false
end

local function step(now)
	local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
	local brain = EntityService.brain
	if not mission or not brain then
		return
	end
	local out = brain:update(now, sense(mission, now))
	local model = EntityService.model

	if out.events then
		for _, e in out.events do
			deps.MissionService.presenterEvent(e.name, e.data or {})
		end
	end

	if out.catchId then
		deps.MissionService.caught(out.catchId, EntityService.activeEventId)
	end

	if not out.visible then
		park()
	else
		if out.teleportTo then
			place(out.teleportTo, out.face)
		end
		local r = root()
		local h = humanoid()
		if r and h and model.Parent == Workspace then
			if out.moveTo then
				local goal = toV3(out.moveTo) + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				if out.state == "Hunting" and out.chasing ~= nil then
					followPath(goal, out.speed or 14)
					openDoorsAhead()
				elseif (out.speed or 0) > 0 then
					local flat = Vector3.new(goal.X - r.Position.X, 0, goal.Z - r.Position.Z)
					if flat.Magnitude > TELEPORT_DRIFT then
						place(out.moveTo, out.face)
					else
						h.WalkSpeed = math.max(out.speed, 2)
						h:MoveTo(goal)
					end
					openDoorsAhead()
				else
					h:MoveTo(r.Position)
					face(out.face)
				end
			elseif out.face then
				face(out.face)
			end
			local moving = out.state == "Hunting" and out.huntPhase == "chase"
			if checkStuck(now, moving) then
				-- Fairness: a stuck pursuer gives up rather than camping a doorway.
				brain:vanish("Stuck")
				deps.MissionService.presenterEvent("HuntEnd", { reason = "Stuck" })
				park()
			end
		end
	end

	if out.state ~= EntityService.state then
		EntityService.state = out.state
	end
	if model then
		model:SetAttribute("State", out.state)
		model:SetAttribute("HuntPhase", out.huntPhase or "")
		model:SetAttribute("TargetId", out.targetId or 0)
	end
end

function EntityService.init(dependencies)
	deps = dependencies
	parked = Instance.new("Folder")
	parked.Name = "PresenterParking"
	parked.Parent = ServerStorage
	EntityService.reset(nil)

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(TICK)
			local ok, err = pcall(step, Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
			if not ok then
				warn("[EntityService] " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end)
end

return EntityService
```

## src/server/Services/EvidenceService.luau

```lua
--[[
	EvidenceService — permanent unlocks: evidence tapes, case tapes (replayable in
	the field office), hidden frames and master recordings.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local Evidence = require(Shared.Config.Evidence)

local Router = require(script.Parent.Router)

local EvidenceService = {}

local FRAME_RESEARCH = 15

local deps

-- Applies a mission "Unlock" effect to everyone it names.
function EvidenceService.applyUnlock(effect)
	for _, userId in effect.userIds or {} do
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
		local profile = player and deps.DataService.get(player)
		if profile then
			if effect.evidence and Evidence.Tapes[effect.evidence] then
				profile.evidence[effect.evidence] = true
			end
			if effect.tape then
				profile.tapes[effect.tape] = true
			end
			if effect.frame then
				profile.frames[effect.frame] = true
			end
			deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
			deps.LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		end
	end
end

-- Hidden frames found while replaying tapes in the field office archive.
function EvidenceService.markArchiveFrame(player, tapeId, cameraId, t)
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	if not profile then
		return { ok = false, message = "Profile not loaded." }
	end
	if not profile.tapes[tapeId] then
		return { ok = false, message = "You have not recovered that tape." }
	end
	for _, case in CaseRegistry.Cases do
		for _, hf in case.hiddenFrames or {} do
			if hf.tape == tapeId and hf.camera == cameraId and math.abs(t - hf.t) <= (hf.window or 0.5) then
				if profile.frames[hf.id] then
					return { ok = false, message = "Already marked." }
				end
				profile.frames[hf.id] = true
				profile.research += FRAME_RESEARCH
				deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
				deps.LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
				return { ok = true, frame = hf.id, message = hf.text }
			end
		end
	end
	return { ok = false, message = "Nothing unusual on this frame." }
end

function EvidenceService.init(dependencies)
	deps = dependencies
	Router.onRequest("GetArchive", function(player)
		local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
		if not profile then
			return { ok = false }
		end
		return {
			ok = true,
			evidence = profile.evidence,
			tapes = profile.tapes,
			frames = profile.frames,
			masters = profile.masters,
		}
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 3 })
end

return EvidenceService
```

## src/server/Services/InteractionService.luau

```lua
--[[
	InteractionService — ProximityPrompts for everything interactive.

	ProximityPrompts give PC (E), gamepad (X) and touch (tap) support for free.
	Prompt text/visibility mirrors MissionCore:promptState so players always see
	what an object does (or why it won't work). Every trigger is range-checked and
	rate-limited on the server before it reaches the mission.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local InteractionService = {}

local MAX_DISTANCE = 9
local DISTANCE_SLACK = 4

InteractionService.missionPrompts = {} -- [interactId] = prompt
InteractionService.doorPrompts = {} -- [doorId] = prompt
InteractionService.hqPrompts = {}
InteractionService.lastTrigger = {}

local deps

local function makePrompt(parent, action, object, hold)
	local prompt = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
	prompt.ActionText = action or "Interact"
	prompt.ObjectText = object or ""
	prompt.HoldDuration = hold or 0
	prompt.MaxActivationDistance = MAX_DISTANCE
	prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
	prompt.KeyboardKeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.E
	prompt.GamepadKeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX
	-- Drawn by the client's PromptController in the game's own typeface.
	prompt.Style = Enum.ProximityPromptStyle.Custom
	prompt.ClickablePrompt = true
	prompt.Parent = parent
	return prompt
end

local function inRange(player, prompt)
	local character = player.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local holder = prompt.Parent
	if not root or not holder or not holder:IsA("BasePart") then
		return false
	end
	return (root.Position - holder.Position).Magnitude <= prompt.MaxActivationDistance + DISTANCE_SLACK
end

local function throttled(player)
	local now = os.clock()
	local last = InteractionService.lastTrigger[player]
	if last and now - last < 0.15 then
		return true
	end
	InteractionService.lastTrigger[player] = now
	return false
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Mission prompts
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function InteractionService.bindMissionAnchor(anchorPart, interactId)
	local existing = InteractionService.missionPrompts[interactId]
	if existing then
		existing:Destroy()
	end
	local prompt = makePrompt(anchorPart, "Interact", "", 0)
	prompt.Enabled = false
	prompt.Triggered:Connect(function(player)
		if throttled(player) or not inRange(player, prompt) then
			return
		end
		deps.MissionService.interact(player, interactId)
	end)
	InteractionService.missionPrompts[interactId] = prompt
	return prompt
end

function InteractionService.unbindMissionAnchor(interactId)
	local prompt = InteractionService.missionPrompts[interactId]
	if prompt then
		prompt:Destroy()
		InteractionService.missionPrompts[interactId] = nil
	end
end

function InteractionService.bindStation(station)
	for id, prompt in InteractionService.missionPrompts do
		prompt:Destroy()
		InteractionService.missionPrompts[id] = nil
	end
	for id, prompt in InteractionService.doorPrompts do
		prompt:Destroy()
		InteractionService.doorPrompts[id] = nil
	end
	for _, anchorPart in station.Anchors:GetChildren() do
		local id = anchorPart:GetAttribute("InteractId")
		if id then
			InteractionService.bindMissionAnchor(anchorPart, id)
		end
	end
	for doorId, entry in deps.WorldService.doors do
		if #entry.panels > 0 then
			local prompt = makePrompt(entry.panels[1].part, "Open", entry.model:GetAttribute("Label") or "", 0)
			prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 7
			prompt.Exclusivity = Enum.ProximityPromptExclusivity.OnePerButton
			prompt.Triggered:Connect(function(player)
				if throttled(player) or not inRange(player, prompt) then
					return
				end
				deps.MissionService.useDoor(player, doorId)
			end)
			InteractionService.doorPrompts[doorId] = prompt
		end
	end
	InteractionService.refreshDoors()
end

function InteractionService.refresh(mission)
	if not mission then
		for _, prompt in InteractionService.missionPrompts do
			prompt.Enabled = false
		end
		return
	end
	for id, prompt in InteractionService.missionPrompts do
		local state = mission:promptState(id)
		prompt.Enabled = state.visible == true
		if state.visible then
			prompt.ActionText = state.action or "Interact"
			prompt.ObjectText = state.object or ""
			prompt.HoldDuration = state.hold or 0
		end
	end
end

function InteractionService.refreshDoors()
	for doorId, prompt in InteractionService.doorPrompts do
		local entry = deps.WorldService.door(doorId)
		if entry then
			if entry.locked then
				prompt.ActionText = "Locked"
			elseif entry.kind == "freight" then
				prompt.ActionText = entry.open and "Open" or "Closed"
				prompt.Enabled = not entry.open
			else
				prompt.ActionText = entry.open and "Close" or "Open"
			end
			if entry.kind ~= "freight" then
				prompt.Enabled = true
			end
		end
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Field office prompts
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function InteractionService.bindHQ(hq, terminals)
	for _, anchorPart in hq.Anchors:GetChildren() do
		local id = anchorPart:GetAttribute("InteractId")
		local def = terminals[id]
		if def then
			local prompt = makePrompt(anchorPart, def.action, def.label, 0)
			prompt.Triggered:Connect(function(player)
				if throttled(player) or not inRange(player, prompt) then
					return
				end
				deps.LobbyService.openTerminal(player, id)
			end)
			InteractionService.hqPrompts[id] = prompt
		end
	end
end

function InteractionService.init(dependencies)
	deps = dependencies
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		InteractionService.lastTrigger[player] = nil
	end)
	table.insert(deps.WorldService.onDoorChanged, function()
		InteractionService.refreshDoors()
	end)
end

return InteractionService
```

## src/server/Services/LobbyService.luau

```lua
--[[
	LobbyService — the Division field office (HQ) between cases.

	Party and ready-up, case/mode selection, deployment, profile sync, equipment
	and cosmetics, the credits shop, settings, desk decorations and the Archive
	Challenge leaderboard.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local Cosmetics = require(Shared.Config.Cosmetics)
local Typeface = require(Shared.Config.Typeface)
local ProfileSchema = require(Shared.Core.ProfileSchema)
local Variants = require(Shared.Core.Variants)
local MapBuilder = require(Shared.World.MapBuilder)
local Outfits = require(Shared.World.Outfits)

local Router = require(script.Parent.Router)

local LobbyService = {}

local DEPLOY_COUNTDOWN = 6

local deps, remotes
local ready = {} -- [player] = true
local selection = { caseId = "case01", mode = "Standard" }
local countdownEnd = nil
local desks = {} -- [index] = player

local TERMINAL_PAGES = {
	HQ_CASES = "Cases",
	HQ_ARCHIVE = "Archive",
	HQ_LOCKER = "Equipment",
	HQ_STORE = "Store",
	HQ_SETTINGS = "Settings",
}

local function inMission(player)
	local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return false
	end
	local p = mission:participant(player.UserId)
	return p ~= nil and p.status ~= "Disconnected"
end

local function hqPlayers()
	local list = {}
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if not inMission(player) and deps.DataService.get(player) then
			table.insert(list, player)
		end
	end
	return list
end

local function clearanceOf(player)
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	return profile and profile.clearance or 1
end

function LobbyService.broadcast()
	local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
	local members = {}
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		table.insert(members, {
			userId = player.UserId,
			name = player.DisplayName,
			ready = ready[player] == true,
			clearance = clearanceOf(player),
			inMission = inMission(player),
		})
	end
	local _, week = Variants.weeklySeed(os.time())
	local payload = {
		members = members,
		selection = selection,
		countdownEnd = countdownEnd,
		missionActive = mission ~= nil,
		missionPhase = mission and mission.phase or nil,
		missionCase = mission and mission.case.id or nil,
		joinable = mission ~= nil
			and (mission.phase == "Briefing" or mission.phase == "Active")
			and not mission.flags.transmitter_armed
			and mission:activeParticipantCount() < (mission.case.maxParticipants or 4),
		week = week,
		serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
	}
	remotes.Party:FireAllClients(payload)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Profile sync
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	if not profile then
		return
	end
	local passes = deps.MarketService.passes(player)
	local owned = {}
	for id in Cosmetics.Items do
		if Cosmetics.owns(id, profile, passes) then
			owned[id] = true
		end
	end
	for id in Cosmetics.Decor do
		if Cosmetics.owns(id, profile, passes) then
			owned[id] = true
		end
	end
	remotes.Profile:FireClient(player, {
		credits = profile.credits,
		research = profile.research,
		clearance = profile.clearance,
		evidence = profile.evidence,
		tapes = profile.tapes,
		frames = profile.frames,
		masters = profile.masters,
		cases = profile.cases,
		challenges = profile.challenges,
		owned = owned,
		equipped = profile.cosmetics.equipped,
		equipment = profile.equipment,
		settings = profile.settings,
		presets = profile.presets,
		presetSlots = Cosmetics.presetSlots(passes),
		decor = profile.decor,
		storedRevives = profile.storedRevives,
		storedTeamTokens = profile.storedTeamTokens,
		passes = passes,
		offline = deps.DataService.isOffline(player),
		archiveBest = profile.archiveChallengeBest,
		stats = profile.stats,
		tutorialSeen = profile.tutorialSeen,
	})
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Desks, nameplates and decor
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function deskModel(index)
	local hq = deps.WorldService.hq
	return hq and hq:FindFirstChild("Desks") and hq.Desks:FindFirstChild("Desk" .. index)
end

local function refreshDesk(index)
	local model = deskModel(index)
	if not model then
		return
	end
	local player = desks[index]
	local plate = model:FindFirstChild("Nameplate")
	local label = plate and plate:FindFirstChild("Label") and plate.Label:FindFirstChild("Name")
	if label then
		label.Text = player and string.upper(player.DisplayName) or "VACANT"
	end
	local profile = player and deps.DataService.get(player)
	local passes = player and deps.MarketService.passes(player) or {}
	local decorRoot = model:FindFirstChild("Decor")
	if decorRoot then
		for _, holder in decorRoot:GetChildren() do
			local show = profile ~= nil and profile.decor[holder.Name] == true and Cosmetics.owns(holder.Name, profile, passes)
			for _, d in holder:GetDescendants() do
				if d:IsA("BasePart") then
					d.Transparency = show and (d:GetAttribute("BaseTransparency") or 0) or 1
				elseif d:IsA("SurfaceGui") or d:IsA("Light") then
					d.Enabled = show
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function assignDesk(player)
	for i = 1, #MapBuilder.HQDesks do
		if desks[i] == nil then
			desks[i] = player
			refreshDesk(i)
			return
		end
	end
end

local function releaseDesk(player)
	for i, owner in desks do
		if owner == player then
			desks[i] = nil
			refreshDesk(i)
		end
	end
end

local function deskOf(player)
	for i, owner in desks do
		if owner == player then
			return i
		end
	end
	return nil
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Character dressing (outfit, nameplate, flashlight)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Office clothes in the field office, field kit in the station.
function LobbyService.outfit(player, character)
	character = character or player.Character
	if not character or character.Parent == nil then
		return
	end
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	local equipped = profile and profile.cosmetics.equipped or Cosmetics.Defaults
	local uniform = Cosmetics.Items[equipped.uniform] or Cosmetics.Items.uniform_standard
	local colour = Color3.fromRGB(uniform.colour[1], uniform.colour[2], uniform.colour[3])
	local mode = player:GetAttribute("InMission") == true and "Field" or "Lobby"
	Outfits.apply(character, mode, colour, player.DisplayName)
end

function LobbyService.dress(player, character)
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	task.spawn(function()
		local torso = character:WaitForChild("UpperTorso", 5) or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
		if not torso or not head or character.Parent == nil then
			return
		end
		local equipped = profile and profile.cosmetics.equipped or Cosmetics.Defaults
		LobbyService.outfit(player, character)

		local plateItem = Cosmetics.Items[equipped.nameplate]
		if plateItem and plateItem.text then
			local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
			billboard.Name = "Nameplate"
			billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 24)
			billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0)
			billboard.AlwaysOnTop = false
			billboard.MaxDistance = 40
			billboard.Adornee = head
			local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			text.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			text.FontFace = Typeface.bold()
			text.TextScaled = true
			text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(196, 186, 158)
			text.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
			text.Text = plateItem.text
			text.Parent = billboard
			billboard.Parent = head
		end

		local light = Instance.new("SpotLight")
		light.Name = "Flashlight"
		light.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
		light.Angle = 55
		light.Range = 38
		light.Brightness = 2.2
		light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 230, 210)
		light.Shadows = true
		light.Enabled = player:GetAttribute("Flashlight") == true
		light.Parent = head
	end)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Deployment
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function validateSelection(caseId, mode, players)
	local case = CaseRegistry.get(caseId)
	if not case then
		return false, "That case is classified."
	end
	local modeDef = case.modes[mode]
	if not modeDef then
		return false, "Unknown mode."
	end
	if modeDef.requiresClearance then
		for _, player in players do
			if clearanceOf(player) < modeDef.requiresClearance then
				return false, string.format("%s needs clearance %d for %s (complete Case 01 first).", player.DisplayName, modeDef.requiresClearance, modeDef.label)
			end
		end
	end
	return true
end

local function tryCountdown()
	local list = hqPlayers()
	if deps.MissionService.mission or #list == 0 then
		countdownEnd = nil
		return
	end
	for _, player in list do
		if not ready[player] then
			countdownEnd = nil
			return
		end
	end
	local ok = validateSelection(selection.caseId, selection.mode, list)
	if not ok then
		countdownEnd = nil
		return
	end
	if not countdownEnd then
		countdownEnd = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + DEPLOY_COUNTDOWN
	end
end

local function deploy()
	local list = hqPlayers()
	table.sort(list, function(a, b)
		return (ready[a] and 1 or 0) > (ready[b] and 1 or 0)
	end)
	local team = {}
	for _, player in list do
		if ready[player] then
			table.insert(team, player)
		end
	end
	countdownEnd = nil
	for player in ready do
		ready[player] = nil
	end
	if #team == 0 then
		return
	end
	local ok, reason = deps.MissionService.start(team, selection.caseId, selection.mode)
	if not ok then
		for _, player in team do
			remotes.Notify:FireClient(player, { text = reason })
		end
	end
	LobbyService.broadcast()
end

function LobbyService.openTerminal(player, terminalId)
	local page = TERMINAL_PAGES[terminalId]
	if page then
		remotes.Cue:FireClient(player, { cue = "OpenMenu", page = page })
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Init
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function profileOf(player)
	return deps.DataService.get(player)
end

function LobbyService.init(dependencies, remoteTable)
	deps = dependencies
	remotes = remoteTable

	deps.DataService.onLoaded(function(player, _, offline)
		assignDesk(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		LobbyService.broadcast()
		if offline then
			remotes.Notify:FireClient(player, { text = "Progress will not be saved this session (data service unavailable)." })
		end
		deps.MissionService.spawnAt(player, deps.WorldService.hqSpawn())
	end)

	table.insert(deps.MarketService.onPassChanged, function(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		local index = deskOf(player)
		if index then
			refreshDesk(index)
		end
	end)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		task.spawn(deps.DataService.load, player)
	end)
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		task.spawn(deps.DataService.load, player)
	end
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		ready[player] = nil
		releaseDesk(player)
		task.defer(function()
			tryCountdown()
			LobbyService.broadcast()
		end)
	end)

	Router.onRequest("GetProfile", function(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		LobbyService.broadcast()
		return { ok = profileOf(player) ~= nil }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 3 })

	Router.onAction("SetReady", function(player, payload)
		if inMission(player) or not profileOf(player) then
			return
		end
		ready[player] = payload.ready == true or nil
		tryCountdown()
		LobbyService.broadcast()
	end, { rate = 3, burst = 5 })

	Router.onRequest("SelectCase", function(player, payload)
		if not Router.string(payload.case, 16) or not Router.string(payload.mode, 24) then
			return { ok = false, message = "Malformed." }
		end
		local ok, reason = validateSelection(payload.case, payload.mode, { player })
		if not ok then
			return { ok = false, message = reason }
		end
		selection = { caseId = payload.case, mode = payload.mode }
		countdownEnd = nil
		tryCountdown()
		LobbyService.broadcast()
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	Router.onRequest("SetEquipment", function(player, payload)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		if not profile or not Router.string(payload.equipment, 24) or not ProfileSchema.Equipment[payload.equipment] then
			return { ok = false, message = "Unknown equipment." }
		end
		if inMission(player) then
			return { ok = false, message = "Change equipment in the field office." }
		end
		profile.equipment = payload.equipment
		deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	Router.onRequest("SetCosmetic", function(player, payload)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		local item = Router.string(payload.item, 40) and Cosmetics.Items[payload.item]
		if not profile or not item then
			return { ok = false, message = "Unknown item." }
		end
		if not Cosmetics.owns(payload.item, profile, deps.MarketService.passes(player)) then
			return { ok = false, message = "You don't own that yet." }
		end
		profile.cosmetics.equipped[item.slot] = payload.item
		deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		if item.slot == "uniform" and player.Character then
			LobbyService.outfit(player)
		end
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 3, burst = 6 })

	Router.onRequest("BuyCosmetic", function(player, payload)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		local item = Router.string(payload.item, 40) and Cosmetics.Items[payload.item]
		if not profile or not item or item.source ~= "credits" then
			return { ok = false, message = "That can't be bought with credits." }
		end
		if profile.cosmetics.owned[payload.item] then
			return { ok = false, message = "Already owned." }
		end
		if profile.credits < item.price then
			return { ok = false, message = "Not enough Recovery Credits." }
		end
		profile.credits -= item.price
		profile.cosmetics.owned[payload.item] = true
		deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	Router.onRequest("SetDecor", function(player, payload)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		if not profile or not Router.string(payload.decor, 32) or not Cosmetics.Decor[payload.decor] then
			return { ok = false, message = "Unknown decoration." }
		end
		if not Cosmetics.owns(payload.decor, profile, deps.MarketService.passes(player)) then
			return { ok = false, message = "You don't own that decoration." }
		end
		profile.decor[payload.decor] = payload.on == true or nil
		deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		local index = deskOf(player)
		if index then
			refreshDesk(index)
		end
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 3, burst = 6 })

	Router.onRequest("SavePreset", function(player, payload)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		local slots = Cosmetics.presetSlots(deps.MarketService.passes(player))
		if not profile or not Router.number(payload.slot, 1, slots) then
			return { ok = false, message = "No such preset slot." }
		end
		profile.presets[tostring(math.floor(payload.slot))] = {
			equipment = profile.equipment,
			equipped = table.clone(profile.cosmetics.equipped),
		}
		deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	Router.onRequest("LoadPreset", function(player, payload)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		if not profile or not Router.number(payload.slot, 1, 16) then
			return { ok = false }
		end
		local preset = profile.presets[tostring(math.floor(payload.slot))]
		if not preset then
			return { ok = false, message = "That preset is empty." }
		end
		local passes = deps.MarketService.passes(player)
		if ProfileSchema.Equipment[preset.equipment] then
			profile.equipment = preset.equipment
		end
		for slot, itemId in preset.equipped do
			if Cosmetics.owns(itemId, profile, passes) then
				profile.cosmetics.equipped[slot] = itemId
			end
		end
		deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	Router.onRequest("SaveSettings", function(player, payload)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		if not profile then
			return { ok = false }
		end
		profile.settings = ProfileSchema.sanitizeSettings(profile.settings, payload.settings)
		deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		return { ok = true, settings = profile.settings }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 3 })

	Router.onRequest("SetTutorialSeen", function(player)
		local profile = profileOf(player)
		if not profile then
			return { ok = false }
		end
		if not profile.tutorialSeen then
			profile.tutorialSeen = true
			deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
		end
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 3 })

	Router.onRequest("GetStore", function(player)
		return deps.MarketService.catalogue(player)
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 3 })

	Router.onRequest("PromptPass", function(player, payload)
		if not Router.string(payload.pass, 24) then
			return { ok = false }
		end
		local ok, reason = deps.MarketService.promptPass(player, payload.pass)
		return { ok = ok, message = reason }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 2 })

	Router.onRequest("PromptBundle", function(player, payload)
		-- Only cosmetic bundles are bought from the store; re-entries are offered
		-- in context when eligible, never pushed.
		if payload.product ~= "EvidenceBundle" and payload.product ~= "LobbyBundle" then
			return { ok = false }
		end
		local ok, reason = deps.MarketService.promptProduct(player, payload.product, nil)
		return { ok = ok, message = reason }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 2 })

	Router.onRequest("GetLeaderboard", function()
		local _, week = Variants.weeklySeed(os.time())
		return { ok = true, week = week, rows = deps.DataService.topScores(week) }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 2 })

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(0.25)
			if countdownEnd then
				if deps.MissionService.mission then
					countdownEnd = nil
					LobbyService.broadcast()
				elseif workspace:GetServerTimeNow() >= countdownEnd then
					local ok, err = pcall(deploy)
					if not ok then
						warn("[LobbyService] deploy failed: " .. tostring(err))
						countdownEnd = nil
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

return LobbyService
```

## src/server/Services/MarketService.luau

```lua
--[[
	MarketService — gamepasses and developer products.

	* Pass ownership is checked on the server (UserOwnsGamePassAsync) and cached.
	* Developer products are granted only in ProcessReceipt, exactly once per
	  receipt: the PurchaseId is recorded in the player's profile and saved before
	  PurchaseGranted is returned.
	* Re-entry and Team Recovery purchases are stored on the profile, then spent by
	  MissionService when eligible - a purchase that arrives after the case ended is
	  simply kept for next time, never lost.
	* Nothing here is required to finish the game.
]]

local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local Monetisation = require(Shared.Config.Monetisation)
local Cosmetics = require(Shared.Config.Cosmetics)

local MarketService = {}

local MAX_RECEIPTS_KEPT = 60

MarketService.passCache = {} -- [player] = { [passKey] = true }
MarketService.onGranted = {} -- callbacks(player, productKey)
MarketService.onPassChanged = {} -- callbacks(player)
MarketService.pending = {} -- [player] = { productKey = intent }

local deps

function MarketService.ownsPass(player, passKey)
	local cache = MarketService.passCache[player]
	return cache ~= nil and cache[passKey] == true
end

function MarketService.passes(player)
	return MarketService.passCache[player] or {}
end

function MarketService.refreshPasses(player)
	local cache = {}
	for key, pass in Monetisation.Passes do
		if pass.id ~= 0 then
			local ok, owns = pcall(function()
				return MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(player.UserId, pass.id)
			end)
			cache[key] = ok and owns or false
		end
	end
	MarketService.passCache[player] = cache
	for _, callback in MarketService.onPassChanged do
		task.spawn(callback, player)
	end
end

function MarketService.promptPass(player, passKey)
	local pass = Monetisation.Passes[passKey]
	if not pass or pass.id == 0 then
		return false, "This pass is not available yet."
	end
	if MarketService.ownsPass(player, passKey) then
		return false, "You already own this."
	end
	MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase(player, pass.id)
	return true
end

-- intent is remembered so the purchase can be applied right away when granted.
function MarketService.promptProduct(player, productKey, intent)
	local product = Monetisation.Products[productKey]
	if not product or product.id == 0 then
		return false, "This item is not available yet."
	end
	MarketService.pending[player] = MarketService.pending[player] or {}
	MarketService.pending[player][productKey] = intent or true
	MarketplaceService:PromptProductPurchase(player, product.id)
	return true
end

function MarketService.takeIntent(player, productKey)
	local pending = MarketService.pending[player]
	if not pending then
		return nil
	end
	local intent = pending[productKey]
	pending[productKey] = nil
	return intent
end

local function grant(profile, productKey)
	if productKey == "Revive" then
		profile.storedRevives += 1
	elseif productKey == "TeamToken" then
		profile.storedTeamTokens += 1
	elseif productKey == "EvidenceBundle" or productKey == "LobbyBundle" then
		for id, item in Cosmetics.Items do
			if item.source == "product" and item.product == productKey then
				profile.cosmetics.owned[id] = true
			end
		end
		for id, item in Cosmetics.Decor do
			if item.source == "product" and item.product == productKey then
				profile.cosmetics.owned[id] = true
			end
		end
	end
end

local function processReceipt(receipt)
	local productKey = Monetisation.productByAssetId(receipt.ProductId)
	if not productKey then
		warn("[MarketService] Receipt for unknown product " .. tostring(receipt.ProductId))
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end
	local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(receipt.PlayerId)
	if not player then
		-- Roblox retries when they next join.
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	if not profile then
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end
	profile.receipts = profile.receipts or {}
	local purchaseId = tostring(receipt.PurchaseId)
	for _, id in profile.receipts do
		if id == purchaseId then
			return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
		end
	end
	grant(profile, productKey)
	table.insert(profile.receipts, purchaseId)
	while #profile.receipts > MAX_RECEIPTS_KEPT do
		table.remove(profile.receipts, 1)
	end
	deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
	if not deps.DataService.saveNow(player) then
		-- The in-memory record above stops a double grant on retry this session;
		-- if the server dies before saving, neither the grant nor the record persist.
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end
	for _, callback in MarketService.onGranted do
		task.spawn(callback, player, productKey)
	end
	return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
end

function MarketService.init(dependencies)
	deps = dependencies
	MarketplaceService.ProcessReceipt = processReceipt

	MarketplaceService.PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished:Connect(function(player, passId, purchased)
		if purchased then
			local key = Monetisation.passByAssetId(passId)
			if key then
				MarketService.passCache[player] = MarketService.passCache[player] or {}
				MarketService.passCache[player][key] = true
				for _, callback in MarketService.onPassChanged do
					task.spawn(callback, player)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	MarketplaceService.PromptProductPurchaseFinished:Connect(function(userId, productId, purchased)
		if not purchased then
			-- Cancelled or failed: forget the intent. Nothing was charged.
			local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
			local key = Monetisation.productByAssetId(productId)
			if player and key then
				MarketService.takeIntent(player, key)
			end
		end
	end)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		MarketService.refreshPasses(player)
	end)
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		task.spawn(MarketService.refreshPasses, player)
	end
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		MarketService.passCache[player] = nil
		MarketService.pending[player] = nil
	end)
end

-- Store listing for the client (only configured items).
function MarketService.catalogue(player)
	local passes, products = {}, {}
	for _, key in Monetisation.PassOrder do
		local pass = Monetisation.Passes[key]
		if pass.id ~= 0 then
			table.insert(passes, {
				key = key,
				name = pass.name,
				description = pass.description,
				owned = MarketService.ownsPass(player, key),
			})
		end
	end
	for _, key in Monetisation.ProductOrder do
		local product = Monetisation.Products[key]
		if product.id ~= 0 then
			table.insert(products, { key = key, name = product.name, description = product.description })
		end
	end
	return { passes = passes, products = products }
end

return MarketService
```

## src/server/Services/MissionService.luau

```lua
--[[
	MissionService — runs one case at a time on this server.

	Lifecycle: Lobby -> start() -> Briefing -> Active -> ExtractionAvailable
	           -> Completed | Failed -> report -> endMission() -> Lobby
	(an aborted case - everyone left - skips straight to cleanup).

	It ticks MissionCore, applies its effects to the world through the other
	services, and replicates the mission snapshot to every participant.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local MissionCore = require(Shared.Core.MissionCore)
local ProfileSchema = require(Shared.Core.ProfileSchema)
local Variants = require(Shared.Core.Variants)

local Router = require(script.Parent.Router)

local MissionService = {}

local TICK = 0.1
local SNAPSHOT_MIN_INTERVAL = 0.1
local SNAPSHOT_HEARTBEAT = 1
local REVIVE_DELAY = 3
local REPORT_LINGER = 35
local GHOST_SAMPLE = 0.5
local GHOST_KEEP = 600
local VOTE_TIME = 20

MissionService.mission = nil
MissionService.caseDef = nil

local deps, remotes
local lastSent = { version = -1, at = 0 }
local promptVersion = -1
local dirty = false
local pendingSpawn = {} -- [player] = CFrame
local returnVotes = {}
local reportAt = nil
local history = {} -- [userId] = { samples }
local lastGhostSample = 0
local vote = nil
local runId = 0

local function now()
	return Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
end

local function toV3(p)
	return Vector3.new(p.x, p.y or 0, p.z)
end

function MissionService.markDirty()
	dirty = true
end

local function missionPlayers()
	local list = {}
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return list
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local p = mission:participant(player.UserId)
		if p and p.status ~= "Disconnected" then
			table.insert(list, player)
		end
	end
	return list
end
MissionService.players = missionPlayers

local function sendAll(remote, payload)
	for _, player in missionPlayers() do
		remote:FireClient(player, payload)
	end
end

local function notify(player, text)
	if player and text then
		remotes.Notify:FireClient(player, { text = text })
	end
end

function MissionService.cue(payload)
	sendAll(remotes.Cue, payload)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Characters
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function spawnAt(player, cf)
	pendingSpawn[player] = cf
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			player:LoadCharacter()
		end)
		if not ok then
			warn("[MissionService] LoadCharacter failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end)
end
MissionService.spawnAt = spawnAt

local function moveCharacter(player, cf)
	local character = player.Character
	local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if hrp then
		character:PivotTo(cf + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
		return true
	end
	return false
end

local function removeCharacter(player, delay)
	task.delay(delay or 0, function()
		local character = player.Character
		if character then
			character:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end

local function freeze(player)
	local character = player.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if humanoid then
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
		humanoid.JumpHeight = 0
	end
	if hrp then
		hrp.Anchored = true
	end
end

local function checkpointFor(checkpoints)
	local presenter = deps.EntityService.position()
	local fallback = nil
	for _, cp in checkpoints do
		local pos = toV3(cp.pos)
		fallback = fallback or pos
		if not presenter or (presenter - pos).Magnitude > 40 then
			return CFrame.new(pos), cp
		end
	end
	return CFrame.new(fallback or Vector3.new(0, 0, 58)), nil
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Snapshot replication
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function replicate(force)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return
	end
	local t = os.clock()
	if not force and mission.version == lastSent.version and t - lastSent.at < SNAPSHOT_HEARTBEAT then
		return
	end
	if not force and t - lastSent.at < SNAPSHOT_MIN_INTERVAL then
		return
	end
	local snapshot = mission:snapshot(now())
	snapshot.serverTime = now()
	snapshot.vote = vote and { proposer = vote.proposerName, expires = vote.expires, yes = vote.yesCount, needed = vote.needed } or nil
	sendAll(remotes.Mission, snapshot)
	lastSent = { version = mission.version, at = t }
end

local function refreshPrompts(force)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return
	end
	if force or mission.version ~= promptVersion then
		promptVersion = mission.version
		deps.InteractionService.refresh(mission)
		for id, def in mission:getInteractables() do
			if def.kind == "Pickup" then
				deps.WorldService.setItemVisible(def.item, mission:promptState(id).visible == true)
			end
		end
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Effects from MissionCore
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function playerById(userId)
	return userId and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId) or nil
end

local function onReport(report)
	reportAt = now()
	local week = select(2, Variants.weeklySeed(os.time()))
	for _, player in missionPlayers() do
		local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
		local summary = nil
		if profile then
			summary = ProfileSchema.applyReport(profile, report, player.UserId)
			if report.mode == "ArchiveChallenge" then
				local best = profile.archiveChallengeBest[tostring(week)] or 0
				if report.score > best then
					profile.archiveChallengeBest[tostring(week)] = report.score
				end
				task.spawn(deps.DataService.submitScore, week, player.UserId, report.score)
			end
			deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
			task.spawn(deps.DataService.save, player, false)
			deps.LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		end
		remotes.Report:FireClient(player, { report = report, personal = summary })
	end
end

local applyEffect

local handlers = {
	Notify = function(e)
		if e.userId then
			notify(playerById(e.userId), e.text)
		else
			sendAll(remotes.Notify, { text = e.text })
		end
	end,
	Subtitle = function(e)
		sendAll(remotes.Subtitle, e)
	end,
	Cue = function(e)
		if e.cue == "Rewind" then
			MissionService.sendGhosts(e.room, e.duration or 30)
		elseif e.cue == "TransmitterArmed" then
			deps.WorldService.setTransmitterArmed(true)
		elseif e.cue == "OnAir" then
			deps.WorldService.setOnAir(true)
		end
		sendAll(remotes.Cue, e)
	end,
	Lights = function(e)
		deps.WorldService.setRoomLights(e.room, e.mode)
	end,
	Door = function(e)
		deps.WorldService.setDoorLocked(e.door, e.locked)
		if e.open then
			deps.WorldService.openDoor(e.door)
		end
		deps.InteractionService.refreshDoors()
	end,
	Relay = function(e)
		deps.WorldService.setRelay(e.relay, e.on)
	end,
	Power = function()
		deps.WorldService.setGeneratorStage(2)
	end,
	Item = function(e)
		if not e.present then
			deps.WorldService.removeItem(e.item)
			deps.InteractionService.unbindMissionAnchor("ITEM_" .. e.item)
		end
	end,
	Presenter = function(e)
		deps.EntityService.command(e.command, e.startTime, { scripted = e.scripted, eventId = e.eventId })
	end,
	ObserveNear = function(e)
		deps.EntityService.observeNear(e.userId, e.startTime)
	end,
	OpenDeck = function(e)
		local player = playerById(e.userId)
		if player then
			remotes.Cue:FireClient(player, { cue = "OpenDeck", deck = e.deck })
		end
	end,
	OpenKeypad = function(e)
		local player = playerById(e.userId)
		if player then
			remotes.Cue:FireClient(player, { cue = "OpenKeypad" })
		end
	end,
	Briefing = function(e)
		local player = playerById(e.userId)
		if player then
			remotes.Cue:FireClient(player, { cue = "ReplayBriefing" })
		end
	end,
	Eliminated = function(e)
		local player = playerById(e.userId)
		sendAll(remotes.Cue, { cue = "Eliminated", userId = e.userId })
		if player then
			freeze(player)
			removeCharacter(player, 1.6)
		end
	end,
	Revive = function(e)
		local player = playerById(e.userId)
		if not player then
			return
		end
		local cf = checkpointFor(e.checkpoints or {})
		local token = runId
		task.delay(REVIVE_DELAY, function()
			if token == runId and MissionService.mission and MissionService.mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
				spawnAt(player, cf)
				remotes.Cue:FireClient(player, { cue = "Revived", invulnerableUntil = e.invulnerableUntil })
			end
		end)
	end,
	Extracted = function(e)
		local player = playerById(e.userId)
		if player then
			remotes.Cue:FireClient(player, { cue = "Extracted" })
			freeze(player)
			removeCharacter(player, 4.5)
		end
	end,
	Phase = function(e)
		if e.phase == "Aborted" then
			task.defer(MissionService.endMission)
		end
		deps.LobbyService.broadcast()
	end,
	Report = function(e)
		onReport(e.report)
	end,
	Unlock = function(e)
		deps.EvidenceService.applyUnlock(e)
	end,
}

applyEffect = function(e)
	local handler = handlers[e.type]
	if handler then
		local ok, err = pcall(handler, e)
		if not ok then
			warn(string.format("[MissionService] effect %s failed: %s", tostring(e.type), tostring(err)))
		end
	end
end

local function applyEffects()
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return
	end
	for _, e in mission:drainEffects() do
		applyEffect(e)
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Ghost replay (the Rewinding state: a room replays its earlier occupants)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function sampleGhosts(t)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	for _, player in missionPlayers() do
		local character = player.Character
		local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hrp and mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
			local list = history[player.UserId]
			if not list then
				list = {}
				history[player.UserId] = list
			end
			local p = hrp.Position
			local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
			table.insert(list, { t = t, x = p.X, z = p.Z, lx = look.X, lz = look.Z })
			while #list > 0 and t - list[1].t > GHOST_KEEP do
				table.remove(list, 1)
			end
		end
	end
end

function MissionService.sendGhosts(roomId, duration)
	local room = MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
	if not room then
		return
	end
	local t = now()
	local tracks = {}
	for userId, list in history do
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
		local samples = {}
		for _, s in list do
			if t - s.t > 20 and s.x >= room.x0 and s.x <= room.x1 and s.z >= room.z0 and s.z <= room.z1 then
				table.insert(samples, s)
			end
		end
		-- Keep the most recent visit, at most `duration` seconds of it.
		local trimmed = {}
		for i = #samples, 1, -1 do
			local s = samples[i]
			if #trimmed > 0 and trimmed[1].t - s.t > duration then
				break
			end
			table.insert(trimmed, 1, s)
		end
		if #trimmed > 1 then
			local base = trimmed[1].t
			local out = {}
			for _, s in trimmed do
				table.insert(out, { s.t - base, s.x, s.z, s.lx, s.lz })
			end
			table.insert(tracks, { name = player and player.DisplayName or "?", samples = out })
		end
	end
	sendAll(remotes.Ghosts, { room = roomId, duration = duration, tracks = tracks })
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Public API
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionService.start(players, caseId, mode)
	if MissionService.mission then
		return false, "A case is already running."
	end
	local caseDef = CaseRegistry.get(caseId)
	if not caseDef or not caseDef.modes[mode] then
		return false, "Unknown case."
	end
	runId += 1
	local seed
	if caseDef.modes[mode].fixedSeed then
		seed = Variants.weeklySeed(os.time())
	else
		seed = Random.new():NextInteger(1, 2147483646)
	end
	deps.WorldService.reset()
	deps.EntityService.reset(caseDef.modes[mode])
	deps.TimelineService.closeAll()

	local list = {}
	for i, player in players do
		if i > (caseDef.maxParticipants or 4) then
			break
		end
		table.insert(list, { userId = player.UserId, name = player.DisplayName })
	end
	local t = now()
	local mission = MissionCore.new(caseDef, { mode = mode, seed = seed, now = t, participants = list })
	MissionService.mission = mission
	MissionService.caseDef = caseDef
	history = {}
	returnVotes = {}
	reportAt = nil
	vote = nil
	lastSent = { version = -1, at = 0 }
	promptVersion = -1

	-- Physical items for this run's seeded spots.
	for id, def in mission:getInteractables() do
		if def.kind == "Pickup" then
			local _, anchorPart = deps.WorldService.spawnItem(caseDef, def.item, def.pos, true)
			if anchorPart then
				deps.InteractionService.bindMissionAnchor(anchorPart, id)
			end
		end
	end
	-- Non-interactive displays: the valve in its cage, the board in its rack.
	local cage = caseDef.interactables.I_CAGE
	if cage then
		deps.WorldService.spawnItem(caseDef, "VALVE", { x = cage.pos.x, y = 3.25, z = cage.pos.z }, false)
	end
	local rack = caseDef.interactables.I_RACK
	if rack then
		deps.WorldService.spawnItem(caseDef, "BOARD", { x = rack.pos.x - 1.3, y = 3, z = rack.pos.z }, false)
	end

	applyEffects()
	deps.WorldService.setGeneratorStage(0)
	deps.WorldService.setRelay("WEST", true)
	deps.WorldService.setRelay("EAST", true)
	refreshPrompts(true)

	local spawns = deps.WorldService.spawnPoints()
	for i, entry in list do
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(entry.userId)
		if player then
			player:SetAttribute("InMission", true)
			local cf = spawns[((i - 1) % math.max(1, #spawns)) + 1] or CFrame.new(0, 0, 58)
			if moveCharacter(player, cf) then
				deps.LobbyService.outfit(player)
			else
				spawnAt(player, cf)
			end
		end
	end
	replicate(true)
	deps.LobbyService.broadcast()
	print(string.format("[MissionService] %s started (%s, seed %d) with %d investigator(s)", caseId, mode, seed, #list))
	return true
end

function MissionService.endMission()
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return
	end
	runId += 1
	-- Only those still in the case: anyone who left is already back in the
	-- field office and must not be respawned a second time.
	local players = {}
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local p = mission:participant(player.UserId)
		if p and p.status ~= "Disconnected" then
			table.insert(players, player)
		end
	end
	MissionService.mission = nil
	MissionService.caseDef = nil
	vote = nil
	deps.EntityService.reset(nil)
	deps.TimelineService.closeAll()
	deps.InteractionService.refresh(nil)
	deps.WorldService.reset()
	deps.WorldService.setIdleLights()
	for _, player in players do
		player:SetAttribute("InMission", false)
		player:SetAttribute("Flashlight", false)
		remotes.Mission:FireClient(player, { phase = "None" })
		spawnAt(player, deps.WorldService.hqSpawn())
	end
	deps.LobbyService.broadcast()
end

function MissionService.join(player)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return false, "No case is running."
	end
	local ok, reason = mission:addParticipant(player.UserId, player.DisplayName, now())
	if not ok then
		return false, reason
	end
	player:SetAttribute("InMission", true)
	local spawns = deps.WorldService.spawnPoints()
	local cf = spawns[1] or CFrame.new(0, 0, 58)
	if moveCharacter(player, cf) then
		deps.LobbyService.outfit(player)
	else
		spawnAt(player, cf)
	end
	dirty = true
	replicate(true)
	deps.LobbyService.broadcast()
	return true
end

function MissionService.leave(player)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission or not mission:participant(player.UserId) then
		return
	end
	mission:removeParticipant(player.UserId, now())
	player:SetAttribute("InMission", false)
	remotes.Mission:FireClient(player, { phase = "None" })
	spawnAt(player, deps.WorldService.hqSpawn())
	applyEffects()
	deps.LobbyService.broadcast()
end

function MissionService.interact(player, interactId)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if not mission then
		return
	end
	local ok, message = mission:interact(player.UserId, interactId, now())
	if not ok and message then
		notify(player, message)
	end
	applyEffects()
	refreshPrompts(false)
	replicate(false)
end

function MissionService.useDoor(player, doorId)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	local entry = deps.WorldService.door(doorId)
	if not entry then
		return
	end
	if mission and mission:participant(player.UserId) and not mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
		return
	end
	if entry.locked then
		local reason = mission and mission:doorLockReason(doorId) or "Locked."
		notify(player, reason)
		remotes.Cue:FireClient(player, { cue = "DoorLocked", door = doorId })
		return
	end
	if deps.WorldService.toggleDoor(doorId) and mission then
		mission:addNoise(2, player.UserId)
	end
end

function MissionService.presenterEvent(name, data)
	if name == "HuntStart" then
		sendAll(remotes.Subtitle, { kind = "cue", text = "[TAPE FLUTTER — IT IS HUNTING]" })
		sendAll(remotes.Cue, { cue = "HuntStart", targetId = data.targetId })
	elseif name == "HuntEnd" then
		sendAll(remotes.Cue, { cue = "HuntEnd", reason = data.reason })
		if data.reason == "Lost" or data.reason == "TimeLimit" or data.reason == "Stuck" then
			sendAll(remotes.Subtitle, { kind = "cue", text = "[THE SIGNAL CLEARS]" })
		end
	elseif name == "Searching" then
		sendAll(remotes.Subtitle, { kind = "cue", text = "[IT HAS LOST SIGHT OF YOU]" })
	elseif name == "Appear" then
		sendAll(remotes.Cue, { cue = "PresenterAppear", kind = data.kind })
	elseif name == "Vanish" then
		sendAll(remotes.Cue, { cue = "PresenterVanish" })
	end
end

function MissionService.caught(userId, eventId)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	if mission then
		mission:eliminate(userId, now(), { eventId = eventId })
		applyEffects()
		replicate(true)
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Revives (free stored tokens first; purchases are optional)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function useStoredRevive(player)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	if not mission or not profile or profile.storedRevives <= 0 then
		return false
	end
	if not mission:canRevive(player.UserId, "Personal") then
		return false
	end
	profile.storedRevives -= 1
	deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
	mission:revive(player.UserId, now(), "Personal")
	applyEffects()
	replicate(true)
	deps.LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
	return true
end

local function useStoredTeamToken(player)
	local mission = MissionService.mission
	local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
	if not mission or not profile or profile.storedTeamTokens <= 0 then
		return false
	end
	if not mission:canTeamRecover() then
		return false
	end
	profile.storedTeamTokens -= 1
	deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
	mission:teamRecover(now())
	applyEffects()
	replicate(true)
	deps.LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
	return true
end

local function finishVote(passed)
	local v = vote
	vote = nil
	dirty = true
	if not v then
		return
	end
	local proposer = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(v.proposer)
	if not passed then
		sendAll(remotes.Notify, { text = "Team recovery cancelled." })
		return
	end
	if not proposer then
		return
	end
	if not useStoredTeamToken(proposer) then
		local ok, reason = deps.MarketService.promptProduct(proposer, "TeamToken", { runId = runId })
		if not ok then
			notify(proposer, reason)
		end
	end
end

function MissionService.init(dependencies, remoteTable)
	deps = dependencies
	remotes = remoteTable

	local function setupPlayer(player)
		player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			local cf = pendingSpawn[player]
			pendingSpawn[player] = nil
			local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
			if cf and hrp then
				task.wait()
				character:PivotTo(cf + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
			end
			deps.LobbyService.dress(player, character)
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid then
				humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
					-- Deaths that are not the Presenter (it never kills; it takes
					-- players off-air) respawn at a checkpoint without penalty.
					local mission = MissionService.mission
					if mission and mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
						local spot = checkpointFor(mission:checkpoints())
						task.delay(REVIVE_DELAY, function()
							if MissionService.mission == mission and mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
								spawnAt(player, spot)
							end
						end)
					elseif not mission or not mission:participant(player.UserId) then
						task.delay(REVIVE_DELAY, function()
							spawnAt(player, deps.WorldService.hqSpawn())
						end)
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(setupPlayer)
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		setupPlayer(player)
	end

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		pendingSpawn[player] = nil
		returnVotes[player.UserId] = nil
		local mission = MissionService.mission
		if mission and mission:participant(player.UserId) then
			mission:removeParticipant(player.UserId, now())
			applyEffects()
			replicate(true)
		end
		history[player.UserId] = nil
	end)

	Router.onAction("Flashlight", function(player, payload)
		local on = payload.on == true
		player:SetAttribute("Flashlight", on)
		local character = player.Character
		local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local light = head and head:FindFirstChild("Flashlight")
		if light then
			light.Enabled = on
		end
	end, { rate = 4, burst = 6 })

	Router.onAction("ReturnToHQ", function(player)
		local mission = MissionService.mission
		if not mission then
			return
		end
		if mission.phase == "Completed" or mission.phase == "Failed" then
			returnVotes[player.UserId] = true
			local all = true
			for _, p in missionPlayers() do
				if not returnVotes[p.UserId] then
					all = false
				end
			end
			if all then
				MissionService.endMission()
			end
		end
	end)

	Router.onRequest("SubmitCode", function(player, payload)
		local mission = MissionService.mission
		local cage = mission and mission.case.interactables.I_CAGE
		if not mission or not cage or not Router.string(payload.code, 8) then
			return { ok = false, message = "Nothing to unlock." }
		end
		local character = player.Character
		local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp or (hrp.Position - toV3(cage.pos)).Magnitude > 12 then
			return { ok = false, message = "Stand at the cage keypad." }
		end
		local ok, message = mission:submitCode(player.UserId, payload.code, now())
		applyEffects()
		replicate(true)
		return { ok = ok, message = message }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 3 })

	-- Camcorder evidence: filming the Presenter earns a little research.
	local recorded = {} -- [userId] = research granted this case
	Router.onAction("RecordAnomaly", function(player)
		local mission = MissionService.mission
		local presenter = deps.EntityService.position()
		if not mission or not presenter or not mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
			return
		end
		local character = player.Character
		local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if not head or (head.Position - presenter).Magnitude > 60 then
			return
		end
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		params.RespectCanCollide = true
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character, deps.EntityService.model }
		local target = presenter + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		if Workspace:Raycast(head.Position, target - head.Position, params) then
			return
		end
		local key = tostring(runId) .. ":" .. player.UserId
		local granted = recorded[key] or 0
		if granted >= 30 then
			return
		end
		local profile = deps.DataService.get(player)
		if profile then
			recorded[key] = granted + 5
			profile.research += 5
			deps.DataService.markDirty(player)
			notify(player, "Anomaly recorded (+5 research).")
			deps.LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
		end
	end, { rate = 0.2, burst = 1 })

	Router.onAction("LeaveCase", function(player)
		MissionService.leave(player)
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 2 })

	Router.onRequest("JoinMission", function(player)
		local ok, reason = MissionService.join(player)
		return { ok = ok, message = reason }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 2 })

	Router.onRequest("Revive", function(player)
		local mission = MissionService.mission
		if not mission then
			return { ok = false, message = "No case is running." }
		end
		local ok, reason = mission:canRevive(player.UserId, "Personal")
		if not ok then
			return { ok = false, message = reason }
		end
		if useStoredRevive(player) then
			return { ok = true, message = "Re-entering..." }
		end
		local prompted, why = deps.MarketService.promptProduct(player, "Revive", { runId = runId })
		return { ok = prompted, prompted = prompted, message = why }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 2 })

	Router.onRequest("TeamRecovery", function(player)
		local mission = MissionService.mission
		if not mission then
			return { ok = false, message = "No case is running." }
		end
		if not mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
			return { ok = false, message = "Only investigators still inside can call it in." }
		end
		local ok, reason = mission:canTeamRecover()
		if not ok then
			return { ok = false, message = reason }
		end
		if vote then
			return { ok = false, message = "A vote is already running." }
		end
		local needed = 0
		for _, p in missionPlayers() do
			if mission:isAlive(p.UserId) then
				needed += 1
			end
		end
		vote = {
			proposer = player.UserId,
			proposerName = player.DisplayName,
			yes = { [player.UserId] = true },
			yesCount = 1,
			needed = needed,
			expires = now() + VOTE_TIME,
		}
		dirty = true
		if vote.yesCount >= vote.needed then
			finishVote(true)
		else
			sendAll(remotes.Cue, { cue = "TeamVote", proposer = player.DisplayName })
		end
		return { ok = true }
	end, { rate = 1, burst = 2 })

	Router.onAction("TeamVoteReply", function(player, payload)
		local mission = MissionService.mission
		if not vote or not mission or not mission:isAlive(player.UserId) then
			return
		end
		if payload.yes ~= true then
			finishVote(false)
			return
		end
		if not vote.yes[player.UserId] then
			vote.yes[player.UserId] = true
			vote.yesCount += 1
			dirty = true
		end
		if vote.yesCount >= vote.needed then
			finishVote(true)
		end
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	table.insert(deps.MarketService.onGranted, function(player, productKey)
		local intent = deps.MarketService.takeIntent(player, productKey)
		if productKey == "Revive" then
			if not (intent and type(intent) == "table" and intent.runId == runId and useStoredRevive(player)) then
				notify(player, "Emergency re-entry stored for a future case.")
			end
		elseif productKey == "TeamToken" then
			if not (intent and type(intent) == "table" and intent.runId == runId and useStoredTeamToken(player)) then
				notify(player, "Team recovery stored for a future case.")
			end
		end
		deps.LobbyService.sendProfile(player)
	end)

	-- Door sounds for everyone, in the station and the field office alike.
	table.insert(deps.WorldService.onDoorMoved, function(doorId, open, kind)
		remotes.Cue:FireAllClients({ cue = "DoorMoved", door = doorId, open = open, kind = kind })
	end)

	table.insert(deps.WorldService.onReset, function(station)
		deps.InteractionService.bindStation(station)
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(TICK)
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				local mission = MissionService.mission
				if not mission then
					return
				end
				local t = now()
				mission:update(t, { presenterIdle = deps.EntityService.isIdle() })
				applyEffects()
				if t - lastGhostSample >= GHOST_SAMPLE then
					lastGhostSample = t
					sampleGhosts(t)
				end
				if vote then
					local alive = mission:canTeamRecover()
					if t > vote.expires or not alive then
						finishVote(false)
					end
				end
				refreshPrompts(false)
				if dirty then
					dirty = false
					replicate(true)
				else
					replicate(false)
				end
				if reportAt and t - reportAt > REPORT_LINGER then
					MissionService.endMission()
				end
			end)
			if not ok then
				warn("[MissionService] tick failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end)
end

return MissionService
```

## src/server/Services/Router.luau

```lua
--[[
	Router — the only entry point for client -> server traffic.

	Every message is a table with a string `type`. Handlers are registered per
	type; anything unknown, malformed or over the rate limit is dropped. Handlers
	must still validate every field they read.
]]

local Router = {}
Router.actions = {}
Router.requests = {}
Router.buckets = {}

-- Token bucket per player per message type.
local DEFAULT_LIMIT = { rate = 8, burst = 16 }

local function allow(player, kind, limit)
	limit = limit or DEFAULT_LIMIT
	local userBuckets = Router.buckets[player]
	if not userBuckets then
		userBuckets = {}
		Router.buckets[player] = userBuckets
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	local bucket = userBuckets[kind]
	if not bucket then
		bucket = { tokens = limit.burst, at = now }
		userBuckets[kind] = bucket
	end
	bucket.tokens = math.min(limit.burst, bucket.tokens + (now - bucket.at) * limit.rate)
	bucket.at = now
	if bucket.tokens < 1 then
		return false
	end
	bucket.tokens -= 1
	return true
end

function Router.onAction(kind, handler, limit)
	Router.actions[kind] = { handler = handler, limit = limit }
end

function Router.onRequest(kind, handler, limit)
	Router.requests[kind] = { handler = handler, limit = limit }
end

function Router.init(remotes, players)
	remotes.Action.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, payload)
		if type(payload) ~= "table" or type(payload.type) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local entry = Router.actions[payload.type]
		if not entry or not allow(player, payload.type, entry.limit) then
			return
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(entry.handler, player, payload)
		if not ok then
			warn(string.format("[Router] action %s from %s failed: %s", payload.type, player.Name, tostring(err)))
		end
	end)

	remotes.Request.OnServerInvoke = function(player, payload)
		if type(payload) ~= "table" or type(payload.type) ~= "string" then
			return { ok = false, message = "Malformed request." }
		end
		local entry = Router.requests[payload.type]
		if not entry then
			return { ok = false, message = "Unknown request." }
		end
		if not allow(player, payload.type, entry.limit) then
			return { ok = false, message = "Slow down." }
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(entry.handler, player, payload)
		if not ok then
			warn(string.format("[Router] request %s from %s failed: %s", payload.type, player.Name, tostring(result)))
			return { ok = false, message = "Something went wrong." }
		end
		return result or { ok = true }
	end

	players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		Router.buckets[player] = nil
	end)
end

-- Small validation helpers.
function Router.string(value, maxLength)
	return type(value) == "string" and #value <= (maxLength or 64)
end

function Router.number(value, min, max)
	return type(value) == "number" and value == value and value >= min and value <= max
end

return Router
```

## src/server/Services/SignalService.luau

```lua
--[[
	SignalService — deterministic signal quality.

	Per player (HUD meter, replicated as the "Signal" attribute) and per camera
	(what a deck can show, and whether an edit can be made). Uses the shared
	SignalModel so the client's deck renders exactly what the server validates.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local SignalModel = require(Shared.Core.SignalModel)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)

local SignalService = {}

local deps

local function toV3(p)
	return Vector3.new(p.x, p.y or 0, p.z)
end

local function anomalyIn(mission, rooms)
	if not mission or not mission.loop.active then
```
