```lua
		return false
	end
	return table.find(rooms, mission.case.loop.room) ~= nil
end

function SignalService.cameraQuality(mission, deckId, cameraId)
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	local deck = mission.case.decks[deckId]
	if not camera then
		return 0, "NO CAMERA"
	end
	local presenter = deps.EntityService.position()
	local relayOn = true
	if camera.relay == "WEST" or camera.relay == "EAST" then
		relayOn = mission.relays[camera.relay] ~= false
	end
	return SignalModel.cameraQuality({
		relay = camera.relay,
		powerOn = mission.flags.generator_restored == true,
		relayOn = relayOn,
		deckBattery = deck ~= nil and deck.battery == true,
		presenterDistance = presenter and (presenter - toV3(camera.pos)).Magnitude or nil,
		anomaly = anomalyIn(mission, camera.rooms),
	})
end

-- An edit needs at least one camera showing the event with a usable signal.
function SignalService.canEdit(mission, deckId, eventId)
	local ev = mission.timeline:getEvent(eventId)
	if not ev then
		return false
	end
	for _, cameraId in ev.cameras or {} do
		if SignalService.cameraQuality(mission, deckId, cameraId) >= SignalModel.WEAK then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function update()
	local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
	local presenter = deps.EntityService.position()
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		local quality = 100
		local character = player.Character
		local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if mission and hrp and mission:participant(player.UserId) then
			local roomId = MapLayout.roomAt(hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Z)
			local room = roomId and MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
			local wing = room and room.wing
			quality = SignalModel.playerQuality({
				powerOn = mission.flags.generator_restored == true,
				wingRelayOn = (wing == "WEST" or wing == "EAST") and mission.relays[wing] ~= false or nil,
				presenterDistance = presenter and (presenter - hrp.Position).Magnitude or nil,
				anomaly = mission.loop.active and roomId == mission.case.loop.room,
			})
		end
		if player:GetAttribute("Signal") ~= quality then
			player:SetAttribute("Signal", quality)
		end
	end
end

function SignalService.init(dependencies)
	deps = dependencies
	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(0.5)
			local ok, err = pcall(update)
			if not ok then
				warn("[SignalService] " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end)
end

return SignalService
```

## src/server/Services/TimelineService.luau

```lua
--[[
	TimelineService — the Time-Shift Deck's server side.

	Execution rules from the brief:
	  1. The client sends an event id and requested state.
	  2. The server checks the mission, the player (spectators can't edit), deck
	     access and interaction range.
	  3-4. MissionCore/TimelineCore verify the event, prerequisites and charges and
	     deduct them atomically.
	  5-6. The edit is recorded, consequences update, every client gets the new
	     timeline in the next mission snapshot.
	  7. Invalid requests are rejected without spending anything, and every edit is
	     logged for debugging and scoring.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)

local Router = require(script.Parent.Router)

local TimelineService = {}

local DECK_RANGE = 14

TimelineService.openDecks = {} -- [player] = deckId | "HANDHELD" | "ARCHIVE"

local deps

local function nearDeck(player, mission, deckId)
	local deck = mission.case.decks[deckId]
	local character = player.Character
	local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not deck or not hrp then
		return false
	end
	local pos = Vector3.new(deck.pos.x, deck.pos.y, deck.pos.z)
	return (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude <= DECK_RANGE
end

function TimelineService.init(dependencies)
	deps = dependencies

	Router.onAction("DeckOpen", function(player, payload)
		local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
		if not mission or not mission:participant(player.UserId) then
			return
		end
		local deckId = payload.deck
		if deckId == "HANDHELD" then
			TimelineService.openDecks[player] = "HANDHELD"
			mission:setWatching(player.UserId, true)
			return
		end
		if not Router.string(deckId, 32) or not mission.case.decks[deckId] or not nearDeck(player, mission, deckId) then
			return
		end
		local tapeId = Router.string(payload.tape, 16) and payload.tape or nil
		if mission:deckOpened(player.UserId, deckId, tapeId, workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) then
			TimelineService.openDecks[player] = deckId
			deps.MissionService.markDirty()
		end
	end, { rate = 4, burst = 8 })

	Router.onAction("DeckClose", function(player)
		local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
		TimelineService.openDecks[player] = nil
		if mission then
			mission:deckClosed(player.UserId)
			mission:setWatching(player.UserId, false)
		end
	end)

	Router.onAction("InspectEvent", function(player, payload)
		local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
		if mission and Router.string(payload.event, 40) then
			mission:inspectEvent(player.UserId, payload.event, workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
		end
	end)

	Router.onRequest("CommitEdit", function(player, payload)
		local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
		if not mission then
			return { ok = false, code = "PHASE", message = "No case is running." }
		end
		if not Router.string(payload.event, 40) or not Router.string(payload.state, 32) or not Router.string(payload.deck, 32) then
			return { ok = false, code = "INVALID_STATE", message = "Malformed edit." }
		end
		local deckId = payload.deck
		if TimelineService.openDecks[player] ~= deckId or not nearDeck(player, mission, deckId) then
			return { ok = false, code = "NO_DECK", message = "Stand at the deck to edit." }
		end
		local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
		local ok, code, message = mission:commitEdit(player.UserId, payload.event, payload.state, {
			deckId = deckId,
			expectedState = Router.string(payload.expected, 32) and payload.expected or nil,
			signalOk = deps.SignalService.canEdit(mission, deckId, payload.event),
		}, now)
		print(string.format("[Timeline] %s edit %s -> %s : %s", player.Name, payload.event, payload.state, ok and "OK" or tostring(code)))
		deps.MissionService.markDirty()
		return { ok = ok, code = code, message = message, charges = mission.charges }
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	Router.onRequest("MarkFrame", function(player, payload)
		if not Router.string(payload.tape, 16) or not Router.string(payload.camera, 16) or not Router.number(payload.t, 0, 600) then
			return { ok = false, message = "Malformed." }
		end
		local mission = deps.MissionService.mission
		if mission and mission:participant(player.UserId) and mission:tapeFound(payload.tape) then
			local ok, result = mission:markFrame(player.UserId, payload.tape, payload.camera, payload.t, workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
			deps.MissionService.markDirty()
			return { ok = ok, message = ok and nil or result, frame = ok and result or nil }
		end
		return deps.EvidenceService.markArchiveFrame(player, payload.tape, payload.camera, payload.t)
	end, { rate = 2, burst = 4 })

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		TimelineService.openDecks[player] = nil
	end)
end

function TimelineService.closeAll()
	TimelineService.openDecks = {}
end

-- Used by the camera feed list in the deck UI.
TimelineService.Cameras = MapLayout.Cameras

return TimelineService
```

## src/server/Services/WorldService.luau

```lua
--[[
	WorldService — the physical station.

	Keeps a pristine template of Station04 (built by MapBuilder, or whatever was
	saved in the place file - so Studio edits to the map are respected) and
	swaps in a fresh copy for every case, which makes mission cleanup trivial.
	Applies world state decided by MissionCore: room lights, door locks and
	motion, relays, generator lamps and item props.
]]

local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared")
local MapBuilder = require(Shared.World.MapBuilder)
local MapLayout = require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local Props = require(Shared.World.Props)
local CaseRegistry = require(Shared.Config.CaseRegistry)

local WorldService = {}

WorldService.station = nil
WorldService.hq = nil
WorldService.template = nil
WorldService.doors = {}
WorldService.rooms = {}
WorldService.anchors = {}
WorldService.items = {}
WorldService.glass = {}
WorldService.onReset = {}
WorldService.onDoorChanged = {}

local FLUORESCENT_ON = Props.rgb(MapLayout.Palette.fluorescent)
local FLUORESCENT_OFF = Color3.fromRGB(70, 74, 70)
local AMBER = Props.rgb(MapLayout.Palette.amber)
local LAMP_OFF = Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 42)
local LAMP_GOOD = Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 120)

local function setLightPart(partObj, on, colourOn, colourOff)
	partObj.Material = on and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	partObj.Color = on and colourOn or colourOff
	local light = partObj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Light")
	if light then
		light.Enabled = on
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Indexing
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function indexStation(station)
	WorldService.doors = {}
	WorldService.rooms = {}
	WorldService.anchors = {}
	WorldService.items = {}
	WorldService.glass = {}

	for _, roomFolder in station.Rooms:GetChildren() do
		local entry = { fluorescent = {}, emergency = {}, mode = nil, folder = roomFolder }
		for _, d in roomFolder:GetDescendants() do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then
				local kind = d:GetAttribute("LightKind")
				if kind == "Fluorescent" then
					table.insert(entry.fluorescent, d)
				elseif kind == "Emergency" then
					table.insert(entry.emergency, d)
				end
			end
		end
		WorldService.rooms[roomFolder.Name] = entry
	end

	for _, model in station.Doors:GetChildren() do
		local id = model:GetAttribute("DoorId")
		if id then
			local entry = {
				id = id,
				model = model,
				kind = model:GetAttribute("Kind"),
				refuge = model:GetAttribute("Refuge") == true,
				swing = model:GetAttribute("SwingSign") or 1,
				width = model:GetAttribute("PanelWidth") or 4,
				open = false,
				locked = false,
				panels = {},
				layout = MapLayout.doorById(id),
			}
			for _, child in model:GetChildren() do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Anchored and (child.Name == "Panel" or child.Name == "PanelL" or child.Name == "PanelR") then
					table.insert(entry.panels, { part = child, closed = child.CFrame })
				end
			end
			WorldService.doors[id] = entry
		end
	end

	for _, anchorPart in station.Anchors:GetChildren() do
		WorldService.anchors[anchorPart.Name] = anchorPart
	end

	for _, d in station:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d:GetAttribute("SeeThrough") then
			table.insert(WorldService.glass, d)
		end
	end

	local items = station:FindFirstChild("Items")
	if not items then
		items = Instance.new("Folder")
		items.Name = "Items"
		items.Parent = station
	end
	WorldService.itemsFolder = items
end

function WorldService.init()
	local station = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Station04")
	local hq = Workspace:FindFirstChild("FieldOffice")
	if not station or not hq then
		if station then
			station:Destroy()
		end
		if hq then
			hq:Destroy()
		end
		station, hq = MapBuilder.build(Workspace, CaseRegistry.get("case01"))
	end
	WorldService.hq = hq
	WorldService.template = station:Clone()
	WorldService.template.Name = "Station04Template"
	WorldService.template.Parent = ServerStorage
	WorldService.station = station
	indexStation(station)
end

-- Replaces the live station with a clean copy of the template.
function WorldService.reset()
	if WorldService.station then
		WorldService.station:Destroy()
	end
	local fresh = WorldService.template:Clone()
	fresh.Name = "Station04"
	fresh.Parent = Workspace
	WorldService.station = fresh
	indexStation(fresh)
	for _, callback in WorldService.onReset do
		callback(fresh)
	end
	return fresh
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Lights
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function WorldService.setRoomLights(roomId, mode)
	local room = WorldService.rooms[roomId]
	if not room then
		return
	end
	room.mode = mode
	room.folder:SetAttribute("LightMode", mode)
	for _, p in room.fluorescent do
		setLightPart(p, mode == "on", FLUORESCENT_ON, FLUORESCENT_OFF)
	end
	for _, p in room.emergency do
		setLightPart(p, mode == "emergency", AMBER, LAMP_OFF)
	end
end

-- Between cases the station sits dark apart from its battery emergency lights.
function WorldService.setIdleLights()
	for roomId in WorldService.rooms do
		local room = MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
		WorldService.setRoomLights(roomId, room and room.emergency and "emergency" or "off")
	end
	WorldService.setGeneratorStage(0)
end

function WorldService.roomLightMode(roomId)
	local room = WorldService.rooms[roomId]
	return room and room.mode or "off"
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Doors
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function doorTarget(entry, panel, open)
	if not open then
		return panel.closed
	end
	if entry.kind == "freight" then
		return panel.closed + Vector3.new(0, 10.5, 0)
	end
	local width = entry.width
	if entry.kind == "double" then
		width = panel.part.Size.X
	end
	local hinge = (panel.closed * CFrame.new(-width / 2, 0, 0)).Position
	local angle = math.rad(90) * entry.swing
	return CFrame.new(hinge) * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0) * CFrame.new(-hinge) * panel.closed
end

local function moveDoor(entry, open)
	entry.open = open
	entry.model:SetAttribute("Open", open)
	local duration = entry.kind == "freight" and 3 or 0.45
	for _, panel in entry.panels do
		local tween = TweenService:Create(panel.part, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			CFrame = doorTarget(entry, panel, open),
		})
		tween:Play()
	end
	for _, callback in WorldService.onDoorChanged do
		callback(entry.id, entry)
	end
end

local function updateModifiers(entry)
	for _, d in entry.model:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("PathfindingModifier") then
			local passable = not entry.locked and (entry.kind == "door" or entry.open)
			d.Label = passable and "Door" or "LockedDoor"
			d.PassThrough = passable
		end
	end
end

function WorldService.door(doorId)
	return WorldService.doors[doorId]
end

function WorldService.setDoorLocked(doorId, locked)
	local entry = WorldService.doors[doorId]
	if not entry then
		return
	end
	entry.locked = locked
	entry.model:SetAttribute("Locked", locked)
	if locked and entry.open then
		moveDoor(entry, false)
	end
	updateModifiers(entry)
end

-- Called with (doorId, open, kind) whenever a door actually moves, so every
-- client can play its sound where it is.
WorldService.onDoorMoved = {}

local function doorMoved(doorId, entry)
	for _, fn in WorldService.onDoorMoved do
		local ok, err = pcall(fn, doorId, entry.open, entry.kind)
		if not ok then
			warn("[WorldService] door listener failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end
end

function WorldService.openDoor(doorId)
	local entry = WorldService.doors[doorId]
	if not entry or entry.locked or entry.open or #entry.panels == 0 then
		return false
	end
	moveDoor(entry, true)
	updateModifiers(entry)
	doorMoved(doorId, entry)
	return true
end

function WorldService.closeDoor(doorId)
	local entry = WorldService.doors[doorId]
	if not entry or not entry.open then
		return false
	end
	moveDoor(entry, false)
	updateModifiers(entry)
	doorMoved(doorId, entry)
	return true
end

function WorldService.toggleDoor(doorId)
	local entry = WorldService.doors[doorId]
	if not entry then
		return false
	end
	if entry.open then
		return WorldService.closeDoor(doorId)
	end
	return WorldService.openDoor(doorId)
end

-- Closed, unlocked hinged doors near a point (for the Presenter).
function WorldService.closedDoorsNear(position, radius)
	local list = {}
	for id, entry in WorldService.doors do
		if entry.kind == "door" and not entry.open and not entry.locked and #entry.panels > 0 then
			local p = entry.panels[1].closed.Position
			local flat = Vector3.new(p.X - position.X, 0, p.Z - position.Z)
			if flat.Magnitude <= radius then
				table.insert(list, id)
			end
		end
	end
	return list
end

-- A refuge room counts as sealed while every hinged door into it is shut.
function WorldService.refugeSealed(roomId)
	local any = false
	for _, entry in WorldService.doors do
		if entry.refuge and entry.layout and table.find(entry.layout.rooms, roomId) then
			any = true
			if entry.open then
				return false
			end
		end
	end
	return any
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Equipment state
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function eachTagged(attribute, fn)
	if not WorldService.station then
		return
	end
	for _, d in WorldService.station:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d:GetAttribute(attribute) ~= nil then
			fn(d, d:GetAttribute(attribute))
		end
	end
end

function WorldService.setGeneratorStage(stage)
	eachTagged("GeneratorStage", function(lamp, lampStage)
		local colour = lampStage == 0 and Color3.fromRGB(150, 70, 50) or (lampStage == 1 and AMBER or LAMP_GOOD)
		setLightPart(lamp, lampStage == stage, colour, LAMP_OFF)
	end)
end

function WorldService.setRelay(relayId, on)
	local relay = MapLayout.Relays[relayId]
	if not relay then
		return
	end
	local room = WorldService.rooms[relay.room]
	if not room then
		return
	end
	for _, d in room.folder:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d:GetAttribute("RelayLamp") then
			setLightPart(d, true, on and LAMP_GOOD or AMBER, LAMP_OFF)
		end
	end
end

function WorldService.setTransmitterArmed(armed)
	eachTagged("TransmitterLamp", function(lamp)
		-- The chamber's single red status lamp: red is reserved for this kind of beat.
		setLightPart(lamp, armed, Props.rgb(MapLayout.Palette.warning), Color3.fromRGB(70, 30, 26))
	end)
end

function WorldService.setOnAir(on)
	eachTagged("OnAirSign", function(sign)
		local gui = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
		local label = gui and gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
		if label then
			label.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(230, 70, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 40, 36)
		end
		sign.Material = on and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
		sign.Color = on and Color3.fromRGB(60, 16, 14) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 22)
	end)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Items
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function WorldService.spawnItem(caseDef, itemId, pos, interactive)
	local item = caseDef.items[itemId]
	local cf = CFrame.new(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z)
	local model
	if item.kind == "Tape" then
		local tape = caseDef.tapes[item.tape]
		local c = tape.colour
		model = Props.vhsTape(WorldService.itemsFolder, cf, item.label, Color3.fromRGB(c[1], c[2], c[3]))
	elseif item.kind == "Evidence" then
		local colour = item.bonus and Color3.fromRGB(168, 70, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(150, 141, 117)
		model = Props.vhsTape(WorldService.itemsFolder, cf, item.label, colour)
	else
		model = Props.component(WorldService.itemsFolder, cf, itemId)
	end
	model.Name = itemId
	model:SetAttribute("ItemId", itemId)
	local anchorPart = nil
	if interactive then
		anchorPart = Props.part(model, "ITEM_" .. itemId, Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2), cf * CFrame.new(0, 0.6, 0), "black", {
			collide = false,
			shadow = false,
			transparency = 1,
		})
		anchorPart.CanQuery = false
		anchorPart:SetAttribute("InteractId", "ITEM_" .. itemId)
		WorldService.anchors["ITEM_" .. itemId] = anchorPart
	end
	-- A faint glow so objective items read in the dark (never pixel-hunting).
	local glow = Instance.new("PointLight")
	glow.Range = 4
	glow.Brightness = 0.35
	glow.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 220, 200)
	glow.Shadows = false
	glow.Parent = model.PrimaryPart
	WorldService.items[itemId] = model
	return model, anchorPart
end

function WorldService.removeItem(itemId)
	local model = WorldService.items[itemId]
	if model then
		model:Destroy()
		WorldService.items[itemId] = nil
	end
	WorldService.anchors["ITEM_" .. itemId] = nil
end

function WorldService.setItemVisible(itemId, visible)
	local model = WorldService.items[itemId]
	if not model then
		return
	end
	for _, d in model:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and not string.match(d.Name, "^ITEM_") then
			if d:GetAttribute("BaseTransparency") == nil then
				d:SetAttribute("BaseTransparency", d.Transparency)
			end
			d.Transparency = visible and d:GetAttribute("BaseTransparency") or 1
		elseif d:IsA("SurfaceGui") or d:IsA("Light") then
			d.Enabled = visible
		end
	end
end

function WorldService.spawnPoints()
	local list = {}
	if WorldService.station and WorldService.station:FindFirstChild("Spawns") then
		for _, s in WorldService.station.Spawns:GetChildren() do
			table.insert(list, s.CFrame)
		end
	end
	table.sort(list, function(a, b)
		return a.Position.X < b.Position.X
	end)
	return list
end

function WorldService.hqSpawn()
	local spawn = WorldService.hq and WorldService.hq:FindFirstChild("HQSpawn")
	if spawn then
		return spawn.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
	end
	local p = MapLayout.HQSpawn
	return CFrame.new(p.x, 3, p.z)
end

return WorldService
```

## src/shared/Net.luau

```lua
--[[
	Remote names. The server creates them under ReplicatedStorage.Remotes; the
	client waits for them. All client -> server traffic goes through `Action`
	(fire-and-forget) or `Request` (needs an answer) and is validated and
	rate-limited on the server.
]]

local Net = {}

Net.FolderName = "Remotes"

Net.Events = {
	"Action", -- C->S { type = "...", ... }
	"Mission", -- S->C mission snapshot
	"Subtitle", -- S->C subtitle / radio line
	"Cue", -- S->C presentation cue
	"Notify", -- S->C toast
	"Report", -- S->C case report
	"Profile", -- S->C profile summary
	"Party", -- S->C HQ party / ready state
	"Ghosts", -- S->C recorded positions for a Rewind replay
}

Net.Functions = {
	"Request", -- C->S->C { type = "...", ... } -> result table
}

function Net.create(replicatedStorage)
	local folder = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(Net.FolderName)
	if not folder then
		folder = Instance.new("Folder")
		folder.Name = Net.FolderName
		folder.Parent = replicatedStorage
	end
	local remotes = {}
	for _, name in Net.Events do
		local remote = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
		if not remote then
			remote = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
			remote.Name = name
			remote.Parent = folder
		end
		remotes[name] = remote
	end
	for _, name in Net.Functions do
		local remote = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
		if not remote then
			remote = Instance.new("RemoteFunction")
			remote.Name = name
			remote.Parent = folder
		end
		remotes[name] = remote
	end
	return remotes
end

function Net.wait(replicatedStorage)
	local folder = replicatedStorage:WaitForChild(Net.FolderName)
	local remotes = {}
	for _, name in Net.Events do
		remotes[name] = folder:WaitForChild(name)
	end
	for _, name in Net.Functions do
		remotes[name] = folder:WaitForChild(name)
	end
	return remotes
end

return Net
```

## src/shared/Config/Audio.luau

```lua
--[[
	Audio design: how each sound is played and which game moment plays it.

	The sounds themselves are original, generated by scripts/audio/ into four
	banks; Config/AudioBank (generated) says where each one sits in its bank and
	Config/AudioAssets holds the uploaded bank ids. Every cue that matters for
	gameplay also has a subtitle and a visual, so the game is fully playable
	muted, with subtitles only, or before the banks are uploaded.

	group: Music | Ambience | Effects | Interface | Cinematic. Music, Ambience and
	Effects follow their sliders in Settings; Interface follows Effects;
	Cinematic (the end-of-transmission tone) plays through the false-safety
	silence. Master scales everything.
]]

local Audio = {}

Audio.Groups = { "Music", "Ambience", "Effects", "Interface", "Cinematic" }

-- Defaults by sound: volume (0..), pitch = { min, max } random per play,
-- spatial = { min, max } roll-off distances when played at a position.
local DEFAULTS = { group = "Effects", volume = 0.7 }

Audio.Sounds = {
	-- Interface
	UIHover = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.25 },
	UIClick = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	UIBack = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	UIToggle = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	UISlider = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.35 },
	UIError = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	UINotify = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.55 },
	MenuOpen = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	MenuClose = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	TutorialPage = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.7 },
	Countdown = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	Ready = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.7 },
	Deploy = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.8 },
	RewardTick = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	Unlock = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.7 },
	ReportKey = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.5, pitch = { 0.92, 1.08 } },
	ReportStamp = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.8 },
	VoteChime = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.7 },

	-- The investigator
	FlashlightOn = { volume = 0.5 },
	FlashlightOff = { volume = 0.5 },
	BreathOut = { volume = 0.5 },
	BreathIn = { volume = 0.4 },
	Heartbeat = { volume = 0.9 },
	ToolEquip = { volume = 0.5 },
	ToolUnequip = { volume = 0.5 },
	RecBeep = { volume = 0.5 },
	Inspect = { volume = 0.35 },
	PromptHold = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.4 },
	PickupTape = { volume = 0.8 },
	PickupMetal = { volume = 0.7 },
	PickupKey = { volume = 0.7 },

	-- The building (played where they happen)
	DoorOpen = { volume = 0.8, spatial = { 6, 70 }, pitch = { 0.95, 1.05 } },
	DoorClose = { volume = 0.8, spatial = { 6, 70 }, pitch = { 0.95, 1.05 } },
	DoorLocked = { volume = 0.7 },
	DoorForced = { volume = 1.0, spatial = { 10, 140 } },
	FreightDoor = { volume = 1.0, spatial = { 12, 160 } },
	KeypadPress = { volume = 0.6, pitch = { 0.98, 1.02 } },
	KeypadWrong = { volume = 0.7 },
	KeypadRight = { volume = 0.7 },
	CageOpen = { volume = 0.8, spatial = { 6, 60 } },
	Install = { volume = 0.9 },
	Relay = { volume = 0.9, spatial = { 8, 90 } },
	GeneratorPrime = { volume = 0.9, spatial = { 8, 110 } },
	GeneratorStart = { volume = 1.0, spatial = { 10, 160 } },
	PowerOn = { volume = 0.8 },
	LightsFail = { volume = 0.9 },
	Alarm = { volume = 0.9 },
	PhoneRing = { volume = 0.6 },
	TransmitterArmed = { volume = 1.0 },
	OnAir = { volume = 0.7 },
	EndOfTransmission = { group = "Cinematic", volume = 0.6 },
	VanDoorSlam = { volume = 0.9 },
	ChairScrape = { volume = 0.6, spatial = { 8, 90 } },
	ShelfCreak = { volume = 0.6, spatial = { 8, 90 } },
	MetalCreak = { volume = 0.7, spatial = { 10, 120 } },
	TapeFlutter = { volume = 0.6, spatial = { 8, 100 } },

	-- The deck
	DeckOpen = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	DeckClose = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	TapeInsert = { volume = 0.7 },
	TapeEject = { volume = 0.7 },
	DeckPlay = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	DeckPause = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	FrameStep = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.45, pitch = { 0.95, 1.05 } },
	ChannelSwitch = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	MarkFrame = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.7 },
	EditCommit = { volume = 0.9 },
	EditRejected = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.6 },
	Rewind = { volume = 0.8 },

	-- Signal
	StaticShort = { volume = 0.5 },
	StaticLong = { volume = 0.7 },
	SignalLost = { volume = 0.7 },
	RadioIn = { volume = 0.5 },
	RadioOut = { volume = 0.45 },
	Reentry = { volume = 0.8 },

	-- The Presenter
	PresenterStep = { volume = 1.0, spatial = { 10, 120 }, pitch = { 0.94, 1.04 } },
	PresenterAppear = { volume = 0.8 },
	PresenterVanish = { volume = 0.7 },
	HuntStart = { volume = 1.0 },
	HuntEnd = { volume = 0.8 },
	Eliminated = { volume = 1.0 },
	EditorShadow = { volume = 0.8 },

	-- The Loop
	LoopStart = { volume = 0.9 },
	LoopReset = { volume = 0.7 },
	LoopBreak = { volume = 0.9 },

	-- Stingers
	StingDiscovery = { group = "Music", volume = 0.7 },
	StingScare = { volume = 0.9 },
	StingSuccess = { group = "Music", volume = 0.8 },
	StingFail = { group = "Music", volume = 0.8 },

	-- Beds and machinery (looping)
	AmbStation = { group = "Ambience", volume = 0.8 },
	AmbDark = { group = "Ambience", volume = 0.9 },
	AmbHQ = { group = "Ambience", volume = 0.6 },
	AmbOutdoor = { group = "Ambience", volume = 0.8 },
	Fluorescent = { group = "Ambience", volume = 0.35 },
	GeneratorLoop = { group = "Ambience", volume = 1.0, spatial = { 10, 140 } },
	TransmitterLoop = { group = "Ambience", volume = 1.0, spatial = { 10, 140 } },
	VanIdle = { group = "Ambience", volume = 0.9, spatial = { 8, 110 } },
	PresenterNear = { volume = 0.9 },
	PresenterStatic = { volume = 0.8, spatial = { 4, 45 } },
	RadioBed = { volume = 0.25 },
	DeckHum = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.35 },
	TapeScrub = { group = "Interface", volume = 0.45 },

	-- Music
	MusicMenu = { group = "Music", volume = 0.55 },
	MusicLobby = { group = "Music", volume = 0.4 },
	MusicBriefing = { group = "Music", volume = 0.5 },
	MusicExplore = { group = "Music", volume = 0.5 },
	MusicTension = { group = "Music", volume = 0.55 },
	MusicHunt = { group = "Music", volume = 0.6 },
	MusicExtract = { group = "Music", volume = 0.55 },
	MusicReport = { group = "Music", volume = 0.5 },
}

-- Footsteps: Step_<kind>_<1..4> in the bank, picked by floor surface.
Audio.StepKinds = { "tile", "concrete", "carpet", "metal" }
Audio.StepVariants = 4
Audio.StepDefaults = { volume = 0.55, pitch = { 0.93, 1.07 }, spatial = { 4, 45 } }

-- Floor materials -> footstep sound.
Audio.StepSurface = {
	CeramicTiles = "tile",
	Slate = "tile",
	Marble = "tile",
	Granite = "tile",
	Pavement = "tile",
	SmoothPlastic = "tile",
	Plastic = "tile",
	Carpet = "carpet",
	Fabric = "carpet",
	Grass = "carpet",
	LeafyGrass = "carpet",
	Metal = "metal",
	DiamondPlate = "metal",
	CorrodedMetal = "metal",
	Foil = "metal",
}

function Audio.def(name)
	if string.sub(name, 1, 5) == "Step_" then
		return Audio.StepDefaults
	end
	return Audio.Sounds[name] or DEFAULTS
end

-- Server cue -> sound. A table plays several; `spatial` plays at the cue's
-- position (door, relay...) when the client can work one out.
Audio.Cues = {
	HuntStart = "HuntStart",
	HuntEnd = "HuntEnd",
	PresenterAppear = "PresenterAppear",
	PresenterVanish = "PresenterVanish",
	LightsFail = "LightsFail",
	PowerOn = "PowerOn",
	GeneratorPrime = "GeneratorPrime",
	DoorLocked = "DoorLocked",
	DoorForced = "DoorForced",
	EditCommitted = "EditCommit",
	LocalEditCommitted = "EditCommit",
	Alarm = "Alarm",
	LoopStart = "LoopStart",
	LoopReset = "LoopReset",
	LoopBreak = "LoopBreak",
	Rewind = "Rewind",
	Install = "Install",
	CageOpen = "CageOpen",
	TransmitterArmed = "TransmitterArmed",
	OnAir = "OnAir",
	BroadcastStart = "OnAir",
	EditorShadow = "EditorShadow",
	TeamVote = "VoteChime",
	Revived = "Reentry",
}

-- Subtitle sound descriptions written into the case -> the sound itself.
-- First match wins; patterns are plain substrings of the upper-case text.
Audio.SubtitleSounds = {
	{ "FOOTSTEPS STOP", "PresenterStep", 2 },
	{ "FOOTSTEPS", "PresenterStep", 4 },
	{ "CHAIR SCRAPES", "ChairScrape", 1 },
	{ "SHELVES CREAK", "ShelfCreak", 1 },
	{ "METAL CREAKS", "MetalCreak", 1 },
	{ "TAPE FLUTTER", "TapeFlutter", 1 },
}

-- What plays where.
Audio.Music = {
	Menu = "MusicMenu",
	Lobby = "MusicLobby",
	Briefing = "MusicBriefing",
	Explore = "MusicExplore",
	Tension = "MusicTension",
	Hunt = "MusicHunt",
	Extract = "MusicExtract",
	Report = "MusicReport",
}

return Audio
```

## src/shared/Config/AudioAssets.luau

```lua
--[[
	Roblox asset ids for the four audio banks in assets/audio/.

	Every sound in the game (effects, ambience and music) is original and
	generated by scripts/audio/build_banks.py, packed into these four files so
	the whole game costs four audio uploads. Until the ids are filled in, the
	game runs silently: every important cue still has a subtitle and a visual.

	To fill them in, either:
	  * run scripts/audio/upload.py with an Open Cloud API key (it uploads the
	    banks and rewrites this file), or
	  * upload assets/audio/DeadAir_*.ogg on the Creator Dashboard and paste
	    each id below as "rbxassetid://<number>".

	Upload as the same user or group that owns the experience, or the sounds
	won't be allowed to play.
]]

local AudioAssets = {}

AudioAssets.Banks = {
	Sfx = "",
	Ambience = "",
	Music1 = "",
	Music2 = "",
}

return AudioAssets
```

## src/shared/Config/CaseRegistry.luau

```lua
--[[
	All cases in the campaign. Only Case 01 is playable in this build; the others
	exist so Case Select shows the campaign and so save data has stable ids.
]]

local Case01 = require(script.Parent.Cases.Case01)

local CaseRegistry = {}

CaseRegistry.Cases = {
	case01 = Case01,
}

CaseRegistry.Order = {
	{ id = "case01", number = 1, title = Case01.title, location = Case01.location, playable = true },
	{ id = "case02", number = 2, title = "CLASSIFIED", location = "RELAY SITE 11", playable = false },
	{ id = "case03", number = 3, title = "CLASSIFIED", location = "STUDIO COMPLEX B", playable = false },
	{ id = "case04", number = 4, title = "CLASSIFIED", location = "ARCHIVE VAULT 2", playable = false },
	{ id = "case05", number = 5, title = "CLASSIFIED", location = "MOBILE UNIT 3", playable = false },
	{ id = "case06", number = 6, title = "CLASSIFIED", location = "TRANSMITTER 2", playable = false },
	{ id = "case07", number = 7, title = "CLASSIFIED", location = "CHANNEL ZERO", playable = false },
}

function CaseRegistry.get(caseId: string)
	return CaseRegistry.Cases[caseId]
end

return CaseRegistry
```

## src/shared/Config/Cosmetics.luau

```lua
--[[
	Cosmetics never change gameplay information or survival. Sources:
	  default  everyone owns it
	  credits  bought with Recovery Credits earned in cases
	  pass     comes with a gamepass (see Monetisation.Passes)
	  product  comes with a developer product bundle
	  challenge unlocked by an investigator challenge
]]

local Cosmetics = {}

Cosmetics.Slots = { "uniform", "camcorder", "rec", "menu", "tapeLabel", "nameplate" }

Cosmetics.SlotLabels = {
	uniform = "UNIFORM",
	camcorder = "CAMCORDER SHELL",
	rec = "REC OVERLAY",
	menu = "MENU THEME",
	tapeLabel = "TAPE LABELS",
	nameplate = "NAMEPLATE",
}

Cosmetics.Items = {
	-- Uniform vests (worn over the avatar)
	uniform_standard = { slot = "uniform", label = "Division Standard", colour = { 92, 108, 122 }, source = "default" },
	uniform_field = { slot = "uniform", label = "Field Olive", colour = { 104, 122, 100 }, source = "credits", price = 150 },
	uniform_archive = { slot = "uniform", label = "Archive Brown", colour = { 104, 86, 66 }, source = "credits", price = 150 },
	uniform_night = { slot = "uniform", label = "Night Shift", colour = { 40, 44, 42 }, source = "credits", price = 300 },
	uniform_locker = { slot = "uniform", label = "Locker Reserve", colour = { 150, 141, 117 }, source = "pass", pass = "Locker" },
	uniform_supporter = { slot = "uniform", label = "Supporter Jacket", colour = { 120, 96, 60 }, source = "pass", pass = "Supporter" },
	uniform_loss = { slot = "uniform", label = "Nobody Left Behind", colour = { 70, 84, 92 }, source = "challenge", challenge = "C01_NOLOSS" },

	-- Camcorder viewfinder shells
	cam_standard = { slot = "camcorder", label = "Standard Issue", colour = { 196, 186, 158 }, source = "default" },
	cam_graphite = { slot = "camcorder", label = "Graphite", colour = { 120, 116, 110 }, source = "credits", price = 200 },
	cam_classic = { slot = "camcorder", label = "Classic Broadcast", colour = { 214, 150, 62 }, source = "pass", pass = "ClassicPack" },

	-- REC overlays (layout differs; information stays identical)
	rec_standard = { slot = "rec", label = "Standard REC", style = "standard", source = "default" },
	rec_minimal = { slot = "rec", label = "Minimal REC", style = "minimal", source = "credits", price = 100 },
	rec_classic = { slot = "rec", label = "Classic Broadcast REC", style = "classic", source = "pass", pass = "ClassicPack" },

	-- Menu CRT themes
	menu_green = { slot = "menu", label = "Phosphor Green", colour = { 150, 210, 160 }, source = "default" },
	menu_amber = { slot = "menu", label = "Amber Terminal", colour = { 230, 170, 90 }, source = "credits", price = 120 },
	menu_white = { slot = "menu", label = "Studio White", colour = { 220, 226, 220 }, source = "pass", pass = "ClassicPack" },
	menu_blue = { slot = "menu", label = "Test Card Blue", colour = { 150, 180, 220 }, source = "pass", pass = "ClassicPack" },

	-- Tape label skins (Evidence Archive)
	label_plain = { slot = "tapeLabel", label = "Plain Labels", colour = { 196, 186, 158 }, source = "default" },
	label_red = { slot = "tapeLabel", label = "Red Master Labels", colour = { 168, 80, 70 }, source = "product", product = "EvidenceBundle" },
	label_blue = { slot = "tapeLabel", label = "Archive Blue Labels", colour = { 92, 120, 160 }, source = "product", product = "EvidenceBundle" },

	-- Nameplates
	plate_none = { slot = "nameplate", label = "None", source = "default" },
	plate_investigator = { slot = "nameplate", label = "INVESTIGATOR", text = "INVESTIGATOR", source = "credits", price = 80 },
	plate_supporter = { slot = "nameplate", label = "SUPPORTER", text = "SUPPORTER", source = "pass", pass = "Supporter" },
	plate_archivist = { slot = "nameplate", label = "ARCHIVIST", text = "ARCHIVIST", source = "challenge", challenge = "C01_EVIDENCE" },
}

-- Desk decorations for Personal Quarters (field office desks).
Cosmetics.Decor = {
	decor_poster = { label = "Station Poster", source = "pass", pass = "Quarters" },
	decor_lamp = { label = "Desk Lamp", source = "pass", pass = "Quarters" },
	decor_shelf = { label = "Tape Display Shelf", source = "pass", pass = "Quarters" },
	decor_plant = { label = "Office Plant", source = "product", product = "LobbyBundle" },
	decor_radio = { label = "Old Radio", source = "product", product = "LobbyBundle" },
	decor_tv = { label = "Portable TV", source = "product", product = "LobbyBundle" },
}

Cosmetics.Defaults = {
	uniform = "uniform_standard",
	camcorder = "cam_standard",
	rec = "rec_standard",
	menu = "menu_green",
	tapeLabel = "label_plain",
	nameplate = "plate_none",
}

-- Loadout presets: one free, more with passes.
Cosmetics.BasePresetSlots = 1
Cosmetics.PresetSlotsPerPass = { Locker = 2, ExtraPresets = 3 }

-- Whether a player owns an item, given owned flags, owned passes and challenges.
function Cosmetics.owns(itemId: string, profile, passes)
	local item = Cosmetics.Items[itemId] or Cosmetics.Decor[itemId]
	if not item then
		return false
	end
	if item.source == "default" then
		return true
	elseif item.source == "pass" then
		return passes and passes[item.pass] == true
	elseif item.source == "challenge" then
		return profile.challenges and profile.challenges[item.challenge] == true
	end
	return profile.cosmetics and profile.cosmetics.owned and profile.cosmetics.owned[itemId] == true
end

function Cosmetics.presetSlots(passes)
	local slots = Cosmetics.BasePresetSlots
	for pass, extra in Cosmetics.PresetSlotsPerPass do
		if passes and passes[pass] then
			slots += extra
		end
	end
	return slots
end

return Cosmetics
```

## src/shared/Config/Evidence.luau

```lua
--[[
	Evidence tapes and master recordings. Recovered entries are saved permanently
	and can be replayed from the Evidence Archive in the field office.

	Each transcript line is { t = seconds, speaker = "...", text = "..." }.
	Lines with `frame = true` are visual descriptions shown in the replay viewer.
]]

local Evidence = {}

Evidence.Tapes = {
	EV_ORIENTATION = {
		title = "STAFF ORIENTATION — 1996",
		label = "VHS · TRAINING",
		case = "case01",
		research = 10,
		transcript = {
			{ t = 0, frame = true, text = "Colour bars. A cheerful jingle, slightly slow." },
			{ t = 4, speaker = "NARRATOR", text = "Welcome to Station 04, the voice of the valley since 1971!" },
			{ t = 10, speaker = "NARRATOR", text = "Our Night Bulletin airs at midnight. After sign-off, the Time-Shift Deck is the engineer's responsibility." },
			{ t = 17, speaker = "NARRATOR", text = "Please do not adjust the Deck after sign-off. Please do not watch it for long." },
			{ t = 23, frame = true, text = "A staff photo. One face has been carefully scratched off the tape itself." },
			{ t = 28, speaker = "NARRATOR", text = "Remember: if a programme is running that isn't on the schedule, it is not your programme. Go home." },
		},
	},
	EV_ENGINEER = {
		title = "NIGHT ENGINEER LOG — M. HARROW",
		label = "VHS · HANDWRITTEN LABEL",
		case = "case01",
		research = 15,
		transcript = {
			{ t = 0, speaker = "HARROW", text = "Log, the fourteenth. The Deck is showing tomorrow's programme again." },
			{ t = 6, speaker = "HARROW", text = "I changed one thing. The light in the corridor. It worked. The corridor stayed lit." },
			{ t = 12, speaker = "HARROW", text = "But it didn't go away. It went somewhere else. It went to the room I was in." },
			{ t = 19, speaker = "HARROW", text = "It doesn't see the switches. It doesn't care about the switches. It sees the tape." },
			{ t = 25, frame = true, text = "Harrow looks past the camera for a long time." },
			{ t = 31, speaker = "HARROW", text = "If you are watching this, you have three changes. I used mine. Don't waste yours." },
		},
	},
	EV_WEATHER = {
		title = "WEATHER BULLETIN — 00:12",
		label = "BETACAM · AIRCHECK",
		case = "case01",
		research = 10,
		transcript = {
			{ t = 0, speaker = "FORECASTER", text = "Good evening. Tonight's weather for the valley." },
			{ t = 5, speaker = "FORECASTER", text = "A front arrives at seventeen minutes past midnight. You will not hear it arrive." },
			{ t = 11, frame = true, text = "The map behind her shows the station, not the valley." },
			{ t = 15, speaker = "FORECASTER", text = "Visibility will be poor. Stay where the lights are on — no. Sorry. That was yesterday's forecast." },
			{ t = 22, speaker = "FORECASTER", text = "Tomorrow: the same as tonight." },
		},
	},
	EV_TESTCARD = {
		title = "TEST CARD — PRESENTER",
		label = "U-MATIC · NO LABEL",
		case = "case01",
		research = 15,
		transcript = {
			{ t = 0, frame = true, text = "A test card. In the centre circle, where the girl and the chalkboard should be, a man in a suit." },
			{ t = 5, frame = true, text = "His face is a smaller television, showing the same test card." },
			{ t = 10, speaker = "CAPTION", text = "PLEASE STAND BY." },
			{ t = 15, speaker = "CAPTION", text = "YOUR PROGRAMME WILL CONTINUE WITH YOU." },
			{ t = 20, frame = true, text = "He has moved closer to the edge of the circle. The card itself has not changed." },
		},
	},
	EV_FINAL_BULLETIN = {
		title = "TAPE 07 — THE FINAL BULLETIN",
		label = "MASTER DUB · RED LABEL",
		case = "case01",
		research = 25,
		bonus = true,
		transcript = {
			{ t = 0, frame = true, text = "Studio A, lit for broadcast. The anchor desk is empty, then it is not." },
			{ t = 5, speaker = "THE PRESENTER", text = "Good evening. Here is tomorrow's news." },
			{ t = 10, speaker = "THE PRESENTER", text = "A recovery team will leave Station 04 by the freight door." },
			{ t = 15, speaker = "THE PRESENTER", text = "They will think they edited the programme. They were always in it." },
			{ t = 21, frame = true, text = "Behind the Presenter, a monitor shows the loading dock. The van's rear doors are open." },
			{ t = 26, speaker = "THE PRESENTER", text = "We will continue after this. We always continue after this." },
		},
	},
}

-- The campaign's seven master recordings. Only Case 01 ships in this build; the
-- rest are listed so the archive and case select show the campaign's shape.
Evidence.MasterRecordings = {
	{
		id = "MASTER_01",
		title = "MASTER 01 · SIGN-OFF",
		case = "case01",
		available = true,
		summary = "Station 04's last scheduled sign-off, 23:59, 1997. The Deck kept recording after the station went dark.",
		transcript = {
			{ t = 0, speaker = "ANNOUNCER", text = "This concludes tonight's programmes on Station 04." },
			{ t = 5, frame = true, text = "The sign-off slide holds. Then the station clock on the slide starts moving." },
			{ t = 10, speaker = "ANNOUNCER", text = "Programmes will resume at midnight." },
			{ t = 14, speaker = "ANNOUNCER", text = "Programmes have resumed." },
			{ t = 19, frame = true, text = "A list of broadcast dates scrolls past: every night since 1997. Tonight's date is near the end." },
			{ t = 25, speaker = "CAPTION", text = "SOURCE: TRANSMITTER 2 · LOCATION: NOT STATION 04" },
		},
	},
	{ id = "MASTER_02", title = "MASTER 02 · CLASSIFIED", available = false, summary = "Requires a future case." },
	{ id = "MASTER_03", title = "MASTER 03 · CLASSIFIED", available = false, summary = "Requires a future case." },
	{ id = "MASTER_04", title = "MASTER 04 · CLASSIFIED", available = false, summary = "Requires a future case." },
	{ id = "MASTER_05", title = "MASTER 05 · CLASSIFIED", available = false, summary = "Requires a future case." },
	{ id = "MASTER_06", title = "MASTER 06 · CLASSIFIED", available = false, summary = "Requires a future case." },
	{ id = "MASTER_07", title = "MASTER 07 · CLASSIFIED", available = false, summary = "Requires a future case." },
}

function Evidence.master(id: string)
	for _, master in Evidence.MasterRecordings do
		if master.id == id then
			return master
		end
	end
	return nil
end

return Evidence
```

## src/shared/Config/MapLayout.luau

```lua
--[[
	Broadcast Station 04 floor plan (plus the Division field office HQ).

	Everything is axis-aligned rectangles on a single floor (Y = 0) so that the map
	builder, the footage renderer and the game core can all reason about the same
	geometry. Coordinates are studs; +X is east and +Z is south.

	Rooms:      x0..x1, z0..z1 footprint, h = ceiling height.
	Doors:      a gap in the shared boundary between two rooms. `plane` names the
	            axis the boundary lies on ("z" = a wall running east-west at z = at).
	Windows:    glass panels in a shared boundary (the Presenter can see through them).
	Cameras:    CCTV positions used by tapes, monitors and footage.
]]

local Vec = require(script.Parent.Parent.Util.Vec)

local MapLayout = {}

local DOOR_HEIGHT = 9
MapLayout.DOOR_HEIGHT = DOOR_HEIGHT
MapLayout.WALL_THICKNESS = 1

-- Palette from the brief: muted green, dirty cream, warm grey, dusty brown and
-- faded industrial blue. Red is reserved for warnings.
MapLayout.Palette = {
	cream = { 196, 186, 158 },
	creamDark = { 150, 141, 117 },
	green = { 104, 122, 100 },
	greenDark = { 70, 84, 70 },
	grey = { 120, 116, 110 },
	greyDark = { 72, 70, 68 },
	brown = { 104, 86, 66 },
	brownDark = { 66, 54, 42 },
	blue = { 92, 108, 122 },
	blueDark = { 58, 68, 80 },
	concrete = { 110, 110, 104 },
	vinyl = { 128, 124, 104 },
	black = { 22, 24, 22 },
	warning = { 168, 52, 40 },
	amber = { 214, 150, 62 },
	fluorescent = { 222, 240, 226 },
}

--[[
	style: wall/floor colour keys and materials (Roblox Enum.Material names).
	lights: "grid" | "line" | "none" fluorescent layout.
	emergency: battery lights that work before the generator is restored.
	wing: which signal relay serves the room ("WEST", "EAST" or "CORE").
	refuge: a soundproof room - the Presenter cannot sense players inside while
	        its door is shut.
]]
MapLayout.Rooms = {
	Lobby = {
		label = "RECEPTION",
		x0 = -24, x1 = 24, z0 = 36, z1 = 72, h = 14,
		wall = "cream", trim = "brown", floor = "vinyl", floorMaterial = "Slate", wallMaterial = "Plaster",
		lights = "grid", emergency = true, wing = "CORE",
	},

		lights = "line", emergency = false, wing = "CORE",
	},

		lights = "grid", emergency = true, wing = "CORE",
	},

		lights = "line", emergency = false, wing = "WEST",
	},

	},

	},
	LoadingDock = {

		x0 = -30, x1 = 30, z0 = -140, z1 = -104, h = 0, outdoor = true,

		lights = "none", emergency = true, emergencyColor = "amber", wing = "CORE",
	},
	-- Division field office (pre-mission lobby), far south of the station.

}

--[[
	kind: "door" (hinged single), "double" (main entrance), "freight" (sliding shutter),
	      "opening" (no panel - just an archway).
	window: door panel has a glass vision panel.
	outside: the door leads off-map (only the main entrance).
]]
MapLayout.Doors = {

	{ id = "D_SH_CR", rooms = { "SouthHall", "ControlRoom" }, plane = "z", at = 20, center = 0, width = 5, kind = "door", window = true, label = "Control Room" },
	{ id = "D_Lobby_Staff", rooms = { "Lobby", "StaffPassage" }, plane = "x", at = -24, center = 54, width = 5, kind = "door", label = "Staff Only" },
	{ id = "A_Staff_Link", rooms = { "StaffPassage", "StaffLink" }, plane = "z", at = 50, center = -60, width = 8, kind = "opening" },
	{ id = "D_Link_WC", rooms = { "StaffLink", "WestCorridor" }, plane = "z", at = 6, center = -60, width = 5, kind = "door", label = "Staff Passage" },
	{ id = "D_CR_WC", rooms = { "ControlRoom", "WestCorridor" }, plane = "x", at = -20, center = 0, width = 5, kind = "door", window = true, label = "West Wing" },
	{ id = "D_WC_Gen", rooms = { "WestCorridor", "Generator" }, plane = "x", at = -100, center = 0, width = 6, kind = "door", label = "Generator Room" },
	{ id = "D_WC_ES", rooms = { "WestCorridor", "EditingSuite" }, plane = "z", at = -6, center = -78, width = 5, kind = "door", refuge = true, label = "Editing Suite" },
	{ id = "A_WC_SL", rooms = { "WestCorridor", "ServiceLink" }, plane = "z", at = -6, center = -36, width = 8, kind = "opening" },
	{ id = "A_SL_SVH", rooms = { "ServiceLink", "ServiceHall" }, plane = "z", at = -40, center = -36, width = 8, kind = "opening" },
	{ id = "D_SVH_Studio", rooms = { "ServiceHall", "StudioA" }, plane = "x", at = -40, center = -44, width = 5, kind = "door", label = "Studio A" },
	{ id = "D_CR_NH", rooms = { "ControlRoom", "NorthHall" }, plane = "z", at = -20, center = 0, width = 5, kind = "door", window = true, label = "North Hall" },
	{ id = "A_NH_SVH", rooms = { "NorthHall", "ServiceHall" }, plane = "z", at = -40, center = 0, width = 10, kind = "opening" },
	{ id = "A_SVH_TH", rooms = { "ServiceHall", "TransmitterHall" }, plane = "z", at = -48, center = 0, width = 10, kind = "opening" },
	{ id = "D_TH_Trans", rooms = { "TransmitterHall", "Transmitter" }, plane = "z", at = -56, center = 0, width = 6, kind = "door", label = "Transmitter Chamber" },
	{ id = "D_SVH_ML", rooms = { "ServiceHall", "MaintLink" }, plane = "z", at = -40, center = 26, width = 5, kind = "door", label = "Maintenance" },
	{ id = "A_ML_MP", rooms = { "MaintLink", "MaintPassage" }, plane = "z", at = -14, center = 26, width = 8, kind = "opening" },
	{ id = "D_MP_EC", rooms = { "MaintPassage", "EastCorridor" }, plane = "z", at = -6, center = 62, width = 4, kind = "door", label = "Crossover" },
	{ id = "D_MP_Arch", rooms = { "MaintPassage", "Archive" }, plane = "x", at = 104, center = -10, width = 4, kind = "door", label = "Archive (Rear)" },
	{ id = "D_CR_EC", rooms = { "ControlRoom", "EastCorridor" }, plane = "x", at = 20, center = 0, width = 5, kind = "door", window = true, label = "East Wing" },
	{ id = "D_EC_Arch", rooms = { "EastCorridor", "Archive" }, plane = "x", at = 104, center = 0, width = 6, kind = "door", window = true, label = "Tape Archive" },
	{ id = "D_Freight", rooms = { "Transmitter", "LoadingDock" }, plane = "z", at = -104, center = 0, width = 12, kind = "freight", label = "Freight Door" },
	-- HQ
	{ id = "D_HQ_Deploy", rooms = { "HQ" }, plane = "z", at = 284, center = 0, width = 8, kind = "double", outside = true, label = "Deployment Bay" },
}

-- Glass between the east corridor and the maintenance passage: the "silhouette
-- through glass" beat. Kept west of the crossover door on purpose (see Case 01).
MapLayout.Windows = {
	{ id = "W_EC_MP_1", rooms = { "EastCorridor", "MaintPassage" }, plane = "z", at = -6, center = 34, width = 6, y0 = 3.5, y1 = 7 },
	{ id = "W_EC_MP_2", rooms = { "EastCorridor", "MaintPassage" }, plane = "z", at = -6, center = 46, width = 6, y0 = 3.5, y1 = 7 },
}

local function P(roomId: string, u: number, v: number, y: number?)
	local room = MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
	assert(room, "Unknown room " .. tostring(roomId))
	return Vec.new(room.x0 + (room.x1 - room.x0) * u, y or 0, room.z0 + (room.z1 - room.z0) * v)
end

-- Point inside a room from normalised coordinates: u runs west->east, v runs
-- north->south, y is height above the floor.
MapLayout.point = P

--[[
	CCTV cameras. `rooms` lists the rooms the camera can see (used to decide which
	actors appear on footage). `relay` is the signal relay the feed depends on;
	"LOCAL" cameras have their own battery and work before the generator is fixed;
	"MAINS" cameras need station power but no relay.
]]
MapLayout.Cameras = {
	CAM01 = { label = "CAM 01 · RECEPTION", pos = Vec.new(-22, 12.5, 70), look = Vec.new(6, 3, 42), rooms = { "Lobby" }, relay = "LOCAL" },
	CAM02 = { label = "CAM 02 · SOUTH HALL", pos = Vec.new(4, 9.5, 35), look = Vec.new(0, 4, 21), rooms = { "SouthHall" }, relay = "LOCAL" },
	CAM03 = { label = "CAM 03 · WEST CORRIDOR", pos = Vec.new(-22, 9.5, 5), look = Vec.new(-98, 3, 0), rooms = { "WestCorridor" }, relay = "WEST" },
	CAM04 = { label = "CAM 04 · GENERATOR", pos = Vec.new(-102, 12, 18), look = Vec.new(-125, 2, -10), rooms = { "Generator" }, relay = "WEST" },
	CAM05 = { label = "CAM 05 · EDITING SUITE", pos = Vec.new(-68, 9.5, -8), look = Vec.new(-85, 3, -30), rooms = { "EditingSuite" }, relay = "WEST" },
	CAM06 = { label = "CAM 06 · EAST CORRIDOR", pos = Vec.new(22, 9.5, 5), look = Vec.new(100, 3, 0), rooms = { "EastCorridor" }, relay = "EAST" },
	CAM07 = { label = "CAM 07 · MAINTENANCE", pos = Vec.new(24, 7.5, -7), look = Vec.new(100, 2, -12), rooms = { "MaintPassage" }, relay = "EAST" },
	CAM08 = { label = "CAM 08 · TAPE ARCHIVE", pos = Vec.new(106, 10.5, 22), look = Vec.new(130, 2, -15), rooms = { "Archive" }, relay = "EAST" },
	CAM09 = { label = "CAM 09 · CONTROL ROOM", pos = Vec.new(-18, 11, 18), look = Vec.new(10, 2, -12), rooms = { "ControlRoom" }, relay = "LOCAL" },
	CAM10 = { label = "CAM 10 · SERVICE HALL", pos = Vec.new(-38, 8.5, -41), look = Vec.new(28, 2, -46), rooms = { "ServiceHall" }, relay = "WEST" },
	CAM11 = { label = "CAM 11 · TRANSMITTER", pos = Vec.new(30, 22, -58), look = Vec.new(-10, 4, -95), rooms = { "Transmitter", "TransmitterHall" }, relay = "MAINS" },
	CAM12 = { label = "CAM 12 · LOADING DOCK", pos = Vec.new(28, 10, -106), look = Vec.new(-10, 1, -135), rooms = { "LoadingDock" }, relay = "MAINS" },
	CAM13 = { label = "CAM 13 · STUDIO A", pos = Vec.new(-42, 13, -42), look = Vec.new(-65, 3, -68), rooms = { "StudioA" }, relay = "WEST" },
}

MapLayout.CameraOrder = {
	"CAM01", "CAM02", "CAM03", "CAM04", "CAM05", "CAM06", "CAM07",
	"CAM08", "CAM09", "CAM10", "CAM11", "CAM12", "CAM13",
}

MapLayout.Relays = {
	WEST = { label = "WEST RELAY", room = "Generator", pos = P("Generator", 0.92, 0.12, 0) },
	EAST = { label = "EAST RELAY", room = "MaintPassage", pos = P("MaintPassage", 0.9, 0.12, 0) },
}

MapLayout.HQSpawn = P("HQ", 0.5, 0.45, 0)

-- Returns the room containing a point, preferring the smallest match so that
-- doorway overlaps resolve to the corridor rather than the big room.
function MapLayout.roomAt(x: number, z: number, margin: number?): string?
	margin = margin or 0
	local best, bestArea = nil, math.huge
	for id, room in MapLayout.Rooms do
		if x >= room.x0 - margin and x <= room.x1 + margin and z >= room.z0 - margin and z <= room.z1 + margin then
			local area = (room.x1 - room.x0) * (room.z1 - room.z0)
			if area < bestArea then
				best, bestArea = id, area
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end

function MapLayout.inRooms(pos, rooms: { string }, margin: number?): boolean
	margin = margin or 0
	for _, id in rooms do
		local room = MapLayout.Rooms[id]
		if room and pos.x >= room.x0 - margin and pos.x <= room.x1 + margin and pos.z >= room.z0 - margin and pos.z <= room.z1 + margin then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function MapLayout.roomCenter(roomId: string)
	return P(roomId, 0.5, 0.5, 0)
end

function MapLayout.doorById(doorId: string)
	for _, door in MapLayout.Doors do
		if door.id == doorId then
			return door
		end
	end
	return nil
end

-- World-space centre of a door gap at floor level.
function MapLayout.doorPosition(door)
	if door.plane == "z" then
		return Vec.new(door.center, 0, door.at)
	end
	return Vec.new(door.at, 0, door.center)
end

return MapLayout
```

## src/shared/Config/Monetisation.luau

```lua
--[[
	Gamepasses and developer products.

	IDs are 0 until you create the passes/products in the Creator Dashboard
	(Monetization > Passes / Developer Products) and paste their IDs here.
	Anything with id = 0 is hidden in the store and can never be prompted.

	Prices are the brief's starting hypotheses (Robux) for you to test; the price
	actually charged is whatever you set on the dashboard.

	Rules enforced in code (MarketService):
	  * The game is completable without spending anything.
	  * No paid invulnerability, unlimited edits, guaranteed escape, ending skip
	    or exclusive story clues.
	  * Re-entry is at most once per player per case, at a safe checkpoint, with
	    a short delay; it never restores spent edit charges.
	  * Purchases are granted server-side from ProcessReceipt, once per receipt.
]]

local Monetisation = {}

Monetisation.Passes = {
	Locker = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Investigator's Locker",
		priceHint = "79-129 Robux",
		description = "Two extra loadout preset slots and the Locker Reserve uniform. No gameplay advantage.",
	},
	ClassicPack = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Classic Broadcast Pack",
		priceHint = "99-149 Robux",
		description = "Extra CRT menu themes, the Classic camcorder shell and the Classic REC overlay. All information stays equally readable.",
	},
	Quarters = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Personal Quarters",
		priceHint = "79-149 Robux",
		description = "Decorate your desk in the field office with a poster, lamp and tape display shelf.",
	},
	Supporter = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Supporter Pass",
		priceHint = "149-249 Robux",
		description = "Supporter nameplate and jacket. No story clues or survival tools.",
	},
	ExtraPresets = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Extra Loadout Presets",
		priceHint = "49-79 Robux",
		description = "Three more loadout preset slots.",
	},
}
Monetisation.PassOrder = { "Locker", "ClassicPack", "Quarters", "Supporter", "ExtraPresets" }

Monetisation.Products = {
	Revive = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Emergency Re-entry",
		priceHint = "15-29 Robux",
		description = "Return once this case at a safe checkpoint after a short delay. Does not restore spent edit charges or skip objectives. If the case has ended, it is kept for your next case.",
	},
	TeamToken = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Team Recovery",
		priceHint = "39-59 Robux",
		description = "Brings back every eliminated teammate at a checkpoint. Needs the team to agree. After a team recovery, no more re-entries can be bought this case.",
	},
	EvidenceBundle = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Evidence Label Bundle",
		priceHint = "19-49 Robux",
		description = "Red and blue tape label skins for your Evidence Archive. No lore.",
	},
	LobbyBundle = {
		id = 0,
		name = "Office Decoration Bundle",
		priceHint = "29-79 Robux",
		description = "A plant, an old radio and a portable TV for your field office desk.",
	},
}
Monetisation.ProductOrder = { "Revive", "TeamToken", "EvidenceBundle", "LobbyBundle" }

function Monetisation.productByAssetId(assetId: number)
	for key, product in Monetisation.Products do
		if product.id ~= 0 and product.id == assetId then
			return key, product
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function Monetisation.passByAssetId(assetId: number)
	for key, pass in Monetisation.Passes do
		if pass.id ~= 0 and pass.id == assetId then
			return key, pass
		end
	end
	return nil
end

return Monetisation
```

## src/shared/Config/Surfaces.luau

```lua
--[[
	Surfaces — what every part in the world is made of.

	Builders ask for a surface by what it is ("paintedMetal", "lino",
	"vinylCasing") instead of picking a Roblox material directly. Each surface
	maps to one of Roblox's physically based materials (wood grain, brushed and
	painted metal, plaster, carpet, ceramic tile, leather, rubber, fabric and so
	on - real textures with normal and roughness maps, rendered by the engine),
	plus a small amount of colour variation so a row of identical props does
	not look stamped out.

	Photographic textures: Roblox cannot load image files from this repository,
	so the station ships with the built-in materials. To go further, upload PBR
	texture sets (colour, normal, roughness, metalness) to Roblox and paste their
	asset ids into Surfaces.Variants below. Every surface that names a variant
	then uses it automatically (the server installs the MaterialVariants into
	MaterialService at start-up); empty entries are skipped.
]]

local Surfaces = {}

--[[
	material: Roblox material name
	vary:     +/- fraction of colour variation between parts (0 = exact)
	variant:  optional key into Surfaces.Variants
]]
Surfaces.List = {
	-- Building fabric
	plaster = { material = "Plaster", vary = 0.015, variant = "DeadAir_PaintedPlaster" },
	concrete = { material = "Concrete", vary = 0.03, variant = "DeadAir_Concrete" },
	breezeBlock = { material = "Brick", vary = 0.02 },
	ceilingTile = { material = "Plaster", vary = 0.02, variant = "DeadAir_CeilingTile" },
	lino = { material = "CeramicTiles", vary = 0.02, variant = "DeadAir_Linoleum" },
	carpet = { material = "Carpet", vary = 0.015, variant = "DeadAir_Carpet" },
	checkerPlate = { material = "DiamondPlate", vary = 0.03 },
	acousticFoam = { material = "Fabric", vary = 0.02 },
	panelling = { material = "Wood", vary = 0.03, variant = "DeadAir_Veneer" },
	asphalt = { material = "Asphalt", vary = 0.02 },
	ground = { material = "Ground", vary = 0.03 },

	-- Furniture and fittings
	veneer = { material = "Wood", vary = 0.05, variant = "DeadAir_Veneer" },
	planks = { material = "WoodPlanks", vary = 0.06 },
	upholstery = { material = "Fabric", vary = 0.04 },
	leather = { material = "Leather", vary = 0.05 },
	cardboard = { material = "Cardboard", vary = 0.06 },
	paper = { material = "Cardboard", vary = 0.04 },
	pottery = { material = "Slate", vary = 0.05 },
	foliage = { material = "LeafyGrass", vary = 0.06 },

	-- Metals
	paintedMetal = { material = "Metal", vary = 0.03, variant = "DeadAir_PaintedSteel" },
	bareMetal = { material = "Metal", vary = 0.02 },
	rust = { material = "CorrodedMetal", vary = 0.05 },
	foil = { material = "Foil", vary = 0.02 },
	mesh = { material = "DiamondPlate", vary = 0.02 },

	-- Equipment
	vinylCasing = { material = "Plastic", vary = 0.03 }, -- beige electronics casings
	blackPlastic = { material = "Plastic", vary = 0.02 },
	rubber = { material = "Rubber", vary = 0.02 },
	glass = { material = "Glass", vary = 0 },
	screen = { material = "Glass", vary = 0 },
	glow = { material = "Neon", vary = 0 },
	enamel = { material = "SmoothPlastic", vary = 0.01 }, -- signs, painted lettering
}

--[[
	Optional photographic texture sets (MaterialVariants). Paste uploaded asset
	ids ("rbxassetid://123...") to enable one. base is the Roblox material it
	overrides on the parts that ask for it.
]]
Surfaces.Variants = {
	DeadAir_PaintedPlaster = { base = "Plaster", studsPerTile = 10, colorMap = "", normalMap = "", roughnessMap = "", metalnessMap = "" },
	DeadAir_Concrete = { base = "Concrete", studsPerTile = 12, colorMap = "", normalMap = "", roughnessMap = "", metalnessMap = "" },
	DeadAir_CeilingTile = { base = "Plaster", studsPerTile = 4, colorMap = "", normalMap = "", roughnessMap = "", metalnessMap = "" },
	DeadAir_Linoleum = { base = "CeramicTiles", studsPerTile = 6, colorMap = "", normalMap = "", roughnessMap = "", metalnessMap = "" },
	DeadAir_Carpet = { base = "Carpet", studsPerTile = 6, colorMap = "", normalMap = "", roughnessMap = "", metalnessMap = "" },
	DeadAir_Veneer = { base = "Wood", studsPerTile = 6, colorMap = "", normalMap = "", roughnessMap = "", metalnessMap = "" },
	DeadAir_PaintedSteel = { base = "Metal", studsPerTile = 6, colorMap = "", normalMap = "", roughnessMap = "", metalnessMap = "" },
}

function Surfaces.get(name)
	local s = Surfaces.List[name]
	assert(s, "Unknown surface " .. tostring(name))
	return s
end

function Surfaces.material(name)
	return Enum.Material[Surfaces.get(name).material]
end

-- The variant name to use for a surface, or nil when none is configured.
function Surfaces.variantFor(name)
	local s = Surfaces.List[name]
	local v = s and s.variant and Surfaces.Variants[s.variant]
	if v and v.colorMap ~= "" then
		return s.variant
	end
	return nil
end

-- Deterministic small colour variation from a position, so the same prop
-- always looks the same on every server and in every footage clone.
function Surfaces.vary(colour, amount, position)
	if amount <= 0 or not position then
		return colour
	end
	local h = math.abs(math.sin(position.X * 12.9898 + position.Y * 78.233 + position.Z * 37.719) * 43758.5453) % 1
	local k = 1 + (h * 2 - 1) * amount
	return Color3.new(math.clamp(colour.R * k, 0, 1), math.clamp(colour.G * k, 0, 1), math.clamp(colour.B * k, 0, 1))
end

-- Installs configured MaterialVariants (server, once at start-up).
function Surfaces.install(materialService)
	local count = 0
	for name, v in Surfaces.Variants do
		if v.colorMap ~= "" and not materialService:FindFirstChild(name) then
			local variant = Instance.new("MaterialVariant")
			variant.Name = name
			variant.BaseMaterial = Enum.Material[v.base]
			variant.StudsPerTile = v.studsPerTile
			variant.ColorMap = v.colorMap
			variant.NormalMap = v.normalMap
			variant.RoughnessMap = v.roughnessMap
			variant.MetalnessMap = v.metalnessMap
			variant.Parent = materialService
			count += 1
		end
	end
	return count
end

return Surfaces
```

## src/shared/Config/Typeface.luau

```lua
--[[
	The one typeface used for every piece of text in the game: menus, HUD,
	subtitles, the deck, CCTV overlays, signs on the map and nameplates.
	Roboto Mono reads like broadcast test equipment and stays legible at small
	sizes; weights give hierarchy without a second family.
]]

local Typeface = {}

Typeface.Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/RobotoMono.json"

function Typeface.regular()
	return Font.new(Typeface.Family, Enum.FontWeight.Regular)
end

function Typeface.medium()
	return Font.new(Typeface.Family, Enum.FontWeight.Medium)
end

function Typeface.bold()
	return Font.new(Typeface.Family, Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
end

return Typeface
```

## src/shared/Config/Cases/Case01.luau

```lua
--[[
	CASE 01 · THE 00:17 TRANSMISSION
	Broadcast Station 04

	The whole case is data. The timeline, the Presenter, footage on every tape
	deck and the real world are all driven from these definitions, so what a tape
	shows is exactly what the server will do unless the team edits it.

	Event chain overview (4 editable events, 3 charges in Standard mode):

	TAPE 01  lobby_lights (02:30)        OFF -> presenter_entry LOBBY      (it walks in through the main doors)
	                                     ON  -> presenter_entry SOUTH_HALL (it waits outside the control room)
	TAPE 02  east_corridor_lights (+1:30 after power)
	                                     OFF -> presenter_route_east EAST_CORRIDOR (dark corridor patrol, east relay trips)
	                                     ON  -> presenter_route_east CONTROL_ROOM  (the hub becomes unsafe)
	TAPE 03  presenter_ambush (+0:20 after the sync board is taken)
	                                     ARCHIVE       -> it hunts inside the archive (two exits)
	                                     EAST_CORRIDOR -> it blocks the corridor... and transmitter_watch CHAMBER later
	         control_room_rewind (+1:15) fixed: the control room replays itself; north door relocks for 30 s
	TAPE 04  freight_door (+0:30 after the transmitter is armed)
	                                     JAMMED -> climax_route CHAMBER_HUNT (restart the motor while it hunts)
	                                     OPEN   -> climax_route DOCK_PATROL  (door opens, it patrols the dock)
	         transmitter_watch (+0:03)   derived from presenter_ambush
]]

local MapLayout = require(script.Parent.Parent.MapLayout)
local P = MapLayout.point

local Case01 = {}

Case01.id = "case01"
Case01.number = 1
Case01.title = "THE 00:17 TRANSMISSION"
Case01.location = "BROADCAST STATION 04"
Case01.summary = "A transmission failed at 00:17 in a station that has been empty for nineteen years. Recover the components, shut down tonight's broadcast and get out."
Case01.mapId = "Station04"
Case01.masterRecording = "MASTER_01"
Case01.timeLimit = 30 * 60
Case01.maxParticipants = 4

Case01.modes = {
	Standard = { charges = 3, awarenessScale = 1, huntSpeed = 14.5, showClues = true, label = "STANDARD" },
	Restricted = { charges = 2, awarenessScale = 1.35, huntSpeed = 15.5, showClues = false, label = "RESTRICTED", requiresClearance = 2 },
	ArchiveChallenge = { charges = 3, awarenessScale = 1.15, huntSpeed = 15, showClues = true, label = "ARCHIVE CHALLENGE", fixedSeed = true, requiresClearance = 2 },
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Briefing (played in the reception before the broadcast starts)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Case01.briefing = {
	duration = 40,
	lines = {
		{ at = 0, text = "Recovery team, this is Division. You are inside Broadcast Station 04." },
		{ at = 6, text = "The station transmits at 00:00 every night. Tonight's programme is about you." },
		{ at = 12, text = "Recover three transmission components and install them in the transmitter. Then leave by the freight door." },
		{ at = 19, text = "The tapes show what happens next. You can change a few conditions on them. Each change costs a charge, and something else moves to make room." },
		{ at = 27, text = "Division only speaks on Channel 2, and we stamp every message with the live tape clock. Anything else is not us." },
		{ at = 35, text = "Broadcast begins now. Doors are sealing." },
	},
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Decks: places where tapes can be inspected and edited.
-- battery decks work before the generator is restored.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Case01.decks = {
	DECK_LOBBY = { label = "RECEPTION VCR", room = "Lobby", pos = P("Lobby", 0.6, 0.3, 3.6), battery = true },
	DECK_EDIT = { label = "EDIT BAY DECK", room = "EditingSuite", pos = P("EditingSuite", 0.5, 0.16, 3.6), battery = true },
	DECK_MAIN = { label = "TIME-SHIFT DECK", room = "ControlRoom", pos = P("ControlRoom", 0.5, 0.25, 3.6), battery = false },
	DECK_TRANS = { label = "MASTER CONTROL", room = "Transmitter", pos = P("Transmitter", 0.18, 0.75, 3.6), battery = false },
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Tapes. Each tape's clock is relative to its anchor flag.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Case01.tapes = {
	TAPE01 = {
		label = "TAPE 01",
		title = "RECEPTION / 00:00",
		anchor = "broadcast_start",
		duration = 210,
		cameras = { "CAM01", "CAM02" },
		colour = { 196, 186, 158 },
		baseLights = { CAM01 = "emergency", CAM02 = "off" },
		-- Footage-only figures: the team as the tape "remembers" them.
		actors = {
			CAM01 = {
				{ from = 0, to = 48, pos = P("Lobby", 0.42, 0.66), look = P("Lobby", 0.5, 0.4) },
				{ from = 0, to = 48, pos = P("Lobby", 0.47, 0.68), look = P("Lobby", 0.5, 0.4) },
				{ from = 0, to = 48, pos = P("Lobby", 0.53, 0.68), look = P("Lobby", 0.5, 0.4) },
				-- One of them faces the wall while the real team faces the desk.
				{ from = 0, to = 48, pos = P("Lobby", 0.58, 0.66), look = P("Lobby", 1, 0.66), wrong = true },
			},
		},
	},
	TAPE02 = {
		label = "TAPE 02",
		title = "B-ROLL / POWER",
		anchor = "generator_restored",
		duration = 260,
		cameras = { "CAM06", "CAM07", "CAM09" },
		colour = { 104, 122, 100 },
		baseLights = { CAM06 = "on", CAM07 = "on", CAM09 = "on" },
		actors = {},
	},
	TAPE03 = {
		label = "TAPE 03",
		title = "ARCHIVE / DUB 3",
		anchor = "board_recovered",
		duration = 140,
		cameras = { "CAM08", "CAM06", "CAM07", "CAM09" },
		colour = { 104, 86, 66 },
		baseLights = { CAM08 = "on", CAM06 = "on", CAM07 = "on", CAM09 = "on" },
		actors = {},
	},
	TAPE04 = {
		label = "TAPE 04",
		title = "TRANSMITTER / FINAL",
		anchor = "transmitter_armed",
		duration = 120,
		cameras = { "CAM11", "CAM12" },
		colour = { 92, 108, 122 },
		baseLights = { CAM11 = "on", CAM12 = "emergency" },
		actors = {},
	},
}
Case01.tapeOrder = { "TAPE01", "TAPE02", "TAPE03", "TAPE04" }

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Timeline events
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
local LOBBY_SIGHT = { range = 40, dark = 24, fov = 120, proximity = 7 }

-- Radio script. stamp: "live" (correct broadcast clock) or a number of

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Case01.radio = {
	{ anchor = "broadcast_start", offset = 5, source = "HQ", text = "TAPE 01 is on the reception desk. Play it on the reception VCR." },
	{ anchor = "broadcast_start", offset = 70, source = "CALLER", stamp = -600, text = "Division here. Stay in the reception. The lights will hold." },
	{ anchor = "generator_restored", offset = 3, source = "HQ", text = "Power's up. The modulator valve is in the generator room parts cage. Cage code {code}." },
	{ anchor = "generator_restored", offset = 14, source = "CALLER", stamp = 420, text = "Correction: the cage code is {fakeCode}. Then wait in the east corridor." },
	{ anchor = "generator_restored", offset = 26, source = "HQ", text = "Carrier coil should be in the editing suite. The sync board is in the tape archive, east wing. TAPE 03 just ejected in the control room." },
	{ anchor = "loop_active", offset = 4, source = "HQ", text = "You're in a loop. Watch two cycles. One thing changes. Touch it." },
	{ anchor = "board_recovered", offset = 3, source = "CALLER", stamp = -300, text = "Stay in the archive. It cannot reach you in there." },
	{ anchor = "board_recovered", offset = 5, source = "HQ", text = "Get out of the archive. Use whichever door the tape shows clear." },
	{ anchor = "components_complete", offset = 2, source = "HQ", text = "That's all three components. Transmitter chamber is north, past the service hall." },
	{ anchor = "transmitter_armed", offset = 2, source = "HQ", text = "Transmitter is live. The freight door should cycle in thirty seconds." },
	{ anchor = "transmitter_armed", offset = 9, source = "CALLER", stamp = 900, text = "Extraction is cancelled. Stay exactly where you are." },
	{ anchor = "extraction_open", offset = 1, source = "HQ", text = "Freight door is open. Get to the van." },
	{ anchor = "extraction_open", offset = 7, source = "HQ", text = "Studio A just unlocked by itself. Whatever is in there is optional. Your call." },
}

	{ id = "C01_CALLER", label = "Wrong Number", text = "Never enter the Caller's code." },
	{ id = "C01_FRAME", label = "Frame by Frame", text = "Mark a hidden frame during a case." },
	{ id = "C01_SOLO", label = "Lone Signal", text = "Complete the case alone." },
	{ id = "C01_RESTRICTED", label = "Restricted Clearance", text = "Complete the case in Restricted mode." },
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Seeded variants.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Case01.variants = {
	-- When true for a seed, the east-corridor patrol starts at the control room end.
	patrolFlipEvent = "presenter_route_east",
}

return Case01
```

## src/shared/Core/Footage.luau

```lua
--[[
	Footage — what a camera on a tape shows at tape time t.

	Footage is not a recording of the 3D world. It is computed from the same event
	definitions and timeline state the server uses, so a tape always predicts what
	the server will actually do (until someone edits it).
]]

local MapLayout = require(script.Parent.Parent.Config.MapLayout)
local PresenterBrain = require(script.Parent.PresenterBrain)
local Vec = require(script.Parent.Parent.Util.Vec)

local Footage = {}

local function eventsOnTape(caseDef, eventDefs, tapeId)
	local list = {}
	for _, base in caseDef.events do
		local ev = eventDefs[base.id] or base
		if ev.tape == tapeId then
			table.insert(list, ev)
		end
	end
	return list
end
Footage.eventsOnTape = eventsOnTape

local function stateOf(snapshot, ev)
	local rec = snapshot and snapshot.events and snapshot.events[ev.id]
	return (rec and rec.state) or ev.originalState
end

-- Light mode for a camera at tape time t: "on", "off" or "emergency".
function Footage.lightsAt(caseDef, eventDefs, snapshot, tapeId, cameraId, t)
	local tape = caseDef.tapes[tapeId]
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	local mode = (tape.baseLights and tape.baseLights[cameraId]) or "on"
	local latest = -math.huge
	for _, ev in eventsOnTape(caseDef, eventDefs, tapeId) do
		local stateDef = ev.states[stateOf(snapshot, ev)]
		for _, action in (stateDef and stateDef.actions) or {} do
			if action.type == "Lights" and table.find(camera.rooms, action.room) then
				local start = ev.offset + (action.at or 0)
				local stop = action.duration and (start + action.duration) or math.huge
				if t >= start and t < stop and start >= latest then
					mode = action.mode
					latest = start
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return mode
end

-- Presenter position (if any) predicted by an event at tape time t.
local function presenterAt(ev, stateDef, t)
	local cmd = stateDef and stateDef.presenter
	if not cmd then
		return nil
	end
	local start = ev.offset + (cmd.at or 0)
	local elapsed = t - start
	if elapsed < 0 then
		return nil
	end
	if cmd.kind == "Displaced" then
		if elapsed > cmd.duration then
			return nil
		end
		local pos, _, look, index = PresenterBrain.pathPosition(cmd.path, elapsed)
		if not look then
			local nextKey = cmd.path[math.min(index + 1, #cmd.path)]
			if nextKey and Vec.flatDistance(nextKey.pos, pos) > 0.1 then
				look = nextKey.pos
			end
		end
		return pos, look
	elseif cmd.kind == "Ambush" then
		-- Footage shows the arrival and the wind-up; what follows depends on you.
		if elapsed > (cmd.windup or 2.5) + 2 then
			return nil
		end
		return cmd.pos, cmd.look
	elseif cmd.kind == "Rewind" or cmd.kind == "Observe" then
		if elapsed > (cmd.duration or 8) then
			return nil
		end
		return cmd.pos, cmd.look
	end
	return nil
end

--[[
	Returns {
		lights = "on" | "off" | "emergency",
		actors = { { kind = "Presenter" | "Investigator", pos, look, wrong, eventId } },
		hiddenFrame = { id, text } | nil,
	}
]]
function Footage.frame(caseDef, eventDefs, snapshot, tapeId, cameraId, t)
	local tape = caseDef.tapes[tapeId]
	local camera = MapLayout.Cameras[cameraId]
	local frame = {
		lights = Footage.lightsAt(caseDef, eventDefs, snapshot, tapeId, cameraId, t),
		actors = {},
		hiddenFrame = nil,
	}
	for _, ev in eventsOnTape(caseDef, eventDefs, tapeId) do
		local pos, look = presenterAt(ev, ev.states[stateOf(snapshot, ev)], t)
		if pos and MapLayout.inRooms(pos, camera.rooms, 1.5) then
			table.insert(frame.actors, { kind = "Presenter", pos = pos, look = look, eventId = ev.id })
		end
	end
	for _, actor in (tape.actors and tape.actors[cameraId]) or {} do
		if t >= actor.from and t < actor.to then
			table.insert(frame.actors, {
				kind = "Investigator",
				pos = actor.pos,
				look = actor.look,
				wrong = actor.wrong == true,
			})
		end
	end
	for _, hf in caseDef.hiddenFrames or {} do
		if hf.tape == tapeId and hf.camera == cameraId and math.abs(t - hf.t) <= (hf.window or 0.5) then
			frame.hiddenFrame = { id = hf.id, text = hf.text }
			if hf.id == "HF_T01_FIFTH" then
				-- The extra figure stands behind the group.
				table.insert(frame.actors, {
```
