```lua
					kind = "Investigator",
					pos = MapLayout.point("Lobby", 0.5, 0.8),
					look = MapLayout.point("Lobby", 0.5, 0.4),
					wrong = true,
				})
			end
		end
	end
	return frame
end

-- Event markers for the scrubber.
function Footage.markers(caseDef, eventDefs, snapshot, tapeId)
	local list = {}
	for _, ev in eventsOnTape(caseDef, eventDefs, tapeId) do
		local rec = snapshot and snapshot.events and snapshot.events[ev.id]
		table.insert(list, {
			eventId = ev.id,
			t = ev.offset,
			label = ev.label,
			editable = ev.isEditable == true,
			state = (rec and rec.state) or ev.originalState,
			fired = rec and rec.fired or false,
			locked = rec and rec.locked or false,
			committed = rec and rec.committed or nil,
			cameras = ev.cameras,
		})
	end
	table.sort(list, function(a, b)
		return a.t < b.t
	end)
	return list
end

-- Which cameras on the tape show a given event.
function Footage.camerasFor(caseDef, eventId)
	for _, ev in caseDef.events do
		if ev.id == eventId then
			return ev.cameras or {}
		end
	end
	return {}
end

return Footage
```

## src/shared/Core/Grading.luau

```lua
--[[
	Case grading and rewards. Grades reward survival, evidence, correct
	predictions and smart use of edits - not speed.
]]

local Grading = {}

Grading.Thresholds = {
	{ grade = "S", min = 90 },
	{ grade = "A", min = 75 },
	{ grade = "B", min = 60 },
	{ grade = "C", min = 40 },
	{ grade = "D", min = 0 },
}

local RESULT_POINTS = { Success = 35, Partial = 18, Failed = 0 }

local function ratio(a: number, b: number): number
	if b <= 0 then
		return 0
	end
	return math.clamp(a / b, 0, 1)
end

--[[
	stats = {
		result = "Success" | "Partial" | "Failed",
		participants, survivors,
		evidenceFound, evidenceTotal,
		predictionsInterpreted, predictionsTotal,
		editsMade, maxCharges, eliminationsDuringEdits,
		loopFirstTry, callerFooled,
	}
]]
function Grading.compute(stats)
	local breakdown = {}
	breakdown.result = RESULT_POINTS[stats.result] or 0
	breakdown.survival = 20 * ratio(stats.survivors or 0, stats.participants or 0)
	breakdown.evidence = 15 * ratio(stats.evidenceFound or 0, stats.evidenceTotal or 0)
	breakdown.predictions = 15 * ratio(stats.predictionsInterpreted or 0, stats.predictionsTotal or 0)
	local unused = math.max(0, (stats.maxCharges or 0) - (stats.editsMade or 0))
	local edits = 5 * ratio(unused, stats.maxCharges or 0)
	if (stats.editsMade or 0) > 0 and (stats.eliminationsDuringEdits or 0) == 0 then
		edits += 5
	end
	breakdown.edits = edits
	breakdown.anomalies = (stats.loopFirstTry and 5 or 0) + (stats.callerFooled and 0 or 5)

	local score = 0
	for _, v in breakdown do
		score += v
	end
	score = math.clamp(math.floor(score + 0.5), 0, 100)

	local grade = "D"
	for _, t in Grading.Thresholds do
		if score >= t.min then
			grade = t.grade
			break
		end
	end
	-- A failed case never grades above D.
	if stats.result == "Failed" then
		grade = "D"
	end
	return { score = score, grade = grade, breakdown = breakdown }
end

local BASE_CREDITS = { Success = 120, Partial = 70, Failed = 20 }
local GRADE_BONUS = { S = 80, A = 50, B = 30, C = 10, D = 0 }

--[[
	personal = { itemsRecovered, evidenceRecovered, framesMarked, extracted }
	team     = { result, grade, anomaliesIdentified, bonusTapeExtracted }
]]
function Grading.rewards(team, personal)
	local credits = (BASE_CREDITS[team.result] or 0) + (GRADE_BONUS[team.grade] or 0)
	local contribution = (personal.itemsRecovered or 0) * 10 + (personal.evidenceRecovered or 0) * 10
	credits += math.min(contribution, 60)
	if team.result ~= "Failed" and not personal.extracted then
		-- Left behind or eliminated: still paid, but less.
		credits = math.floor(credits * 0.6)
	end
	local research = (team.anomaliesIdentified or 0) * 10
		+ (personal.framesMarked or 0) * 15
		+ (personal.evidenceRecovered or 0) * 10
	if team.bonusTapeExtracted then
		research += 25
	end
	return { credits = credits, research = research }
end

return Grading
```

## src/shared/Core/MissionCore.luau

```lua
--[[
	MissionCore — one run of a case, independent of Roblox.

	Owns: mission phase, objectives, flags, team inventory, shared edit charges,
	the timeline, the Loop and Caller anomalies, doors/lights/relays state,
	Presenter awareness, eliminations/revives, extraction, scoring and the report.

	The server glue calls the action methods (interact, commitEdit, ...) after its
	own range/rate checks, calls update(now, world) every tick, applies whatever
	drainEffects() returns to the world, and replicates snapshot(now) to clients.

	Phases: Briefing -> Active -> ExtractionAvailable -> Completed | Failed (| Aborted)
]]

local TimelineCore = require(script.Parent.TimelineCore)
local Grading = require(script.Parent.Grading)
local Variants = require(script.Parent.Variants)
local Format = require(script.Parent.Parent.Util.Format)
local MapLayout = require(script.Parent.Parent.Config.MapLayout)

local MissionCore = {}
MissionCore.__index = MissionCore

local AWARENESS_DECAY = 0.6 -- per second
local AWARENESS_DECK = 1.2 -- per second per open deck
local AWARENESS_EDIT = 15
local AWARENESS_THRESHOLD = 100
local OBSERVE_COOLDOWN = 40
local FAIL_GRACE = 12
local REVIVE_INVULNERABILITY = 6

local ACTIVE_PHASES = { Active = true, ExtractionAvailable = true }
local PLAYABLE_PHASES = { Briefing = true, Active = true, ExtractionAvailable = true }

local function key(userId)
	return tostring(userId)
end

--[[
	opts = {
		mode = "Standard" | "Restricted" | "ArchiveChallenge",
		seed = number,
		now = number,
		participants = { { userId, name } },
	}
]]
function MissionCore.new(caseDef, opts)
	local self = setmetatable({}, MissionCore)
	self.case = caseDef
	self.mode = opts.mode or "Standard"
	self.modeDef = caseDef.modes[self.mode] or caseDef.modes.Standard
	self.seed = opts.seed or 1
	self.variants = Variants.compute(caseDef, self.seed)
	self.timeline = TimelineCore.new(caseDef, { patrolFlip = self.variants.patrolFlip })

	local now = opts.now or 0
	self.createdAt = now
	self.phase = "Briefing"
	self.briefingEnd = now + caseDef.briefing.duration
	self.briefingDone = {}
	self.broadcastStart = nil
	self.endedAt = nil

	self.flags = {}
	self.flagTimes = {}
	self.maxCharges = self.modeDef.charges
	self.charges = self.maxCharges
	self.inventory = {}
	self.installed = {}
	self.participants = {}
	self.participantOrder = {}

	self.awareness = 0
	self.lastNoiseUser = nil
	self.lastObserveAt = -math.huge
	self.deckUsers = {}

	self.generatorStage = 0
	self.relays = { WEST = true, EAST = true }
	self.doorOverrides = {}
	self.loopLocks = {}
	self.doorState = {}
	self.lightOverrides = {}
	self.lights = {}

	self.loop = { active = false, solved = false, cycleStart = 0, attempts = 0, odd = self.variants.loopOdd }
	self.keypad = { opened = false, lockoutUntil = 0, wrongAttempts = 0 }
	self.radioLog = {}
	self.radioDone = {}

	self.teamTokenUsed = false
	self.failPendingUntil = nil
	self.endReason = nil

	self.stats = {
		inspected = {},
		editsMade = 0,
		eliminations = 0,
		eliminationsDuringEdits = 0,
		callerFooled = false,
		framesMarked = {},
		evidenceFound = {},
		tapesFound = {},
		anomaliesIdentified = 0,
		loopFirstTry = false,
	}

	self.effects = {}
	self.version = 0
	self.report = nil

	self:_buildInteractables()
	for _, p in opts.participants or {} do
		self:addParticipant(p.userId, p.name, now)
	end
	self:_refreshDoors()
	self:_refreshLights(now)
	return self
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Internals
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:_emit(effect)
	table.insert(self.effects, effect)
end

function MissionCore:_dirty()
	self.version += 1
end

function MissionCore:drainEffects()
	local list = self.effects
	self.effects = {}
	return list
end

function MissionCore:broadcastClock(now)
	if not self.broadcastStart then
		return 0
	end
	return now - self.broadcastStart
end

function MissionCore:_notify(text, userId)
	self:_emit({ type = "Notify", text = text, userId = userId })
end

function MissionCore:_subtitle(text, kind, extra)
	local effect = { type = "Subtitle", text = text, kind = kind or "cue" }
	for k, v in extra or {} do
		effect[k] = v
	end
	self:_emit(effect)
end

function MissionCore:_buildInteractables()
	local list = {}
	for id, def in self.case.interactables do
		local entry = table.clone(def)
		entry.id = id
		if def.kind == "Deck" then
			local deck = self.case.decks[def.deck]
			entry.pos = deck.pos
			entry.room = deck.room
			entry.object = deck.label
		end
		list[id] = entry
	end
	for itemId, item in self.case.items do
		if item.spawns and #item.spawns > 0 then
			local index = self.variants.itemSpawns[itemId] or 1
			list["ITEM_" .. itemId] = {
				id = "ITEM_" .. itemId,
				kind = "Pickup",
				item = itemId,
				pos = item.spawns[index],
				object = item.label,
			}
		end
	end
	-- Loop props only show their prompts while the Loop is running.
	if self.case.loop then
		for _, prop in self.case.loop.props do
			list[prop.id] = {
				id = prop.id,
				kind = "LoopProp",
				prop = prop.id,
				pos = prop.pos,
				object = prop.label,
				room = self.case.loop.room,
			}
		end
	end
	self.interactables = list
end

function MissionCore:getInteractables()
	return self.interactables
end

function MissionCore:hasFlag(flag)
	return self.flags[flag] == true
end

function MissionCore:setFlag(flag, now)
	if self.flags[flag] then
		return false
	end
	self.flags[flag] = true
	self.flagTimes[flag] = now
	self.timeline:setAnchor(flag, now)

	if flag == "generator_restored" then
		self.relays.WEST = true
		self.relays.EAST = true
		self:_emit({ type = "Power", on = true })
		self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "PowerOn" })
	elseif flag == "extraction_open" then
		if self.phase == "Active" then
			self.phase = "ExtractionAvailable"
			self:_emit({ type = "Phase", phase = self.phase })
		end
		self.doorOverrides.D_Freight = false
		self:_emit({ type = "Door", door = "D_Freight", locked = false, open = true })
	end

	self:_refreshDoors()
	self:_refreshLights(now)
	self:_dirty()
	return true
end

function MissionCore:_doorLocked(doorId)
	if self.loopLocks[doorId] then
		return true
	end
	local override = self.doorOverrides[doorId]
	if override ~= nil then
		return override
	end
	local rule = self.case.doorLocks[doorId]
	if not rule then
		return false
	end
	if rule.unlockFlag and self.flags[rule.unlockFlag] then
		return false
	end
	return rule.locked == true
end

function MissionCore:doorLockReason(doorId)
	if self.loopLocks[doorId] then
		return "It won't open. The room is repeating."
	end
	local rule = self.case.doorLocks[doorId]
	if self.doorOverrides[doorId] == true and doorId == "D_CR_NH" then
		return "Jammed. The room is replaying an earlier version of itself."
	end
	return (rule and rule.reason) or "Locked."
end

function MissionCore:_refreshDoors()
	local ids = {}
	for id in self.case.doorLocks do
		ids[id] = true
	end
	for id in self.doorOverrides do
		ids[id] = true
	end
	for id in self.loopLocks do
		ids[id] = true
	end
	for id in ids do
		local locked = self:_doorLocked(id)
		if self.doorState[id] ~= locked then
			self.doorState[id] = locked
			self:_emit({ type = "Door", door = id, locked = locked })
		end
	end
end

function MissionCore:isDoorLocked(doorId)
	return self.doorState[doorId] == true
end

function MissionCore:_roomLightMode(roomId, room, now)
	local override = self.lightOverrides[roomId]
	if override and now < override.untilTime then
		return override.mode
	end
	local powered = self.flags.generator_restored
	if powered and room.lights ~= "none" then
		return "on"
	end
	if room.emergency then
		return "emergency"
	end
	return "off"
end

function MissionCore:_refreshLights(now)
	for _, roomId in MapLayout.StationRooms do
		local mode = self:_roomLightMode(roomId, MapLayout.Rooms[roomId], now)
		if self.lights[roomId] ~= mode then
			self.lights[roomId] = mode
			self:_emit({ type = "Lights", room = roomId, mode = mode })
		end
	end
end

function MissionCore:addNoise(amount, userId)
	if not ACTIVE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return
	end
	self.awareness += amount * (self.modeDef.awarenessScale or 1)
	if userId then
		self.lastNoiseUser = userId
	end
end

function MissionCore:participant(userId)
	return self.participants[key(userId)]
end

function MissionCore:isAlive(userId)
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	return p ~= nil and p.status == "Alive"
end

function MissionCore:_counts()
	local alive, extracted, eliminated, total = 0, 0, 0, 0
	for _, k in self.participantOrder do
		local p = self.participants[k]
		if p.status ~= "Disconnected" then
			total += 1
			if p.status == "Alive" then
				alive += 1
			elseif p.status == "Extracted" then
				extracted += 1
			elseif p.status == "Eliminated" then
				eliminated += 1
			end
		end
	end
	return alive, extracted, eliminated, total
end

function MissionCore:activeParticipantCount()
	local _, _, _, total = self:_counts()
	return total
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Participants
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:addParticipant(userId, name, now)
	local k = key(userId)
	local existing = self.participants[k]
	if existing then
		if existing.status == "Disconnected" then
			return false, "You left this case. Wait for the next one."
		end
		return false, "Already part of the case."
	end
	if not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] or self.flags.transmitter_armed then
		return false, "This case is too far along to join."
	end
	if self:activeParticipantCount() >= (self.case.maxParticipants or 4) then
		return false, "The team is full."
	end
	self.participants[k] = {
		userId = userId,
		name = name,
		status = "Alive",
		joinedAt = now,
		items = 0,
		evidence = 0,
		frames = 0,
		revivesUsed = 0,
		invulnerableUntil = 0,
	}
	table.insert(self.participantOrder, k)
	self:_dirty()
	return true
end

function MissionCore:removeParticipant(userId, now)
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	if not p or p.status == "Disconnected" then
		return
	end
	p.status = "Disconnected"
	self.deckUsers[key(userId)] = nil
	self:_notify(p.name .. " lost contact with the team.")
	self:_dirty()
	self:_checkEnd(now)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Interactions
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function componentHints(self)
	local hints = {}
	for _, id in self.case.components do
		if not self.inventory[id] then
			table.insert(hints, self.case.items[id].hint)
		end
	end
	return table.concat(hints, " · ")
end

function MissionCore:_componentsHeld()
	local n = 0
	for _, id in self.case.components do
		if self.inventory[id] then
			n += 1
		end
	end
	return n
end

function MissionCore:_evidenceCounts()
	local found = 0
	for _, id in self.case.optionalEvidence do
		if self.inventory[id] then
			found += 1
		end
	end
	return found, #self.case.optionalEvidence
end

function MissionCore:_giveItem(userId, itemId, now)
	if self.inventory[itemId] then
		return false
	end
	local item = self.case.items[itemId]
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	self.inventory[itemId] = { by = userId, name = p and p.name or "?", at = now }
	if p then
		if item.kind == "Evidence" then
			p.evidence += 1
		else
			p.items += 1
		end
	end
	if item.kind == "Tape" then
		self.stats.tapesFound[item.tape] = true
		self:_emit({ type = "Unlock", tape = item.tape, userIds = self:_teamUserIds() })
	end
	if item.kind == "Evidence" then
		self.stats.evidenceFound[item.evidence] = true
		self:_emit({ type = "Unlock", evidence = item.evidence, userIds = self:_teamUserIds() })
	end
	self:_emit({ type = "Item", item = itemId, present = false })
	self:_notify(string.format("%s recovered %s.", p and p.name or "Someone", item.label))
	self:setFlag("item_" .. itemId, now)
	if itemId == "BOARD" then
		self:setFlag("board_recovered", now)
	end
	if self:_componentsHeld() == #self.case.components then
		self:setFlag("components_complete", now)
	end
	local found, total = self:_evidenceCounts()
	if found == total then
		self:setFlag("all_evidence", now)
	end
	if item.bonus then
		-- The bonus tape is never free: the studio lights go to air and it comes.
		self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "OnAir", room = "StudioA" })
		self:_subtitle("[ON AIR LIGHT — STUDIO A]", "cue")
		self:_emit({
			type = "Presenter",
			scripted = false,
			startTime = now,
			eventId = "bonus_tape",
			command = {
				kind = "Ambush",
				-- Far corner from the only door, so the exit is always reachable.
				pos = MapLayout.point("StudioA", 0.15, 0.12),
				look = MapLayout.point("StudioA", 0.5, 0.3),
				radius = 60,
				windup = 2.5,
				maxDuration = 35,
			},
		})
	end
	self:_dirty()
	return true
end

function MissionCore:_teamUserIds()
	local ids = {}
	for _, k in self.participantOrder do
		local p = self.participants[k]
		if p.status ~= "Disconnected" then
			table.insert(ids, p.userId)
		end
	end
	return ids
end

function MissionCore:_deckAvailable(deckId)
	local deck = self.case.decks[deckId]
	if not deck then
		return false, "Unknown deck."
	end
	if not deck.battery and not self.flags.generator_restored then
		return false, deck.label .. " has no power. Restore the generator first."
	end
	return true
end

function MissionCore:deckAvailable(deckId)
	return self:_deckAvailable(deckId)
end

--[[
	Prompt presentation for an interactable: { visible, action, object, hold }.
	The glue mirrors this onto ProximityPrompts whenever the version changes.
]]
function MissionCore:promptState(id)
	local def = self.interactables[id]
	if not def then
		return { visible = false }
	end
	local kind = def.kind
	local flags = self.flags
	if kind == "Pickup" then
		local item = self.case.items[def.item]
		local visible = not self.inventory[def.item] and (not item.visibleFlag or flags[item.visibleFlag] == true)
		return { visible = visible, action = "Take", object = item.label, hold = 0 }
	elseif kind == "Deck" then
		local ok = self:_deckAvailable(def.deck)
		return { visible = true, action = ok and "Open Deck" or "No Power", object = def.object, hold = 0 }
	elseif kind == "Briefing" then
		return { visible = true, action = "Replay Briefing", object = def.object, hold = 0 }
	elseif kind == "Generator" then
		if self.generatorStage == 0 then
			return { visible = true, action = "Prime Fuel Pump", object = "Generator · UNPOWERED", hold = 2.5 }
		elseif self.generatorStage == 1 then
			return { visible = true, action = "Pull Starter", object = "Generator · PRIMED", hold = 5 }
		end
		return { visible = false }
	elseif kind == "Cage" then
		if self.keypad.opened then
			return { visible = false }
		end
		return { visible = true, action = "Enter Code", object = def.object, hold = 0 }
	elseif kind == "Relay" then
		if self.relays[def.relay] or not flags.generator_restored then
			return { visible = false }
		end
		return { visible = true, action = "Reset Relay", object = def.relay .. " RELAY · TRIPPED", hold = 2 }
	elseif kind == "BoardRack" then
		if self.inventory.BOARD then
			return { visible = false }
		elseif self.loop.solved then
			return { visible = true, action = "Take Sync Board", object = def.object, hold = 0 }
		elseif self.loop.active then
			return { visible = true, action = "Sealed", object = def.object, hold = 0 }
		end
		return { visible = true, action = "Open Rack", object = def.object, hold = 0 }
	elseif kind == "LoopProp" then
		return { visible = self.loop.active, action = "Inspect", object = def.object, hold = 0 }
	elseif kind == "Socket" then
		if self.installed[def.item] then
			return { visible = false }
		end
		local label = self.case.items[def.item].label
		return {
			visible = true,
			action = self.inventory[def.item] and ("Install " .. label) or ("Needs " .. label),
			object = def.object,
			hold = self.inventory[def.item] and 1.5 or 0,
		}
	elseif kind == "Lever" then
		local visible = flags.freight_jammed == true and not flags.extraction_open
		return { visible = visible, action = "Restart Motor", object = def.object, hold = 8 }
	elseif kind == "Van" then
		return { visible = self.phase == "ExtractionAvailable", action = "Leave the Station", object = def.object, hold = 2 }
	end
	return { visible = false }
end

-- Returns ok, message (message is shown to the acting player).
function MissionCore:interact(userId, id, now)
	local def = self.interactables[id]
	if not def then
		return false, "Nothing to do here."
	end
	if not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return false, "The case is over."
	end
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	if not p or p.status ~= "Alive" then
		return false, "Spectators cannot interact."
	end
	local kind = def.kind
	local state = self:promptState(id)
	if not state.visible then
		return false, "Nothing to do here."
	end

	if kind == "Pickup" then
		self:_giveItem(userId, def.item, now)
		return true
	elseif kind == "Deck" then
		local ok, reason = self:_deckAvailable(def.deck)
		if not ok then
			return false, reason
		end
		self:_emit({ type = "OpenDeck", userId = userId, deck = def.deck })
		return true
	elseif kind == "Briefing" then
		self:_emit({ type = "Briefing", userId = userId })
		return true
	elseif kind == "Generator" then
		if self.phase == "Briefing" then
			return false, "Wait for the briefing to finish."
		end
		if self.generatorStage == 0 then
			self.generatorStage = 1
			self:addNoise(10, userId)
			self:_subtitle("[FUEL PUMP RATTLES]", "cue")
			self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "GeneratorPrime", room = "Generator" })
			self:_dirty()
			return true
		elseif self.generatorStage == 1 then
			self.generatorStage = 2
			self:addNoise(20, userId)
			self:_subtitle("[GENERATOR ROARS TO LIFE]", "cue")
			self:setFlag("generator_restored", now)
			return true
		end
		return false, "Already running."
	elseif kind == "Cage" then
		if not self.flags.generator_restored then
			return false, "The keypad is dark. Restore power first."
		end
		if now < self.keypad.lockoutUntil then
			return false, "Keypad locked. Wait a few seconds."
		end
		self:_emit({ type = "OpenKeypad", userId = userId })
		return true
	elseif kind == "Relay" then
		self.relays[def.relay] = true
		self:addNoise(5, userId)
		self:_subtitle("[RELAY CLICKS BACK ON]", "cue")
		self:_emit({ type = "Relay", relay = def.relay, on = true })
		self:_dirty()
		return true
	elseif kind == "BoardRack" then
		if self.loop.solved then
			self:_giveItem(userId, "BOARD", now)
			return true
		elseif self.loop.active then
			return false, "The rack is sealed. The room is repeating."
		end
		if not self.flags.generator_restored then
			return false, "The rack's maglock has no power."
		end
		self:_startLoop(now)
		return true
	elseif kind == "LoopProp" then
		return self:_inspectLoopProp(userId, def, now)
	elseif kind == "Socket" then
		if not self.inventory[def.item] then
			return false, "You need the " .. self.case.items[def.item].label .. "."
		end
		self.installed[def.item] = true
		self:_subtitle("[COMPONENT SEATS WITH A CLUNK]", "cue")
		self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "Install", item = def.item })
		local all = true
		for _, comp in self.case.components do
			if not self.installed[comp] then
				all = false
			end
		end
		if all then
			self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "TransmitterArmed", room = "Transmitter" })
			self:setFlag("transmitter_armed", now)
		end
		self:_dirty()
		return true
	elseif kind == "Lever" then
		self.doorOverrides.D_Freight = false
		self:_emit({ type = "Door", door = "D_Freight", locked = false, open = true })
		self:_subtitle("[FREIGHT MOTOR CATCHES — DOOR RISING]", "cue")
		self:addNoise(15, userId)
		self:setFlag("extraction_open", now)
		return true
	elseif kind == "Van" then
		return self:extract(userId, now)
	end
	return false, "Nothing to do here."
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- The Loop
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:_startLoop(now)
	local loop = self.loop
	loop.active = true
	loop.cycleStart = now
	for _, doorId in self.case.loop.doors do
		self.loopLocks[doorId] = true
	end
	self:_subtitle("[DOORS SLAM — THE ARCHIVE GOES QUIET]", "cue")
	self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "LoopStart", room = self.case.loop.room })
	self:setFlag("loop_active", now)
	self:_refreshDoors()
	self:_dirty()
end

function MissionCore:loopCycle(now)
	local loop = self.loop
	local length = self.case.loop.cycleLength
	local index = math.floor((now - loop.cycleStart) / length) + 1
	return index, index % 2 == 0
end

function MissionCore:_inspectLoopProp(_userId, def, now)
	local loop = self.loop
	if not loop.active then
		return false, "Nothing unusual."
	end
	local _, oddNow = self:loopCycle(now)
	local _, oddBefore = self:loopCycle(now - (self.case.loop.tolerance or 0))
	local prop
	for _, candidate in self.case.loop.props do
		if candidate.id == def.prop then
			prop = candidate
		end
	end
	if def.prop == loop.odd and (oddNow or oddBefore) then
		loop.active = false
		loop.solved = true
		self.stats.loopFirstTry = loop.attempts == 0
		self.stats.anomaliesIdentified += 1
		self.loopLocks = {}
		self:_subtitle(string.format("The %s was %s. The loop breaks.", string.lower(prop.label), prop.odd), "event")
		self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "LoopBreak", room = self.case.loop.room })
		self:setFlag("loop_solved", now)
		self:_refreshDoors()
		self:_dirty()
		return true
	end
	loop.attempts += 1
	loop.cycleStart = now
	self:_subtitle(string.format("Nothing is wrong with the %s. The loop resets.", string.lower(prop.label)), "event")
	self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "LoopReset", room = self.case.loop.room })
	self:_dirty()
	return false, "Wrong detail. The loop resets — watch two full cycles."
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Keypad (the Caller lies about the code)
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:submitCode(userId, code, now)
	if not self:isAlive(userId) then
		return false, "Spectators cannot interact."
	end
	if self.keypad.opened then
		return true
	end
	if not self.flags.generator_restored then
		return false, "The keypad is dark."
	end
	if now < self.keypad.lockoutUntil then
		return false, "Keypad locked. Wait a few seconds."
	end
	if type(code) ~= "string" or #code ~= (self.case.keypad.digits or 4) or not string.match(code, "^%d+$") then
		return false, "Enter all digits."
	end
	if code == self.variants.code then
		self.keypad.opened = true
		if not self.stats.callerFooled then
			self.stats.anomaliesIdentified += 1
		end
		self:_subtitle("[CAGE LOCK RELEASES]", "cue")
		self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "CageOpen", room = "Generator" })
		self:_giveItem(userId, "VALVE", now)
		self:_dirty()
		return true
	end
	if code == self.variants.fakeCode then
		self.stats.callerFooled = true
	end
	self.keypad.wrongAttempts += 1
	self.keypad.lockoutUntil = now + (self.case.keypad.lockout or 5)
	self:addNoise(self.case.keypad.alarmAwareness or 35, userId)
	self:_subtitle("[ALARM BELL — GENERATOR ROOM]", "cue")
	self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "Alarm", room = "Generator" })
	self:_dirty()
	return false, "Wrong code. The alarm is ringing."
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Decks and editing
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:tapeFound(tapeId)
	return self.stats.tapesFound[tapeId] == true
end

function MissionCore:deckOpened(userId, deckId, tapeId, now)
	if not self:isAlive(userId) then
		return false
	end
	local ok = self:_deckAvailable(deckId)
	if not ok then
		return false
	end
	self.deckUsers[key(userId)] = deckId
	if tapeId and self:tapeFound(tapeId) then
		self:setFlag("viewed_" .. tapeId, now)
	end
	return true
end

function MissionCore:deckClosed(userId)
	self.deckUsers[key(userId)] = nil
end

-- The handheld monitor shows live cameras anywhere; watching it raises awareness
-- like a deck does, but it can never be used to edit.
function MissionCore:setWatching(userId, watching)
	if watching and self:isAlive(userId) then
		self.deckUsers[key(userId)] = "HANDHELD"
	elseif self.deckUsers[key(userId)] == "HANDHELD" then
		self.deckUsers[key(userId)] = nil
	end
end

function MissionCore:inspectEvent(userId, eventId, now)
	local ev = self.timeline:getEvent(eventId)
	if not ev or not self:participant(userId) then
		return
	end
	if not self:tapeFound(ev.tape) then
		return
	end
	local rec = self.timeline.records[eventId]
	if not rec.fired and not self.stats.inspected[eventId] then
		self.stats.inspected[eventId] = now
		self:_dirty()
	end
end

--[[
	ctx = { deckId, expectedState, signalOk }
	Returns ok, code, message. Nothing is spent unless ok.
]]
function MissionCore:commitEdit(userId, eventId, newState, ctx, now)
	ctx = ctx or {}
	if not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return false, "PHASE", "The case is over."
	end
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	if not p or p.status ~= "Alive" then
		return false, "SPECTATOR", "Spectators can watch the timeline but cannot edit it."
	end
	if not ctx.deckId then
		return false, "NO_DECK", "Edits are made at a tape deck."
	end
	local deckOk, deckReason = self:_deckAvailable(ctx.deckId)
	if not deckOk then
		return false, "NO_DECK", deckReason
	end
	local ev = self.timeline:getEvent(eventId)
	if ctx.signalOk == false then
		return false, "SIGNAL", "Signal too weak to edit. Reset the relay or try another camera."
	end
	local ok, code, message = self.timeline:validateEdit(eventId, newState, now, {
		flags = self.flags,
		charges = self.charges,
		tapeFound = ev ~= nil and self:tapeFound(ev.tape),
		expectedState = ctx.expectedState,
	})
	if not ok then
		return false, code, message
	end
	local cost = ev.editCost or 1
	-- Atomic within this call: validate -> deduct -> commit.
	self.charges -= cost
	local entry = self.timeline:commit(eventId, newState, userId, p.name, now)
	self.stats.editsMade += 1
	if not self.stats.inspected[eventId] then
		self.stats.inspected[eventId] = now
	end
	self:addNoise(AWARENESS_EDIT, userId)
	self:setFlag("decided_" .. eventId, now)
	self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "EditCommitted", eventId = eventId, userId = userId, entry = entry })
	local label = ev.stateLabels[newState] or newState
	self:_notify(string.format("%s edited %s: %s", p.name, ev.label, label))
	self:_dirty()
	return true, "OK", nil
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Hidden frames
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:markFrame(userId, tapeId, cameraId, t, _now)
	if not self:participant(userId) then
		return false, "Not part of this case."
	end
	if not self:tapeFound(tapeId) then
		return false, "Your team does not have that tape."
	end
	for _, hf in self.case.hiddenFrames do
		if hf.tape == tapeId and hf.camera == cameraId and type(t) == "number" and math.abs(t - hf.t) <= (hf.window or 0.5) then
			if self.stats.framesMarked[hf.id] then
				return false, "Already marked."
			end
			self.stats.framesMarked[hf.id] = userId
			local p = self:participant(userId)
			p.frames += 1
			self:_emit({ type = "Unlock", frame = hf.id, userIds = { userId } })
			self:_notify(string.format("%s marked a hidden frame: %s", p.name, hf.text))
			self:_dirty()
			return true, hf.id
		end
	end
	return false, "Nothing unusual on this frame."
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Eliminations, revives, extraction
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:isInvulnerable(userId, now)
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	return p ~= nil and now < (p.invulnerableUntil or 0)
end

function MissionCore:eliminate(userId, now, cause)
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	if not p or p.status ~= "Alive" or not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return false
	end
	if now < (p.invulnerableUntil or 0) then
		return false
	end
	p.status = "Eliminated"
	p.eliminatedAt = now
	self.stats.eliminations += 1
	local eventId = cause and cause.eventId
	if eventId then
		local ev = self.timeline:getEvent(eventId)
		local sourceId = ev and (ev.derivedFrom or ev.id)
		if sourceId and self.timeline.records[sourceId] and self.timeline.records[sourceId].edits > 0 then
			self.stats.eliminationsDuringEdits += 1
		end
	end
	self.deckUsers[key(userId)] = nil
	self.awareness = 30
	self:_emit({ type = "Eliminated", userId = userId })
	self:_notify(string.format("SIGNAL LOST — %s", string.upper(p.name)))
	self:_dirty()
	self:_checkEnd(now)
	return true
end

function MissionCore:canRevive(userId, kind)
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	if not p or p.status ~= "Eliminated" then
		return false, "Only eliminated investigators can re-enter."
	end
	if not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return false, "The case is over."
	end
	if self.teamTokenUsed then
		return false, "The team recovery has been used. No more re-entries this case."
	end
	if kind == "Personal" and p.revivesUsed >= 1 then
		return false, "You have already re-entered once this case."
	end
	return true
end

function MissionCore:canTeamRecover()
	if not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return false, "The case is over."
	end
	if self.teamTokenUsed then
		return false, "Already used this case."
	end
	local alive, _, eliminated = self:_counts()
	if eliminated == 0 then
		return false, "Nobody needs recovering."
	end
	if alive == 0 then
		return false, "Someone still inside has to call it in."
	end
	return true
end

-- Checkpoints unlocked so far, most advanced first.
function MissionCore:checkpoints()
	local list = {}
	for _, cp in self.case.checkpoints do
		if not cp.requires or self.flags[cp.requires] then
			table.insert(list, cp)
		end
	end
	return list
end

function MissionCore:_reviveOne(p, now)
	p.status = "Alive"
	p.invulnerableUntil = now + REVIVE_INVULNERABILITY
	self.failPendingUntil = nil
	self:_emit({ type = "Revive", userId = p.userId, invulnerableUntil = p.invulnerableUntil, checkpoints = self:checkpoints() })
	self:_notify(string.format("%s re-entered the station.", p.name))
end

function MissionCore:revive(userId, now, kind)
	local ok, reason = self:canRevive(userId, kind)
	if not ok then
		return false, reason
	end
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	if kind == "Personal" then
		p.revivesUsed += 1
	end
	self:_reviveOne(p, now)
	self:_dirty()
	return true
end

function MissionCore:teamRecover(now)
	local ok, reason = self:canTeamRecover()
	if not ok then
		return false, reason
	end
	self.teamTokenUsed = true
	for _, k in self.participantOrder do
		local p = self.participants[k]
		if p.status == "Eliminated" then
			self:_reviveOne(p, now)
		end
	end
	self:_dirty()
	return true
end

function MissionCore:extract(userId, now)
	if self.phase ~= "ExtractionAvailable" then
		return false, "The freight door is still closed."
	end
	local p = self:participant(userId)
	if not p or p.status ~= "Alive" then
		return false, "Spectators cannot extract."
	end
	p.status = "Extracted"
	p.extractedAt = now
	self.deckUsers[key(userId)] = nil
	self:_emit({ type = "Extracted", userId = userId })
	self:_notify(string.format("%s boarded the van.", p.name))
	self:_dirty()
	self:_checkEnd(now)
	return true
end

function MissionCore:_checkEnd(now)
	if not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return
	end
	local alive, extracted, eliminated, total = self:_counts()
	if total == 0 then
		self:_finish(now, "Aborted", "Everyone left.")
		return
	end
	if alive > 0 then
		self.failPendingUntil = nil
		return
	end
	if extracted > 0 then
		self:_finish(now, eliminated == 0 and "Success" or "Partial", nil)
		return
	end
	-- Everyone is down. Leave a short window for an optional re-entry.
	if not self.failPendingUntil then
		self.failPendingUntil = now + FAIL_GRACE
		self:_dirty()
	elseif now >= self.failPendingUntil then
		self:_finish(now, "Failed", "The whole team went off-air.")
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Update loop
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function resolveTokens(self, text)
	text = string.gsub(text, "{code}", self.variants.code)
	text = string.gsub(text, "{fakeCode}", self.variants.fakeCode)
	return text
end

function MissionCore:_radio(now, msg, index)
	local channelDef = self.case.radioChannels[msg.source]
	local live = self:broadcastClock(now)
	local stamped = live
	if type(msg.stamp) == "number" then
		stamped = math.max(0, live + msg.stamp)
	end
	local entry = {
		index = index,
		time = now,
		stamp = Format.clock(stamped),
		sourceId = channelDef.id,
		channel = channelDef.channel,
		text = resolveTokens(self, msg.text),
		caller = msg.source == "CALLER",
	}
	table.insert(self.radioLog, entry)
	while #self.radioLog > 8 do
		table.remove(self.radioLog, 1)
	end
	self:_emit({
		type = "Subtitle",
		kind = "radio",
		text = entry.text,
		stamp = entry.stamp,
		sourceId = entry.sourceId,
		channel = entry.channel,
		caller = entry.caller,
	})
	self:_dirty()
end

function MissionCore:_applyTimelineOutput(output, now)
	if output.kind == "fired" then
		self:setFlag("fired_" .. output.eventId, now)
		return
	end
	if output.kind == "presenter" then
		self:_emit({
			type = "Presenter",
			scripted = true,
			command = output.command,
			startTime = output.startTime,
			eventId = output.eventId,
		})
		return
	end
	local action = output.action
	if action.type == "Lights" then
		self.lightOverrides[action.room] = {
			mode = action.mode,
			untilTime = action.duration and (output.time + action.duration) or math.huge,
		}
		self:_refreshLights(now)
	elseif action.type == "Door" then
		self.doorOverrides[action.door] = action.locked
		if action.open then
			self:_emit({ type = "Door", door = action.door, locked = action.locked, open = true })
		end
		self:_refreshDoors()
	elseif action.type == "Relay" then
		self.relays[action.relay] = action.on
		self:_emit({ type = "Relay", relay = action.relay, on = action.on })
		if not action.on then
			self:_subtitle("[" .. action.relay .. " RELAY TRIPS]", "cue")
		end
	elseif action.type == "Cue" then
		local cue = table.clone(action)
		cue.type = "Cue"
		cue.eventId = output.eventId
		if action.event then
			local rec = self.timeline.records[action.event]
			cue.userId = rec and rec.committedBy or nil
		end
		self:_emit(cue)
	elseif action.type == "Subtitle" then
		self:_subtitle(action.text, action.kind)
	elseif action.type == "Flag" then
		self:setFlag(action.flag, now)
	end
	self:_dirty()
end

--[[
	world = { presenterIdle = boolean }
]]
function MissionCore:update(now, world)
	world = world or {}
	if self.phase == "Briefing" then
		for i, line in self.case.briefing.lines do
			if not self.briefingDone[i] and now >= self.createdAt + line.at then
				self.briefingDone[i] = true
				self:_emit({
					type = "Subtitle",
					kind = "radio",
					text = line.text,
					stamp = "00:00:00",
					sourceId = self.case.radioChannels.HQ.id,
					channel = self.case.radioChannels.HQ.channel,
					caller = false,
				})
			end
		end
		if now >= self.briefingEnd then
			self.phase = "Active"
			self.broadcastStart = now
			self:_emit({ type = "Phase", phase = "Active" })
			self:_emit({ type = "Cue", cue = "BroadcastStart" })
			self:setFlag("broadcast_start", now)
			self:_dirty()
		end
	end

	if PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		for _, output in self.timeline:update(now) do
			self:_applyTimelineOutput(output, now)
		end

		for i, msg in self.case.radio do
			if not self.radioDone[i] then
				local t = self.flagTimes[msg.anchor]
				if t and now >= t + msg.offset then
					self.radioDone[i] = true
					self:_radio(now, msg, i)
				end
			end
		end

		self:_refreshLights(now)
	end

	if ACTIVE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		local dt = math.max(0, now - (self.lastUpdate or now))
		self.awareness = math.max(0, self.awareness - AWARENESS_DECAY * dt)
		for userKey in self.deckUsers do
			self.awareness += AWARENESS_DECK * dt * (self.modeDef.awarenessScale or 1)
			self.lastNoiseUser = self.lastNoiseUser or tonumber(userKey)
		end
		if
			self.awareness >= AWARENESS_THRESHOLD
			and world.presenterIdle
			and now - self.lastObserveAt >= OBSERVE_COOLDOWN
		then
			local target = self.lastNoiseUser
			if not (target and self:isAlive(target)) then
				target = nil
				for _, k in self.participantOrder do
					if self.participants[k].status == "Alive" then
						target = self.participants[k].userId
						break
					end
				end
			end
			if target then
				self.lastObserveAt = now
				self.awareness = 35
				self:_emit({ type = "ObserveNear", userId = target, startTime = now })
			end
		end

		if self.broadcastStart and now - self.broadcastStart > self.case.timeLimit then
			self:_finish(now, "Failed", "The broadcast ended with the team still inside.")
		end
	end

	if PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		self:_checkEnd(now)
	end
	self.lastUpdate = now
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Objectives and snapshot
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:_resolveObjectiveText(text)
	local tl = self.timeline
	text = string.gsub(text, "{mark:([%w_]+)}", function(eventId)
		local ev = tl:getEvent(eventId)
		if not ev then
			return "?"
		end
		local anchor = tl:anchorTime(ev.anchor)
		if anchor and self.broadcastStart then
			return Format.clock(anchor - self.broadcastStart + ev.offset)
		end
		return Format.relative(ev.offset)
	end)
	text = string.gsub(text, "{time:([%w_]+)}", function(eventId)
		local fire = tl:fireTime(eventId)
		if fire and self.broadcastStart then
			return Format.clock(fire - self.broadcastStart)
		end
		local ev = tl:getEvent(eventId)
		return ev and Format.relative(ev.offset) or "?"
	end)
	local found, total = self:_evidenceCounts()
	text = string.gsub(text, "{components}", tostring(self:_componentsHeld()))
	text = string.gsub(text, "{componentHints}", componentHints(self))
	text = string.gsub(text, "{evidenceTotal}", tostring(total))
	text = string.gsub(text, "{evidence}", tostring(found))
	return text
end

function MissionCore:currentObjective()
	for _, obj in self.case.objectives do
		local done = self.flags[obj.doneFlag] or (obj.altDoneFlag and self.flags[obj.altDoneFlag])
		local shown = not obj.showFlag or self.flags[obj.showFlag]
		if not done and shown then
			return {
				id = obj.id,
				text = self:_resolveObjectiveText(obj.text),
				clue = (self.modeDef.showClues and obj.clue) and self:_resolveObjectiveText(obj.clue) or nil,
			}
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function MissionCore:optionalObjectives()
	local list = {}
	for _, obj in self.case.optionalObjectives or {} do
		if (not obj.showFlag or self.flags[obj.showFlag]) and not self.flags[obj.doneFlag] then
			table.insert(list, self:_resolveObjectiveText(obj.text))
		end
	end
	return list
end

function MissionCore:snapshot(now)
	local participants = {}
	for _, k in self.participantOrder do
		local p = self.participants[k]
		participants[k] = {
			name = p.name,
			status = p.status,
			invulnerableUntil = p.invulnerableUntil,
			revivesUsed = p.revivesUsed,
		}
	end
	local inventory = {}
	for itemId, entry in self.inventory do
		inventory[itemId] = entry.name
	end
	local flags = {}
	for flag in self.flags do
		flags[flag] = true
	end
	local doors = table.clone(self.doorState)
	return {
		caseId = self.case.id,
		mode = self.mode,
		modeLabel = self.modeDef.label,
		phase = self.phase,
		version = self.version,
		createdAt = self.createdAt,
		briefingEnd = self.briefingEnd,
		broadcastStart = self.broadcastStart,
		timeLimit = self.case.timeLimit,
		charges = self.charges,
		maxCharges = self.maxCharges,
		flags = flags,
		inventory = inventory,
		installed = table.clone(self.installed),
		participants = participants,
		objective = self:currentObjective(),
		optional = self:optionalObjectives(),
		radioLog = self.radioLog,
		loop = {
			active = self.loop.active,
			solved = self.loop.solved,
			cycleStart = self.loop.cycleStart,
			cycleLength = self.case.loop.cycleLength,
			odd = self.loop.active and self.loop.odd or nil,
		},
		keypad = { opened = self.keypad.opened, lockoutUntil = self.keypad.lockoutUntil },
		generatorStage = self.generatorStage,
		relays = table.clone(self.relays),
		doors = doors,
		lights = table.clone(self.lights),
		patrolFlip = self.variants.patrolFlip,
		timeline = self.timeline:snapshot(now),
		teamTokenUsed = self.teamTokenUsed,
		failPendingUntil = self.failPendingUntil,
		endReason = self.endReason,
	}
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Ending and report
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function MissionCore:_finish(now, result, reason)
	if not PLAYABLE_PHASES[self.phase] then
		return
	end
	self.endedAt = now
	self.endReason = reason
	if result == "Aborted" then
		self.phase = "Aborted"
		self:_emit({ type = "Phase", phase = "Aborted" })
		self:_dirty()
		return
	end
	self.phase = (result == "Failed") and "Failed" or "Completed"
	self.report = self:_buildReport(now, result)
	self:_emit({ type = "Phase", phase = self.phase })
	self:_emit({ type = "Report", report = self.report })
	self:_dirty()
end

function MissionCore:_buildReport(now, result)
	local case = self.case
	local participants = {}
	local survivors, counted = 0, 0
	for _, k in self.participantOrder do
		local p = self.participants[k]
		if p.status ~= "Disconnected" then
			counted += 1
			if p.status == "Extracted" or p.status == "Alive" then
				survivors += 1
			end
		end
		table.insert(participants, {
			userId = p.userId,
			name = p.name,
			status = p.status,
			items = p.items,
			evidence = p.evidence,
			frames = p.frames,
		})
	end

	-- Predictions: events on recovered tapes that someone inspected before they fired.
	local predictionsTotal, interpreted = 0, 0
	for _, ev in case.events do
		if self:tapeFound(ev.tape) then
			predictionsTotal += 1
			local seenAt = self.stats.inspected[ev.id]
			local fire = self.timeline:fireTime(ev.id)
			if seenAt and (not fire or seenAt <= fire) then
				interpreted += 1
			end
		end
	end

	local evidenceFound, evidenceTotal = self:_evidenceCounts()
	local tapesFound = 0
	for _ in self.stats.tapesFound do
		tapesFound += 1
	end

	local gradeStats = {
		result = result,
		participants = counted,
		survivors = survivors,
		evidenceFound = evidenceFound,
		evidenceTotal = evidenceTotal,
		predictionsInterpreted = interpreted,
		predictionsTotal = predictionsTotal,
		editsMade = self.stats.editsMade,
		maxCharges = self.maxCharges,
		eliminationsDuringEdits = self.stats.eliminationsDuringEdits,
		loopFirstTry = self.stats.loopFirstTry,
		callerFooled = self.stats.callerFooled,
	}
	local grading = Grading.compute(gradeStats)
	local bonusExtracted = self.inventory.TAPE07 ~= nil and result ~= "Failed"

	local edits = {}
	for _, entry in self.timeline.editLog do
		local ev = self.timeline:getEvent(entry.eventId)
		table.insert(edits, {
			label = ev.label,
			from = ev.stateLabels[entry.from] or entry.from,
			to = ev.stateLabels[entry.to] or entry.to,
			by = entry.byName,
		})
	end

	local teamChallenges = {}
	local success = result == "Success" or result == "Partial"
	if success and self.charges > 0 then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_UNUSED")
	end
	if evidenceFound == evidenceTotal then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_EVIDENCE")
	end
	if result == "Success" then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_NOLOSS")
	end
	if self.loop.solved and self.stats.loopFirstTry then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_LOOP")
	end
	if self.keypad.opened and not self.stats.callerFooled then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_CALLER")
	end
	if next(self.stats.framesMarked) then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_FRAME")
	end
	if success and counted == 1 then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_SOLO")
	end
	if success and self.mode == "Restricted" then
		table.insert(teamChallenges, "C01_RESTRICTED")
	end

	local rewards = {}
	for _, entry in participants do
		if entry.status ~= "Disconnected" then
			rewards[key(entry.userId)] = Grading.rewards({
				result = result,
				grade = grading.grade,
				anomaliesIdentified = self.stats.anomaliesIdentified,
				bonusTapeExtracted = bonusExtracted,
			}, {
				itemsRecovered = entry.items,
				evidenceRecovered = entry.evidence,
				framesMarked = entry.frames,
				extracted = entry.status == "Extracted",
			})
		end
	end

	local components = {}
	for _, id in case.components do
		components[id] = self.inventory[id] ~= nil
	end

	return {
		caseId = case.id,
		title = case.title,
		mode = self.mode,
		modeLabel = self.modeDef.label,
		seed = self.seed,
		result = result,
		reason = self.endReason,
		grade = grading.grade,
		score = grading.score,
		breakdown = grading.breakdown,
		components = components,
		componentsCount = self:_componentsHeld(),
		componentsInstalled = self.flags.transmitter_armed == true,
		tapesFound = tapesFound,
		tapesTotal = #case.tapeOrder,
		evidenceFound = evidenceFound,
		evidenceTotal = evidenceTotal,
		predictionsInterpreted = interpreted,
		predictionsTotal = predictionsTotal,
		editsMade = self.stats.editsMade,
		maxCharges = self.maxCharges,
		edits = edits,
		eliminations = self.stats.eliminations,
		loopSolved = self.loop.solved,
		loopFirstTry = self.stats.loopFirstTry,
		callerFooled = self.stats.callerFooled,
		bonusTape = bonusExtracted,
		participants = participants,
		rewards = rewards,
		challenges = teamChallenges,
		masterRecording = success and case.masterRecording or nil,
		duration = self.broadcastStart and (now - self.broadcastStart) or 0,
		evidenceIds = (function()
			local ids = {}
			for id in self.stats.evidenceFound do
				table.insert(ids, id)
			end
			table.sort(ids)
			return ids
		end)(),
	}
end

function MissionCore:getReport()
	return self.report
end


return MissionCore
```

## src/shared/Core/PresenterBrain.luau

```lua
--[[
	PresenterBrain — decision making for the Presenter, independent of Roblox.

	States (from the brief):
	  Dormant    appears only in footage. Not in the world.
	  Observing  stands at a distance and watches. Comes closer only if you do.
	  Displaced  follows an authored route caused by a timeline event.
	  Hunting    pursues one player after a telegraphed wind-up.
	  Rewinding  stands still while a room replays its earlier state.

	Fairness rules baked in:
	  * Every hunt starts with a wind-up (it stops, the cue plays) before it moves.
	  * It needs line of sight to start a hunt and loses you after a few seconds
	    out of sight; it then searches your last known position and gives up.
	  * Hunts have a hard time limit, and after any hunt it vanishes (release).
	  * Players inside a closed refuge, or who were just revived, are invisible to it.

	The glue layer feeds `sense` each tick and applies the returned intent:
	  sense = {
	    pos = {x,y,z} | nil,   -- current world position (nil while not in the world)
	    players = { { id, pos, dist, los, angle, lit, flashlight, hidden, invulnerable } },
	  }
	  output = {
	    state, visible, teleportTo, moveTo, speed, face, targetId, catchId, events = {...}
	  }
]]

local Vec = require(script.Parent.Parent.Util.Vec)

local PresenterBrain = {}
PresenterBrain.__index = PresenterBrain

PresenterBrain.DefaultConfig = {
	huntSpeed = 14.5,
	catchRadius = 3.6,
	windup = 2.5,
	loseSightTime = 6,
	searchTime = 4,
	maxHunt = 45,
	ambushKnowledge = 3,
	observeDuration = 8,
	observeTrigger = 12,
	huntSight = { range = 90, dark = 45, fov = 220, proximity = 10 },
	defaultSight = { range = 40, dark = 26, fov = 120, proximity = 6 },
}

function PresenterBrain.new(config)
	local merged = table.clone(PresenterBrain.DefaultConfig)
	for k, v in config or {} do
		merged[k] = v
	end
	local self = setmetatable({}, PresenterBrain)
	self.config = merged
	self.state = "Dormant"
	self.command = nil
	self.hunt = nil
	self.pendingEvents = {}
	self.needsAppear = false
	self.anchorPos = nil
	self.anchorLook = nil
	return self
end

local function canSee(p, sight)
	if p.hidden or p.invulnerable or not p.los then
		return false
	end
	local range = (p.lit or p.flashlight) and sight.range or sight.dark
	if p.dist > range then
		return false
	end
	if p.dist <= (sight.proximity or 0) then
		return true
	end
	return p.angle <= (sight.fov or 120) / 2
end
PresenterBrain.canSee = canSee

local function findPlayer(players, id)
	for _, p in players do
		if p.id == id then
			return p
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function nearest(players, predicate)
	local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
	for _, p in players do
		if predicate(p) and p.dist < bestDist then
			best, bestDist = p, p.dist
		end
	end
	return best
end

function PresenterBrain:_emit(name, data)
	table.insert(self.pendingEvents, { name = name, data = data })
end

function PresenterBrain:isIdle()
	return self.state == "Dormant"
end

function PresenterBrain:hasScriptedCommand()
	return self.command ~= nil and self.command.scripted == true
end

-- Ends whatever it is doing and leaves the world.
function PresenterBrain:vanish(reason)
	local wasVisible = self.state ~= "Dormant"
	self.state = "Dormant"
	self.command = nil
	self.hunt = nil
	self.anchorPos = nil
	if wasVisible then
		self:_emit("Vanish", { reason = reason })
	end
end

--[[
	cmd kinds:
	  { kind = "Displaced", path, duration, huntOnSight, sight }
	  { kind = "Ambush", pos, look, radius, windup, maxDuration }
	  { kind = "Observe", pos, look, duration, triggerRadius }
	  { kind = "Rewind", pos, look, duration, sight }
	  { kind = "Dormant" }
	meta.scripted = true for timeline events (they override awareness behaviour).
]]
function PresenterBrain:setCommand(cmd, startTime, meta)
	meta = meta or {}
	if self.hunt then
		self:_emit("HuntEnd", { reason = "Overridden", targetId = self.hunt.targetId })
	end
	self.hunt = nil
	if cmd.kind == "Dormant" then
		self:vanish("Command")
		return
	end
	self.command = { def = cmd, start = startTime, scripted = meta.scripted == true, eventId = meta.eventId }
	self.needsAppear = true
	if cmd.kind == "Displaced" then
		self.state = "Displaced"
	elseif cmd.kind == "Ambush" then
		self.anchorPos = cmd.pos
		self.anchorLook = cmd.look
		self.state = "Hunting"
		self.hunt = {
			phase = "windup",
			phaseStart = startTime,
			started = startTime,
			windup = cmd.windup or self.config.windup,
			targetId = nil,
			ambush = true,
			maxDuration = cmd.maxDuration or self.config.maxHunt,
		}
		self:_emit("HuntStart", { ambush = true })
	elseif cmd.kind == "Observe" then
		self.anchorPos = cmd.pos
		self.anchorLook = cmd.look
		self.state = "Observing"
	elseif cmd.kind == "Rewind" then
		self.anchorPos = cmd.pos
		self.anchorLook = cmd.look
		self.state = "Rewinding"
	end
	self:_emit("Appear", { kind = cmd.kind, eventId = meta.eventId })
end

local function pathPosition(path, elapsed)
	if elapsed <= path[1].t then
		return Vec.copy(path[1].pos), 0, path[1].look, 1
	end
	for i = 1, #path - 1 do
		local a, b = path[i], path[i + 1]
		if elapsed >= a.t and elapsed < b.t then
			local span = b.t - a.t
			local alpha = (elapsed - a.t) / span
			local speed = Vec.flatDistance(a.pos, b.pos) / span
			return Vec.lerp(a.pos, b.pos, alpha), speed, b.look, i
		end
	end
	local last = path[#path]
	return Vec.copy(last.pos), 0, last.look, #path
end
PresenterBrain.pathPosition = pathPosition

function PresenterBrain:_startHunt(now, target, windup, knowledge)
	self.state = "Hunting"
	self.hunt = {
		phase = "windup",
		phaseStart = now,
		started = now,
		windup = windup or self.config.windup,
		targetId = target and target.id or nil,
		lastKnown = target and Vec.copy(target.pos) or nil,
		lastSeen = now,
		knowsUntil = knowledge and (now + knowledge) or nil,
		maxDuration = self.config.maxHunt,
	}
	self:_emit("HuntStart", { targetId = self.hunt.targetId })
end

function PresenterBrain:_endHunt(reason)
	local targetId = self.hunt and self.hunt.targetId
	self:_emit("HuntEnd", { reason = reason, targetId = targetId })
	self:vanish(reason)
end

function PresenterBrain:update(now, sense)
	local out = {
		state = self.state,
		visible = false,
		events = nil,
	}
	local players = sense.players or {}
	local cfg = self.config
	local cmd = self.command and self.command.def

	if self.state == "Displaced" and cmd then
		local elapsed = now - self.command.start
		if elapsed >= (cmd.duration or 0) then
			self:vanish("RouteComplete")
		else
			local pos, speed, look = pathPosition(cmd.path, elapsed)
			if self.needsAppear then
				out.teleportTo = pos
				self.needsAppear = false
			end
			out.moveTo = pos
			out.speed = math.clamp(speed, 0, 16)
			out.face = look
			if cmd.huntOnSight then
				local sight = cmd.sight or cfg.defaultSight
				local seen = nearest(players, function(p)
					return canSee(p, sight)
				end)
				if seen then
					self:_startHunt(now, seen, cfg.windup)
				end
			end
		end
	elseif (self.state == "Observing" or self.state == "Rewinding") and cmd then
		local elapsed = now - self.command.start
		local duration = cmd.duration or cfg.observeDuration
		if elapsed >= duration then
			self:vanish(self.state == "Observing" and "ObserveComplete" or "RewindComplete")
		else
			if self.needsAppear then
				out.teleportTo = self.anchorPos
				self.needsAppear = false
			end
			out.moveTo = self.anchorPos
			out.speed = 0
			local closest = nearest(players, function(p)
				return not p.hidden and not p.invulnerable
			end)
			out.face = (self.state == "Observing" and closest and closest.pos) or self.anchorLook
			local trigger
			if self.state == "Observing" then
				local radius = cmd.triggerRadius or cfg.observeTrigger
				trigger = nearest(players, function(p)
					return p.los and not p.hidden and not p.invulnerable and p.dist <= radius
				end)
			else
				local sight = cmd.sight or cfg.defaultSight
				trigger = nearest(players, function(p)
					return canSee(p, sight)
				end)
			end
			if trigger then
				self:_startHunt(now, trigger, self.state == "Observing" and 1.5 or 2)
			end
		end
	end

	-- Hunting is evaluated after the transitions above so a hunt can start and
	-- report its wind-up on the same tick.
	if self.state == "Hunting" and self.hunt then
		local hunt = self.hunt
		if self.needsAppear and self.anchorPos then
			out.teleportTo = self.anchorPos
			self.needsAppear = false
		end
		if now - hunt.started > hunt.maxDuration then
			self:_endHunt("TimeLimit")
		elseif hunt.phase == "windup" then
			out.speed = 0
			if hunt.targetId then
				local t = findPlayer(players, hunt.targetId)
				out.face = t and t.pos or nil
			else
				out.face = self.anchorLook
			end
			if now - hunt.phaseStart >= hunt.windup then
				if hunt.ambush then
					local radius = (cmd and cmd.radius) or 70
					local target = nearest(players, function(p)
						return not p.hidden and not p.invulnerable and p.dist <= radius
					end)
					if target then
						hunt.targetId = target.id
						hunt.lastKnown = Vec.copy(target.pos)
						hunt.lastSeen = now
						hunt.knowsUntil = now + cfg.ambushKnowledge
						hunt.phase = "chase"
						hunt.phaseStart = now
					else
						-- Nobody here: it watches the room for a while instead.
						local pos = self.anchorPos
						local look = self.anchorLook
						self.hunt = nil
						self.state = "Observing"
						self.command = {
							def = { kind = "Observe", pos = pos, look = look, duration = 12 },
							start = now,
							scripted = self.command and self.command.scripted or false,
						}
						self:_emit("HuntEnd", { reason = "NoTarget" })
					end
				else
					hunt.phase = "chase"
					hunt.phaseStart = now
				end
			end
		end

		if self.hunt and (self.hunt.phase == "chase" or self.hunt.phase == "search") then
			hunt = self.hunt
			local target = hunt.targetId and findPlayer(players, hunt.targetId)
			if target and (target.hidden or target.invulnerable) then
				target = nil
			end
			local seesTarget = target and (canSee(target, cfg.huntSight) or (hunt.knowsUntil and now < hunt.knowsUntil))
			if seesTarget then
				hunt.lastKnown = Vec.copy(target.pos)
				hunt.lastSeen = now
				if hunt.phase == "search" then
					hunt.phase = "chase"
					hunt.phaseStart = now
				end
			end
			if target and seesTarget and target.dist <= cfg.catchRadius and target.los then
				out.catchId = target.id
				self:_endHunt("Caught")
			elseif hunt.phase == "chase" and now - hunt.lastSeen > cfg.loseSightTime then
				hunt.phase = "search"
				hunt.phaseStart = now
				self:_emit("Searching", { targetId = hunt.targetId })
			elseif hunt.phase == "search" and now - hunt.phaseStart > cfg.searchTime then
				self:_endHunt("Lost")
			end
			if self.hunt then
				out.moveTo = hunt.lastKnown
				out.speed = hunt.phase == "chase" and cfg.huntSpeed or cfg.huntSpeed * 0.6
				out.face = hunt.lastKnown
				out.targetId = hunt.targetId
				out.chasing = hunt.phase == "chase"
			end
		end
	end

	out.state = self.state
	out.visible = self.state ~= "Dormant"
	if self.hunt then
		out.huntPhase = self.hunt.phase
	end
	if #self.pendingEvents > 0 then
		out.events = self.pendingEvents
		self.pendingEvents = {}
	end
	return out
end

return PresenterBrain
```

## src/shared/Core/ProfileSchema.luau

```lua
--[[
	Saved player progression: defaults, migration, settings sanitising and applying
	a finished case report. Pure so it can be tested; DataService persists it.
]]

local Cosmetics = require(script.Parent.Parent.Config.Cosmetics)

local ProfileSchema = {}

ProfileSchema.VERSION = 1

ProfileSchema.GradeOrder = { D = 1, C = 2, B = 3, A = 4, S = 5 }

ProfileSchema.Equipment = {
	Camcorder = { label = "CAMCORDER", description = "Night-vision viewfinder. Recording anomalies on screen earns research." },
	Analyser = { label = "SIGNAL ANALYSER", description = "Shows signal strength and the direction of the strongest interference." },
	Recorder = { label = "AUDIO RECORDER", description = "Logs every radio message with its channel and timestamp, for review." },
	Monitor = { label = "HANDHELD MONITOR", description = "View live station cameras anywhere. Watching raises its awareness." },
}
ProfileSchema.EquipmentOrder = { "Camcorder", "Analyser", "Recorder", "Monitor" }

ProfileSchema.DefaultSettings = {
	sensitivity = 1,
	subtitles = true,
	radioLog = true,
	cameraShake = 1,
	distortion = 1,
	flicker = 1,
	lowIntensity = false,
	master = 1,
	music = 0.8,
	ambience = 0.8,
	effects = 1,
	textScale = 1,
	-- Camera and comfort
	fieldOfView = 70,
	headBob = 1,
	motionBlur = 1,
	vignette = 1,
	toggleSprint = false,
	crosshair = true,
	tips = true,
}

local SETTING_RANGES = {
	sensitivity = { 0.2, 3 },
	cameraShake = { 0, 1 },
	distortion = { 0, 1 },
	flicker = { 0, 1 },
	master = { 0, 1 },
	music = { 0, 1 },
	ambience = { 0, 1 },
	effects = { 0, 1 },
	textScale = { 0.8, 1.4 },
	fieldOfView = { 60, 90 },
	headBob = { 0, 1 },
	motionBlur = { 0, 1 },
	vignette = { 0, 1 },
}

local function deepCopy(value)
	if type(value) ~= "table" then
		return value
	end
	local out = {}
	for k, v in value do
		out[k] = deepCopy(v)
	end
	return out
end

function ProfileSchema.default()
	return {
		version = ProfileSchema.VERSION,
		credits = 0,
		research = 0,
		clearance = 1,
		evidence = {},
		tapes = {},
		frames = {},
		masters = {},
		cases = {},
		challenges = {},
		cosmetics = { owned = {}, equipped = table.clone(Cosmetics.Defaults) },
		equipment = "Camcorder",
		presets = {},
		decor = {},
		settings = table.clone(ProfileSchema.DefaultSettings),
		storedRevives = 0,
		storedTeamTokens = 0,
		stats = { casesPlayed = 0, casesCompleted = 0, eliminations = 0, editsMade = 0 },
		archiveChallengeBest = {},
		tutorialSeen = false,
	}
end

-- Fills any missing keys with defaults without overwriting saved values.
local function fill(target, defaults)
	for k, v in defaults do
		if target[k] == nil then
			target[k] = deepCopy(v)
		elseif type(v) == "table" and type(target[k]) == "table" and next(v) ~= nil then
			fill(target[k], v)
		end
	end
end

function ProfileSchema.migrate(data)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return ProfileSchema.default()
	end
	fill(data, ProfileSchema.default())
	data.version = ProfileSchema.VERSION
	return data
end

-- Settings come from the client: accept only known keys with sane values.
function ProfileSchema.sanitizeSettings(current, incoming)
	local out = table.clone(current or ProfileSchema.DefaultSettings)
	if type(incoming) ~= "table" then
		return out
	end
	for k, default in ProfileSchema.DefaultSettings do
		local v = incoming[k]
		if type(default) == "boolean" and type(v) == "boolean" then
			out[k] = v
		elseif type(default) == "number" and type(v) == "number" and v == v then
			local range = SETTING_RANGES[k]
			out[k] = range and math.clamp(v, range[1], range[2]) or v
		end
	end
	return out
end

--[[
	Applies a finished case report for one player. Returns a summary of what is
	new so the case report screen can list unlocks.
]]
function ProfileSchema.applyReport(profile, report, userId)
	local summary = { evidence = {}, frames = {}, challenges = {}, master = nil, clearance = nil, firstCompletion = false }
	local key = tostring(userId)
	local reward = report.rewards and report.rewards[key]
	if reward then
		profile.credits += reward.credits
		profile.research += reward.research
		summary.credits = reward.credits
		summary.research = reward.research
	end

	for _, id in report.evidenceIds or {} do
		if not profile.evidence[id] then
			profile.evidence[id] = true
			table.insert(summary.evidence, id)
		end
	end

	for _, id in report.challenges or {} do
		if not profile.challenges[id] then
			profile.challenges[id] = true
			table.insert(summary.challenges, id)
		end
	end

	local caseStats = profile.cases[report.caseId] or { runs = 0, completed = 0, bestGrade = nil, bestScore = 0 }
	profile.cases[report.caseId] = caseStats
	caseStats.runs += 1
	profile.stats.casesPlayed += 1
	profile.stats.editsMade += report.editsMade or 0

	local success = report.result == "Success" or report.result == "Partial"
	if success then
		if caseStats.completed == 0 then
			summary.firstCompletion = true
		end
		caseStats.completed += 1
		profile.stats.casesCompleted += 1
		if report.masterRecording and not profile.masters[report.masterRecording] then
			profile.masters[report.masterRecording] = true
			summary.master = report.masterRecording
		end
		-- Clearance comes from story milestones, not combat: completing Case 01
		-- grants clearance 2 (Restricted mode and the Archive Challenge).
		if profile.clearance < 2 then
			profile.clearance = 2
			summary.clearance = 2
		end
	end
	local old = caseStats.bestGrade and ProfileSchema.GradeOrder[caseStats.bestGrade] or 0
	if ProfileSchema.GradeOrder[report.grade] and ProfileSchema.GradeOrder[report.grade] > old then
		caseStats.bestGrade = report.grade
	end
	caseStats.bestScore = math.max(caseStats.bestScore or 0, report.score or 0)
	return summary
end

return ProfileSchema
```

## src/shared/Core/SignalModel.luau

```lua
--[[
	Deterministic signal quality (0-100). No randomness: the same inputs always
	give the same number, and signal never harms players directly.

	Deck footage quality controls what the Time-Shift Deck can show:
	  CLEAR (>= 70) timestamps and event markers fully visible
	  WEAK  (40-69) heavier noise; markers visible, clue text partly garbled
	  LOST  (< 40)  markers hidden - reset the relay or use another camera
]]

local SignalModel = {}

SignalModel.CLEAR = 70
SignalModel.WEAK = 40

function SignalModel.band(quality: number): string
	if quality >= SignalModel.CLEAR then
		return "CLEAR"
	elseif quality >= SignalModel.WEAK then
		return "WEAK"
	end
	return "LOST"
end

local function interference(distance: number?, radius: number, strength: number): number
	if not distance or distance >= radius then
		return 0
	end
	return (1 - distance / radius) * strength
end

--[[
	params = {
		relay = "LOCAL" | "MAINS" | "WEST" | "EAST",  -- camera's feed
		powerOn = boolean,                              -- station generator running
		relayOn = boolean,                              -- the camera's relay is not tripped
		deckBattery = boolean,                          -- viewing on a battery deck
		presenterDistance = number?,                    -- Presenter to camera (nil if dormant)
		anomaly = boolean,                              -- an anomaly is active in the camera's room
	}
	Returns quality, reason.
]]
function SignalModel.cameraQuality(params): (number, string?)
	local relay = params.relay
	if relay ~= "LOCAL" and not params.powerOn then
		return 0, "NO CARRIER — CAMERA UNPOWERED"
	end
	local quality = params.deckBattery and 64 or 96
	local reason = nil
	if (relay == "WEST" or relay == "EAST") and params.relayOn == false then
		quality -= 55
		reason = relay .. " RELAY TRIPPED"
	end
	quality -= interference(params.presenterDistance, 40, 35)
	if params.anomaly then
		quality -= 40
		reason = reason or "ANOMALOUS INTERFERENCE"
	end
	quality = math.clamp(math.floor(quality + 0.5), 0, 100)
	if quality < SignalModel.WEAK and not reason then
		reason = "INTERFERENCE"
	end
	return quality, reason
end

--[[
	The HUD signal meter: a proximity sense for the Presenter and anomalies.
	params = { powerOn, wingRelayOn, presenterDistance, anomaly }
]]
function SignalModel.playerQuality(params): number
	local quality = params.powerOn and 92 or 68
	if params.wingRelayOn == false then
		quality -= 15
	end
	quality -= interference(params.presenterDistance, 60, 55)
	if params.anomaly then
		quality -= 35
	end
	return math.clamp(math.floor(quality + 0.5), 0, 100)
end

return SignalModel
```

## src/shared/Core/TimelineCore.luau

```lua
--[[
	TimelineCore — the authoritative model of a case's predicted events.

	Rules (from the design brief):
	  * Footage is a genuine prediction unless players interfere.
	  * Players edit conditions (an event's state), never the monster or the mission.
	  * Every edit has an authored, deterministic consequence: derived events read
	    their state from the event they depend on through a fixed `stateMap`.
	  * Invalid edits never spend charges; no event is edited twice unless its
	    `maxEdits` allows it; once an event (or one of its consequences) starts to
	    play out, its state is locked.

	This module is engine-independent. `update(now)` returns what became due so the
	mission layer can apply it to the world; nothing here touches Roblox.
]]

local TimelineCore = {}
TimelineCore.__index = TimelineCore

TimelineCore.Messages = {
	UNKNOWN_EVENT = "That event is not on this tape.",
	NOT_EDITABLE = "This event cannot be edited directly. It follows from another event.",
	TAPE_MISSING = "Your team has not recovered the tape that holds this event.",
	INVALID_STATE = "That state is not one of this event's allowed alternatives.",
	ALREADY_OCCURRED = "This event has already happened.",
	ALREADY_EDITED = "This event has already been edited.",
	NO_CHANGE = "That is already the predicted state.",
	PREREQUISITE = "This event cannot be edited yet.",
	LOCKED = "Too late. The event is already playing out.",
	NO_CHARGES = "No edit charges left.",
	VERSION = "The timeline changed while you were looking. Review the update first.",
}

local function deepCopy(value)
	if type(value) ~= "table" then
		return value
	end
	local out = {}
	for k, v in value do
		out[k] = deepCopy(v)
	end
	return out
end
TimelineCore.deepCopy = deepCopy

local function samePos(a, b)
	return a and b and a.x == b.x and a.z == b.z
end

-- Reverses an A/B patrol by swapping its two end points.
local function flipPatrol(path)
	if #path < 2 then
		return path
	end
	local a, b = path[1].pos, path[2].pos
	for _, key in path do
		if samePos(key.pos, a) then
			key.pos = deepCopy(b)
		elseif samePos(key.pos, b) then
			key.pos = deepCopy(a)
		end
	end
	return path
end

-- Window of time (relative to the anchor) during which an event's Presenter command
-- controls the entity. Used for schedule-conflict validation.
local function presenterWindow(ev, stateDef)
	local cmd = stateDef and stateDef.presenter
	if not cmd then
		return nil
	end
	local start = ev.offset + (cmd.at or 0)
	local length
	if cmd.kind == "Ambush" then
		length = (cmd.windup or 0) + (cmd.maxDuration or 0)
	else
		length = cmd.duration or 0
	end
	return start, start + length
end
TimelineCore.presenterWindow = presenterWindow

function TimelineCore.new(caseDef, options)
	options = options or {}
	local self = setmetatable({}, TimelineCore)
	self.case = caseDef
	self.events = {}
	self.order = {}
	self.records = {}
	self.anchors = {}
	self.version = 0
	self.editLog = {}

	for _, def in caseDef.events do
		local ev = def
		if options.patrolFlip and caseDef.variants and caseDef.variants.patrolFlipEvent == def.id then
			ev = deepCopy(def)
			for _, stateDef in ev.states do
				if stateDef.presenter and stateDef.presenter.path then
					flipPatrol(stateDef.presenter.path)
				end
			end
		end
		self.events[ev.id] = ev
		table.insert(self.order, ev.id)
		self.records[ev.id] = {
			committedState = nil,
			committedBy = nil,
			committedName = nil,
			committedAt = nil,
			edits = 0,
			fired = false,
			lockedState = nil,
			actionsDone = {},
			presenterDone = false,
		}
	end
	return self
end

function TimelineCore:getEvent(id)
	return self.events[id]
end

function TimelineCore:anchorTime(flag)
	return self.anchors[flag]
end

function TimelineCore:setAnchor(flag, now)
	if self.anchors[flag] ~= nil then
		return false
	end
	self.anchors[flag] = now
	self.version += 1
	return true
end

function TimelineCore:fireTime(id)
	local ev = self.events[id]
	local anchor = ev and self.anchors[ev.anchor]
	if not anchor then
		return nil
	end
	return anchor + ev.offset
end

function TimelineCore:effectiveState(id)
	local ev = self.events[id]
	local rec = self.records[id]
	if rec.lockedState then
		return rec.lockedState
	end
	if ev.derivedFrom then
		local sourceState = self:effectiveState(ev.derivedFrom)
		return ev.stateMap[sourceState] or ev.originalState
	end
	return rec.committedState or ev.originalState
end

function TimelineCore:dependents(id)
	local list = {}
	for _, otherId in self.order do
		if self.events[otherId].derivedFrom == id then
			table.insert(list, otherId)
		end
	end
	return list
end

local function hasStarted(self, id)
	local rec = self.records[id]
	if rec.lockedState ~= nil then
		return true
	end
	for _, dep in self:dependents(id) do
		if hasStarted(self, dep) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function TimelineCore:isLocked(id, now)
	local ev = self.events[id]
	if hasStarted(self, id) then
		return true
	end
	local fire = self:fireTime(id)
	if fire and now >= fire - (ev.lockBefore or 5) then
		return true
	end
	-- A consequence that is about to play also locks the source.
	for _, dep in self:dependents(id) do
		local depFire = self:fireTime(dep)
		local depEv = self.events[dep]
		if depFire and now >= depFire - (depEv.lockBefore or ev.lockBefore or 5) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

--[[
	context = {
		flags = { [flag] = true },
		charges = number,
		tapeFound = boolean,
		expectedState = string?     -- the event state the client was looking at
	}
	Returns ok, code, message.
]]
function TimelineCore:validateEdit(id, newState, now, context)
	local ev = self.events[id]
	if not ev then
		return false, "UNKNOWN_EVENT", TimelineCore.Messages.UNKNOWN_EVENT
	end
	if not ev.isEditable then
		return false, "NOT_EDITABLE", TimelineCore.Messages.NOT_EDITABLE
	end
	if not context.tapeFound then
		return false, "TAPE_MISSING", TimelineCore.Messages.TAPE_MISSING
	end
	if type(newState) ~= "string" or not table.find(ev.allowedStates, newState) then
		return false, "INVALID_STATE", TimelineCore.Messages.INVALID_STATE
	end
	local rec = self.records[id]
	if rec.fired then
		return false, "ALREADY_OCCURRED", TimelineCore.Messages.ALREADY_OCCURRED
	end
	if rec.edits >= (ev.maxEdits or 1) then
		local who = rec.committedName and (" by " .. rec.committedName) or ""
		return false, "ALREADY_EDITED", "This event has already been edited" .. who .. "."
	end
	-- Two investigators reviewing the same event: the second commit sees that the
	-- state changed underneath them and is refused without spending a charge.
	if context.expectedState ~= nil and context.expectedState ~= self:effectiveState(id) then
		return false, "VERSION", TimelineCore.Messages.VERSION
	end
	if newState == self:effectiveState(id) then
		return false, "NO_CHANGE", TimelineCore.Messages.NO_CHANGE
	end
	for _, flag in ev.prerequisites or {} do
		if not (context.flags and context.flags[flag]) then
			local label = (self.case.flagLabels and self.case.flagLabels[flag]) or flag
			return false, "PREREQUISITE", "Requires: " .. label .. "."
		end
	end
	if self:isLocked(id, now) then
		return false, "LOCKED", TimelineCore.Messages.LOCKED
	end
	if (context.charges or 0) < (ev.editCost or 1) then
		return false, "NO_CHARGES", TimelineCore.Messages.NO_CHARGES
	end
	return true, "OK", nil
end

-- Applies a validated edit. Returns a summary of what changed.
function TimelineCore:commit(id, newState, byUserId, byName, now)
	local ev = self.events[id]
	local rec = self.records[id]
	local from = self:effectiveState(id)
	local before = {}
	for _, dep in self:dependents(id) do
		before[dep] = self:effectiveState(dep)
	end
	rec.committedState = newState
	rec.committedBy = byUserId
	rec.committedName = byName
	rec.committedAt = now
	rec.edits += 1
	self.version += 1
	local consequences = {}
	for _, dep in self:dependents(id) do
		table.insert(consequences, { eventId = dep, from = before[dep], to = self:effectiveState(dep) })
	end
	local entry = {
		eventId = id,
		from = from,
		to = newState,
		by = byUserId,
		byName = byName,
		at = now,
		cost = ev.editCost or 1,
		consequences = consequences,
	}
	table.insert(self.editLog, entry)
	return entry
end

local function lockState(rec, state)
	if rec.lockedState == nil then
		rec.lockedState = state
	end
end

--[[
	Advances the timeline. Returns a list of outputs in time order:
	  { kind = "action", eventId, action, time }
	  { kind = "presenter", eventId, command, startTime }
	  { kind = "fired", eventId, state }
]]
function TimelineCore:update(now)
	local out = {}
	for _, id in self.order do
		local ev = self.events[id]
		local fire = self:fireTime(id)
		if fire then
			local rec = self.records[id]
			local state = self:effectiveState(id)
			local stateDef = ev.states[state]
			if stateDef then
				for index, action in stateDef.actions or {} do
					local t = fire + (action.at or 0)
					if now >= t and not rec.actionsDone[index] then
						lockState(rec, state)
						rec.actionsDone[index] = true
						table.insert(out, { kind = "action", eventId = id, action = action, time = t })
					end
				end
				local cmd = stateDef.presenter
				if cmd and not rec.presenterDone then
					local t = fire + (cmd.at or 0)
					if now >= t then
						lockState(rec, state)
						rec.presenterDone = true
						table.insert(out, { kind = "presenter", eventId = id, command = cmd, startTime = t })
					end
				end
			end
			if now >= fire and not rec.fired then
				lockState(rec, state)
				rec.fired = true
				self.version += 1
				table.insert(out, { kind = "fired", eventId = id, state = state })
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(out, function(a, b)
		local ta = a.time or a.startTime or 0
		local tb = b.time or b.startTime or 0
		return ta < tb
	end)
	return out
end

-- Serialisable view for clients.
function TimelineCore:snapshot(now)
	local events = {}
	for _, id in self.order do
		local rec = self.records[id]
		events[id] = {
			state = self:effectiveState(id),
			fired = rec.fired,
			fireTime = self:fireTime(id),
			edits = rec.edits,
			locked = self:isLocked(id, now),
			committed = rec.committedState and {
				state = rec.committedState,
				by = rec.committedBy,
				name = rec.committedName,
				at = rec.committedAt,
			} or nil,
		}
	end
	return {
		version = self.version,
```
