```lua
		anchors = table.clone(self.anchors),
		events = events,
	}
end

-- Presenter paths actually in use (after seeded variants) for footage rendering.
function TimelineCore:eventDefinitions()
	return self.events
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Static validation of a case definition. Returns a list of error strings.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function TimelineCore.validateCase(caseDef, mapLayout)
	local errors = {}
	local function fail(fmt, ...)
		table.insert(errors, string.format(fmt, ...))
	end

	local byId = {}
	for _, ev in caseDef.events do
		if byId[ev.id] then
			fail("duplicate event id %s", ev.id)
		end
		byId[ev.id] = ev
	end
	local anchors = {}
	for _, a in caseDef.anchors or {} do
		anchors[a] = true
	end

	for _, ev in caseDef.events do
		local id = ev.id
		if not anchors[ev.anchor] then
			fail("%s: anchor %s is not declared in case.anchors", id, tostring(ev.anchor))
		end
		if type(ev.offset) ~= "number" or ev.offset < 0 then
			fail("%s: offset must be a non-negative number", id)
		end
		local tape = caseDef.tapes[ev.tape]
		if not tape then
			fail("%s: unknown tape %s", id, tostring(ev.tape))
		else
			if tape.anchor ~= ev.anchor then
				fail("%s: anchor %s differs from its tape's anchor %s", id, ev.anchor, tape.anchor)
			end
			if ev.offset > tape.duration then
				fail("%s: offset %d is past the end of %s", id, ev.offset, ev.tape)
			end
			for _, cam in ev.cameras or {} do
				if not table.find(tape.cameras, cam) then
					fail("%s: camera %s is not recorded on %s", id, cam, ev.tape)
				end
				if mapLayout and not mapLayout.Cameras[cam] then
					fail("%s: camera %s does not exist on the map", id, cam)
				end
			end
		end
		if not table.find(ev.allowedStates, ev.originalState) then
			fail("%s: originalState %s is not allowed", id, tostring(ev.originalState))
		end
		for _, state in ev.allowedStates do
			if not ev.states[state] then
				fail("%s: allowed state %s has no definition", id, state)
			end
			if not (ev.stateLabels and ev.stateLabels[state]) then
				fail("%s: allowed state %s has no label", id, state)
			end
		end
		if ev.isEditable then
			if ev.derivedFrom then
				fail("%s: an editable event cannot also be derived", id)
			end
			if type(ev.editCost) ~= "number" or ev.editCost < 1 then
				fail("%s: editable events must cost at least one charge", id)
			end
			if #ev.allowedStates < 2 then
				fail("%s: editable events need at least two states", id)
			end
		end
		if ev.derivedFrom then
			local src = byId[ev.derivedFrom]
			if not src then
				fail("%s: derivedFrom %s does not exist", id, ev.derivedFrom)
			else
				for _, srcState in src.allowedStates do
					local mapped = ev.stateMap and ev.stateMap[srcState]
					if not mapped then
						fail("%s: stateMap does not cover %s.%s", id, src.id, srcState)
					elseif not table.find(ev.allowedStates, mapped) then
						fail("%s: stateMap maps %s to unknown state %s", id, srcState, mapped)
					end
				end
				if not table.find(src.consequenceEventIds or {}, id) then
					fail("%s: source %s does not list it as a consequence", id, src.id)
				end
			end
		end
		for _, cid in ev.consequenceEventIds or {} do
			local c = byId[cid]
			if not c then
				fail("%s: consequence %s does not exist", id, cid)
			elseif c.derivedFrom ~= id then
				fail("%s: consequence %s is not derived from it", id, cid)
			end
		end
		-- Actions may only run before the fire time if edits are already locked by then.
		local lockBefore = ev.lockBefore or 5
		for state, stateDef in ev.states do
			for _, action in stateDef.actions or {} do
				if (action.at or 0) < -lockBefore then
					fail("%s.%s: action at %s runs before edits lock", id, state, tostring(action.at))
				end
			end
			local cmd = stateDef.presenter
			if cmd and cmd.path then
				local last = -1
				for i, key in cmd.path do
					if key.t <= last then
						fail("%s.%s: path keyframe %d is out of order", id, state, i)
					end
					last = key.t
				end
				if cmd.path[1] and cmd.path[1].t ~= 0 then
					fail("%s.%s: path must start at t = 0", id, state)
				end
				if cmd.duration and last > cmd.duration then
					fail("%s.%s: path runs past the command duration", id, state)
				end
			end
		end
		-- A derived event on the same anchor must not start before its source locks.
		if ev.derivedFrom and byId[ev.derivedFrom] and byId[ev.derivedFrom].anchor == ev.anchor then
			local src = byId[ev.derivedFrom]
			local srcLock = src.offset - (src.lockBefore or 5)
			for state, stateDef in ev.states do
				for _, action in stateDef.actions or {} do
					if ev.offset + (action.at or 0) < srcLock then
						fail("%s.%s: action starts before %s locks", id, state, src.id)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Derivation cycles.
	for _, ev in caseDef.events do
		local seen = {}
		local node = ev
		while node and node.derivedFrom do
			if seen[node.id] then
				fail("derivation cycle through %s", ev.id)
				break
			end
			seen[node.id] = true
			node = byId[node.derivedFrom]
		end
	end

	-- Contradictory Presenter schedules: for every combination of editable
	-- states, no two Presenter windows on the same anchor may overlap.
	local editable = {}
	for _, ev in caseDef.events do
		if ev.isEditable then
			table.insert(editable, ev)
		end
	end
	local function effective(ev, choice)
		if ev.derivedFrom then
			return ev.stateMap[effective(byId[ev.derivedFrom], choice)]
		end
		return choice[ev.id] or ev.originalState
	end
	local combos = 1
	for _, ev in editable do
		combos *= #ev.allowedStates
	end
	for combo = 0, combos - 1 do
		local choice = {}
		local rest = combo
		for _, ev in editable do
			local n = #ev.allowedStates
			choice[ev.id] = ev.allowedStates[(rest % n) + 1]
			rest = rest // n
		end
		local windows = {}
		for _, ev in caseDef.events do
			local s, e = presenterWindow(ev, ev.states[effective(ev, choice)])
			if s then
				windows[ev.anchor] = windows[ev.anchor] or {}
				table.insert(windows[ev.anchor], { id = ev.id, s = s, e = e })
			end
		end
		for anchor, list in windows do
			for i = 1, #list do
				for j = i + 1, #list do
					local a, b = list[i], list[j]
					if a.s < b.e and b.s < a.e then
						fail("presenter windows of %s and %s overlap on anchor %s", a.id, b.id, anchor)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Hidden frames.
	for _, hf in caseDef.hiddenFrames or {} do
		local tape = caseDef.tapes[hf.tape]
		if not tape then
			fail("hidden frame %s: unknown tape", hf.id)
		elseif not table.find(tape.cameras, hf.camera) then
			fail("hidden frame %s: camera %s not on %s", hf.id, hf.camera, hf.tape)
		elseif hf.t > tape.duration then
			fail("hidden frame %s: past the end of the tape", hf.id)
		end
	end

	-- De-duplicate (the overlap check repeats per combination).
	local unique, seenErr = {}, {}
	for _, e in errors do
		if not seenErr[e] then
			seenErr[e] = true
			table.insert(unique, e)
		end
	end
	return unique
end

return TimelineCore
```

## src/shared/Core/Variants.luau

```lua
--[[
	Case variants derived from a run seed. Repeat runs move selected items, change
	the keypad codes and the Loop's odd detail, and may reverse a patrol, while the
	story logic and event graph stay the same.
]]

local Rng = require(script.Parent.Parent.Util.Rng)

local Variants = {}

local function code(rng, digits)
	local out = {}
	for i = 1, digits do
		-- No leading zero so the code reads clearly on subtitles.
		out[i] = tostring(i == 1 and rng:int(1, 9) or rng:int(0, 9))
	end
	return table.concat(out)
end

function Variants.compute(caseDef, seed: number)
	local result = {
		seed = seed,
		itemSpawns = {},
	}

	local spawnRng = Rng.forPurpose(seed, "items")
	local itemIds = {}
	for id in caseDef.items do
		table.insert(itemIds, id)
	end
	table.sort(itemIds)
	for _, id in itemIds do
		local item = caseDef.items[id]
		if item.spawns and #item.spawns > 0 then
			result.itemSpawns[id] = spawnRng:int(1, #item.spawns)
		end
	end

	local digits = (caseDef.keypad and caseDef.keypad.digits) or 4
	local codeRng = Rng.forPurpose(seed, "keypad")
	result.code = code(codeRng, digits)
	repeat
		result.fakeCode = code(codeRng, digits)
	until result.fakeCode ~= result.code

	if caseDef.loop then
		local loopRng = Rng.forPurpose(seed, "loop")
		result.loopOdd = caseDef.loop.props[loopRng:int(1, #caseDef.loop.props)].id
	end

	result.patrolFlip = Rng.forPurpose(seed, "patrol"):int(0, 1) == 1
	return result
end

-- Week-long fixed seed for the Archive Challenge leaderboard.
function Variants.weeklySeed(unixTime: number): (number, number)
	local week = unixTime // (7 * 24 * 3600)
	return 7919 * week + 104729, week
end

return Variants
```

## src/shared/Util/Format.luau

```lua
local Format = {}

local FRAMES_PER_SECOND = 30

-- 00:MM:SS broadcast clock. Negative values clamp to zero.
function Format.clock(seconds: number): string
	seconds = math.max(0, math.floor(seconds))
	local h = seconds // 3600
	local m = (seconds % 3600) // 60
	local s = seconds % 60
	return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", h, m, s)
end

-- MM:SS for short countdowns.
function Format.short(seconds: number): string
	seconds = math.max(0, math.floor(seconds))
	return string.format("%02d:%02d", seconds // 60, seconds % 60)
end

-- HH:MM:SS:FF tape timecode.
function Format.timecode(seconds: number): string
	seconds = math.max(0, seconds)
	local whole = math.floor(seconds)
	local frames = math.floor((seconds - whole) * FRAMES_PER_SECOND)
	return string.format("%s:%02d", Format.clock(whole), frames)
end

function Format.frameNumber(seconds: number): number
	return math.floor(math.max(0, seconds) * FRAMES_PER_SECOND)
end

-- Relative tape offset such as "T+02:30".
function Format.relative(seconds: number): string
	local sign = seconds < 0 and "-" or "+"
	return "T" .. sign .. Format.short(math.abs(seconds))
end

Format.FRAMES_PER_SECOND = FRAMES_PER_SECOND

return Format
```

## src/shared/Util/Rng.luau

```lua
--[[
	Deterministic xorshift32 generator.

	Case variants (item spots, keypad codes, the Loop's odd detail, patrol phase)
	must come out identically on every server and in tests for the same seed, so the
	game never uses Roblox's Random for anything that affects gameplay.
]]

local Rng = {}
Rng.__index = Rng

function Rng.new(seed: number)
	local state = math.floor(seed) % 4294967296
	if state == 0 then
		state = 0x9E3779B9
	end
	local self = setmetatable({ state = state }, Rng)
	-- Discard the first outputs so nearby seeds diverge quickly.
	for _ = 1, 4 do
		self:nextU32()
	end
	return self
end

function Rng:nextU32(): number
	local x = self.state
	x = bit32.bxor(x, bit32.lshift(x, 13))
	x = bit32.bxor(x, bit32.rshift(x, 17))
	x = bit32.bxor(x, bit32.lshift(x, 5))
	self.state = x
	return x
end

-- Float in [0, 1).
function Rng:float(): number
	return self:nextU32() / 4294967296
end

-- Integer in [low, high], inclusive.
function Rng:int(low: number, high: number): number
	return low + (self:nextU32() % (high - low + 1))
end

function Rng:pick(list)
	return list[self:int(1, #list)]
end

-- Derives an independent generator for a named purpose so adding a new random
-- choice never shifts the results of existing ones.
function Rng.forPurpose(seed: number, purpose: string)
	local h = 2166136261
	for i = 1, #purpose do
		h = bit32.bxor(h, string.byte(purpose, i))
		-- 16777619 = 2^24 + 403; split so the product stays exact in a double.
		h = (bit32.lshift(h, 24) + h * 403) % 4294967296
	end
	return Rng.new(bit32.bxor(math.floor(seed) % 4294967296, h))
end

return Rng
```

## src/shared/Util/Signal.luau

```lua
-- Minimal synchronous signal used by client state stores.
local Signal = {}
Signal.__index = Signal

function Signal.new()
	return setmetatable({ handlers = {} }, Signal)
end

function Signal:Connect(fn)
	local handlers = self.handlers
	local entry = { fn = fn, connected = true }
	table.insert(handlers, entry)
	return {
		Disconnect = function()
			entry.connected = false
			local index = table.find(handlers, entry)
			if index then
				table.remove(handlers, index)
			end
		end,
	}
end

function Signal:Fire(...)
	-- Copy so handlers can disconnect while firing.
	local snapshot = table.clone(self.handlers)
	for _, entry in snapshot do
		if entry.connected then
			local ok, err = pcall(entry.fn, ...)
			if not ok then
				warn("Signal handler error: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end
end

return Signal
```

## src/shared/Util/Vec.luau

```lua
--[[
	Plain-table 3D vectors ({x=, y=, z=}) for the engine-independent game core.
	Server glue converts them to Vector3 at the boundary.
]]

local Vec = {}

function Vec.new(x: number, y: number, z: number)
	return { x = x, y = y, z = z }
end

function Vec.sub(a, b)
	return { x = a.x - b.x, y = a.y - b.y, z = a.z - b.z }
end

function Vec.add(a, b)
	return { x = a.x + b.x, y = a.y + b.y, z = a.z + b.z }
end

function Vec.scale(a, s: number)
	return { x = a.x * s, y = a.y * s, z = a.z * s }
end

function Vec.length(a): number
	return math.sqrt(a.x * a.x + a.y * a.y + a.z * a.z)
end

function Vec.distance(a, b): number
	return Vec.length(Vec.sub(a, b))
end

-- Horizontal (XZ) distance; the Presenter and players share a floor plane.
function Vec.flatDistance(a, b): number
	local dx, dz = a.x - b.x, a.z - b.z
	return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
end

function Vec.lerp(a, b, t: number)
	return {
		x = a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t,
		y = a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t,
		z = a.z + (b.z - a.z) * t,
	}
end

function Vec.copy(a)
	return { x = a.x, y = a.y, z = a.z }
end

return Vec
```

## src/shared/World/Figures.luau

```lua
--[[
	The Presenter and footage silhouettes.

	The Presenter: one readable silhouette - tall, narrow-shouldered, long limbs, an
	outdated formal suit, and a small television where the face should be. Built from
	a handful of parts welded to a HumanoidRootPart so the server can move it with
	Humanoid:MoveTo and PathfindingService. No gore, no high part count.

	Height is 8.4 studs so it fits the 9-stud doors and pathfinding agent height.
]]

local Props = require(script.Parent.Props)
local Outfits = require(script.Parent.Outfits)

local Figures = {}

Figures.PRESENTER_HEIGHT = 8.4
Figures.LEG_LENGTH = 4.2

local SUIT = Color3.fromRGB(58, 52, 46)
local SUIT_DARK = Color3.fromRGB(38, 34, 30)
local SHIRT = Color3.fromRGB(176, 170, 152)
local SKIN = Color3.fromRGB(150, 140, 126)
local GHOST = Color3.fromRGB(14, 16, 14)

local function weldTo(root, p)
	p.Anchored = false
	p.Massless = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	Props.weld(root, p)
end

--[[
	opts.ghost: build an anchored, all-black silhouette for footage/ghost replays
	(no Humanoid). Otherwise builds the live entity rig.
	Origin: feet at y = 0, facing -Z.
]]
function Figures.presenter(parent, opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local ghost = opts.ghost == true
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = ghost and "PresenterGhost" or "Presenter"

	local legs = Figures.LEG_LENGTH
	local function c(col)
		return ghost and GHOST or col
	end
	local partOpts = { collide = false, shadow = not ghost, material = Enum.Material.Fabric }

	local root = Props.part(model, "HumanoidRootPart", Vector3.new(2, 2, 1), CFrame.new(0, legs + 1, 0), Color3.new(0, 0, 0), {
		transparency = 1,
		collide = not ghost,
		shadow = false,
		anchored = ghost,
	})
	local pieces = {}
	local function add(name, size, cf, col, material)
		local o = table.clone(partOpts)
		o.material = material or partOpts.material
		local p = Props.part(model, name, size, cf, c(col), o)
		table.insert(pieces, p)
		return p
	end

	add("LeftLeg", Vector3.new(0.65, legs, 0.7), CFrame.new(-0.42, legs / 2, 0), SUIT_DARK)
	add("RightLeg", Vector3.new(0.65, legs, 0.7), CFrame.new(0.42, legs / 2, 0), SUIT_DARK)
	add("LeftShoe", Vector3.new(0.7, 0.3, 1.1), CFrame.new(-0.42, 0.15, -0.15), Color3.fromRGB(20, 18, 16), Enum.Material.Leather)
	add("RightShoe", Vector3.new(0.7, 0.3, 1.1), CFrame.new(0.42, 0.15, -0.15), Color3.fromRGB(20, 18, 16), Enum.Material.Leather)
	local torsoY = legs + 1.3
	add("Torso", Vector3.new(1.9, 2.6, 0.95), CFrame.new(0, torsoY, 0), SUIT)
	add("Shirt", Vector3.new(0.5, 1.4, 0.05), CFrame.new(0, torsoY + 0.5, -0.49), SHIRT, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
	add("Tie", Vector3.new(0.22, 1.2, 0.06), CFrame.new(0, torsoY + 0.45, -0.52), Color3.fromRGB(70, 62, 56), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
	add("LapelL", Vector3.new(0.45, 1.5, 0.06), CFrame.new(-0.42, torsoY + 0.45, -0.5) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(-12)), SUIT_DARK)
	add("LapelR", Vector3.new(0.45, 1.5, 0.06), CFrame.new(0.42, torsoY + 0.45, -0.5) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(12)), SUIT_DARK)
	-- Long arms hanging slightly too low.
	local armLength = 3.5
	local shoulderY = torsoY + 1.15
	local leftArm = add("LeftArm", Vector3.new(0.5, armLength, 0.55), CFrame.new(-1.22, shoulderY - armLength / 2, 0), SUIT)
	local rightArm = add("RightArm", Vector3.new(0.5, armLength, 0.55), CFrame.new(1.22, shoulderY - armLength / 2, 0), SUIT)
	leftArm:SetAttribute("Sway", -1)
	rightArm:SetAttribute("Sway", 1)
	add("LeftHand", Vector3.new(0.4, 0.7, 0.45), CFrame.new(-1.22, shoulderY - armLength - 0.3, 0), SKIN, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
	add("RightHand", Vector3.new(0.4, 0.7, 0.45), CFrame.new(1.22, shoulderY - armLength - 0.3, 0), SKIN, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
	add("Neck", Vector3.new(0.45, 0.3, 0.45), CFrame.new(0, torsoY + 1.45, 0), SKIN, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)

	-- Television head.
	local headY = Figures.PRESENTER_HEIGHT - 0.6
	local head = add("Head", Vector3.new(1.45, 1.2, 1.25), CFrame.new(0, headY, 0), Color3.fromRGB(84, 76, 66), Enum.Material.Plastic)
	add("Antenna", Vector3.new(0.08, 0.7, 0.08), CFrame.new(0.3, headY + 0.9, 0.1) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(-20)), Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30), Enum.Material.Metal)
	local screen = add("Screen", Vector3.new(1.15, 0.9, 0.05), CFrame.new(0, headY, -0.64), Color3.fromRGB(200, 205, 200), Enum.Material.Neon)
	if ghost then
		screen.Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 150)
	else
		-- The client animates static on this surface.
		local gui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
		gui.Name = "Static"
		gui.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
		gui.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.PixelsPerStud
		gui.PixelsPerStud = 40
		gui.LightInfluence = 0
		gui.Brightness = 1.5
		gui.Parent = screen
		local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
		bg.Name = "Background"
		bg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 150)
		bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
		bg.Parent = gui
		model:AddTag("Presenter")
	end
	head:SetAttribute("PresenterHead", true)

	for _, p in pieces do
		if ghost then
			p.Anchored = true
		else
			weldTo(root, p)
		end
	end

	model.PrimaryPart = root
	model:SetAttribute("RootHeight", legs + 1)
	if not ghost then
		local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")
		humanoid.Name = "Humanoid"
		humanoid.RigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
		humanoid.HipHeight = legs
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = 6
		humanoid.AutoRotate = true
		humanoid.RequiresNeck = false
		humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
		humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
		humanoid.HealthDisplayType = Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOff
		humanoid.MaxHealth = math.huge
		humanoid.Health = math.huge
		humanoid.Parent = model
		root.CanCollide = true
	end
	model.Parent = parent
	return model
end

-- A blocky investigator silhouette for footage (the team as the tape sees it).
-- A person in field kit, for CCTV footage and rewind ghosts.
function Figures.investigator(parent, colour)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "InvestigatorGhost"
	local col = colour or Color3.fromRGB(40, 46, 42)
	local function add(name, size, cf, c, material)
		return Props.part(model, name, size, cf, c or col, { collide = false, shadow = false, material = material or Enum.Material.Fabric })
	end
	local root = add("Torso", Vector3.new(2, 2, 1), CFrame.new(0, 3, 0))
	for _, side in { -1, 1 } do
		add(side < 0 and "LeftLeg" or "RightLeg", Vector3.new(0.92, 2, 0.95), CFrame.new(side * 0.5, 1, 0), col:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.35))
		add(side < 0 and "LeftArm" or "RightArm", Vector3.new(0.8, 1.9, 0.85), CFrame.new(side * 1.42, 3.02, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(side * 4)))
		add("Boot", Vector3.new(0.95, 0.35, 1.15), CFrame.new(side * 0.5, 0.18, -0.08), Color3.fromRGB(30, 26, 22), Enum.Material.Leather)
	end
	add("Head", Vector3.new(1.15, 1.2, 1.15), CFrame.new(0, 4.62, 0), colour or Color3.fromRGB(150, 128, 110), Enum.Material.Plastic)
	add("Neck", Vector3.new(0.6, 0.3, 0.6), CFrame.new(0, 4.05, 0), colour or Color3.fromRGB(150, 128, 110), Enum.Material.Plastic)
	Outfits.apply(model, "Field", colour or Color3.fromRGB(92, 108, 122))
	model.PrimaryPart = root
	model:SetAttribute("RootHeight", 3)
	model.Parent = parent
	return model
end

-- Height of a figure's PrimaryPart above its feet; place figures with
-- model:PivotTo(feetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, Figures.rootHeight(model), 0)).
function Figures.rootHeight(model)
	return model:GetAttribute("RootHeight") or 0
end

return Figures
```

## src/shared/World/MapBuilder.luau

```lua
--[[
	Builds Broadcast Station 04 and the Division field office from MapLayout.

	Greybox-first, modular construction: every room is a floor, a ceiling and four
	walls with openings cut where MapLayout puts doors and windows. Props are simple
	boxes with readable silhouettes. All interactive things carry attributes and
	tags so the server can find them even after the map is edited in Studio:

	  Station04
	    Rooms/<RoomId>/{Shell, Lights, Props, Signs}
	    Doors/<DoorId>        Model, attributes DoorId, Kind, SwingSign; Panel part(s)
	    Windows/Glass         attribute SeeThrough
	    Cameras/<CamId>       CCTV housings
	    Anchors/<InteractId>  invisible parts that hold ProximityPrompts
	    LoopProps/<PropId>    Normal / Odd variants for the Loop anomaly
	    Spawns/Spawn<n>
	  FieldOffice (HQ)
]]

local MapLayout = require(script.Parent.Parent.Config.MapLayout)
local Rng = require(script.Parent.Parent.Util.Rng)
local Props = require(script.Parent.Props)

local MapBuilder = {}

local T = MapLayout.WALL_THICKNESS
local DOOR_H = MapLayout.DOOR_HEIGHT
local part = Props.part

local function V(p, y)
	return Vector3.new(p.x, y or p.y or 0, p.z)
end

local function folder(parent, name)
	local f = Instance.new("Folder")
	f.Name = name
	f.Parent = parent
	return f
end

-- CFrame at a floor point whose front (-Z) faces the given direction.
local function facing(pos, dir)
	return CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + dir)
end

local NORTH, SOUTH, EAST, WEST = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Room shell
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function doorTop(door)
	if door.kind == "opening" then
		local lowest = math.huge
		for _, id in door.rooms do
			lowest = math.min(lowest, MapLayout.Rooms[id].h)
		end
		return math.max(DOOR_H, lowest - 1)
	elseif door.kind == "freight" then
		return 10
	end
	return DOOR_H
end

-- Openings along one edge of a room: { a, b, bottom, top }
local function edgeOpenings(roomId, room, edge)
	local list = {}
	local function onEdge(entry)
		if not table.find(entry.rooms, roomId) then
			return false
		end
		if entry.plane == "z" then
			return (edge == "N" and entry.at == room.z0) or (edge == "S" and entry.at == room.z1)
		end
		return (edge == "W" and entry.at == room.x0) or (edge == "E" and entry.at == room.x1)
	end
	for _, door in MapLayout.Doors do
		if onEdge(door) then
			table.insert(list, { a = door.center - door.width / 2, b = door.center + door.width / 2, bottom = 0, top = doorTop(door) })
		end
	end
	for _, win in MapLayout.Windows do
		if onEdge(win) then
			table.insert(list, { a = win.center - win.width / 2, b = win.center + win.width / 2, bottom = win.y0, top = win.y1 })
		end
	end
	table.sort(list, function(x, y)
		return x.a < y.a
	end)
	return list
end

-- Room style names (MapLayout) -> surfaces. Concrete walls are painted block.
local WALL_SURFACE = { Plaster = "plaster", SmoothPlastic = "plaster", Concrete = "breezeBlock", Fabric = "acousticFoam", Wood = "panelling" }
local FLOOR_SURFACE = { Slate = "lino", Carpet = "carpet", Concrete = "concrete", DiamondPlate = "checkerPlate" }

local function wallSurface(room)
	return WALL_SURFACE[room.wallMaterial or "Plaster"] or "plaster"
end

-- The plane of a wall's room-facing side, offset into the room by `inset`.
local function interiorFace(room, edge, along, y, inset)
	if edge == "N" then
		return Vector3.new(along, y, room.z0 + T + inset)
	elseif edge == "S" then
		return Vector3.new(along, y, room.z1 - T - inset)
	elseif edge == "W" then
		return Vector3.new(room.x0 + T + inset, y, along)
	end
	return Vector3.new(room.x1 - T - inset, y, along)
end

local function faceSize(edge, len, height, thickness)
	if edge == "N" or edge == "S" then
		return Vector3.new(len, height, thickness)
	end
	return Vector3.new(thickness, height, len)
end

local function wallPiece(parent, room, edge, a, b, y0, y1, colour, surface)
	if b - a < 0.01 or y1 - y0 < 0.01 then
		return
	end
	local len, height = b - a, y1 - y0
	local mid, y = (a + b) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2
	local size, pos
	if edge == "N" then
		size, pos = Vector3.new(len, height, T), Vector3.new(mid, y, room.z0 + T / 2)
	elseif edge == "S" then
		size, pos = Vector3.new(len, height, T), Vector3.new(mid, y, room.z1 - T / 2)
	elseif edge == "W" then
		size, pos = Vector3.new(T, height, len), Vector3.new(room.x0 + T / 2, y, mid)
	else
		size, pos = Vector3.new(T, height, len), Vector3.new(room.x1 - T / 2, y, mid)
	end
	part(parent, "Wall", size, CFrame.new(pos), colour, { surface = surface })
	-- Two-tone institutional paint: a darker lower band (with a timber chair
	-- rail on plaster) along every piece that reaches the floor.
	if room.outdoor or y0 > 0 or len < 0.5 or (surface ~= "plaster" and surface ~= "breezeBlock") then
		return
	end
	local band = math.min(y1, surface == "plaster" and 3 or 4)
	local inset = len - 0.02
	part(parent, "Wainscot", faceSize(edge, inset, band, 0.04), CFrame.new(interiorFace(room, edge, mid, band / 2, 0.02)), Props.darker(colour, 0.22), {
		surface = surface,
		collide = false,
		shadow = false,
	})
	if surface == "plaster" and y1 > band + 0.2 then
		part(parent, "ChairRail", faceSize(edge, inset, 0.18, 0.12), CFrame.new(interiorFace(room, edge, mid, band, 0.06)), room.trim, {
			surface = "veneer",
			collide = false,
			shadow = false,
		})
	end
end

local function buildWall(parent, roomId, room, edge, height)
	local wallColour = room.wall
	local material = wallSurface(room)
	local s0, s1
	if edge == "N" or edge == "S" then
		s0, s1 = room.x0, room.x1
	else
		s0, s1 = room.z0, room.z1
	end
	local cursor = s0
	for _, o in edgeOpenings(roomId, room, edge) do
		wallPiece(parent, room, edge, cursor, o.a, 0, height, wallColour, material)
		if o.bottom > 0 then
			wallPiece(parent, room, edge, o.a, o.b, 0, o.bottom, wallColour, material)
		end
		if o.top < height then
			wallPiece(parent, room, edge, o.a, o.b, o.top, height, wallColour, material)
		end
		cursor = math.max(cursor, o.b)
	end
	wallPiece(parent, room, edge, cursor, s1, 0, height, wallColour, material)
	-- Skirting board: a darker trim line that helps rooms read in low light.
	if not room.outdoor then
		local trimParent = parent
		if edge == "N" or edge == "S" then
			local z = edge == "N" and (room.z0 + T + 0.05) or (room.z1 - T - 0.05)
			part(trimParent, "Skirting", Vector3.new(room.x1 - room.x0 - 2 * T, 0.6, 0.1), CFrame.new((room.x0 + room.x1) / 2, 0.3, z), room.trim, { collide = false, shadow = false, surface = "veneer" })
		else
			local x = edge == "W" and (room.x0 + T + 0.05) or (room.x1 - T - 0.05)
			part(trimParent, "Skirting", Vector3.new(0.1, 0.6, room.z1 - room.z0 - 2 * T), CFrame.new(x, 0.3, (room.z0 + room.z1) / 2), room.trim, { collide = false, shadow = false, surface = "veneer" })
		end
	end
end

-- Office-like rooms get a suspended tile ceiling; plant rooms bare concrete.
local function hasDropCeiling(room)
	local surface = wallSurface(room)
	return not room.outdoor and room.h <= 13 and (surface == "plaster" or surface == "panelling")
end

local function hashString(text)
	local h = 2166136261
	for i = 1, #text do
		h = bit32.bxor(h, string.byte(text, i))
		h = (h * 16777619) % 4294967296
	end
	return h
end

-- Wear and tear: water stains, scuffs, worn walkways, ceiling grid and vents.
-- Deterministic per room so every server (and every footage clone) matches.
local function dressRoom(shell, roomId, room)
	local rng = Rng.new(hashString(roomId))
	local w, d = room.x1 - room.x0, room.z1 - room.z0
	local cx, cz = (room.x0 + room.x1) / 2, (room.z0 + room.z1) / 2
	local detail = Props.detail

	-- Suspended ceiling grid: T-bars every four studs.
	if hasDropCeiling(room) then
		local y = room.h - 0.03
		local bar = Color3.fromRGB(196, 192, 180)
		for x = room.x0 + 4, room.x1 - 1, 4 do
			detail(shell, "TBar", Vector3.new(0.12, 0.06, d - 2 * T), CFrame.new(x, y, cz), bar, "paintedMetal")
		end
		for z = room.z0 + 4, room.z1 - 1, 4 do
			detail(shell, "TBar", Vector3.new(w - 2 * T, 0.06, 0.12), CFrame.new(cx, y, z), bar, "paintedMetal")
		end
		-- A couple of stained tiles.
		for _ = 1, math.clamp(math.floor(w * d / 600), 1, 4) do
			local tx = room.x0 + 4 * rng:int(1, math.max(1, math.floor(w / 4) - 1)) - 2
			local tz = room.z0 + 4 * rng:int(1, math.max(1, math.floor(d / 4) - 1)) - 2
			detail(shell, "StainedTile", Vector3.new(3.6, 0.02, 3.6), CFrame.new(tx, room.h - 0.012, tz), Color3.fromRGB(150, 120, 74), "ceilingTile", { transparency = 0.45 + rng:float() * 0.3 })
		end
	end

	if room.outdoor then
		return
	end

	-- Air-handling grille in the ceiling.
	local gx = room.x0 + w * (0.25 + rng:float() * 0.5)
	local gz = room.z0 + d * (0.25 + rng:float() * 0.5)
	detail(shell, "VentGrille", Vector3.new(2, 0.06, 2), CFrame.new(gx, room.h - 0.04, gz), Color3.fromRGB(150, 148, 140), "paintedMetal")
	for i = 0, 5 do
		detail(shell, "VentSlat", Vector3.new(1.8, 0.07, 0.1), CFrame.new(gx, room.h - 0.08, gz - 0.75 + i * 0.3), Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 24), "blackPlastic")
	end

	-- Stains on the walls: damp creeping down from the ceiling, scuffs and dirt
	-- along the bottom. The field office is kept tidier.
	local count = room.hq and 1 or math.clamp(math.floor((w + d) / 12), 2, 9)
	local edges = { "N", "S", "W", "E" }
	for _ = 1, count do
		local edge = rng:pick(edges)
		local s0, s1
		if edge == "N" or edge == "S" then
			s0, s1 = room.x0 + T + 1, room.x1 - T - 1
		else
			s0, s1 = room.z0 + T + 1, room.z1 - T - 1
		end
		local along = s0 + (s1 - s0) * rng:float()
		local clear = true
		for _, o in edgeOpenings(roomId, room, edge) do
			if along > o.a - 2 and along < o.b + 2 then
				clear = false
			end
		end
		if clear and s1 > s0 then
			local top = rng:float() < 0.5
			local width = top and (0.8 + rng:float() * 1.6) or (1.5 + rng:float() * 3)
			local height = top and (1.2 + rng:float() * 2.4) or (0.5 + rng:float() * 0.9)
			local y = top and (room.h - height / 2) or (0.6 + height / 2)
			local colour = top and Color3.fromRGB(96, 78, 52) or Color3.fromRGB(34, 30, 26)
			detail(shell, top and "DampStain" or "Scuff", faceSize(edge, width, height, 0.02), CFrame.new(interiorFace(room, edge, along, y, 0.05)), colour, "concrete", {
				transparency = 0.72 + rng:float() * 0.16,
			})
		end
	end

	-- Worn walkway down the middle of corridors and halls.
	if string.find(roomId, "Corridor") or string.find(roomId, "Hall") or string.find(roomId, "Passage") then
		local along = w >= d
		local size = along and Vector3.new(w - 2 * T, 0.02, math.min(d * 0.45, 3.5)) or Vector3.new(math.min(w * 0.45, 3.5), 0.02, d - 2 * T)
		detail(shell, "WornPath", size, CFrame.new(cx, 0.011, cz), Props.darker(room.floor, 0.2), FLOOR_SURFACE[room.floorMaterial or "Concrete"] or "concrete", { transparency = 0.55 })
	end
end

local function buildShell(roomFolder, roomId, room)
	local shell = folder(roomFolder, "Shell")
	local w, d = room.x1 - room.x0, room.z1 - room.z0
	local cx, cz = (room.x0 + room.x1) / 2, (room.z0 + room.z1) / 2
	local floor = part(shell, "Floor", Vector3.new(w, 1, d), CFrame.new(cx, -0.5, cz), room.floor, {
		surface = FLOOR_SURFACE[room.floorMaterial or "Concrete"] or "concrete",
	})
	floor:SetAttribute("RoomId", roomId)
	if room.outdoor then
		for _, edge in { "N", "W", "E" } do
			buildWall(shell, roomId, room, edge, 10)
		end
		dressRoom(shell, roomId, room)
		return
	end
	local drop = hasDropCeiling(room)
	part(shell, "Ceiling", Vector3.new(w, 1, d), CFrame.new(cx, room.h + 0.5, cz), drop and Color3.fromRGB(178, 174, 160) or "greyDark", {
		surface = drop and "ceilingTile" or "concrete",
		shadow = true,
	})
	for _, edge in { "N", "S", "W", "E" } do
		buildWall(shell, roomId, room, edge, room.h)
	end
	dressRoom(shell, roomId, room)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Lighting
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local FLUORESCENT = Props.rgb(MapLayout.Palette.fluorescent)
local AMBER = Props.rgb(MapLayout.Palette.amber)

local function fluorescent(parent, pos, along, roomId, range, shadows, ceiling)
	local size = along == "x" and Vector3.new(4, 0.2, 1.2) or Vector3.new(1.2, 0.2, 4)
	if ceiling and ceiling - pos.Y > 0.5 then
		local drop = ceiling - pos.Y
		for _, side in { -1, 1 } do
			local offset = along == "x" and Vector3.new(side * 1.6, 0, 0) or Vector3.new(0, 0, side * 1.6)
			part(parent, "Rod", Vector3.new(0.08, drop, 0.08), CFrame.new(pos + offset + Vector3.new(0, drop / 2, 0)), "greyDark", {
				collide = false,
				shadow = false,
				surface = "paintedMetal",
			})
		end
	end
	local panel = part(parent, "Fluorescent", size, CFrame.new(pos), FLUORESCENT, {
		surface = "glow",
		collide = false,
		shadow = false,
	})
	panel:SetAttribute("LightKind", "Fluorescent")
	panel:SetAttribute("RoomId", roomId)
	local light = Instance.new("SurfaceLight")
	light.Name = "Light"
	light.Face = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
	light.Angle = 120
	light.Range = range
	light.Brightness = 1.3
	light.Color = FLUORESCENT
	light.Shadows = shadows == true
	light.Parent = panel
	return panel
end

local function emergencyLight(parent, pos, roomId, colour)
	local box = part(parent, "Emergency", Vector3.new(0.9, 0.5, 0.5), CFrame.new(pos), colour or AMBER, {
		surface = "glow",
		collide = false,
		shadow = false,
	})
	box:SetAttribute("LightKind", "Emergency")
	box:SetAttribute("RoomId", roomId)
	local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Name = "Light"
	light.Range = 20
	light.Brightness = 0.9
	light.Color = colour or AMBER
	light.Shadows = false
	light.Parent = box
	return box
end

local function buildLights(roomFolder, roomId, room)
	local lights = folder(roomFolder, "Lights")
	local w, d = room.x1 - room.x0, room.z1 - room.z0
	-- Tall rooms (the transmitter chamber is 26 studs) hang their fixtures on rods
	-- so the light still reaches the floor.
	local ceiling = room.h - 0.15
	local y = math.min(ceiling, 15)
	local range = math.min(y + 7, 22)
	if room.lights == "grid" then
		local spacing = (w * d > 2000) and 20 or 17
		local nx = math.max(1, math.floor(w / spacing + 0.5))
		local nz = math.max(1, math.floor(d / spacing + 0.5))
		for i = 1, nx do
			for j = 1, nz do
				local x = room.x0 + w * (i - 0.5) / nx
				local z = room.z0 + d * (j - 0.5) / nz
				-- Only the first fixture casts shadows: atmosphere without the cost.
				local panel = fluorescent(lights, Vector3.new(x, y, z), "x", roomId, range, i == 1 and j == 1 and not room.hq, ceiling)
				if room.hq then
					-- The field office is meant to feel safe: a soft omni fill lights
					-- the ceiling and walls (Roblox has no bounce light).
					local fill = Instance.new("PointLight")
					fill.Name = "Fill"
					fill.Range = 24
					fill.Brightness = 0.45
					fill.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 206)
					fill.Shadows = false
					fill.Parent = panel
				end
			end
		end
	elseif room.lights == "line" then
		local along = w >= d and "x" or "z"
		local length = math.max(w, d)
		local n = math.max(1, math.floor(length / 16 + 0.5))
		for i = 1, n do
			local t = (i - 0.5) / n
			local pos = along == "x" and Vector3.new(room.x0 + w * t, y, (room.z0 + room.z1) / 2)
				or Vector3.new((room.x0 + room.x1) / 2, y, room.z0 + d * t)
			fluorescent(lights, pos, along, roomId, range, false, ceiling)
		end
	end
	if room.emergency then
		local colour = AMBER
		if room.outdoor then
			return lights
		end
		local ey = math.min(room.h - 1.2, 12)
		emergencyLight(lights, Vector3.new((room.x0 + room.x1) / 2, ey, room.z0 + T + 0.3), roomId, colour)
		if w * d > 1200 then
			emergencyLight(lights, Vector3.new((room.x0 + room.x1) / 2, ey, room.z1 - T - 0.3), roomId, colour)
		end
	end
	return lights
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Doors and windows
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- The depth (perpendicular to the wall) at which panels and trim sit.
local function doorDepth(door)
	if #door.rooms == 2 then
		return door.at
	end
	local room = MapLayout.Rooms[door.rooms[1]]
	if door.plane == "z" then
		return door.at == room.z0 and door.at + T / 2 or door.at - T / 2
	end
	return door.at == room.x0 and door.at + T / 2 or door.at - T / 2
end

-- World CFrame at a point on the door's plane, with the panel's X axis running
-- along the wall.
local function doorFrame(door, along, y)
	local depth = doorDepth(door)
	if door.plane == "z" then
		return CFrame.new(along, y, depth)
	end
	return CFrame.new(depth, y, along) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)
end

local function roomArea(id)
	local r = MapLayout.Rooms[id]
	return (r.x1 - r.x0) * (r.z1 - r.z0)
end

-- Which way (+1/-1 around Y) the panel swings so it opens into the bigger room.
local function swingSign(door)
	local target = door.rooms[1]
	if #door.rooms == 2 and roomArea(door.rooms[2]) > roomArea(door.rooms[1]) then
		target = door.rooms[2]
	end
	local room = MapLayout.Rooms[target]
	if door.plane == "z" then
		-- +90 deg swings toward -Z (north).
		local roomIsNorth = room.z1 == door.at
		return roomIsNorth and 1 or -1
	end
	-- Rotated panels: +90 deg swings toward -X (west).
	local roomIsWest = room.x1 == door.at
	return roomIsWest and 1 or -1
end

local function addPathModifier(target, label, passThrough)
	local modifier = Instance.new("PathfindingModifier")
	modifier.Label = label
	modifier.PassThrough = passThrough
	modifier.Parent = target
end

local function buildPanel(model, door, centre, width, colour, withWindow)
	local ph = DOOR_H - 0.2
	local thickness = 0.3
	local base = doorFrame(door, centre, 0)
	if not withWindow then
		local panel = part(model, "Panel", Vector3.new(width, ph, thickness), base * CFrame.new(0, ph / 2, 0), colour, { surface = "veneer" })
		return panel
	end
	local winBottom, winTop, winWidth = 4.6, 7.2, math.min(1.6, width - 1)
	local lower = part(model, "Panel", Vector3.new(width, winBottom, thickness), base * CFrame.new(0, winBottom / 2, 0), colour, { surface = "veneer" })
	local pieces = {
		part(model, "PanelUpper", Vector3.new(width, ph - winTop, thickness), base * CFrame.new(0, (winTop + ph) / 2, 0), colour, { surface = "veneer", anchored = false, massless = true }),
		part(model, "PanelLeft", Vector3.new((width - winWidth) / 2, winTop - winBottom, thickness), base * CFrame.new(-(width + winWidth) / 4, (winBottom + winTop) / 2, 0), colour, { surface = "veneer", anchored = false, massless = true }),
		part(model, "PanelRight", Vector3.new((width - winWidth) / 2, winTop - winBottom, thickness), base * CFrame.new((width + winWidth) / 4, (winBottom + winTop) / 2, 0), colour, { surface = "veneer", anchored = false, massless = true }),
	}
	local glass = part(model, "VisionGlass", Vector3.new(winWidth, winTop - winBottom, 0.12), base * CFrame.new(0, (winBottom + winTop) / 2, 0), Color3.fromRGB(150, 170, 160), {
		surface = "glass",
		transparency = 0.55,
		anchored = false,
		massless = true,
		shadow = false,
	})
	glass:SetAttribute("SeeThrough", true)
	table.insert(pieces, glass)
	for _, piece in pieces do
		Props.weld(lower, piece)
	end
	return lower
end

local function buildDoor(doorsFolder, door, trimColour)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = door.id
	model:SetAttribute("DoorId", door.id)
	model:SetAttribute("Kind", door.kind)
	model:SetAttribute("Label", door.label or "")
	model:SetAttribute("Refuge", door.refuge == true)
	model.Parent = doorsFolder

	local depthSize = (#door.rooms == 2) and (2 * T + 0.2) or (T + 0.2)
	local top = doorTop(door)
	local half = door.width / 2
	-- Trim: jambs and header around the opening.
	for _, side in { -1, 1 } do
		part(model, "Jamb", Vector3.new(0.3, top, depthSize), doorFrame(door, door.center + side * (half + 0.15), top / 2), trimColour, { collide = false })
	end
	part(model, "Header", Vector3.new(door.width + 0.6, 0.4, depthSize), doorFrame(door, door.center, top + 0.2), trimColour, { collide = false })

	if door.kind == "opening" then
		return model
	end

	local panelColour = door.window and "brown" or "brownDark"
	if door.kind == "freight" then
		local shutter = part(model, "Panel", Vector3.new(door.width, top, 0.5), doorFrame(door, door.center, top / 2), "greyDark", { surface = "rust" })
		for i = 1, 8 do
			part(model, "Slat" .. i, Vector3.new(door.width, 0.1, 0.55), doorFrame(door, door.center, i * top / 9), "black", {
				collide = false,
				shadow = false,
				anchored = false,
				massless = true,
			})
		end
		for _, child in model:GetChildren() do
			if string.sub(child.Name, 1, 4) == "Slat" then
				Props.weld(shutter, child)
			end
		end
		model.PrimaryPart = shutter
		addPathModifier(shutter, "LockedDoor", false)
		return model
	end

	if door.kind == "double" then
		local w = door.width / 2 - 0.1
		local left = buildPanel(model, door, door.center - door.width / 4, w, "greyDark", true)
		left.Name = "PanelL"
		local right = buildPanel(model, door, door.center + door.width / 4, w, "greyDark", true)
		right.Name = "PanelR"
		-- buildPanel names the lower piece "Panel"; keep one primary.
		model.PrimaryPart = left
		addPathModifier(left, "LockedDoor", false)
		addPathModifier(right, "LockedDoor", false)
		return model
	end

	local panel = buildPanel(model, door, door.center, door.width - 0.2, panelColour, door.window)
	model.PrimaryPart = panel
	model:SetAttribute("SwingSign", swingSign(door))
	model:SetAttribute("PanelWidth", door.width - 0.2)
	-- Pathfinding treats closed (unlocked) doors as passable; the server opens
	-- them when the Presenter arrives. Locked doors are relabelled at runtime.
	addPathModifier(panel, "Door", true)
	local handle = part(model, "Handle", Vector3.new(0.4, 0.15, 0.5), doorFrame(door, door.center + half - 0.6, 3.6), "grey", {
		collide = false,
		shadow = false,
		anchored = false,
		massless = true,
		surface = "paintedMetal",
	})
	Props.weld(panel, handle)
	return model
end

local function buildWindows(windowsFolder)
	for _, win in MapLayout.Windows do
		local depthSize = 2 * T
		local cf
		if win.plane == "z" then
			cf = CFrame.new(win.center, (win.y0 + win.y1) / 2, win.at)
		else
			cf = CFrame.new(win.at, (win.y0 + win.y1) / 2, win.center) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)
		end
		local glass = part(windowsFolder, "Glass", Vector3.new(win.width, win.y1 - win.y0, 0.2), cf, Color3.fromRGB(140, 160, 150), {
			surface = "glass",
			transparency = 0.6,
			shadow = false,
		})
		glass:SetAttribute("SeeThrough", true)
		glass:SetAttribute("WindowId", win.id)
		part(windowsFolder, "Sill", Vector3.new(win.width + 0.4, 0.25, depthSize + 0.2), cf * CFrame.new(0, -(win.y1 - win.y0) / 2 - 0.1, 0), "creamDark", {
			collide = false,
		})
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Signage: each doorway is labelled with where it leads.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function buildSigns(station)
	local signs = folder(station, "Signs")
	for _, door in MapLayout.Doors do
		if #door.rooms == 2 then
			for i, roomId in door.rooms do
				local other = MapLayout.Rooms[door.rooms[3 - i]]
				local room = MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
				local normal, depth
				if door.plane == "z" then
					if door.at == room.z0 then
						normal, depth = SOUTH, door.at + T + 0.06
					else
						normal, depth = NORTH, door.at - T - 0.06
					end
				else
					if door.at == room.x0 then
						normal, depth = EAST, door.at + T + 0.06
					else
						normal, depth = WEST, door.at - T - 0.06
					end
				end
				local top = math.min(doorTop(door) + 0.9, room.h - 0.6)
				local pos
				if door.plane == "z" then
					pos = Vector3.new(door.center, top, depth)
				else
					pos = Vector3.new(depth, top, door.center)
				end
				if top > DOOR_H then
					Props.sign(signs, facing(pos, normal), math.min(5.5, door.width + 1.5), 0.8, other.label, "creamDark")
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return signs
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Furnishing
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local P = MapLayout.point

local function monitor(parent, cf, scale, cameraId, role)
	local m, screen = Props.crt(parent, cf, scale)
	if cameraId then
		screen:AddTag("CCTVMonitor")
		screen:SetAttribute("CameraId", cameraId)
		if role then
			screen:SetAttribute("MonitorRole", role)
		end
	end
	return m, screen
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- The Loop's props (tape archive): each has a Normal and an Odd variant.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function variantFolder(model, name)
	local f = Instance.new("Model")
	f.Name = name
	f.Parent = model
	return f
end

local function hideVariant(container)
	for _, d in container:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			d:SetAttribute("BaseTransparency", d.Transparency)
			d.Transparency = 1
			d.CanCollide = false
		elseif d:IsA("SurfaceGui") or d:IsA("Light") then
			d.Enabled = false
		end
	end
end

local function rememberVariant(container)
	for _, d in container:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			d:SetAttribute("BaseTransparency", d.Transparency)
			d.CanCollide = false
		end
	end
end

local function buildLoopProps(station, caseDef)
	local root = folder(station, "LoopProps")
	if not caseDef or not caseDef.loop then
		return root
	end
	for _, prop in caseDef.loop.props do
		local model = Props.model(root, prop.id)
		model:SetAttribute("LoopProp", prop.id)
		local normal = variantFolder(model, "Normal")
		local odd = variantFolder(model, "Odd")
		local pos = V(prop.pos)
		if prop.id == "LP_CLOCK" then
			Props.wallClock(normal, facing(pos, WEST), prop.normal)
			Props.wallClock(odd, facing(pos, WEST), prop.odd)
		elseif prop.id == "LP_CHAIR" then
			Props.chair(normal, facing(Vector3.new(pos.X, 0, pos.Z), NORTH), "brownDark")
			Props.chair(odd, facing(Vector3.new(pos.X, 0, pos.Z), SOUTH), "brownDark")
		elseif prop.id == "LP_LAMP" then
			part(model, "LampBase", Vector3.new(0.6, 0.15, 0.6), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.08, 0)), "greyDark", { collide = false })
			part(model, "LampArm", Vector3.new(0.12, 1.2, 0.12), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.7, 0)), "greyDark", { collide = false })
			for _, v in { { normal, Color3.fromRGB(230, 236, 226) }, { odd, Props.rgb(MapLayout.Palette.amber) } } do
				local shade = part(v[1], "Shade", Vector3.new(0.8, 0.45, 0.8), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 1.45, 0)), v[2], {
					surface = "glow",
					collide = false,
					shadow = false,
				})
				local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
				light.Range = 7
				light.Brightness = 0.7
				light.Color = v[2]
				light.Shadows = false
				light.Parent = shade
			end
		elseif prop.id == "LP_TAPE" then
			for _, v in { { normal, prop.normal }, { odd, prop.odd } } do
				local tape = part(v[1], "Spine", Vector3.new(1.3, 0.5, 1.2), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.25, 0)), "creamDark", { collide = false })
				Props.label(tape, v[2], { face = Enum.NormalId.Back, pixelsPerStud = 60 })
			end
		elseif prop.id == "LP_POSTER" then
			Props.poster(normal, facing(pos, NORTH), 3, 4, "SAFETY\nFIRST", "cream")
			Props.poster(odd, facing(pos, NORTH) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(180)), 3, 4, "SAFETY\nFIRST", "cream")
		elseif prop.id == "LP_PHONE" then
			part(model, "PhoneBase", Vector3.new(0.9, 0.3, 0.7), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.15, 0)), "cream", { collide = false })
			part(normal, "Handset", Vector3.new(0.9, 0.2, 0.3), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0)), "creamDark", { collide = false })
			part(odd, "Handset", Vector3.new(0.9, 0.2, 0.3), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0.8, 0.1, 0.5)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(40), 0), "creamDark", { collide = false })
		end
		rememberVariant(normal)
		hideVariant(odd)
	end
	return root
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Interaction anchors
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function anchor(parent, id, pos)
	local a = part(parent, id, Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), CFrame.new(pos), "black", { collide = false, shadow = false, transparency = 1 })
	a.CanQuery = false
	a.CanTouch = false
	a:SetAttribute("InteractId", id)
	return a
end

local function buildAnchors(station, caseDef)
	local anchors = folder(station, "Anchors")
	if not caseDef then
		return anchors
	end
	for id, def in caseDef.interactables do
		local pos
		if def.kind == "Deck" then
			pos = V(caseDef.decks[def.deck].pos)
		elseif def.kind == "Relay" then
			pos = V(MapLayout.Relays[def.relay].pos, 4)
		else
			pos = V(def.pos)
		end
		anchor(anchors, id, pos)
	end
	if caseDef.loop then
		for _, prop in caseDef.loop.props do
			anchor(anchors, prop.id, V(prop.pos) + Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0))
		end
	end
	return anchors
end
```
