## tests/run.luau

```lua
--[[
	Test runner. Usage (from the repository root):
		lune run tests/run.luau            -- every spec
		lune run tests/run.luau timeline   -- specs whose file name contains "timeline"
]]

local fs = require("@lune/fs")
local process = require("@lune/process")
local luau = require("@lune/luau")

local Harness = require("./lib/Harness")
local Engine = require("./lib/Engine")

local filter = process.args[1]

local function deepEqual(a, b)
	if a == b then
		return true
	end
	if type(a) ~= "table" or type(b) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end
	for k, v in a do
		if not deepEqual(v, b[k]) then
			return false
		end
	end
	for k in b do
		if a[k] == nil then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

local function describe(value)
	if type(value) == "string" then
		return string.format("%q", value)
	end
	return tostring(value)
end

local function makeExpect()
	local function expect(actual)
		local api = {}
		function api.toBe(expected)
			if actual ~= expected then
				error(string.format("expected %s to be %s", describe(actual), describe(expected)), 2)
			end
		end
		function api.toEqual(expected)
			if not deepEqual(actual, expected) then
				error(string.format("expected deep equality, got %s", describe(actual)), 2)
			end
		end
		function api.toBeTruthy()
			if not actual then
				error(string.format("expected truthy, got %s", describe(actual)), 2)
			end
		end
		function api.toBeFalsy()
			if actual then
				error(string.format("expected falsy, got %s", describe(actual)), 2)
			end
		end
		function api.toBeNear(expected, epsilon)
			epsilon = epsilon or 1e-6
			if type(actual) ~= "number" or math.abs(actual - expected) > epsilon then
				error(string.format("expected %s to be within %s of %s", describe(actual), epsilon, expected), 2)
			end
		end
		function api.toBeAtLeast(expected)
			if type(actual) ~= "number" or actual < expected then
				error(string.format("expected %s >= %s", describe(actual), describe(expected)), 2)
			end
		end
		function api.toBeAtMost(expected)
			if type(actual) ~= "number" or actual > expected then
				error(string.format("expected %s <= %s", describe(actual), describe(expected)), 2)
			end
		end
		function api.toContain(expected)
			if type(actual) == "string" then
				if not string.find(actual, expected, 1, true) then
					error(string.format("expected %s to contain %s", describe(actual), describe(expected)), 2)
				end
				return
			end
			for _, v in actual do
				if deepEqual(v, expected) then
					return
				end
			end
			error(string.format("expected list to contain %s", describe(expected)), 2)
		end
		return api
	end
	return expect
end

local specDir = "tests/specs"
local files = fs.readDir(specDir)
table.sort(files)

local passed, failed = 0, 0
local failures = {}

for _, file in files do
	if string.sub(file, -10) == ".spec.luau" and (not filter or string.find(file, filter, 1, true)) then
		local source = fs.readFile(specDir .. "/" .. file)
		local tests = {}
		local env = {
			Harness = Harness,
			Engine = Engine,
			roblox = require("@lune/roblox"),
			expect = makeExpect(),
			test = function(name, fn)
				table.insert(tests, { name = name, fn = fn })
			end,
		}
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			local chunk = luau.load(source, { debugName = file, environment = env })
			chunk()
		end)
		if not ok then
			failed += 1
			table.insert(failures, string.format("%s (load): %s", file, tostring(err)))
		end
		for _, case in tests do
			local okCase, errCase = pcall(case.fn)
			if okCase then
				passed += 1
				print(string.format("  PASS  %s :: %s", file, case.name))
			else
				failed += 1
				print(string.format("  FAIL  %s :: %s", file, case.name))
				table.insert(failures, string.format("%s :: %s\n      %s", file, case.name, tostring(errCase)))
			end
		end
	end
end

print(string.format("\n%d passed, %d failed", passed, failed))
if #failures > 0 then
	print("\nFailures:")
	for _, f in failures do
		print("  - " .. f)
	end
	process.exit(1)
end
-- Simulated services leave background threads parked; exit explicitly.
process.exit(0)
```

## tests/lib/Engine.luau

```lua
--[[
	A deterministic stand-in for the Roblox engine, for integration tests.

	Real Instances come from Lune (so every class, property and enum is still
	validated against Roblox's reflection database), wrapped in proxies that add
	what Lune does not have: events, a scheduler with a fake clock, remotes wired
	between the server and client scripts running in this same process, players
	and characters, DataStores, MarketplaceService, pathfinding and tweens.

	It is deliberately simple: no physics (Humanoid:MoveTo glides), raycasts are
	either always blocked or always clear, and replication is instantaneous. The
	point is to execute the real server and client code paths end to end and
	surface runtime errors, not to reproduce Roblox exactly.
]]

local roblox = require("@lune/roblox")

local Engine = {}
Engine.__index = Engine

-- Event names the game connects to (Lune instances have no events).
local EVENTS = {
	Triggered = true, MouseEnter = true, MouseLeave = true, SelectionGained = true, SelectionLost = true,
	Activated = true, InputBegan = true, InputChanged = true, InputEnded = true, Heartbeat = true,
	RenderStepped = true, Stepped = true, PlayerAdded = true, PlayerRemoving = true, CharacterAdded = true,
	Died = true, OnServerEvent = true, OnClientEvent = true, ChildAdded = true, ChildRemoved = true,
	DescendantAdded = true, AncestryChanged = true, Ended = true, Completed = true, Changed = true,
	PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished = true, PromptProductPurchaseFinished = true, PromptButtonHoldBegan = true,
	PromptButtonHoldEnded = true, MoveToFinished = true, FocusLost = true, PromptShown = true, PromptHidden = true,
	CharacterRemoving = true,
}

local CALLBACKS = { OnServerInvoke = true, OnClientInvoke = true, ProcessReceipt = true }

local function newSignal(engine, name)
	local signal = { name = name, handlers = {} }
	function signal:Connect(fn)
		local entry = { fn = fn, side = engine:currentSide(), connected = true }
		table.insert(self.handlers, entry)
		return {
			Connected = true,
			Disconnect = function()
				entry.connected = false
			end,
		}
	end
	function signal:Once(fn)
		local conn
		conn = self:Connect(function(...)
			conn.Disconnect()
			fn(...)
		end)
		return conn
	end
	function signal:Wait()
		local co = coroutine.running()
		local conn
		conn = self:Connect(function(...)
			conn.Disconnect()
			engine:_resume(co, ...)
		end)
		return coroutine.yield()
	end
	function signal:Fire(...)
		for _, entry in table.clone(self.handlers) do
			if entry.connected then
				engine:spawnOnSide(entry.side, entry.fn, ...)
			end
		end
	end
	return signal
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Construction
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Engine.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, Engine)
	self.clock = 1000
	self.waiting = {}
	self.errors = {}
	self.warnings = {}
	self.prints = {}
	self.unreadable = {}
	-- Keyed by real instance and released on Destroy (Luau weak tables do not
	-- break proxy <-> instance cycles, so weak keys would leak everything).
	self.proxyOf = {}
	self.signals = {}
	self.callbacks = {}
	self.stored = {}
	self.moves = {}
	self.velocities = {}
	self.renderBindings = {}
	self.sideOf = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	self.realOf = setmetatable({}, {
		__index = function(_, proxy)
			return type(proxy) == "table" and rawget(proxy, "__real") or nil
		end,
	})
	self.running = nil
	self.sharedMounts = {}
	self.losBlocked = true
	self.actions = {}
	self.dataStores = {}
	self.ordered = {}
	self.prompted = {}
	self.closeHandlers = {}
	self.remoteLog = {}

	self.realGame = roblox.Instance.new("DataModel")
	self.game = self:wrap(self.realGame)
	local ws = self.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
	self.realCamera = roblox.Instance.new("Camera")
	self.realCamera.Name = "Camera"
	self.realCamera.Parent = ws

	self.task = self:_makeTask()
	local engine = self
	self.os = setmetatable({
		clock = function()
			return engine.clock
		end,
		time = function()
			return math.floor(1700000000 + engine.clock)
		end,
	}, { __index = os })
	self.Instance = {
		new = function(className, parent)
			local proxy = engine:wrap(roblox.Instance.new(className))
			if parent then
				proxy.Parent = parent
			end
			return proxy
		end,
	}
	self.RaycastParams = {
		new = function()
			return { FilterType = nil, FilterDescendantsInstances = {}, RespectCanCollide = false }
		end,
	}
	return self
end

function Engine:currentSide()
	return (self.running and self.sideOf[self.running]) or "server"
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Scheduler
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Engine:_resume(co, ...)
	if coroutine.status(co) == "dead" then
		return
	end
	local previous = self.running
	self.running = co
	local ok, err = coroutine.resume(co, ...)
	self.running = previous
	if not ok then
		table.insert(self.errors, debug.traceback(co, tostring(err)))
	end
end

function Engine:spawnOnSide(side, fn, ...)
	local co = type(fn) == "thread" and fn or coroutine.create(fn)
	self.sideOf[co] = self.sideOf[co] or side
	self:_resume(co, ...)
	return co
end

function Engine:spawn(fn, ...)
	return self:spawnOnSide(self:currentSide(), fn, ...)
end

function Engine:_makeTask()
	local engine = self
	local t = {}
	function t.wait(seconds)
		local co = coroutine.running()
		local start = engine.clock
		table.insert(engine.waiting, { co = co, at = engine.clock + (seconds or 0) })
		coroutine.yield()
		return engine.clock - start
	end
	function t.spawn(fn, ...)
		return engine:spawn(fn, ...)
	end
	function t.defer(fn, ...)
		local co = type(fn) == "thread" and fn or coroutine.create(fn)
		engine.sideOf[co] = engine:currentSide()
		table.insert(engine.waiting, { co = co, at = engine.clock, args = table.pack(...) })
		return co
	end
	function t.delay(seconds, fn, ...)
		local co = coroutine.create(fn)
		engine.sideOf[co] = engine:currentSide()
		table.insert(engine.waiting, { co = co, at = engine.clock + (seconds or 0), args = table.pack(...) })
		return co
	end
	function t.cancel(co)
		for i = #engine.waiting, 1, -1 do
			if engine.waiting[i].co == co then
				table.remove(engine.waiting, i)
			end
		end
	end
	return t
end

function Engine:_resumeDue()
	local due, keep = {}, {}
	for _, w in self.waiting do
		if w.at <= self.clock + 1e-9 then
			table.insert(due, w)
		else
			table.insert(keep, w)
		end
	end
	self.waiting = keep
	table.sort(due, function(a, b)
		return a.at < b.at
	end)
	for _, w in due do
		if w.args then
			self:_resume(w.co, table.unpack(w.args, 1, w.args.n))
		else
			self:_resume(w.co)
		end
	end
end

function Engine:service(name)
	return self:wrap(self.realGame:GetService(name))
end

-- Advances simulated time, firing frame events and waking threads.
function Engine:_forget(real)
	self.proxyOf[real] = nil
	self.signals[real] = nil
	self.callbacks[real] = nil
	self.stored[real] = nil
	self.moves[real] = nil
end

function Engine:advance(seconds, step)
	step = step or 0.1
	local target = self.clock + seconds
	local run = self.realGame:GetService("RunService")
	while self.clock < target - 1e-9 do
		self.clock += step
		self:_stepMoves(step)
		self:signalFor(run, "Stepped"):Fire(self.clock, step)
		self:signalFor(run, "Heartbeat"):Fire(step)
		self:signalFor(run, "RenderStepped"):Fire(step)
		self:_resumeDue()
	end
	self:_resumeDue()
end

-- Runs a function on a side ("server" or "client") inside the scheduler.
function Engine:run(side, fn, ...)
	return self:spawnOnSide(side, fn, ...)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Proxies
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Engine:signalFor(real, name)
	local byName = self.signals[real]
	if not byName then
		byName = {}
		self.signals[real] = byName
	end
	if not byName[name] then
		byName[name] = newSignal(self, name)
	end
	return byName[name]
end

function Engine:unwrap(value)
	if type(value) == "table" then
		local real = self.realOf[value]
		if real then
			return real
		end
	end
	return value
end

local function isInstance(v)
	return typeof(v) == "Instance"
end

function Engine:wrapValue(v)
	if isInstance(v) then
		return self:wrap(v)
	end
	if type(v) == "table" then
		-- Lists of instances (GetChildren etc.).
		local out = {}
		for k, item in v do
			out[k] = isInstance(item) and self:wrap(item) or item
		end
		return out
	end
	return v
end

function Engine:unwrapArgs(...)
	local args = table.pack(...)
	for i = 1, args.n do
		local v = args[i]
		if type(v) == "table" and not self.realOf[v] then
			-- Tables of proxies (e.g. FilterDescendantsInstances) stay tables.
			args[i] = v
		else
			args[i] = self:unwrap(v)
		end
	end
	return table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)
end

local ProxyMT = {}

function Engine:wrap(real)
	if real == nil then
		return nil
	end
	local existing = self.proxyOf[real]
	if existing then
		return existing
	end
	local proxy = setmetatable({}, ProxyMT)
	self.proxyOf[real] = proxy
	rawset(proxy, "__real", real)
	rawset(proxy, "__engine", self)
	return proxy
end

local methods = {}

ProxyMT.__index = function(proxy, key)
	local engine = rawget(proxy, "__engine")
	local real = engine.realOf[proxy]
	if EVENTS[key] then
		return engine:signalFor(real, key)
	end
	local stored = engine.stored[real]
	if stored and stored[key] ~= nil then
		return stored[key]
	end
	local cb = engine.callbacks[real]
	if CALLBACKS[key] then
		return cb and cb[key] or nil
	end
	local override = methods[key]
	if override then
		local okOverride, handled, result = pcall(override, engine, proxy, real, nil, true)
		if not okOverride then
			if string.find(tostring(handled), "has been destroyed", 1, true) then
				return function() end
			end
			error(handled, 2)
		end
		if handled then
			if type(result) == "function" then
				-- Roblox tolerates calls on destroyed instances; mirror that.
				local fn = result
				return function(...)
					local packed = table.pack(pcall(fn, ...))
					if not packed[1] then
						if string.find(tostring(packed[2]), "has been destroyed", 1, true) then
							return nil
						end
						error(packed[2], 2)
					end
					return table.unpack(packed, 2, packed.n)
				end
			end
			return result
		end
	end
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return real[key]
	end)
	if not ok and string.find(tostring(value), "has been destroyed", 1, true) then
		-- Roblox lets scripts touch destroyed instances; Lune does not.
		return key == "Parent" and nil or (function() end)
	end
	if ok then
		if type(value) == "function" then
			return function(selfArg, ...)
				local packed = table.pack(pcall(value, engine:unwrap(selfArg), engine:unwrapArgs(...)))
				if not packed[1] then
					if string.find(tostring(packed[2]), "has been destroyed", 1, true) then
						return nil
					end
					error(packed[2], 2)
				end
				local results = table.pack(table.unpack(packed, 2, packed.n))
				for i = 1, results.n do
					results[i] = engine:wrapValue(results[i])
				end
				return table.unpack(results, 1, results.n)
			end
		end
		return engine:wrapValue(value)
	end
	local message = tostring(value)
	if string.find(message, "missing default value", 1, true) then
		engine.unreadable[real.ClassName .. "." .. key] = true
		return nil
	end
	error(message, 2)
end

ProxyMT.__newindex = function(proxy, key, value)
	local engine = rawget(proxy, "__engine")
	local real = engine.realOf[proxy]
	if CALLBACKS[key] then
		engine.callbacks[real] = engine.callbacks[real] or {}
		engine.callbacks[real][key] = value
		return
	end
	local raw = engine:unwrap(value)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		real[key] = raw
	end)
	if not ok and string.find(tostring(err), "has been destroyed", 1, true) then
		return
	end
	if not ok then
		local message = tostring(err)
		if string.find(message, "not a valid member", 1, true) or string.find(message, "Failed to cast", 1, true) or string.find(message, "Expected", 1, true) then
			error(message, 2)
		end
		engine.stored[real] = engine.stored[real] or {}
		engine.stored[real][key] = value
		return
	end
	-- GetPropertyChangedSignal listeners (only if someone is listening).
	local byName = engine.signals[real]
	local changed = byName and byName["Changed:" .. key]
	if changed then
		changed:Fire()
	end
	if key == "Parent" and raw ~= nil then
		engine:signalFor(raw, "ChildAdded"):Fire(proxy)
		local ancestor = raw
		while ancestor do
			engine:signalFor(ancestor, "DescendantAdded"):Fire(proxy)
			ancestor = ancestor.Parent
		end
	end
end

ProxyMT.__tostring = function(proxy)
	local engine = rawget(proxy, "__engine")
	return tostring(engine.realOf[proxy])
end

ProxyMT.__eq = function(a, b)
	return rawequal(a, b)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Method / property overrides. Each returns handled, value.
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function bound(fn)
	return true, fn
end

local function isA(real, className)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return real:IsA(className)
	end)
	return ok and result
end

methods.GetServerTimeNow = function(engine)
	return bound(function()
		return engine.clock
	end)
end

methods.WaitForChild = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function(_, name, timeout)
		if real.ClassName == "ReplicatedStorage" and name == "Shared" then
			return engine.sharedMounts[engine:currentSide()]
		end
		local waited = 0
		while true do
			local child = real:FindFirstChild(name)
			if child then
				return engine:wrap(child)
			end
			if waited >= (timeout or 30) then
				if timeout then
					return nil
				end
				error("Infinite yield waiting for " .. real:GetFullName() .. "." .. tostring(name))
			end
			engine.task.wait(0.05)
			waited += 0.05
		end
	end)
end

methods.Raycast = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "Workspace" then
		return false
	end
	return bound(function(_, origin)
		if engine.losBlocked then
			return { Position = origin, Instance = nil, Normal = roblox.Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) }
		end
		return nil
	end)
end

methods.GetPlayers = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function()
		local list = {}
		for _, child in real:GetChildren() do
			if child.ClassName == "Player" then
				table.insert(list, engine:wrap(child))
			end
		end
		return list
	end)
end

methods.GetPlayerByUserId = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function(_, userId)
		for _, child in real:GetChildren() do
			if child.ClassName == "Player" and child.UserId == userId then
				return engine:wrap(child)
			end
		end
		return nil
	end)
end

methods.LocalPlayer = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "Players" then
		return false
	end
	if engine:currentSide() == "client" then
		return true, engine.localPlayer
	end
	return true, nil
end

methods.CurrentCamera = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "Workspace" then
		return false
	end
	return true, engine:wrap(engine.realCamera)
end

methods.JobId = function(_, _, real)
	if real.ClassName == "DataModel" then
		return true, "simulated-job"
	end
	return false
end

methods.LoadCharacter = function(engine, proxy)
	return bound(function()
		engine:loadCharacter(proxy)
	end)
end

local function modelParts(real)
	local parts = {}
	for _, d in real:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			table.insert(parts, d)
		end
	end
	if real:IsA("BasePart") then
		table.insert(parts, real)
	end
	return parts
end

methods.GetPivot = function(_, _, real)
	if not isA(real, "PVInstance") then
		return false
	end
	return bound(function()
		if real:IsA("BasePart") then
			return real.CFrame
		end
		local primary = real.PrimaryPart
		if primary then
			return primary.CFrame
		end
		local parts = modelParts(real)
		return parts[1] and parts[1].CFrame or roblox.CFrame.new()
	end)
end

methods.PivotTo = function(engine, proxy, real)
	if not isA(real, "PVInstance") then
		return false
	end
	return bound(function(_, cf)
		local pivot = proxy:GetPivot()
		if not pivot then
			return -- destroyed: nothing to move (Roblox ignores this too)
		end
		local delta = cf * pivot:Inverse()
		for _, p in modelParts(real) do
			p.CFrame = delta * p.CFrame
		end
		engine.moves[real] = nil
	end)
end

methods.MoveTo = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "Humanoid" then
		return false
	end
	return bound(function(_, target)
		engine.moves[real] = target
	end)
end

function Engine:_stepMoves(dt)
	for humanoid, target in self.moves do
		local model = humanoid.Parent
		local root = model and model.PrimaryPart
		if not model or not root then
			self.moves[humanoid] = nil
		else
			local pos = root.CFrame.Position
			local flat = roblox.Vector3.new(target.X - pos.X, 0, target.Z - pos.Z)
			local dist = flat.Magnitude
			local speed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
			if dist < 0.2 or speed <= 0 then
				if dist < 0.2 then
					self.moves[humanoid] = nil
				end
			else
				local stepLen = math.min(dist, speed * dt)
				local delta = roblox.CFrame.new(flat.Unit * stepLen)
				for _, p in modelParts(model) do
					p.CFrame = delta * p.CFrame
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

-- Destroy / ClearAllChildren release engine bookkeeping for the whole subtree.
methods.Destroy = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function()
		-- Destroying twice is harmless in Roblox.
		local ok, descendants = pcall(function()
			return real:GetDescendants()
		end)
		for _, d in ok and descendants or {} do
			engine:_forget(d)
		end
		engine:_forget(real)
		pcall(function()
			real:Destroy()
		end)
	end)
end

methods.ClearAllChildren = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function()
		for _, d in real:GetDescendants() do
			engine:_forget(d)
		end
		real:ClearAllChildren()
	end)
end

local stubs = {
	SetNetworkOwner = true, SetStateEnabled = true, Kick = true, SetCore = true, Play = true, Stop = true,
	Pause = true, Resume = true, SetTitle = true, SetPosition = true, SetImage = true, UnbindAction = true,
}
for name in stubs do
	methods[name] = function(engine, _, real)
		if name == "Play" or name == "Stop" or name == "Pause" or name == "Resume" then
			if not isA(real, "Sound") then
				return false
			end
		end
		return bound(function()
			return nil
		end)
	end
end

methods.BindAction = function(engine)
	return bound(function(_, name, fn, _, ...)
		engine.actions[name] = { fn = fn, inputs = { ... }, side = engine:currentSide() }
	end)
end

methods.BindActionAtPriority = function(engine)
	return bound(function(_, name, fn, _, _, ...)
		engine.actions[name] = { fn = fn, inputs = { ... }, side = engine:currentSide() }
	end)
end

-- RenderStep bindings run every simulated frame, like RenderStepped.
methods.BindToRenderStep = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function(_, name, _, fn)
		local conn = engine:signalFor(real, "RenderStepped"):Connect(fn)
		engine.renderBindings[name] = conn
	end)
end

methods.UnbindFromRenderStep = function(engine)
	return bound(function(_, name)
		local conn = engine.renderBindings[name]
		if conn then
			conn.Disconnect()
			engine.renderBindings[name] = nil
		end
	end)
end

methods.GetConnectedGamepads = function()
	return bound(function()
		return {}
	end)
end

methods.IsTenFootInterface = function()
	return bound(function()
		return false
	end)
end

methods.IsStudio = function()
	return bound(function()
		return true
	end)
end

methods.BindToClose = function(engine)
	return bound(function(_, fn)
		table.insert(engine.closeHandlers, fn)
	end)
end

methods.GetPropertyChangedSignal = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function(_, prop)
		return engine:signalFor(real, "Changed:" .. prop)
	end)
end

methods.WorldToViewportPoint = function()
	return bound(function()
		return roblox.Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), false
	end)
end

methods.GetTagged = function(engine)
	return bound(function(_, tag)
		local list = {}
		for _, d in engine.realGame:GetDescendants() do
			if d:HasTag(tag) then
				table.insert(list, engine:wrap(d))
			end
		end
		return list
	end)
end

methods.GetNameFromUserIdAsync = function()
	return bound(function(_, userId)
		return "Player" .. tostring(userId)
	end)
end

for _, name in { "AbsolutePosition", "AbsoluteSize", "TextBounds" } do
	methods[name] = function()
		return true, roblox.Vector2.new(0, 0)
	end
end
-- Derived properties Lune does not serialise.
methods.Position = function(_, _, real)
	if isA(real, "BasePart") then
		return true, real.CFrame.Position
	end
	return false
end
methods.CameraOffset = function(_, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "Humanoid" then
		return false
	end
	return true, roblox.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end
methods.MoveDirection = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "Humanoid" then
		return false
	end
	-- Tests can set a root velocity to simulate walking.
	local root = real.Parent and real.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local v = root and engine.velocities[root]
	if v and v.Magnitude > 0 then
		return true, roblox.Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z).Unit
	end
	return true, roblox.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end
methods.AssemblyLinearVelocity = function(engine, _, real)
	if not isA(real, "BasePart") then
		return false
	end
	return true, engine.velocities[real] or roblox.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end
methods.FloorMaterial = function(_, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "Humanoid" then
		return false
	end
	return true, roblox.Enum.Material.Concrete
end
for name, value in { TouchEnabled = false, KeyboardEnabled = true, MouseEnabled = true, GamepadEnabled = false } do
	methods[name] = function(_, _, real)
		if real.ClassName ~= "UserInputService" then
			return false
		end
		return true, value
	end
end

-- TweenService:Create -> applies the goal immediately when played.
methods.Create = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "TweenService" then
		return false
	end
	return bound(function(_, target, _, goals)
		local tween = { Completed = newSignal(engine, "Completed") }
		function tween:Play()
			for k, v in goals do
				target[k] = v
			end
			self.Completed:Fire(roblox.Enum.PlaybackState.Completed)
		end
		function tween:Cancel() end
		function tween:Destroy() end
		return tween
	end)
end

-- PathfindingService:CreatePath -> straight-line paths.
methods.CreatePath = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "PathfindingService" then
		return false
	end
	return bound(function()
		local path = { Status = roblox.Enum.PathStatus.NoPath, waypoints = {} }
		function path:ComputeAsync(from, to)
			self.Status = roblox.Enum.PathStatus.Success
			self.waypoints = { { Position = from }, { Position = to } }
		end
		function path:GetWaypoints()
			return self.waypoints
		end
		return path
	end)
end

-- DataStoreService with in-memory stores.
local function deepCopy(v)
	if type(v) ~= "table" then
		return v
	end
	local out = {}
	for k, item in v do
		out[k] = deepCopy(item)
	end
	return out
end

local function makeStore(engine, name)
	local store = { data = {} }
	function store:UpdateAsync(key, fn)
		local old = deepCopy(self.data[key])
		local new = fn(old)
		if new ~= nil then
			self.data[key] = deepCopy(new)
		end
		return deepCopy(new ~= nil and new or self.data[key])
	end
	function store:GetAsync(key)
		return deepCopy(self.data[key])
	end
	function store:SetAsync(key, value)
		self.data[key] = deepCopy(value)
	end
	function store:GetSortedAsync(ascending, pageSize)
		local rows = {}
		for k, v in self.data do
			table.insert(rows, { key = k, value = v })
		end
		table.sort(rows, function(a, b)
			if ascending then
				return a.value < b.value
			end
			return a.value > b.value
		end)
		local page = {}
		for i = 1, math.min(pageSize, #rows) do
			page[i] = rows[i]
		end
		return {
			GetCurrentPage = function()
				return page
			end,
		}
	end
	engine.dataStores[name] = store
	return store
end

methods.GetDataStore = function(engine, _, real)
	if real.ClassName ~= "DataStoreService" then
		return false
	end
	return bound(function(_, name)
		return engine.dataStores[name] or makeStore(engine, name)
	end)
end
methods.GetOrderedDataStore = methods.GetDataStore

methods.UserOwnsGamePassAsync = function()
	return bound(function()
		return false
	end)
end
for _, name in { "PromptGamePassPurchase", "PromptProductPurchase" } do
	methods[name] = function(engine)
		return bound(function(_, player, id)
			table.insert(engine.prompted, { kind = name, player = player, id = id })
		end)
	end
end

-- Remotes: payloads are deep-copied (and checked) as if serialised.
local function checkPayload(engine, value, path)
	local t = type(value)
	if t == "function" or t == "thread" then
		table.insert(engine.warnings, "remote payload contains a " .. t .. " at " .. path)
	elseif t == "table" then
		if engine.realOf[value] then
			return
		end
		local hasNumber, hasString, n = false, false, 0
		for k, v in value do
			if type(k) == "number" then
				hasNumber = true
				n += 1
			else
				hasString = true
			end
			checkPayload(engine, v, path .. "." .. tostring(k))
		end
		if hasNumber and hasString then
			table.insert(engine.warnings, "remote payload mixes array and dictionary keys at " .. path)
		elseif hasNumber and n ~= #value then
			table.insert(engine.warnings, "remote payload has a sparse array at " .. path)
		end
	end
end

local function transmit(engine, ...)
	local args = table.pack(...)
	for i = 1, args.n do
		checkPayload(engine, args[i], "arg" .. i)
		if type(args[i]) == "table" and not engine.realOf[args[i]] then
			args[i] = deepCopy(args[i])
		end
	end
	return table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)
end

methods.FireServer = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function(_, ...)
		table.insert(engine.remoteLog, { dir = "C2S", remote = real.Name })
		local packed = table.pack(transmit(engine, ...))
		for _, entry in table.clone(engine:signalFor(real, "OnServerEvent").handlers) do
			if entry.connected then
				engine:spawnOnSide("server", entry.fn, engine.localPlayer, table.unpack(packed, 1, packed.n))
			end
		end
	end)
end

methods.FireClient = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function(_, player, ...)
		if player ~= engine.localPlayer then
			return
		end
		table.insert(engine.remoteLog, { dir = "S2C", remote = real.Name })
		local packed = table.pack(transmit(engine, ...))
		for _, entry in table.clone(engine:signalFor(real, "OnClientEvent").handlers) do
			if entry.connected then
				engine:spawnOnSide("client", entry.fn, table.unpack(packed, 1, packed.n))
			end
		end
	end)
end

methods.FireAllClients = function(engine, proxy)
	return bound(function(_, ...)
		proxy:FireClient(engine.localPlayer, ...)
	end)
end

methods.InvokeServer = function(engine, _, real)
	return bound(function(_, ...)
		local cb = engine.callbacks[real] and engine.callbacks[real].OnServerInvoke
		if not cb then
			error("OnServerInvoke not set for " .. real.Name)
		end
		local previous = engine.running and engine.sideOf[engine.running]
		if engine.running then
			engine.sideOf[engine.running] = "server"
		end
		local results = table.pack(pcall(cb, engine.localPlayer, transmit(engine, ...)))
		if engine.running then
			engine.sideOf[engine.running] = previous
		end
		if not results[1] then
			error(results[2])
		end
		return transmit(engine, table.unpack(results, 2, results.n))
	end)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Players and characters
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function Engine:addPlayer(userId, name, isLocal)
	local players = self.realGame:GetService("Players")
	local player = roblox.Instance.new("Player")
	player.Name = name
	player.UserId = userId
	player.DisplayName = name
	local gui = roblox.Instance.new("PlayerGui")
	gui.Name = "PlayerGui"
	gui.Parent = player
	player.Parent = players
	local proxy = self:wrap(player)
	if isLocal then
		self.localPlayer = proxy
	end
	self:signalFor(players, "PlayerAdded"):Fire(proxy)
	return proxy
end

function Engine:loadCharacter(playerProxy)
	local player = self.realOf[playerProxy]
	if player.Character then
		player.Character:Destroy()
	end
	local character = roblox.Instance.new("Model")
	character.Name = player.Name
	local function part(name, size, y)
		local p = roblox.Instance.new("Part")
		p.Name = name
		p.Size = size
		p.CFrame = roblox.CFrame.new(0, y, 260)
		p.Parent = character
		return p
	end
	local root = part("HumanoidRootPart", roblox.Vector3.new(2, 2, 1), 3)
	part("UpperTorso", roblox.Vector3.new(2, 1.6, 1), 3.4)
	part("Head", roblox.Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2), 4.8)
	local humanoid = roblox.Instance.new("Humanoid")
	humanoid.WalkSpeed = 12
	humanoid.Parent = character
	character.PrimaryPart = root
	character.Parent = self.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
	player.Character = character
	self:signalFor(player, "CharacterAdded"):Fire(self:wrap(character))
end

-- Sends a remote event / invocation as a non-local player (their client is
-- not simulated, so server -> client traffic to them is dropped).
function Engine:fireServerAs(playerProxy, remoteName, payload)
	local folder = self.realGame:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local remote = folder:FindFirstChild(remoteName)
	local packed = deepCopy(payload)
	for _, entry in table.clone(self:signalFor(remote, "OnServerEvent").handlers) do
		if entry.connected then
			self:spawnOnSide("server", entry.fn, playerProxy, packed)
		end
	end
end

function Engine:invokeServerAs(playerProxy, payload)
	local folder = self.realGame:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local remote = folder:FindFirstChild("Request")
	local cb = self.callbacks[remote].OnServerInvoke
	local result
	self:spawnOnSide("server", function()
		result = cb(playerProxy, deepCopy(payload))
	end)
	return result
end

function Engine:teleport(playerProxy, position)
	local character = self.realOf[playerProxy].Character
	if character then
		self:wrap(character):PivotTo(roblox.CFrame.new(position.X, 3, position.Z))
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Script execution
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Globals injected into every script and module.
function Engine:globals(side, extra)
	local engine = self
	local g = {
		game = self.game,
		workspace = self:service("Workspace"),
		Instance = self.Instance,
		task = self.task,
		os = self.os,
		RaycastParams = self.RaycastParams,
		print = function(...)
			local parts = {}
			for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
				parts[i] = tostring(select(i, ...))
			end
			table.insert(engine.prints, table.concat(parts, " "))
		end,
		warn = function(...)
			local parts = {}
			for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
				parts[i] = tostring(select(i, ...))
			end
			table.insert(engine.warnings, "[" .. side .. "] " .. table.concat(parts, " "))
		end,
	}
	for k, v in extra or {} do
		g[k] = v
	end
	return g
end

return Engine
```

## tests/lib/Harness.luau

```lua
--[[
	Runs the game's Roblox-style ModuleScripts headlessly under Lune.

	Source folders are mounted as a virtual instance tree so that code written for
	Roblox (`require(script.Parent.Foo)`, `script:WaitForChild("Bar")`) resolves to
	files on disk. Real Roblox datatypes and Instances come from Lune's roblox
	library, which validates class, property and enum names against the engine's
	reflection database - so building the map or UI here catches typos that would
	otherwise only surface in Studio.
]]

local fs = require("@lune/fs")
local luau = require("@lune/luau")
local roblox = require("@lune/roblox")
local task = require("@lune/task")

local Harness = {}

local Proxy = {}
Proxy.__index = function(self, key)
	local method = Proxy[key]
	if method then
		return method
	end
	local raw = rawget(self, "_fields")
	if raw[key] ~= nil then
		return raw[key]
	end
	local child = self:FindFirstChild(key)
	if child then
		return child
	end
	error(string.format("%s is not a valid member of %s", tostring(key), raw.Name), 2)
end

local function stripName(fileName)
	for _, suffix in { ".server.luau", ".client.luau", ".luau", ".server.lua", ".client.lua", ".lua" } do
		if string.sub(fileName, -#suffix) == suffix then
			return string.sub(fileName, 1, #fileName - #suffix), suffix
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function newProxy(name, path, isDir, parent, harness)
	local className = "Folder"
	local modulePath = nil
	if isDir then
		for _, init in { "init.luau", "init.lua" } do
			if fs.isFile(path .. "/" .. init) then
				className = "ModuleScript"
				modulePath = path .. "/" .. init
			end
		end
	else
		local _, suffix = stripName(string.match(path, "[^/]+$"))
		if suffix == ".server.luau" or suffix == ".server.lua" then
			className = "Script"
		elseif suffix == ".client.luau" or suffix == ".client.lua" then
			className = "LocalScript"
		else
			className = "ModuleScript"
		end
		modulePath = path
	end
	local self = setmetatable({
		_fields = {
			Name = name,
			Parent = parent,
			ClassName = className,
		},
		_path = path,
		_isDir = isDir,
		_modulePath = modulePath,
		_children = nil,
		_harness = harness,
	}, Proxy)
	return self
end

-- Lune's filesystem calls yield, and metamethods cannot yield, so the whole tree
-- (and every source file) is read eagerly when a folder is mounted.
local function scan(proxy)
	local children = {}
	if rawget(proxy, "_isDir") then
		local path = rawget(proxy, "_path")
		local names = fs.readDir(path)
		table.sort(names)
		for _, entry in names do
			local full = path .. "/" .. entry
			if fs.isDir(full) then
				local child = newProxy(entry, full, true, proxy, rawget(proxy, "_harness"))
				scan(child)
				children[entry] = child
			else
				local base = stripName(entry)
				if base and base ~= "init" then
					local child = newProxy(base, full, false, proxy, rawget(proxy, "_harness"))
					scan(child)
					children[base] = child
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local modulePath = rawget(proxy, "_modulePath")
	if modulePath then
		rawset(proxy, "_source", fs.readFile(modulePath))
	end
	rawset(proxy, "_children", children)
end

function Proxy:_loadChildren()
	return rawget(self, "_children")
end

function Proxy:FindFirstChild(name)
	return self:_loadChildren()[name]
end

function Proxy:WaitForChild(name)
	local child = self:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not child then
		error(string.format("Infinite yield: %s has no child %s", rawget(self, "_fields").Name, tostring(name)), 2)
	end
	return child
end

function Proxy:GetChildren()
	local list = {}
	for _, child in self:_loadChildren() do
		table.insert(list, child)
	end
	table.sort(list, function(a, b)
		return a.Name < b.Name
	end)
	return list
end

function Proxy:IsA(className)
	return rawget(self, "_fields").ClassName == className
end

function Proxy:GetFullName()
	local parts = {}
	local node = self
	while node do
		table.insert(parts, 1, node.Name)
		node = rawget(node, "_fields").Parent
	end
	return table.concat(parts, ".")
end

-- Minimal stand-ins for engine types Lune does not provide.
local function makeRandom()
	local RandomStub = {}
	function RandomStub.new(seed)
		local state = math.floor(seed or 1) % 2147483647
		if state <= 0 then
			state = state + 2147483646
		end
		local r = {}
		function r:NextNumber(a, b)
			state = (state * 48271) % 2147483647
			local f = state / 2147483647
			if a then
				return a + (b - a) * f
			end
			return f
		end
		function r:NextInteger(a, b)
			return a + math.floor(r:NextNumber() * (b - a + 1))
		end
		return r
	end
	return RandomStub
end

local function makeTweenInfo()
	local TweenInfoStub = {}
	function TweenInfoStub.new(time, style, direction, repeatCount, reverses, delayTime)
		return {
			Time = time or 1,
			EasingStyle = style,
			EasingDirection = direction,
			RepeatCount = repeatCount or 0,
			Reverses = reverses or false,
			DelayTime = delayTime or 0,
		}
	end
	return TweenInfoStub
end

function Harness.new(options)
	options = options or {}
	local self = {
		roots = {},
		cache = {},
		game = options.game,
		extraGlobals = options.globals or {},
	}

	function self.mount(name, path)
		local proxy = newProxy(name, path, true, nil, self)
		scan(proxy)
		self.roots[name] = proxy
		return proxy
	end

	function self.require(target)
		if type(target) ~= "table" or getmetatable(target) ~= Proxy then
			error("Harness require expects a mounted module, got " .. tostring(target), 2)
		end
		local modulePath = rawget(target, "_modulePath")
		if not modulePath then
			error(target:GetFullName() .. " is not a ModuleScript", 2)
		end
		local cached = self.cache[modulePath]
		if cached then
			if cached.loading then
				error("Cyclic require detected at " .. modulePath, 2)
			end
			return cached.value
		end
		local entry = { loading = true }
		self.cache[modulePath] = entry
		local source = rawget(target, "_source")
		local env = self.makeEnv(target)
		local chunk = luau.load(source, { debugName = modulePath, environment = env })
		local value = chunk()
		entry.value = value
		entry.loading = false
		return value
	end

	-- Executes a Script/LocalScript (no caching).
	function self.run(target)
		local source = rawget(target, "_source")
		local env = self.makeEnv(target)
		local chunk = luau.load(source, { debugName = rawget(target, "_modulePath"), environment = env })
		return chunk()
	end

	function self.makeEnv(scriptProxy)
		local env = {
			script = scriptProxy,
			require = self.require,
			task = task,
			game = self.game,
			workspace = self.game and self.game:GetService("Workspace") or nil,
			Vector3 = roblox.Vector3,
			Vector2 = roblox.Vector2,
			CFrame = roblox.CFrame,
			Color3 = roblox.Color3,
			UDim = roblox.UDim,
			UDim2 = roblox.UDim2,
			Enum = roblox.Enum,
			Instance = roblox.Instance,
			NumberSequence = roblox.NumberSequence,
			NumberSequenceKeypoint = roblox.NumberSequenceKeypoint,
			ColorSequence = roblox.ColorSequence,
			ColorSequenceKeypoint = roblox.ColorSequenceKeypoint,
			NumberRange = roblox.NumberRange,
			BrickColor = roblox.BrickColor,
			Font = roblox.Font,
			Rect = roblox.Rect,
			PhysicalProperties = roblox.PhysicalProperties,
			Faces = roblox.Faces,
			Axes = roblox.Axes,
			Random = makeRandom(),
			TweenInfo = makeTweenInfo(),
			warn = function(...)
				print("[warn]", ...)
			end,
		}
		for key, value in self.extraGlobals do
			env[key] = value
		end
		return env
	end

	return self
end

return Harness
```

## tests/specs/audio.spec.luau

```lua
-- Audio: every sound the code asks for exists in the banks, every server cue
-- is heard, and a playthrough on the real client produces the right sounds,
-- music and mix.

local fs = require("@lune/fs")
local roblox = require("@lune/roblox")

local game = roblox.Instance.new("DataModel")
local h = Harness.new({ game = game })
local Shared = h.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
local Audio = h.require(Shared.Config.Audio)
local AudioBank = h.require(Shared.Config.AudioBank)
local AudioAssets = h.require(Shared.Config.AudioAssets)

local function sourceFiles(dir, out)
	out = out or {}
	for _, name in fs.readDir(dir) do
		local path = dir .. "/" .. name
		if fs.isDir(path) then
			sourceFiles(path, out)
		elseif string.sub(name, -5) == ".luau" then
			table.insert(out, path)
		end
	end
	return out
end

local function allSource()
	local list = {}
	for _, path in sourceFiles("src") do
		table.insert(list, { path = path, text = fs.readFile(path) })
	end
	return list
end

local function exists(name)
	return AudioBank.Sounds[name] ~= nil
end

test("every sound named in the code exists in the audio banks", function()
	local missing = {}
	local patterns = {
		'Sound%.play%("([%w_]+)"',
		'AudioController%.play%("([%w_]+)"',
		'repeatPlay%("([%w_]+)"',
		'Sound%.loop%("([%w_]+)"',
		'setEmitter%("([%w_]+)"',
		'setLayer%("([%w_]+)"',
		'"ClickSound", "([%w_]+)"',
		'task%.delay%([%d%.]+, AudioController%.play, "([%w_]+)"',
		'task%.delay%([%d%.]+, Sound%.play, "([%w_]+)"',
	}
	local seen = 0
	for _, file in allSource() do
		for _, pattern in patterns do
			for name in string.gmatch(file.text, pattern) do
				seen += 1
				if not exists(name) then
					table.insert(missing, file.path .. ": " .. name)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	expect(seen > 40).toBeTruthy()
	-- Sounds chosen through tables.
	for cue, name in Audio.Cues do
		if not exists(name) then
			table.insert(missing, "Audio.Cues." .. cue .. " -> " .. name)
		end
	end
	for key, name in Audio.Music do
		if not exists(name) then
			table.insert(missing, "Audio.Music." .. key .. " -> " .. name)
		end
	end
	for _, entry in Audio.SubtitleSounds do
		if not exists(entry[2]) then
			table.insert(missing, "SubtitleSounds -> " .. entry[2])
		end
	end
	for _, kind in Audio.StepKinds do
		for i = 1, Audio.StepVariants do
			local name = string.format("Step_%s_%d", kind, i)
			if not exists(name) then
				table.insert(missing, name)
			end
		end
	end
	for name in Audio.Sounds do
		if not exists(name) then
			table.insert(missing, "Audio.Sounds." .. name .. " is not in a bank")
		end
	end
	if #missing > 0 then
		error("missing sounds:\n  " .. table.concat(missing, "\n  "))
	end
end)

test("every bank sound has a mix setting and fits inside its bank", function()
	local problems = {}
	for name, region in AudioBank.Sounds do
		local bank = AudioBank.Banks[region.bank]
		if not bank then
			table.insert(problems, name .. " is in unknown bank " .. region.bank)
		elseif region.start < 0 or region.start + region.length > bank.duration + 1e-3 then
			table.insert(problems, name .. " runs past the end of " .. region.bank)
		end
		if string.sub(name, 1, 5) ~= "Step_" and not Audio.Sounds[name] then
			table.insert(problems, name .. " has no entry in Audio.Sounds")
		end
		local def = Audio.def(name)
		if def.group and not table.find(Audio.Groups, def.group) then
			table.insert(problems, name .. " uses unknown group " .. def.group)
		end
	end
	for _, name in Audio.Music do
		expect(AudioBank.Sounds[name].loop).toBe(true)
	end
	for bankName in AudioBank.Banks do
		expect(AudioAssets.Banks[bankName] ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	end
	if #problems > 0 then
		error(table.concat(problems, "\n  "))
	end
end)

test("every cue the server sends is heard (or deliberately silent)", function()
	-- UI-only cues open screens; their screens make the sounds.
	local silent = { OpenDeck = true, OpenKeypad = true, OpenMenu = true, OpenTutorial = true, ReplayBriefing = true }
	local special = { DoorMoved = true, DoorForced = true, Eliminated = true, Extracted = true, LocalSilence = true, GeneratorPrime = true, PowerOn = true }
	local cues = {}
	for _, file in allSource() do
		for name in string.gmatch(file.text, 'cue = "([%w_]+)"') do
			cues[name] = true
		end
	end
	local deaf = {}
	for name in cues do
		if not silent[name] and not special[name] and not Audio.Cues[name] then
			table.insert(deaf, name)
		end
	end
	table.sort(deaf)
	if #deaf > 0 then
		error("cues with no sound: " .. table.concat(deaf, ", "))
	end
end)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Playthrough on the real client
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function boot()
	local engine = Engine.new()
	local server = Harness.new({ game = engine.game, globals = engine:globals("server") })
	local client = Harness.new({ game = engine.game, globals = engine:globals("client") })
	engine.sharedMounts.server = server.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
	engine.sharedMounts.client = client.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
	-- Pretend the banks are uploaded so real Sound instances are made.
	local assets = client.require(engine.sharedMounts.client.Config.AudioAssets)
	for bank in assets.Banks do
		assets.Banks[bank] = "rbxassetid://1000" .. bank
	end
	local serverRoot = server.mount("Server", "src/server")
	local clientRoot = client.mount("Client", "src/client")
	engine:run("server", function()
		server.run(serverRoot.Main)
	end)
	engine:advance(0.5)
	local player = engine:addPlayer(101, "Ana", true)
	engine:advance(2)
	engine:run("client", function()
		client.run(clientRoot.Main)
	end)
	engine:advance(2)
	return {
		engine = engine,
		server = server,
		client = client,
		player = player,
		State = client.require(clientRoot.State),
		Audio = client.require(clientRoot.Controllers.AudioController),
		serverRoot = serverRoot,
		clientRoot = clientRoot,
	}
end

local function heard(env, name)
	for _, n in env.Audio.history do
		if n == name then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function expectHeard(env, ...)
	local missing = {}
	for _, name in { ... } do
		if not heard(env, name) then
			table.insert(missing, name)
		end
	end
	if #missing > 0 then
		error("did not hear: " .. table.concat(missing, ", ") .. "\nheard: " .. table.concat(env.Audio.history, ", "))
	end
end

local function clearHistory(env)
	table.clear(env.Audio.history)
end

local function assertClean(env, label)
	local e = env.engine
	if #e.errors > 0 then
		error(label .. ": runtime errors\n" .. table.concat(e.errors, "\n----\n"))
	end
	if #e.warnings > 0 then
		error(label .. ": warnings\n" .. table.concat(e.warnings, "\n"))
	end
end

local function soundsNamed(env, name)
	local list = {}
	for _, d in env.engine.realGame:GetDescendants() do
		if d.ClassName == "Sound" and d.Name == name then
			table.insert(list, d)
		end
	end
	return list
end

test("lobby: menu music, office ambience, interface sounds, ready and countdown", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	e:advance(1)
	expect(env.Audio.track).toBe("Menu")
	local menu = soundsNamed(env, "MusicMenu")
	expect(#menu).toBe(1)
	-- The sound plays exactly its slice of the music bank, looping.
	local region = AudioBank.Sounds.MusicMenu
	expect(menu[1].PlaybackRegionsEnabled).toBe(true)
	expect(menu[1].PlaybackRegion.Min).toBeNear(region.start, 1e-3)
	expect(menu[1].LoopRegion.Max).toBeNear(region.start + region.length, 1e-3)
	expect(menu[1].Looped).toBe(true)
	expect(string.find(menu[1].SoundId, "Music", 1, true) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(#soundsNamed(env, "AmbHQ")).toBe(1)

	-- Clicking a menu button clicks; a disabled one buzzes.
	local gui = e.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui
	local function button(text)
		for _, d in gui:GetDescendants() do
			if d.ClassName == "TextButton" and d.Text == text then
				return d
			end
		end
		error("no button " .. text)
	end
	local function click(b)
		e:spawnOnSide("client", function()
			e:signalFor(b, "Activated"):Fire()
		end)
		e:advance(0.2)
	end
	-- Close the first-run tutorial (its pages turn with a sound).
	click(button("NEXT"))
	expectHeard(env, "TutorialPage")
	click(button("SKIP TUTORIAL"))
	click(button("SETTINGS"))
	expectHeard(env, "UIClick")
	click(button("SUBTITLES: ON"))
	expectHeard(env, "UIToggle")

	-- Leaving the menu: field office music.
	e:run("client", function()
		env.client.require(env.clientRoot.Controllers.MenuController).close()
	end)
	e:advance(1)
	expect(env.Audio.track).toBe("Lobby")
	expectHeard(env, "MenuClose")

	-- Ready up: a stamp, then a tick a second until the team deploys.
	clearHistory(env)
	e:run("client", function()
		env.State.action("SetReady", { ready = true })
	end)
	e:advance(3)
	expectHeard(env, "Ready", "Countdown")
	e:advance(5)
	expectHeard(env, "Deploy")
	expect(env.Audio.track).toBe("Briefing")
	assertClean(env, "lobby audio")
end)

test("in the station: ambience by light, radio, doors, pickups, the deck, machines and the hunt", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	e:run("client", function()
		env.State.action("SetReady", { ready = true })
	end)
	e:advance(8)
	expect(env.State.inMission()).toBe(true)
	e:advance(2)
	-- The briefing comes over the radio.
	expectHeard(env, "RadioIn")
	expect(env.Audio.layerLevel("AmbStation") + env.Audio.layerLevel("AmbDark") > 0).toBeTruthy()
	expect(env.Audio.layerLevel("AmbHQ")).toBe(0)

	local start = env.State.mission.briefingEnd
	e:advance(start - e.clock + 1)
	expect(env.State.mission.phase).toBe("Active")
	e:advance(1)
	expect(env.Audio.track).toBe("Explore")

	-- A door, opened on the server, is heard by the client where it is.
	clearHistory(env)
	local WorldService = env.server.require(env.serverRoot.Services.WorldService)
	local doorId
	for id, entry in WorldService.doors do
		if entry.kind == "door" and not entry.locked and not entry.open and #entry.panels > 0 then
			doorId = id
			break
		end
	end
	e:run("server", function()
		WorldService.openDoor(doorId)
	end)
	e:advance(0.3)
	expectHeard(env, "DoorOpen")
	e:run("server", function()
		WorldService.closeDoor(doorId)
	end)
	e:advance(0.3)
	expectHeard(env, "DoorClose")

	-- Picking up the first tape: the pickup and the discovery sting.
	local station = e.realGame:GetService("Workspace").Station04
	local anchor
	for _, d in station:GetDescendants() do
		if d.Name == "ITEM_TAPE01" then
			anchor = d
		end
	end
	local prompt = anchor:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
	local pos = anchor.CFrame.Position
	e:teleport(env.player, roblox.Vector3.new(pos.X + 1.5, 3, pos.Z + 1.5))
	e:signalFor(prompt, "Triggered"):Fire(env.player)
	e:advance(1)
	expectHeard(env, "PickupTape", "StingDiscovery")

	-- The deck: power-on, hum while open, a tape going in, the channel changing.
	clearHistory(env)
	local Deck = env.client.require(env.clientRoot.Controllers.DeckController)
	e:run("client", function()
		Deck.open({ mode = "archive", caseId = "case01", tapeId = "TAPE01" })
	end)
	e:advance(0.5)
	expectHeard(env, "DeckOpen")
	expect(env.Audio.layerLevel("DeckHum")).toBe(1)
	e:run("client", function()
		Deck.selectTape("TAPE01")
		Deck.selectTape("TAPE02")
		Deck.setPlaying(true)
		Deck.step(1 / 24)
		Deck.selectCamera("CAM05")
	end)
	e:advance(1)
	expectHeard(env, "TapeEject", "TapeInsert", "DeckPlay", "FrameStep", "ChannelSwitch")
	e:run("client", function()
		Deck.close()
	end)
	e:advance(0.5)
	expectHeard(env, "DeckClose")
	expect(env.Audio.layerLevel("DeckHum")).toBe(0)

	-- The generator, once running, hums where it stands.
	e:run("server", function()
		local m = env.server.require(env.serverRoot.Services.MissionService).mission
		m.flags.generator_restored = true
		m:_dirty()
	end)
	e:advance(1.5)
	expect(env.Audio.emitterActive("GeneratorLoop")).toBe(true)

	-- A hunting Presenter nearby: the hunt music, its static face, a heartbeat.
	clearHistory(env)
	local character = e.realOf[env.player].Character
	local root = character.HumanoidRootPart
	local model = roblox.Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Presenter"
	local body = roblox.Instance.new("Part")
	body.Name = "Root"
	body.CFrame = roblox.CFrame.new(root.CFrame.Position + roblox.Vector3.new(6, 0, 0))
	body.Parent = model
	model.PrimaryPart = body
	model:SetAttribute("State", "Hunting")
	model.Parent = e.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
	local camera = e.realCamera
	camera.CameraType = roblox.Enum.CameraType.Custom
	camera.CameraSubject = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	e:advance(4)
	expect(env.Audio.track).toBe("Hunt")
	expect(env.Audio.emitterActive("PresenterStatic")).toBe(true)
	expect(env.Audio.layerLevel("PresenterNear") > 0.5).toBeTruthy()
	expectHeard(env, "Heartbeat")
	-- It walks: heavy footsteps where it is.
	for i = 1, 6 do
		body.CFrame = body.CFrame + roblox.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
		e:advance(0.1)
	end
	expectHeard(env, "PresenterStep")
	model:Destroy()
	e:advance(1)
	expect(env.Audio.track).toBe("Explore")
	assertClean(env, "station audio")
end)

test("the mix follows the settings, ducks under radio, and goes silent for the false safety", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	e:advance(2)
	local master = e.realGame:GetService("SoundService").DeadAirMaster
	local music = master.Music
	expect(music.Volume).toBeNear(env.State.settings.music, 0.05)
	e:run("client", function()
		local copy = table.clone(env.State.settings)
		copy.music = 0.2
		copy.master = 0.5
		env.State.applySettings(copy)
	end)
	e:advance(2)
	expect(music.Volume).toBeNear(0.2, 0.02)
	expect(master.Volume).toBeNear(0.5, 1e-3)
	-- A radio line ducks the music.
	e:run("client", function()
		env.State.subtitle:Fire({ kind = "radio", text = "Investigators, this is Division. Hold position.", stamp = "00:00:01", sourceId = "NBRD-HQ", channel = 2 })
	end)
	e:advance(1)
	expect(music.Volume < 0.15).toBeTruthy()
	expectHeard(env, "RadioIn")
	e:advance(6)
	expectHeard(env, "RadioOut")
	expect(music.Volume).toBeNear(0.2, 0.02)
	-- The false safety: everything drops out except the end-of-transmission tone.
	e:run("client", function()
		env.State.cue:Fire({ cue = "LocalSilence", duration = 4 })
	end)
	e:advance(2)
	expect(music.Volume < 0.01).toBeTruthy()
	expect(master.Effects.Volume < 0.01).toBeTruthy()
	expect(master.Cinematic.Volume > 0.5).toBeTruthy()
	e:advance(4)
	expect(music.Volume).toBeNear(0.2, 0.02)
	assertClean(env, "mix")
end)

test("footsteps follow the floor and the default running sound is replaced", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	local character = e.realOf[env.player].Character
	local root = character.HumanoidRootPart
	-- Roblox's default running sound (made by its character sound script).
	local running = roblox.Instance.new("Sound")
	running.Name = "Running"
	running.Volume = 0.65
	e:run("client", function()
		e:wrap(running).Parent = e:wrap(root)
	end)
	e:advance(0.5)
	expect(running.Volume).toBe(0)
	clearHistory(env)
	e:run("client", function()
		env.Audio.footstep(false)
	end)
	-- FloorMaterial is Concrete in the test engine.
	local step = env.Audio.history[#env.Audio.history]
	expect(string.sub(step, 1, 14)).toBe("Step_concrete_")
	assertClean(env, "footsteps")
end)
```

## tests/specs/baked.spec.luau

```lua
test("the baked place's map boots and runs a case", function()
	local fs = require("@lune/fs")
	if not fs.isFile("build/DeadAir.rbxl") then
		return
	end
	local place = roblox.deserializePlace(fs.readFile("build/DeadAir.rbxl"))
	local engine = Engine.new()
	local ws = engine.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
	for _, child in place:GetService("Workspace"):GetChildren() do
		if child.Name == "Station04" or child.Name == "FieldOffice" then
			child.Parent = ws
		end
	end
	local server = Harness.new({ game = engine.game, globals = engine:globals("server") })
	engine.sharedMounts.server = server.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
	local root = server.mount("Server", "src/server")
	engine:run("server", function() server.run(root.Main) end)
	engine:advance(0.5)
	local player = engine:addPlayer(7, "Cal", false)
	engine:advance(3)
	engine:fireServerAs(player, "Action", { type = "SetReady", ready = true })
	engine:advance(50)
	local m = server.require(root.Services.MissionService).mission
	assert(m and m.phase == "Active", "case did not start on the baked map")
	local prompts = 0
	for _, d in ws.Station04:GetDescendants() do
		if d.ClassName == "ProximityPrompt" then
			prompts += 1
		end
	end
	assert(prompts > 40, "too few prompts: " .. prompts)
	if #engine.errors > 0 or #engine.warnings > 0 then
		error(table.concat(engine.errors, "\n") .. table.concat(engine.warnings, "\n"))
	end
end)
```

## tests/specs/core.spec.luau

```lua
-- Unit tests for the engine-independent game core.

local h = Harness.new()
local Shared = h.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
local req = h.require

local Case01 = req(Shared.Config.Cases.Case01)
local MapLayout = req(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local TimelineCore = req(Shared.Core.TimelineCore)
local PresenterBrain = req(Shared.Core.PresenterBrain)
local SignalModel = req(Shared.Core.SignalModel)
local Grading = req(Shared.Core.Grading)
local Footage = req(Shared.Core.Footage)
local Variants = req(Shared.Core.Variants)
local Format = req(Shared.Util.Format)
local Rng = req(Shared.Util.Rng)

test("Case 01 passes static validation (no cycles, no schedule conflicts)", function()
	local errors = TimelineCore.validateCase(Case01, MapLayout)
	if #errors > 0 then
		error("validation errors:\n  " .. table.concat(errors, "\n  "))
	end
end)

test("validator catches overlapping Presenter windows and bad derivations", function()
	local broken = TimelineCore.deepCopy(Case01)
	-- Move the gantry watcher into the climax window.
	for _, ev in broken.events do
		if ev.id == "transmitter_watch" then
			ev.offset = 31
		end
		if ev.id == "presenter_route_east" then
			ev.stateMap.ON = "NOWHERE"
		end
	end
	local errors = TimelineCore.validateCase(broken, MapLayout)
	local text = table.concat(errors, "\n")
	expect(text).toContain("overlap")
	expect(text).toContain("unknown state NOWHERE")
end)

test("every map reference in Case 01 resolves", function()
	for id, def in Case01.interactables do
		if def.kind == "Deck" then
			expect(Case01.decks[def.deck] ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
		elseif def.kind == "Relay" then
			expect(MapLayout.Relays[def.relay] ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
		else
			expect(def.pos ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
		end
		local pos = def.pos or (def.deck and Case01.decks[def.deck].pos)
		if pos then
			local room = MapLayout.roomAt(pos.x, pos.z)
			if room == nil then
				error(id .. " is outside every room")
			end
		end
	end
	for door in Case01.doorLocks do
		expect(MapLayout.doorById(door) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	end
	for itemId, item in Case01.items do
		for _, pos in item.spawns or {} do
			if not MapLayout.roomAt(pos.x, pos.z) then
				error(itemId .. " spawn is outside every room")
			end
		end
	end
end)

test("presenter paths stay inside rooms the footage can see", function()
	for _, ev in Case01.events do
		for state, def in ev.states do
			local cmd = def.presenter
			if cmd and cmd.path then
				for i, key in cmd.path do
					if not MapLayout.roomAt(key.pos.x, key.pos.z) then
						error(string.format("%s.%s keyframe %d is outside the map", ev.id, state, i))
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

test("map doors sit on a shared boundary of the rooms they connect", function()
	for _, door in MapLayout.Doors do
		for _, roomId in door.rooms do
			local room = MapLayout.Rooms[roomId]
			local onEdge
			if door.plane == "z" then
				onEdge = (door.at == room.z0 or door.at == room.z1)
					and door.center - door.width / 2 >= room.x0
					and door.center + door.width / 2 <= room.x1
			else
				onEdge = (door.at == room.x0 or door.at == room.x1)
					and door.center - door.width / 2 >= room.z0
					and door.center + door.width / 2 <= room.z1
			end
			if not onEdge then
				error(door.id .. " is not on the edge of " .. roomId)
			end
		end
	end
end)

test("rooms never overlap", function()
	local ids = {}
	for id in MapLayout.Rooms do
		table.insert(ids, id)
	end
	for i = 1, #ids do
		for j = i + 1, #ids do
			local a, b = MapLayout.Rooms[ids[i]], MapLayout.Rooms[ids[j]]
			local overlap = a.x0 < b.x1 and b.x0 < a.x1 and a.z0 < b.z1 and b.z0 < a.z1
			if overlap then
				error(ids[i] .. " overlaps " .. ids[j])
			end
		end
	end
end)

test("timeline: valid edit changes the event and its linked consequence", function()
	local tl = TimelineCore.new(Case01)
	tl:setAnchor("broadcast_start", 100)
	expect(tl:effectiveState("presenter_entry")).toBe("LOBBY")
	local ok, code = tl:validateEdit("lobby_lights", "ON", 120, { flags = {}, charges = 3, tapeFound = true })
	expect(ok).toBe(true)
	expect(code).toBe("OK")
	local entry = tl:commit("lobby_lights", "ON", 1, "Ana", 120)
	expect(entry.consequences[1].eventId).toBe("presenter_entry")
	expect(entry.consequences[1].to).toBe("SOUTH_HALL")
	expect(tl:effectiveState("presenter_entry")).toBe("SOUTH_HALL")
end)

test("timeline: invalid edits are refused with a reason", function()
	local tl = TimelineCore.new(Case01)
	tl:setAnchor("broadcast_start", 0)
	local ctx = { flags = {}, charges = 3, tapeFound = true }
	local function code(id, state, now, c)
		local _, reason = tl:validateEdit(id, state, now, c or ctx)
		return reason
	end
	expect(code("nope", "ON", 1)).toBe("UNKNOWN_EVENT")
	expect(code("presenter_entry", "SOUTH_HALL", 1)).toBe("NOT_EDITABLE")
	expect(code("lobby_lights", "PURPLE", 1)).toBe("INVALID_STATE")
	expect(code("lobby_lights", "OFF", 1)).toBe("NO_CHANGE")
	expect(code("lobby_lights", "ON", 1, { flags = {}, charges = 0, tapeFound = true })).toBe("NO_CHARGES")
	expect(code("lobby_lights", "ON", 1, { flags = {}, charges = 3, tapeFound = false })).toBe("TAPE_MISSING")
	expect(code("east_corridor_lights", "ON", 1)).toBe("PREREQUISITE")
	-- Locks 5 seconds before it fires (fires at 150).
	expect(code("lobby_lights", "ON", 146)).toBe("LOCKED")
	expect(code("lobby_lights", "ON", 144)).toBe("OK")
	expect(code("lobby_lights", "ON", 144, { flags = {}, charges = 3, tapeFound = true, expectedState = "ON" })).toBe("VERSION")
end)

test("timeline: no event can be edited twice", function()
	local tl = TimelineCore.new(Case01)
	tl:setAnchor("broadcast_start", 0)
	tl:commit("lobby_lights", "ON", 1, "Ana", 10)
	local ok, code, message = tl:validateEdit("lobby_lights", "OFF", 11, { flags = {}, charges = 3, tapeFound = true })
	expect(ok).toBe(false)
	expect(code).toBe("ALREADY_EDITED")
	expect(message).toContain("Ana")
end)

test("timeline: events fire at their time with the committed state, then lock", function()
	local tl = TimelineCore.new(Case01)
	tl:setAnchor("broadcast_start", 0)
	tl:commit("lobby_lights", "ON", 1, "Ana", 10)
	local early = tl:update(140)
	expect(#early).toBe(0)
	local outputs = tl:update(160)
	local kinds = {}
	for _, o in outputs do
		kinds[o.kind .. ":" .. o.eventId] = o
	end
	expect(kinds["fired:lobby_lights"] ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(kinds["presenter:presenter_entry"].command.path[1].pos.z).toBe(MapLayout.point("ControlRoom", 0.5, 0.85).z)
	-- The lights stayed on: no Lights action.
	for _, o in outputs do
		if o.kind == "action" then
			expect(o.action.type ~= "Lights").toBeTruthy()
		end
	end
	expect(tl:isLocked("lobby_lights", 160)).toBe(true)
	-- Running update again emits nothing new.
	expect(#tl:update(165)).toBe(0)
end)

test("timeline: patrol variant reverses the east corridor patrol", function()
	local normal = TimelineCore.new(Case01)
	local flipped = TimelineCore.new(Case01, { patrolFlip = true })
	local a = normal:getEvent("presenter_route_east").states.EAST_CORRIDOR.presenter.path
	local b = flipped:getEvent("presenter_route_east").states.EAST_CORRIDOR.presenter.path
	expect(a[1].pos.x).toBe(b[2].pos.x)
	expect(a[2].pos.x).toBe(b[1].pos.x)
	-- The shared case table is untouched.
	expect(Case01.events[4].states.EAST_CORRIDOR.presenter.path[1].pos.x).toBe(a[1].pos.x)
end)

local function sensed(id, dist, opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	return {
		id = id,
		pos = opts.pos or { x = dist, y = 0, z = 0 },
		dist = dist,
		los = opts.los ~= false,
		angle = opts.angle or 0,
		lit = opts.lit ~= false,
		flashlight = opts.flashlight or false,
		hidden = opts.hidden or false,
		invulnerable = opts.invulnerable or false,
	}
end

local function displacedCmd()
	return {
		kind = "Displaced",
		duration = 60,
		huntOnSight = true,
		sight = { range = 40, dark = 20, fov = 120, proximity = 6 },
		path = { { t = 0, pos = { x = 0, y = 0, z = 0 } }, { t = 60, pos = { x = 0, y = 0, z = 0 } } },
	}
end

test("presenter: hunts only after a wind-up, never instantly", function()
	local brain = PresenterBrain.new()
	brain:setCommand(displacedCmd(), 0, { scripted = true })
	local out = brain:update(0.1, { players = { sensed(1, 20) } })
	expect(out.state).toBe("Hunting")
	expect(out.huntPhase).toBe("windup")
	expect(out.speed).toBe(0)
	out = brain:update(1.5, { players = { sensed(1, 2) } })
	expect(out.catchId).toBe(nil)
	out = brain:update(2.7, { players = { sensed(1, 10) } })
	expect(out.huntPhase).toBe("chase")
	expect(out.moveTo ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
end)

test("presenter: does not see players in the dark beyond dark range, or behind it", function()
	local brain = PresenterBrain.new()
	brain:setCommand(displacedCmd(), 0, { scripted = true })
	local out = brain:update(0.1, { players = { sensed(1, 30, { lit = false }) } })
	expect(out.state).toBe("Displaced")
	out = brain:update(0.2, { players = { sensed(1, 30, { lit = false, flashlight = true }) } })
	expect(out.state).toBe("Hunting")
	brain = PresenterBrain.new()
	brain:setCommand(displacedCmd(), 0, { scripted = true })
	out = brain:update(0.1, { players = { sensed(1, 15, { angle = 170 }) } })
	expect(out.state).toBe("Displaced")
end)

test("presenter: loses a player who breaks line of sight, searches, then gives up", function()
	local brain = PresenterBrain.new()
	brain:setCommand(displacedCmd(), 0, { scripted = true })
	brain:update(0, { players = { sensed(1, 20) } })
	brain:update(3, { players = { sensed(1, 20) } })
	local out = brain:update(4, { players = { sensed(1, 25, { los = false }) } })
	expect(out.huntPhase).toBe("chase")
	out = brain:update(9.2, { players = { sensed(1, 25, { los = false }) } })
	expect(out.huntPhase).toBe("search")
	out = brain:update(14, { players = { sensed(1, 25, { los = false }) } })
	expect(out.state).toBe("Dormant")
	local names = {}
	for _, e in out.events or {} do
		names[e.name] = e.data
	end
	expect(names.HuntEnd.reason).toBe("Lost")
end)

test("presenter: catches only within reach and line of sight", function()
	local brain = PresenterBrain.new()
	brain:setCommand(displacedCmd(), 0, { scripted = true })
	brain:update(0, { players = { sensed(1, 20) } })
	brain:update(3, { players = { sensed(1, 10) } })
	local out = brain:update(3.5, { players = { sensed(1, 3, { los = false }) } })
	expect(out.catchId).toBe(nil)
	out = brain:update(4, { players = { sensed(1, 3) } })
	expect(out.catchId).toBe(1)
	expect(out.state).toBe("Dormant")
end)

test("presenter: refuges and revive protection make players invisible", function()
	local brain = PresenterBrain.new()
	brain:setCommand(displacedCmd(), 0, { scripted = true })
	local out = brain:update(0.1, { players = { sensed(1, 3, { hidden = true }), sensed(2, 3, { invulnerable = true }) } })
	expect(out.state).toBe("Displaced")
end)

test("presenter: hunts end at the time limit", function()
	local brain = PresenterBrain.new({ maxHunt = 10 })
	brain:setCommand(displacedCmd(), 0, { scripted = true })
	brain:update(0, { players = { sensed(1, 30) } })
	local out
	for t = 1, 12 do
		out = brain:update(t, { players = { sensed(1, 30) } })
	end
	expect(out.state).toBe("Dormant")
end)

test("presenter: an ambush with nobody nearby turns into observing", function()
	local brain = PresenterBrain.new()
	brain:setCommand({ kind = "Ambush", pos = { x = 0, y = 0, z = 0 }, look = { x = 1, y = 0, z = 0 }, radius = 20, windup = 2, maxDuration = 30 }, 0, { scripted = true })
	local out = brain:update(0.1, { players = { sensed(1, 50) } })
	expect(out.teleportTo ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(out.state).toBe("Hunting")
	out = brain:update(2.5, { players = { sensed(1, 50) } })
	expect(out.state).toBe("Observing")
end)

test("signal quality is deterministic and never random", function()
	local params = { relay = "EAST", powerOn = true, relayOn = false, deckBattery = false, presenterDistance = 20, anomaly = false }
	local a = SignalModel.cameraQuality(params)
	local b = SignalModel.cameraQuality(params)
	expect(a).toBe(b)
	expect(SignalModel.band(a)).toBe("LOST")
	local unpowered = SignalModel.cameraQuality({ relay = "WEST", powerOn = false })
	expect(unpowered).toBe(0)
	local lobby = SignalModel.cameraQuality({ relay = "LOCAL", powerOn = false, deckBattery = true })
	expect(SignalModel.band(lobby)).toBe("WEAK")
	expect(SignalModel.playerQuality({ powerOn = true, presenterDistance = nil })).toBe(92)
end)

test("grading: rewards survival, evidence and predictions; failure caps at D", function()
	local perfect = Grading.compute({
		result = "Success", participants = 2, survivors = 2,
		evidenceFound = 5, evidenceTotal = 5,
		predictionsInterpreted = 8, predictionsTotal = 8,
		editsMade = 1, maxCharges = 3, eliminationsDuringEdits = 0,
		loopFirstTry = true, callerFooled = false,
	})
	expect(perfect.grade).toBe("S")
	local failed = Grading.compute({ result = "Failed", participants = 1, survivors = 0, maxCharges = 3, loopFirstTry = true })
	expect(failed.grade).toBe("D")
	local rewards = Grading.rewards({ result = "Success", grade = "S", anomaliesIdentified = 2, bonusTapeExtracted = true }, { itemsRecovered = 3, evidenceRecovered = 1, framesMarked = 1, extracted = true })
	expect(rewards.credits).toBe(120 + 80 + 40)
	expect(rewards.research).toBe(20 + 15 + 10 + 25)
end)

test("footage predicts the configured events and updates after an edit", function()
	local tl = TimelineCore.new(Case01)
	local defs = tl:eventDefinitions()
	local snap = tl:snapshot(0)
	-- Original prediction: reception lights fail at 02:30 and it walks in.
	expect(Footage.lightsAt(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 149)).toBe("emergency")
	expect(Footage.lightsAt(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 151)).toBe("off")
	local f = Footage.frame(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 165)
	local presenters = 0
	for _, a in f.actors do
		if a.kind == "Presenter" then
			presenters += 1
		end
	end
	expect(presenters).toBe(1)
	expect(#Footage.frame(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01", "CAM02", 165).actors).toBe(0)
	-- After the edit the lights hold and the figure shows up on CAM 02 instead.
	tl:commit("lobby_lights", "ON", 1, "Ana", 10)
	snap = tl:snapshot(10)
	expect(Footage.lightsAt(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 151)).toBe("emergency")
	expect(#Footage.frame(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 170).actors).toBe(0)
	expect(#Footage.frame(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01", "CAM02", 170).actors).toBe(1)
	local markers = Footage.markers(Case01, defs, snap, "TAPE01")
	expect(markers[1].eventId).toBe("lobby_lights")
	expect(markers[1].state).toBe("ON")
end)

test("footage shows the hidden frame only on its exact frame", function()
	local tl = TimelineCore.new(Case01)
	local f = Footage.frame(Case01, tl:eventDefinitions(), tl:snapshot(0), "TAPE01", "CAM01", 12.4)
	expect(f.hiddenFrame.id).toBe("HF_T01_FIFTH")
	f = Footage.frame(Case01, tl:eventDefinitions(), tl:snapshot(0), "TAPE01", "CAM01", 14)
	expect(f.hiddenFrame).toBe(nil)
end)

test("variants are deterministic per seed and the Caller's code is always wrong", function()
	for seed = 1, 200 do
		local a = Variants.compute(Case01, seed)
		local b = Variants.compute(Case01, seed)
		expect(a.code).toBe(b.code)
		expect(a.loopOdd).toBe(b.loopOdd)
		expect(a.code ~= a.fakeCode).toBeTruthy()
		expect(#a.code).toBe(4)
	end
	local seen = {}
	for seed = 1, 60 do
		seen[Variants.compute(Case01, seed).loopOdd] = true
	end
	local kinds = 0
	for _ in seen do
		kinds += 1
	end
	expect(kinds).toBeAtLeast(4)
end)

test("rng and format helpers", function()
	local r1, r2 = Rng.new(42), Rng.new(42)
	for _ = 1, 20 do
		expect(r1:int(1, 6)).toBe(r2:int(1, 6))
	end
	local r = Rng.new(-5)
	for _ = 1, 50 do
		local v = r:float()
		expect(v >= 0 and v < 1).toBeTruthy()
	end
	expect(Format.clock(3725)).toBe("01:02:05")
	expect(Format.timecode(1.5)).toBe("00:00:01:15")
	expect(Format.relative(150)).toBe("T+02:30")
end)
```
