## tests/specs/map.spec.luau

```lua
-- Builds Broadcast Station 04 headlessly. Lune validates every class, property and
-- enum name against Roblox's reflection database, and the geometry checks below
-- make sure scripted routes and objectives are physically reachable.

local roblox = require("@lune/roblox")

local game = roblox.Instance.new("DataModel")
local h = Harness.new({ game = game })
local Shared = h.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
local req = h.require

local Case01 = req(Shared.Config.Cases.Case01)
local MapLayout = req(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local MapBuilder = req(Shared.World.MapBuilder)
local MissionCore = req(Shared.Core.MissionCore)

local workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local station, hq

local function build()
	if not station then
		station, hq = MapBuilder.build(workspace, Case01)
	end
	return station, hq
end

local function solidParts(root, filter)
	local list = {}
	for _, d in root:GetDescendants() do
		-- Invisible collision boxes (bench frames, tripods) still block.
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide and (not filter or filter(d)) then
			table.insert(list, d)
		end
	end
	return list
end

local function blockedBy(parts, point, radius, y0, y1)
	for _, p in parts do
		local cf, size = p.CFrame, p.Size
		local top = cf.Position.Y + size.Y / 2
		local bottom = cf.Position.Y - size.Y / 2
		-- Rough vertical test using the axis-aligned extent.
		if bottom < y1 and top > y0 then
			local l = cf:PointToObjectSpace(roblox.Vector3.new(point.x, cf.Position.Y, point.z))
			if math.abs(l.X) < size.X / 2 + radius and math.abs(l.Z) < size.Z / 2 + radius then
				return p
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

test("the station builds without engine errors", function()
	build()
	expect(station.Name).toBe("Station04")
	expect(#station.Rooms:GetChildren()).toBe(#MapLayout.StationRooms)
	expect(hq.Name).toBe("FieldOffice")
	local count = 0
	for _, d in station:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			count += 1
		end
	end
	print(string.format("      station parts: %d", count))
	expect(count).toBeAtMost(4000)
end)

test("every door in the layout is built, with a panel unless it is an opening", function()
	build()
	for _, door in MapLayout.Doors do
		local parent = door.rooms[1] == "HQ" and hq.Doors or station.Doors
		local model = parent:FindFirstChild(door.id)
		if not model then
			error("missing door " .. door.id)
		end
		if door.kind ~= "opening" and not model.PrimaryPart then
			error(door.id .. " has no primary panel")
		end
	end
end)

test("every interactable has a prompt anchor", function()
	build()
	for id in Case01.interactables do
		if not station.Anchors:FindFirstChild(id) then
			error("missing anchor " .. id)
		end
	end
	for _, prop in Case01.loop.props do
		expect(station.Anchors:FindFirstChild(prop.id) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
		expect(station.LoopProps:FindFirstChild(prop.id) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	end
	for id in MapBuilder.HQTerminals do
		expect(hq.Anchors:FindFirstChild(id) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	end
end)

test("CCTV monitors reference real cameras", function()
	build()
	local monitors = 0
	for _, d in station:GetDescendants() do
		if d:HasTag("CCTVMonitor") then
			monitors += 1
			expect(MapLayout.Cameras[d:GetAttribute("CameraId")] ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
		end
	end
	expect(monitors).toBeAtLeast(10)
end)

test("Presenter routes never pass through walls or furniture", function()
	build()
	local solids = solidParts(station.Rooms)
	local problems = {}
	for _, ev in Case01.events do
		for state, def in ev.states do
			local cmd = def.presenter
			local points = {}
			if cmd and cmd.path then
				for i = 1, #cmd.path - 1 do
					local a, b = cmd.path[i].pos, cmd.path[i + 1].pos
					local dist = math.sqrt((b.x - a.x) ^ 2 + (b.z - a.z) ^ 2)
					local steps = math.max(1, math.ceil(dist / 0.5))
					for s = 0, steps do
						local t = s / steps
						table.insert(points, { x = a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, z = a.z + (b.z - a.z) * t })
					end
				end
			elseif cmd and cmd.pos then
				table.insert(points, cmd.pos)
			end
			for _, pt in points do
				local hit = blockedBy(solids, pt, 1.0, 0.3, 8.4)
				if hit then
					table.insert(problems, string.format("%s.%s at (%.1f, %.1f) hits %s", ev.id, state, pt.x, pt.z, hit:GetFullName()))
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if #problems > 0 then
		error(table.concat(problems, "\n      "))
	end
end)

test("spawns and checkpoints are standing room", function()
	build()
	local solids = solidParts(station.Rooms)
	for i, pos in Case01.spawns do
		local hit = blockedBy(solids, pos, 1.2, 0.3, 5)
		if hit then
			error("spawn " .. i .. " blocked by " .. hit:GetFullName())
		end
	end
	for _, cp in Case01.checkpoints do
		local hit = blockedBy(solids, cp.pos, 1.2, 0.3, 5)
		if hit then
			error("checkpoint " .. cp.id .. " blocked by " .. hit:GetFullName())
		end
	end
end)

test("items rest on a surface and are not buried inside props", function()
	build()
	local solids = solidParts(station.Rooms)
	local problems = {}
	for itemId, item in Case01.items do
		for i, pos in item.spawns or {} do
			-- Something must be directly under the item (or it sits on the floor).
			local supported = pos.y < 0.2
			for _, p in solids do
				local cf, size = p.CFrame, p.Size
				local l = cf:PointToObjectSpace(roblox.Vector3.new(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z))
				local insideXZ = math.abs(l.X) <= size.X / 2 and math.abs(l.Z) <= size.Z / 2
				local top = cf.Position.Y + size.Y / 2
				if insideXZ and math.abs(top - pos.y) < 0.35 then
					supported = true
				end
				if insideXZ and l.Y > -size.Y / 2 + 0.05 and l.Y < size.Y / 2 - 0.4 then
					table.insert(problems, string.format("%s spawn %d is inside %s", itemId, i, p:GetFullName()))
				end
			end
			if not supported then
				table.insert(problems, string.format("%s spawn %d floats at y=%.2f", itemId, i, pos.y))
			end
		end
	end
	if #problems > 0 then
		error(table.concat(problems, "\n      "))
	end
end)

test("interactables are within reach of open floor", function()
	build()
	local solids = solidParts(station.Rooms)
	local m = MissionCore.new(Case01, { seed = 1, now = 0, participants = {} })
	for id, def in m:getInteractables() do
		local pos = def.pos
		if not pos and def.kind == "Relay" then
			pos = MapLayout.Relays[def.relay].pos
		end
		if pos then
			-- Find a free standing spot within 6 studs.
			local found = false
			for r = 1, 6 do
				for a = 0, 15 do
					local ang = a / 16 * math.pi * 2
					local probe = { x = pos.x + math.cos(ang) * r, z = pos.z + math.sin(ang) * r }
					if MapLayout.roomAt(probe.x, probe.z) and not blockedBy(solids, probe, 1, 0.3, 5) then
						found = true
						break
					end
				end
				if found then
					break
				end
			end
			if not found then
				error(id .. " cannot be reached")
			end
		end
	end
end)

test("the Presenter rig and footage figures build", function()
	local Figures = req(Shared.World.Figures)
	local live = Figures.presenter(workspace)
	expect(live:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(live.Humanoid.HipHeight).toBeNear(Figures.LEG_LENGTH, 1e-4)
	expect(live.PrimaryPart.Name).toBe("HumanoidRootPart")
	expect(live:HasTag("Presenter")).toBe(true)
	local parts = 0
	for _, d in live:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			parts += 1
			if d ~= live.PrimaryPart then
				expect(d.Anchored).toBe(false)
			end
		end
	end
	expect(parts).toBeAtMost(25)
	local ghost = Figures.presenter(workspace, { ghost = true })
	expect(ghost:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")).toBe(nil)
	expect(Figures.rootHeight(ghost)).toBeNear(Figures.LEG_LENGTH + 1, 1e-4)
	local person = Figures.investigator(workspace)
	expect(Figures.rootHeight(person)).toBe(3)
end)

test("every surface is a real Roblox material and photo textures plug in", function()
	local Surfaces = req(Shared.Config.Surfaces)
	for name, def in Surfaces.List do
		local ok = pcall(function()
			return roblox.Enum.Material[def.material]
		end)
		if not ok then
			error(name .. " uses unknown material " .. def.material)
		end
		if def.variant and not Surfaces.Variants[def.variant] then
			error(name .. " names missing variant " .. def.variant)
		end
	end
	-- Nothing configured: no variants, built-in materials only.
	local ms = roblox.Instance.new("MaterialService")
	expect(Surfaces.install(ms)).toBe(0)
	expect(Surfaces.variantFor("lino") == nil).toBeTruthy()
	-- Paste an id and the variant is installed and used.
	Surfaces.Variants.DeadAir_Linoleum.colorMap = "rbxassetid://1"
	expect(Surfaces.install(ms)).toBe(1)
	expect(ms.DeadAir_Linoleum.BaseMaterial).toBe(roblox.Enum.Material.CeramicTiles)
	expect(Surfaces.variantFor("lino")).toBe("DeadAir_Linoleum")
	local Props = req(Shared.World.Props)
	local p = Props.part(workspace, "T", roblox.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), roblox.CFrame.new(), "grey", { surface = "lino" })
	expect(p.MaterialVariant).toBe("DeadAir_Linoleum")
	Surfaces.Variants.DeadAir_Linoleum.colorMap = ""
	p:Destroy()
end)

test("the station and field office are built from textured surfaces", function()
	build()
	local counts, smooth, total = {}, 0, 0
	for _, root in { station, hq } do
		for _, d in root:GetDescendants() do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Transparency < 1 then
				total += 1
				counts[d.Material.Name] = (counts[d.Material.Name] or 0) + 1
				if d.Material == roblox.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic then
					smooth += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end
	-- Untextured smooth plastic is kept for signal lamps and a few painted signs.
	expect(smooth / total < 0.06).toBeTruthy()
	for _, m in { "CeramicTiles", "Carpet", "Plaster", "Brick", "Wood", "Metal", "Plastic", "Rubber", "Fabric", "Leather", "Cardboard", "Glass" } do
		if not counts[m] then
			error("no part uses " .. m)
		end
	end
	-- The lobby is dressed as an office.
	for _, name in { "CorkBoard", "WaterCooler", "WindowGlass", "CoatRack" } do
		if not hq:FindFirstChild(name, true) then
			error("field office missing " .. name)
		end
	end
end)

test("round parts are sized the way Roblox draws them", function()
	-- A cylinder runs along X with diameter min(Y, Z); a ball uses the smallest
	-- side. Unequal sides mean the part renders differently from its size.
	build()
	local problems = {}
	for _, root in { station, hq } do
		for _, d in root:GetDescendants() do
			if d:IsA("Part") then
				local s = d.Size
				if d.Shape == roblox.Enum.PartType.Cylinder and math.abs(s.Y - s.Z) > 1e-3 then
					table.insert(problems, d:GetFullName() .. " cylinder " .. tostring(s))
				elseif d.Shape == roblox.Enum.PartType.Ball and (math.abs(s.X - s.Y) > 1e-3 or math.abs(s.Y - s.Z) > 1e-3) then
					table.insert(problems, d:GetFullName() .. " ball " .. tostring(s))
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if #problems > 0 then
		error(table.concat(problems, "\n      "))
	end
end)

## tests/specs/mission.spec.luau

```lua
-- Simulated playthroughs of Case 01 through MissionCore (the acceptance tests
-- that can be run without Roblox Studio).

local h = Harness.new()
local Shared = h.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
local req = h.require

local Case01 = req(Shared.Config.Cases.Case01)
local MissionCore = req(Shared.Core.MissionCore)

local SEED = 424242

local function newMission(players, mode)
	local list = {}
	for i, name in players do
		table.insert(list, { userId = i, name = name })
	end
	return MissionCore.new(Case01, { mode = mode or "Standard", seed = SEED, now = 0, participants = list })
end

-- Ticks the mission and collects every effect.
local function advance(m, from, to, world)
	local effects = {}
	local t = from
	while t <= to + 1e-9 do
		m:update(t, world or { presenterIdle = true })
		for _, e in m:drainEffects() do
			e._t = t
			table.insert(effects, e)
		end
		t += 0.5
	end
	return effects
end

local function find(effects, predicate)
	for _, e in effects do
		if predicate(e) then
			return e
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function ok(m, user, id, now)
	local success, msg = m:interact(user, id, now)
	if not success then
		error(string.format("interact %s failed: %s", id, tostring(msg)), 2)
	end
end

-- Finds a time >= now at which the odd prop is showing.
local function nextOddTime(m, now)
	local t = now
	for _ = 1, 200 do
		local _, odd = m:loopCycle(t)
		if odd then
			return t + 0.5
		end
		t += 0.5
	end
	error("no odd cycle")
end

test("solo: a single player can start and finish Case 01", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Briefing")
	local fx = advance(m, 0, 41)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Active")
	expect(m.broadcastStart).toBe(40)
	expect(find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Door" and e.door == "D_Lobby_Main" and e.locked
	end) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(m:currentObjective().id).toBe("tape01")

	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_TAPE01", 42)
	expect(m:currentObjective().id).toBe("watch01")
	expect(m:deckOpened(1, "DECK_LOBBY", "TAPE01", 43)).toBe(true)
	expect(m:currentObjective().id).toBe("decide01")
	expect(m:currentObjective().text).toContain("00:02:30")
	m:inspectEvent(1, "lobby_lights", 44)

	-- Invalid edits never spend a charge.
	local good, code = m:commitEdit(1, "lobby_lights", "PURPLE", { deckId = "DECK_LOBBY" }, 45)
	expect(good).toBe(false)
	expect(code).toBe("INVALID_STATE")
	expect(m.charges).toBe(3)
	good, code = m:commitEdit(1, "east_corridor_lights", "ON", { deckId = "DECK_LOBBY" }, 45)
	expect(code).toBe("TAPE_MISSING")
	good, code = m:commitEdit(1, "lobby_lights", "ON", { deckId = "DECK_MAIN" }, 45)
	expect(code).toBe("NO_DECK")
	expect(m.charges).toBe(3)

	-- Keep the reception lights on: it waits outside the control room instead.
	good = m:commitEdit(1, "lobby_lights", "ON", { deckId = "DECK_LOBBY", expectedState = "OFF" }, 46)
	expect(good).toBe(true)
	expect(m.charges).toBe(2)
	expect(m:currentObjective().id).toBe("leave")
	m:deckClosed(1)

	fx = advance(m, 41.5, 200)
	local cmd = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Presenter" and e.eventId == "presenter_entry"
	end)
	expect(cmd ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(cmd._t).toBe(40 + 155)
	expect(cmd.command.path[2].pos.z).toBe(23.2) -- south hall, outside the control room
	expect(find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Lights" and e.room == "Lobby" and e.mode == "off"
	end)).toBe(nil)
	local shadow = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Cue" and e.cue == "EditorShadow"
	end)
	expect(shadow == nil).toBeTruthy() -- fires at 210, after this window
	fx = advance(m, 200.5, 211)
	shadow = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Cue" and e.cue == "EditorShadow"
	end)
	expect(shadow.userId).toBe(1)
	expect(m:currentObjective().id).toBe("power")

	-- Generator: two stages, then power.
	local before = m:promptState("I_DECK_MAIN")
	expect(before.action).toBe("No Power")
	ok(m, 1, "I_GENERATOR", 212)
	expect(m.flags.generator_restored).toBe(nil)
	ok(m, 1, "I_GENERATOR", 214)
	expect(m.flags.generator_restored).toBe(true)
	expect(m:promptState("I_DECK_MAIN").action).toBe("Open Deck")
	expect(m:isDoorLocked("D_EC_Arch")).toBe(false)
	expect(m:isDoorLocked("D_TH_Trans")).toBe(true)
	fx = advance(m, 214, 245)
	local radio = {}
	for _, e in fx do
		if e.type == "Subtitle" and e.kind == "radio" then
			table.insert(radio, e)
		end
	end
	expect(#radio).toBe(3)
	expect(radio[1].channel).toBe(2)
	expect(radio[1].text).toContain(m.variants.code)
	expect(radio[2].channel).toBe(0)
	expect(radio[2].caller).toBe(true)
	expect(radio[2].text).toContain(m.variants.fakeCode)
	expect(radio[2].stamp ~= radio[1].stamp).toBeTruthy()

	-- The keypad: the true code from Channel 2.
	ok(m, 1, "I_CAGE", 246)
	local opened = m:submitCode(1, m.variants.code, 247)
	expect(opened).toBe(true)
	expect(m.inventory.VALVE ~= nil).toBeTruthy()

	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_TAPE02", 250)
	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_COIL", 251)
	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_TAPE03", 252)
	expect(m:currentObjective().text).toContain("2/3")

	-- Leave the east corridor prediction alone (OFF): the patrol starts at +95.
	fx = advance(m, 245.5, 214 + 96)
	local patrol = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Presenter" and e.eventId == "presenter_route_east"
	end)
	expect(patrol ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Relay" and e.relay == "EAST" and e.on == false
	end) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	ok(m, 1, "I_RELAY_EAST", 312)
	expect(m.relays.EAST).toBe(true)

	-- The Loop: a wrong guess resets it; the odd detail on an odd cycle breaks it.
	ok(m, 1, "I_RACK", 320)
	expect(m.loop.active).toBe(true)
	expect(m:isDoorLocked("D_EC_Arch")).toBe(true)
	local wrongProp
	for _, prop in Case01.loop.props do
		if prop.id ~= m.loop.odd then
			wrongProp = prop.id
			break
		end
	end
	local wrong = m:interact(1, wrongProp, 321)
	expect(wrong).toBe(false)
	expect(m.loop.attempts).toBe(1)
	local tOdd = nextOddTime(m, 321)
	ok(m, 1, m.loop.odd, tOdd)
	expect(m.loop.solved).toBe(true)
	expect(m:isDoorLocked("D_EC_Arch")).toBe(false)
	ok(m, 1, "I_RACK", tOdd + 1)
	expect(m.flags.board_recovered).toBe(true)
	expect(m.flags.components_complete).toBe(true)
	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_KEY", tOdd + 2)
	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_TAPE04", tOdd + 3)
	expect(m:isDoorLocked("D_TH_Trans")).toBe(false)

	local tBoard = m.flagTimes.board_recovered
	fx = advance(m, tOdd + 3.5, tBoard + 110)
	local ambush = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Presenter" and e.eventId == "presenter_ambush"
	end)
	expect(ambush.command.kind).toBe("Ambush")
	local rewindLock = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Door" and e.door == "D_CR_NH" and e.locked == true
	end)
	expect(rewindLock ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(m:isDoorLocked("D_CR_NH")).toBe(false) -- unlocked again after 30 s

	-- Install everything; then edit the freight door to open on time.
	local tInstall = tBoard + 111
	ok(m, 1, "I_SOCKET_COIL", tInstall)
	ok(m, 1, "I_SOCKET_VALVE", tInstall)
	ok(m, 1, "I_SOCKET_BOARD", tInstall)
	expect(m.flags.transmitter_armed).toBe(true)
	good = m:commitEdit(1, "freight_door", "OPEN", { deckId = "DECK_TRANS" }, tInstall + 2)
	expect(good).toBe(true)
	expect(m.charges).toBe(1)
	fx = advance(m, tInstall + 0.5, tInstall + 33)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("ExtractionAvailable")
	local dock = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Presenter" and e.eventId == "climax_route"
	end)
	expect(dock.command.path[1].pos.z < -104).toBeTruthy() -- loading dock

	ok(m, 1, "I_VAN", tInstall + 90)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Completed")
	local report = m:getReport()
	expect(report.result).toBe("Success")
	expect(report.componentsCount).toBe(3)
	expect(report.editsMade).toBe(2)
	expect(report.maxCharges).toBe(3)
	expect(report.tapesFound).toBe(4)
	expect(report.loopFirstTry).toBe(false)
	expect(report.callerFooled).toBe(false)
	expect(report.masterRecording).toBe("MASTER_01")
	expect(report.challenges).toContain("C01_UNUSED")
	expect(report.challenges).toContain("C01_SOLO")
	expect(report.challenges).toContain("C01_CALLER")
	expect(report.rewards["1"].credits).toBeAtLeast(120)
	expect(report.predictionsInterpreted).toBeAtLeast(2)
end)

test("co-op: two players share one timeline and one charge pool", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana", "Ben" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	ok(m, 2, "ITEM_TAPE01", 42)
	-- Both open the VCR and race to edit the same event.
	m:deckOpened(1, "DECK_LOBBY", "TAPE01", 43)
	m:deckOpened(2, "DECK_LOBBY", "TAPE01", 43)
	local a = m:commitEdit(1, "lobby_lights", "ON", { deckId = "DECK_LOBBY", expectedState = "OFF" }, 44)
	local b, code, message = m:commitEdit(2, "lobby_lights", "ON", { deckId = "DECK_LOBBY", expectedState = "OFF" }, 44)
	expect(a).toBe(true)
	expect(b).toBe(false)
	expect(code).toBe("ALREADY_EDITED")
	expect(message).toContain("Ana")
	expect(m.charges).toBe(2)
	local snap = m:snapshot(44)
	expect(snap.timeline.events.presenter_entry.state).toBe("SOUTH_HALL")
	expect(snap.inventory.TAPE01).toBe("Ben")
	expect(snap.participants["1"].name).toBe("Ana")
end)

test("co-op: spectators can watch but cannot edit; disconnects do not end the case", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana", "Ben" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_TAPE01", 42)
	m:eliminate(2, 50, {})
	local good, code = m:commitEdit(2, "lobby_lights", "ON", { deckId = "DECK_LOBBY" }, 51)
	expect(good).toBe(false)
	expect(code).toBe("SPECTATOR")
	expect(m.charges).toBe(3)
	expect(m:interact(2, "I_BRIEFING", 51)).toBe(false)
	m:removeParticipant(2, 52)
	advance(m, 52, 60)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Active")
	-- A late joiner can fill the empty slot.
	local joined = m:addParticipant(3, "Cal", 61)
	expect(joined).toBe(true)
	expect(m:activeParticipantCount()).toBe(2)
end)

test("failure: if everyone is eliminated the case fails after a short grace window", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	expect(m:eliminate(1, 100, {})).toBe(true)
	advance(m, 100, 105)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Active")
	expect(m.failPendingUntil).toBe(112)
	advance(m, 105.5, 113)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Failed")
	local report = m:getReport()
	expect(report.result).toBe("Failed")
	expect(report.grade).toBe("D")
	expect(report.masterRecording).toBe(nil)
end)

test("revive: once per player per case, at a checkpoint, with brief invulnerability", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	m:eliminate(1, 100, {})
	local good = m:revive(1, 104, "Personal")
	expect(good).toBe(true)
	expect(m:isAlive(1)).toBe(true)
	expect(m.failPendingUntil).toBe(nil)
	expect(m:isInvulnerable(1, 105)).toBe(true)
	expect(m:eliminate(1, 105, {})).toBe(false) -- protected
	expect(m:isInvulnerable(1, 111)).toBe(false)
	m:eliminate(1, 120, {})
	local again, reason = m:revive(1, 121, "Personal")
	expect(again).toBe(false)
	expect(reason).toContain("already")
	-- Revives never restore spent charges.
	expect(m.charges).toBe(3)
end)

test("team recovery: needs a living teammate, and blocks further re-entries", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana", "Ben" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	m:eliminate(2, 60, {})
	expect(m:canTeamRecover()).toBe(true)
	expect(m:teamRecover(61)).toBe(true)
	expect(m:isAlive(2)).toBe(true)
	m:eliminate(2, 80, {})
	local again = m:teamRecover(81)
	expect(again).toBe(false)
	local personal = m:revive(2, 82, "Personal")
	expect(personal).toBe(false)
end)

test("partial extraction when part of the team is lost", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana", "Ben" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	m:setFlag("generator_restored", 50)
	m:setFlag("extraction_open", 60)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("ExtractionAvailable")
	m:eliminate(2, 61, {})
	m:extract(1, 62)
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Completed")
	expect(m:getReport().result).toBe("Partial")
end)

test("the Caller's code sets off the alarm and raises awareness", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	ok(m, 1, "I_GENERATOR", 50)
	ok(m, 1, "I_GENERATOR", 52)
	local before = m.awareness
	local good, message = m:submitCode(1, m.variants.fakeCode, 53)
	expect(good).toBe(false)
	expect(message).toContain("alarm")
	expect(m.stats.callerFooled).toBe(true)
	expect(m.awareness > before).toBeTruthy()
	local locked = m:submitCode(1, m.variants.code, 54)
	expect(locked).toBe(false) -- lockout
	expect(m:submitCode(1, m.variants.code, 59)).toBe(true)
end)

test("awareness from deck use makes it observe the player - never instantly hunt", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	m:deckOpened(1, "DECK_LOBBY", nil, 41)
	-- Net +0.6/s while watching: roughly 2.5-3 minutes of continuous deck use.
	local fx = advance(m, 41, 260)
	local observe = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "ObserveNear"
	end)
	expect(observe ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(observe.userId).toBe(1)
	-- Cooldown prevents back-to-back observes.
	local count = 0
	for _, e in fx do
		if e.type == "ObserveNear" then
			count += 1
		end
	end
	expect(count).toBeAtMost(4)
end)

test("hidden frames are only accepted on the exact frame, once", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_TAPE01", 42)
	local miss = m:markFrame(1, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 20, 43)
	expect(miss).toBe(false)
	local hit, frameId = m:markFrame(1, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 12.5, 43)
	expect(hit).toBe(true)
	expect(frameId).toBe("HF_T01_FIFTH")
	expect(m:markFrame(1, "TAPE01", "CAM01", 12.5, 44)).toBe(false)
end)

test("restricted mode has fewer charges and hides clues", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" }, "Restricted")
	expect(m.charges).toBe(2)
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	expect(m:currentObjective().clue).toBe(nil)
end)

test("time limit fails a stalled case", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	m:update(40 + Case01.timeLimit + 1, { presenterIdle = true })
	expect(m.phase).toBe("Failed")
end)

test("the bonus tape in Studio A triggers a hunt", function()
	local m = newMission({ "Ana" })
	advance(m, 0, 41)
	m:setFlag("extraction_open", 60)
	ok(m, 1, "ITEM_TAPE07", 61)
	local fx = m:drainEffects()
	local hunt = find(fx, function(e)
		return e.type == "Presenter" and e.eventId == "bonus_tape"
	end)
	expect(hunt.command.kind).toBe("Ambush")
end)
```

## tests/specs/presentation.spec.luau

```lua
-- Presentation: the tutorial, one typeface, camera feel, the tube-TV CCTV
-- picture, custom prompts and the comfort settings, on the real client code.

local function boot()
	local engine = Engine.new()
	local server = Harness.new({ game = engine.game, globals = engine:globals("server") })
	local client = Harness.new({ game = engine.game, globals = engine:globals("client") })
	engine.sharedMounts.server = server.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
	engine.sharedMounts.client = client.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
	local serverRoot = server.mount("Server", "src/server")
	local clientRoot = client.mount("Client", "src/client")
	engine:run("server", function()
		server.run(serverRoot.Main)
	end)
	engine:advance(0.5)
	local player = engine:addPlayer(101, "Ana", true)
	engine:advance(2)
	engine:run("client", function()
		client.run(clientRoot.Main)
	end)
	engine:advance(2)
	return {
		engine = engine,
		server = server,
		client = client,
		player = player,
		State = client.require(clientRoot.State),
		clientRoot = clientRoot,
		serverRoot = serverRoot,
		controller = function(name)
			return client.require(clientRoot.Controllers[name])
		end,
	}
end

local function assertClean(env, label)
	local e = env.engine
	if #e.errors > 0 then
		error(label .. ": runtime errors\n" .. table.concat(e.errors, "\n----\n"))
	end
	if #e.warnings > 0 then
		error(label .. ": warnings\n" .. table.concat(e.warnings, "\n"))
	end
end

local function playerGui(env)
	return env.engine.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui
end

local function buttonWithText(env, screen, text)
	for _, d in playerGui(env)[screen]:GetDescendants() do
		if d.ClassName == "TextButton" and d.Text == text then
			return d
		end
	end
	error("no button " .. text .. " in " .. screen)
end

local function click(env, button)
	env.engine:spawnOnSide("client", function()
		env.engine:signalFor(button, "Activated"):Fire()
	end)
	env.engine:advance(0.2)
end

local function closeOverlays(env)
	local Tutorial = env.controller("TutorialController")
	if Tutorial.isOpen() then
		click(env, buttonWithText(env, "DeadAirTutorial", "SKIP TUTORIAL"))
	end
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		env.controller("MenuController").close()
	end)
	env.engine:advance(0.2)
end

test("first join shows the tutorial; finishing it is saved and it can be replayed", function()
	local env = boot()
	env.engine:advance(1)
	local gui = playerGui(env).DeadAirTutorial
	expect(gui.Enabled).toBe(true)
	expect(env.State.ui.tutorial).toBe(true)
	local Tutorial = env.controller("TutorialController")
	local steps = #Tutorial.Steps
	expect(steps >= 6).toBeTruthy()
	for _ = 1, steps - 1 do
		click(env, buttonWithText(env, "DeadAirTutorial", "NEXT"))
	end
	click(env, buttonWithText(env, "DeadAirTutorial", "FINISH"))
	env.engine:advance(0.5)
	expect(gui.Enabled).toBe(false)
	expect(env.State.ui.tutorial == nil).toBeTruthy()
	local DataService = env.server.require(env.serverRoot.Services.DataService)
	local serverPlayer
	for _, p in env.engine:service("Players"):GetPlayers() do
		serverPlayer = p
	end
	expect(DataService.get(serverPlayer).tutorialSeen).toBe(true)

	-- Replay from Settings.
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		env.controller("MenuController").open("Settings")
	end)
	env.engine:advance(0.2)
	click(env, buttonWithText(env, "DeadAirMenu", "REPLAY TUTORIAL"))
	expect(gui.Enabled).toBe(true)
	click(env, buttonWithText(env, "DeadAirTutorial", "SKIP TUTORIAL"))
	expect(gui.Enabled).toBe(false)
	assertClean(env, "tutorial")
end)

test("every piece of text uses the one game typeface", function()
	local env = boot()
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		env.controller("MenuController").open("Settings")
	end)
	env.engine:advance(0.5)
	local family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/RobotoMono.json"
	local checked = 0
	local function check(root)
		for _, d in root:GetDescendants() do
			if d.ClassName == "TextLabel" or d.ClassName == "TextButton" or d.ClassName == "TextBox" then
				if d.FontFace.Family ~= family then
					error(d:GetFullName() .. " uses " .. d.FontFace.Family)
				end
				checked += 1
			end
		end
	end
	check(playerGui(env))
	check(env.engine.realGame:GetService("Workspace"))
	expect(checked > 200).toBeTruthy()
	assertClean(env, "fonts")
end)

test("head bob moves the view up and down while walking and settles when stopped", function()
	local env = boot()
	closeOverlays(env)
	local e = env.engine
	local camera = e.realCamera
	local character = e.realOf[env.player].Character
	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local root = character.HumanoidRootPart
	camera.CameraType = roblox.Enum.CameraType.Custom
	camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
	local base = roblox.CFrame.new(0, 5, 260)
	local function sampleFrames(n)
		local lo, hi, rolls = math.huge, -math.huge, 0
		for _ = 1, n do
			camera.CFrame = base
			e:advance(0.05, 0.05)
			local offset = base:ToObjectSpace(camera.CFrame)
			lo = math.min(lo, offset.Position.Y)
			hi = math.max(hi, offset.Position.Y)
			local _, _, roll = offset:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
			rolls = math.max(rolls, math.abs(roll))
		end
		return hi - lo, rolls
	end
	local steps = 0
	env.controller("CameraController").onFootstep = function()
		steps += 1
	end
	e.velocities[root] = roblox.Vector3.new(12, 0, 0)
	sampleFrames(20) -- ease in
	local walkRange, walkRoll = sampleFrames(40)
	expect(walkRange > 0.08).toBeTruthy()
	expect(walkRoll > 0).toBeTruthy()
	-- About two steps per second at walking pace (3.1 studs per step).
	expect(steps >= 6).toBeTruthy()
	-- Sprinting bobs harder.
	e.velocities[root] = roblox.Vector3.new(19, 0, 0)
	sampleFrames(20)
	local sprintRange = sampleFrames(40)
	expect(sprintRange > walkRange).toBeTruthy()
	-- Stopping settles it (only the faint breathing remains).
	e.velocities[root] = nil
	sampleFrames(40)
	local stillRange = sampleFrames(20)
	expect(stillRange < 0.03).toBeTruthy()
	-- Head bob off in Settings: walking leaves the view steady.
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		local copy = table.clone(env.State.settings)
		copy.headBob = 0
		env.State.applySettings(copy)
	end)
	e.velocities[root] = roblox.Vector3.new(12, 0, 0)
	sampleFrames(20)
	local offRange = sampleFrames(40)
	expect(offRange < 0.01).toBeTruthy()
	assertClean(env, "head bob")
end)

test("tension builds near a hunting Presenter, then drains slowly; the vignette closes in", function()
	local env = boot()
	closeOverlays(env)
	local e = env.engine
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		env.State.action("SetReady", { ready = true })
	end)
	e:advance(8)
	expect(env.State.inMission()).toBe(true)
	local camera = e.realCamera
	local character = e.realOf[env.player].Character
	camera.CameraType = roblox.Enum.CameraType.Custom
	camera.CameraSubject = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local Camera = env.controller("CameraController")
	e:advance(2)
	local calm = Camera.tension
	expect(calm < 0.35).toBeTruthy()

	-- A hunting figure a few studs away.
	local pos = character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position
	local model = roblox.Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Presenter"
	local torso = roblox.Instance.new("Part")
	torso.Name = "Root"
	torso.CFrame = roblox.CFrame.new(pos + roblox.Vector3.new(6, 0, 0))
	torso.Parent = model
	model.PrimaryPart = torso
	model:SetAttribute("State", "Hunting")
	model.Parent = e.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
	e:advance(3)
	local scared = Camera.tension
	expect(scared > 0.6).toBeTruthy()
	expect(env.State.tension).toBeNear(scared, 0.05)

	local edge = playerGui(env).DeadAirVignette:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame"):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIGradient")
	local darkest = edge.Transparency.Keypoints[1].Value
	expect(darkest < 0.5).toBeTruthy()

	-- It leaves: dread lingers for a while before it fades.
	model:Destroy()
	e:advance(1)
	expect(Camera.tension > 0.45).toBeTruthy()
	e:advance(20)
	expect(Camera.tension < scared * 0.5).toBeTruthy()
	assertClean(env, "tension")
end)

test("the CCTV picture is black and white, rolls on a channel change and warms up", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	local view
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		local FootageView = env.client.require(env.clientRoot.UI.FootageView)
		local screen = roblox.Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		screen.Parent = playerGui(env)
		view = FootageView.new(e:wrap(screen))
		view:setCamera("CAM01")
		view:setActors({ { kind = "Presenter", pos = { x = 0, y = 0, z = 20 } }, { kind = "Investigator", pos = { x = 2, y = 0, z = 20 } } })
		view:setLights("emergency")
		view:powerOn()
		for _ = 1, 20 do
			view:step(1 / 30)
		end
	end)
	local viewport = e.realOf[view.viewport]
	local parts = 0
	for _, d in viewport:GetDescendants() do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then
			parts += 1
			local c = d.Color
			if math.abs(c.R - c.G) > 0.01 or math.abs(c.G - c.B) > 0.01 then
				error(d:GetFullName() .. " is not greyscale")
			end
		end
	end
	expect(parts > 50).toBeTruthy()
	local ambient = viewport.Ambient
	expect(ambient.R).toBeNear(ambient.B, 0.01)
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		view:setCamera("CAM02")
		view:step(1 / 30)
	end)
	expect(view.switchUntil > view.t).toBeTruthy()
	assertClean(env, "footage")
end)

test("interaction prompts are drawn by the game, in its typeface, and go away", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	local ws = e.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
	local part = roblox.Instance.new("Part")
	part.Parent = ws
	local prompt = roblox.Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
	prompt.ActionText = "Insert tape"
	prompt.ObjectText = "Reception VCR"
	prompt.HoldDuration = 1
	prompt.Style = roblox.Enum.ProximityPromptStyle.Custom
	prompt.Parent = part
	local pps = e.realGame:GetService("ProximityPromptService")
	e:spawnOnSide("client", function()
		e:signalFor(pps, "PromptShown"):Fire(e:wrap(prompt), roblox.Enum.ProximityPromptInputType.Keyboard)
	end)
	e:advance(0.1)
	local billboard = playerGui(env):FindFirstChild("DeadAirPrompt")
	expect(billboard ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	local texts = {}
	for _, d in billboard:GetDescendants() do
		if d.ClassName == "TextLabel" then
			texts[d.Text] = true
		end
	end
	expect(texts["E"]).toBe(true)
	expect(texts["INSERT TAPE"]).toBe(true)
	expect(texts["RECEPTION VCR"]).toBe(true)
	expect(texts["HOLD"]).toBe(true)
	e:spawnOnSide("client", function()
		e:signalFor(prompt, "PromptButtonHoldBegan"):Fire()
	end)
	e:advance(0.1)
	-- The server rewrites the prompt while it is shown: the card follows.
	e:run("server", function()
		e:wrap(prompt).ActionText = "Eject tape"
	end)
	e:advance(0.1)
	local redrawn = playerGui(env):FindFirstChild("DeadAirPrompt")
	local found = false
	for _, d in redrawn:GetDescendants() do
		if d.ClassName == "TextLabel" and d.Text == "EJECT TAPE" then
			found = true
		end
	end
	expect(found).toBe(true)
	local cards = 0
	for _, c in playerGui(env):GetChildren() do
		if c.Name == "DeadAirPrompt" then
			cards += 1
		end
	end
	expect(cards).toBe(1)
	e:spawnOnSide("client", function()
		e:signalFor(pps, "PromptHidden"):Fire(e:wrap(prompt))
	end)
	e:advance(0.1)
	expect(playerGui(env):FindFirstChild("DeadAirPrompt") == nil).toBeTruthy()
	assertClean(env, "prompts")
end)

test("comfort settings are sanitised and toggle sprint works", function()
	local env = boot()
	local ProfileSchema = env.client.require(env.engine.sharedMounts.client.Core.ProfileSchema)
	local s = ProfileSchema.sanitizeSettings(nil, { fieldOfView = 200, headBob = -1, motionBlur = 0.5, vignette = 3, toggleSprint = true, crosshair = "yes" })
	expect(s.fieldOfView).toBe(90)
	expect(s.headBob).toBe(0)
	expect(s.motionBlur).toBe(0.5)
	expect(s.vignette).toBe(1)
	expect(s.toggleSprint).toBe(true)
	expect(s.crosshair).toBe(true)

	closeOverlays(env)
	local e = env.engine
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		local copy = table.clone(env.State.settings)
		copy.toggleSprint = true
		env.State.applySettings(copy)
	end)
	local Input = env.controller("InputController")
	local sprint = e.actions.DA_Sprint
	local root = e.realOf[env.player].Character.HumanoidRootPart
	e.velocities[root] = roblox.Vector3.new(12, 0, 0)
	e:spawnOnSide("client", function()
		sprint.fn("DA_Sprint", roblox.Enum.UserInputState.Begin, {})
		sprint.fn("DA_Sprint", roblox.Enum.UserInputState.End, {})
	end)
	e:advance(0.2)
	expect(Input.isSprinting()).toBe(true)
	-- Stopping ends a toggled sprint.
	e.velocities[root] = nil
	e:advance(0.2)
	expect(Input.isSprinting()).toBe(false)
	assertClean(env, "settings")
end)

test("investigators wear office clothes in the field office and field kit in the station", function()
	local env = boot()
	closeOverlays(env)
	local e = env.engine
	local function outfit()
		local character = e.realOf[env.player].Character
		return character and character:FindFirstChild("Outfit")
	end
	e:advance(1)
	expect(outfit():GetAttribute("Mode")).toBe("Lobby")
	expect(outfit():FindFirstChild("IDBadge") ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(outfit():FindFirstChild("Headlamp") == nil).toBeTruthy()
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		env.State.action("SetReady", { ready = true })
	end)
	e:advance(8)
	expect(env.State.inMission()).toBe(true)
	expect(outfit():GetAttribute("Mode")).toBe("Field")
	for _, name in { "Vest", "Radio", "Backpack", "Belt", "Headlamp" } do
		if not outfit():FindFirstChild(name) then
			error("field kit missing " .. name)
		end
	end
	-- Every piece is welded on and never blocks movement.
	for _, p in outfit():GetChildren() do
		expect(p.CanCollide).toBe(false)
		expect(p.Massless).toBe(true)
		expect(p:FindFirstChildOfClass("WeldConstraint") ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	end
	env.engine:run("client", function()
		env.State.action("LeaveCase")
	end)
	e:advance(3)
	expect(outfit():GetAttribute("Mode")).toBe("Lobby")
	assertClean(env, "outfits")
end)
```

## tests/specs/session.spec.luau

```lua
-- Integration: the real server and client scripts running together on the mock
-- engine, driven through the same remotes and prompts a player would use.


local function boot()
	local engine = Engine.new()
	local server = Harness.new({ game = engine.game, globals = engine:globals("server") })
	local client = Harness.new({ game = engine.game, globals = engine:globals("client") })
	engine.sharedMounts.server = server.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
	engine.sharedMounts.client = client.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
	local serverRoot = server.mount("Server", "src/server")
	local clientRoot = client.mount("Client", "src/client")

	engine:run("server", function()
		server.run(serverRoot.Main)
	end)
	engine:advance(0.5)
	local player = engine:addPlayer(101, "Ana", true)
	engine:advance(2)
	engine:run("client", function()
		client.run(clientRoot.Main)
	end)
	engine:advance(2)
	return {
		engine = engine,
		server = server,
		client = client,
		player = player,
		State = client.require(clientRoot.State),
		serverServices = serverRoot.Services,
		clientRoot = clientRoot,
		serverRoot = serverRoot,
	}
end

local function assertClean(env, label)
	local e = env.engine
	if #e.errors > 0 then
		error(label .. ": runtime errors\n" .. table.concat(e.errors, "\n----\n"))
	end
	if #e.warnings > 0 then
		error(label .. ": warnings\n" .. table.concat(e.warnings, "\n"))
	end
end

test("server and client boot cleanly and the player reaches the field office", function()
	local env = boot()
	assertClean(env, "boot")
	local ws = env.engine.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
	expect(ws:FindFirstChild("Station04") ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(ws:FindFirstChild("FieldOffice") ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	local character = env.engine.realOf[env.player].Character
	expect(character ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	local hq = env.engine.realGame:GetService("Workspace").FieldOffice.HQSpawn.CFrame.Position
	expect(math.abs(character.PrimaryPart.CFrame.Position.Z - hq.Z) < 5).toBeTruthy()
	local gui = env.engine.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui
	for _, name in { "DeadAirHUD", "DeadAirDeck", "DeadAirMenu", "DeadAirReport", "DeadAirOverlay", "DeadAirKeypad", "DeadAirSpectate" } do
		if not gui:FindFirstChild(name) then
			error("missing " .. name)
		end
	end
	expect(env.State.profile ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(env.State.party ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(gui.DeadAirMenu.Enabled).toBe(true)
end)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Helpers for driving the game like a player
---------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function ws(env)
	return env.engine.realGame:GetService("Workspace")
end

local function anchorFor(env, id)
	local station = ws(env).Station04
	local a = station.Anchors:FindFirstChild(id)
	if a then
		return a
	end
	for _, d in station.Items:GetDescendants() do
		if d.Name == id then
			return d
		end
	end
	error("no anchor " .. id)
end

local function triggerPrompt(env, part)
	local prompt = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
	if not prompt then
		error("no prompt on " .. part:GetFullName())
	end
	local pos = part.CFrame.Position
	env.engine:teleport(env.player, roblox.Vector3.new(pos.X + 1.5, 3, pos.Z + 1.5))
	env.engine:signalFor(prompt, "Triggered"):Fire(env.player)
	env.engine:advance(0.3)
	return prompt
end

local function interact(env, id)
	return triggerPrompt(env, anchorFor(env, id))
end

local function guiButton(env, screenName, predicate)
	local gui = env.engine.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui[screenName]
	for _, d in gui:GetDescendants() do
		if d.ClassName == "TextButton" and predicate(d.Text, d) then
			return d
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function click(env, button)
	if not button then
		error("button not found")
	end
	env.engine:spawnOnSide("client", function()
		env.engine:signalFor(button, "Activated"):Fire()
	end)
	env.engine:advance(0.3)
end

local function mission(env)
	return env.State.mission
end

local function clientDo(env, fn)
	env.engine:run("client", fn)
	env.engine:advance(0.3)
end

local function editAtDeck(env, deckInteractId, eventId, stateLabel)
	interact(env, deckInteractId)
	env.engine:advance(0.5)
	local DeckController = env.client.require(env.clientRoot.Controllers.DeckController)
	expect(DeckController.isOpen()).toBe(true)
	clientDo(env, function()
		DeckController.selectEvent(eventId)
	end)
	env.engine:advance(0.6)
	click(env, guiButton(env, "DeadAirDeck", function(text)
		return text == "> " .. stateLabel
	end))
	click(env, guiButton(env, "DeadAirDeck", function(text)
		return string.sub(text, 1, 11) == "COMMIT EDIT"
	end))
	env.engine:advance(0.5)
	click(env, guiButton(env, "DeadAirDeck", function(text)
		return text == "CLOSE"
	end))
	expect(DeckController.isOpen()).toBe(false)
end

local function serverMission(env)
	return env.server.require(env.serverServices.MissionService).mission
end

test("a full Case 01 run through the real server, client, UI and remotes", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine

	-- Ready up in the field office; the team deploys after the countdown.
	clientDo(env, function()
		env.State.action("SetReady", { ready = true })
	end)
	expect(env.State.party.countdownEnd ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	e:advance(7)
	expect(mission(env) ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(mission(env).phase).toBe("Briefing")
	expect(e.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui.DeadAirMenu.Enabled).toBe(false)
	assertClean(env, "deploy")

	e:advance(41)
	expect(mission(env).phase).toBe("Active")

	-- TAPE 01 and the reception VCR edit (keep the lights on).
	interact(env, "ITEM_TAPE01")
	expect(mission(env).inventory.TAPE01).toBe("Ana")
	editAtDeck(env, "I_DECK_LOBBY", "lobby_lights", "LIGHTS HOLD")
	expect(mission(env).charges).toBe(2)
	expect(mission(env).timeline.events.presenter_entry.state).toBe("SOUTH_HALL")
	assertClean(env, "first edit")

	-- The predicted Presenter route plays out.
	local start = mission(env).broadcastStart
	e:advance(start + 160 - e.clock)
	local presenter = ws(env):FindFirstChild("Presenter")
	expect(presenter ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(presenter:GetAttribute("State")).toBe("Displaced")
	assertClean(env, "presenter entry")

	-- Restore power.
	interact(env, "I_GENERATOR")
	interact(env, "I_GENERATOR")
	expect(mission(env).flags.generator_restored).toBe(true)
	e:advance(5)
	local station = ws(env).Station04
	expect(station.Rooms.WestCorridor:GetAttribute("LightMode")).toBe("on")
	expect(station.Doors.D_EC_Arch:GetAttribute("Locked")).toBe(false)

	-- The keypad: read Division's Channel 2 code from the radio log and type it.
	local code
	for _, entry in mission(env).radioLog do
		if entry.channel == 2 and string.find(entry.text, "Cage code", 1, true) then
			code = string.match(entry.text, "(%d%d%d%d)")
		end
	end
	expect(code ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	interact(env, "I_CAGE")
	expect(e.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui.DeadAirKeypad.Enabled).toBe(true)
	for i = 1, #code do
		local digit = string.sub(code, i, i)
		click(env, guiButton(env, "DeadAirKeypad", function(text)
			return text == digit
		end))
	end
	click(env, guiButton(env, "DeadAirKeypad", function(text)
		return text == "OK"
	end))
	expect(mission(env).inventory.VALVE ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	assertClean(env, "keypad")

	interact(env, "ITEM_TAPE02")
	interact(env, "ITEM_COIL")
	interact(env, "ITEM_TAPE03")
	expect(mission(env).inventory.TAPE03 ~= nil).toBeTruthy()

	-- Leave the corridor lights alone; the patrol trips the east relay; reset it.
	local power = mission(env).timeline.anchors.generator_restored
	e:advance(power + 98 - e.clock)
	expect(mission(env).relays.EAST).toBe(false)
	interact(env, "I_RELAY_EAST")
	expect(mission(env).relays.EAST).toBe(true)
	assertClean(env, "patrol")

	-- Wait out the patrol, then the Loop.
	e:advance(power + 250 - e.clock)
	interact(env, "I_RACK")
	expect(mission(env).loop.active).toBe(true)
	local odd = mission(env).loop.odd
	local sm = serverMission(env)
	for _ = 1, 80 do
		local _, isOdd = sm:loopCycle(e.clock)
		if isOdd then
			break
		end
		e:advance(0.5)
	end
	e:advance(0.5)
	interact(env, odd)
	expect(mission(env).loop.solved).toBe(true)
	interact(env, "I_RACK")
	expect(mission(env).flags.board_recovered).toBe(true)
	interact(env, "ITEM_KEY")
	interact(env, "ITEM_TAPE04")
	assertClean(env, "loop")

	-- Ride out the ambush and the control room rewind.
	local board = mission(env).timeline.anchors.board_recovered
	e:advance(board + 110 - e.clock)
	expect(station.Doors.D_TH_Trans:GetAttribute("Locked")).toBe(false)

	-- Install the components; edit the freight door open at master control.
	interact(env, "I_SOCKET_COIL")
	interact(env, "I_SOCKET_VALVE")
	interact(env, "I_SOCKET_BOARD")
	expect(mission(env).flags.transmitter_armed).toBe(true)
	editAtDeck(env, "I_DECK_TRANS", "freight_door", "DOOR CYCLES OPEN")
	expect(mission(env).charges).toBe(1)
	e:advance(33)
	expect(mission(env).phase).toBe("ExtractionAvailable")
	expect(station.Doors.D_Freight:GetAttribute("Open")).toBe(true)
	assertClean(env, "transmitter")

	-- Wait for the dock patrol to pass, then board the van.
	e:advance(50)
	interact(env, "I_VAN")
	e:advance(1)
	expect(env.State.report ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	local report = env.State.report.report
	expect(report.result).toBe("Success")
	expect(report.componentsCount).toBe(3)
	expect(report.editsMade).toBe(2)
	expect(e.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui.DeadAirReport.Enabled).toBe(true)
	local personal = env.State.report.personal
	expect(personal.master).toBe("MASTER_01")
	expect(personal.clearance).toBe(2)
	expect(personal.credits > 0).toBeTruthy()

	-- Back to the field office.
	e:advance(6)
	click(env, guiButton(env, "DeadAirReport", function(text)
		return text == "RETURN TO THE FIELD OFFICE"
	end))
	e:advance(3)
	expect(env.State.mission).toBe(nil)
	expect(env.State.profile.clearance).toBe(2)
	expect(env.State.profile.masters.MASTER_01).toBe(true)
	local character = e.realOf[env.player].Character
	expect(character.PrimaryPart.CFrame.Position.Z > 200).toBeTruthy()
	assertClean(env, "return")

	-- Progress was written to the (simulated) DataStore.
	e:advance(130)
	local store = e.dataStores.DeadAir_Profiles_v1
	expect(store ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(store.data.u_101.clearance).toBe(2)
	expect(store.data.u_101.cases.case01.completed).toBe(1)
	assertClean(env, "save")
end)

local function deploySolo(env)
	clientDo(env, function()
		env.State.action("SetReady", { ready = true })
	end)
	env.engine:advance(7)
	env.engine:advance(41)
	expect(mission(env).phase).toBe("Active")
end

test("getting caught: off-air, spectating, a stored re-entry, and leaving the case", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	-- Give the player a stored re-entry, as if bought earlier.
	local DataService = env.server.require(env.serverServices.DataService)
	DataService.get(env.player).storedRevives = 1
	deploySolo(env)
	interact(env, "ITEM_TAPE01")
	-- Stand in reception with clear line of sight as it walks in.
	e.losBlocked = false
	local start = mission(env).broadcastStart
	e:advance(start + 154 - e.clock)
	e:teleport(env.player, roblox.Vector3.new(0, 3, 64))
	for _ = 1, 200 do
		e:advance(0.2)
		local presenter = ws(env):FindFirstChild("Presenter")
		local character = e.realOf[env.player].Character
		if presenter and character then
			-- Walk into it (players get caught by standing their ground).
			e:teleport(env.player, presenter.PrimaryPart.CFrame.Position + roblox.Vector3.new(1, 0, 1))
		end
		if env.State.me() and env.State.me().status == "Eliminated" then
			break
		end
	end
	expect(env.State.me().status).toBe("Eliminated")
	e:advance(2)
	expect(e.realOf[env.player].Character).toBe(nil)
	local spectate = e.realOf[env.player].PlayerGui.DeadAirSpectate
	expect(spectate.Enabled).toBe(true)
	-- The optional re-entry uses the stored one.
	local button = guiButton(env, "DeadAirSpectate", function(text)
		return string.find(text, "RE-ENTRY", 1, true) ~= nil
	end)
	click(env, button)
	e:advance(4)
	expect(env.State.me().status).toBe("Alive")
	expect(e.realOf[env.player].Character ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	expect(DataService.get(env.player).storedRevives).toBe(0)
	expect(mission(env).charges).toBe(3)
	e.losBlocked = true
	-- Leave the case from the pause menu.
	clientDo(env, function()
		env.State.action("LeaveCase")
	end)
	e:advance(3)
	expect(env.State.mission).toBe(nil)
	assertClean(env, "caught")
end)

test("the Caller's code sets off the alarm; the menu pages and archive replay all work", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	-- Every field office page builds without errors.
	local Menu = env.client.require(env.clientRoot.Controllers.MenuController)
	for _, page in { "Home", "Cases", "Archive", "Equipment", "Settings", "Store", "Credits" } do
		clientDo(env, function()
			Menu.show(page)
		end)
	end
	-- Buy and equip a cosmetic with credits.
	local DataService = env.server.require(env.serverServices.DataService)
	DataService.get(env.player).credits = 500
	clientDo(env, function()
		local r1 = env.State.request("BuyCosmetic", { item = "uniform_field" })
		local r2 = env.State.request("SetCosmetic", { item = "uniform_field" })
		local r3 = env.State.request("SetEquipment", { equipment = "Analyser" })
		local r4 = env.State.request("SaveSettings", { settings = { subtitles = false, sensitivity = 9 } })
		assert(r1.ok and r2.ok and r3.ok and r4.ok, "store/equipment requests failed")
	end)
	e:advance(1)
	local profile = DataService.get(env.player)
	expect(profile.credits).toBe(350)
	expect(profile.cosmetics.equipped.uniform).toBe("uniform_field")
	expect(profile.equipment).toBe("Analyser")
	expect(profile.settings.sensitivity).toBe(3) -- clamped
	expect(profile.settings.subtitles).toBe(false)

	deploySolo(env)
	interact(env, "I_GENERATOR")
	interact(env, "I_GENERATOR")
	e:advance(16)
	local fake
	for _, entry in mission(env).radioLog do
		if entry.channel == 0 and string.find(entry.text, "cage code", 1, true) then
			fake = string.match(entry.text, "(%d%d%d%d)")
		end
	end
	expect(fake ~= nil).toBeTruthy()
	interact(env, "I_CAGE")
	local result
	clientDo(env, function()
		result = env.State.request("SubmitCode", { code = fake })
	end)
	expect(result.ok).toBe(false)
	expect(env.server.require(env.serverServices.MissionService).mission.stats.callerFooled).toBe(true)
	-- The pause menu and the analyser tool run in a case too.
	clientDo(env, function()
		Menu.open("Pause")
		Menu.close()
	end)
	local Tools = env.client.require(env.clientRoot.Controllers.ToolController)
	clientDo(env, function()
		Tools.toggle()
	end)
	e:advance(1)
	clientDo(env, function()
		Tools.toggle()
	end)
	assertClean(env, "caller")

	-- Leave, then replay a recovered tape in the archive.
	interact(env, "ITEM_TAPE01")
	clientDo(env, function()
		env.State.action("LeaveCase")
	end)
	e:advance(3)
	expect(DataService.get(env.player).tapes.TAPE01).toBe(true)
	local Deck = env.client.require(env.clientRoot.Controllers.DeckController)
	clientDo(env, function()
		Deck.open({ mode = "archive", caseId = "case01", tapeId = "TAPE01" })
	end)
	e:advance(1)
	clientDo(env, function()
		Deck.seek(12.4)
	end)
	e:advance(0.2)
	click(env, guiButton(env, "DeadAirDeck", function(text)
		return text == "MARK FRAME"
	end))
	e:advance(0.5)
	expect(DataService.get(env.player).frames.HF_T01_FIFTH).toBe(true)
	clientDo(env, function()
		Deck.close()
	end)
	assertClean(env, "archive")
end)

test("two investigators share one timeline; the team recovery vote brings a teammate back", function()
	local env = boot()
	local e = env.engine
	local ben = e:addPlayer(202, "Ben", false)
	e:advance(3)
	e:fireServerAs(ben, "Action", { type = "SetReady", ready = true })
	deploySolo(env)
	local m = env.server.require(env.serverServices.MissionService).mission
	expect(m:activeParticipantCount()).toBe(2)
	interact(env, "ITEM_TAPE01")
	-- Ben (standing at the VCR) and Ana both try to edit the same event.
	e:teleport(ben, roblox.Vector3.new(4.8, 3, 49))
	e:fireServerAs(ben, "Action", { type = "DeckOpen", deck = "DECK_LOBBY" })
	e:advance(0.2)
	local benResult = e:invokeServerAs(ben, { type = "CommitEdit", event = "lobby_lights", state = "ON", deck = "DECK_LOBBY", expected = "OFF" })
	expect(benResult.ok).toBe(true)
	-- Ana tries to reverse it: the deck refuses (already edited) and nothing is spent.
	editAtDeck(env, "I_DECK_LOBBY", "lobby_lights", "LIGHTS FAIL")
	expect(mission(env).charges).toBe(2)
	expect(mission(env).timeline.events.lobby_lights.committed.name).toBe("Ben")
	-- Ben goes off-air; Ana calls a team recovery and agrees by default as proposer.
	m:eliminate(202, e.clock, {})
	e:advance(0.5)
	local DataService = env.server.require(env.serverServices.DataService)
	DataService.get(env.player).storedTeamTokens = 1
	local result
	clientDo(env, function()
		result = env.State.request("TeamRecovery", {})
	end)
	expect(result.ok).toBe(true)
	e:advance(4)
	expect(m:isAlive(202)).toBe(true)
	expect(m.teamTokenUsed).toBe(true)
	expect(DataService.get(env.player).storedTeamTokens).toBe(0)
	assertClean(env, "co-op")
end)
```

## scripts/bake.luau

```lua
--[[
	Bakes Broadcast Station 04 and the field office into a built place file so the
	map is visible (and editable) in Roblox Studio's Explorer.

	Usage (repository root):
		rojo build default.project.json -o build/DeadAir.rbxl
		lune run scripts/bake.luau build/DeadAir.rbxl

	The server also builds the map at runtime if Workspace has no Station04, so the
	game still works when synced with `rojo serve` into an empty place.
]]

local fs = require("@lune/fs")
local process = require("@lune/process")
local roblox = require("@lune/roblox")

local Harness = require("../tests/lib/Harness")

local path = process.args[1] or "build/DeadAir.rbxl"
if not fs.isFile(path) then
	error("Place file not found: " .. path .. " (run rojo build first)")
end

local game = roblox.deserializePlace(fs.readFile(path))
local workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

for _, name in { "Station04", "FieldOffice" } do
	local existing = workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
	if existing then
		existing:Destroy()
	end
end

local h = Harness.new({ game = game })
local Shared = h.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
local Case01 = h.require(Shared.Config.Cases.Case01)
local MapBuilder = h.require(Shared.World.MapBuilder)

local station, hq = MapBuilder.build(workspace, Case01)

local parts = 0
for _, d in station:GetDescendants() do
	if d:IsA("BasePart") then
		parts += 1
	end
end
for _, d in hq:GetDescendants() do
	if d:IsA("BasePart") then
		parts += 1
	end
end

fs.writeFile(path, roblox.serializePlace(game))
print(string.format("Baked Station04 + FieldOffice (%d parts) into %s", parts, path))
```

## scripts/build.sh

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Builds build/DeadAir.rbxl with the map baked in, after running the tests.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

mkdir -p build
echo "== tests"
lune run tests/run.luau
if command -v python3 >/dev/null && command -v ffmpeg >/dev/null && python3 -c "import numpy" 2>/dev/null; then
	echo "== audio banks"
	python3 scripts/audio/check_banks.py
else
	echo "== audio banks (skipped: needs python3, numpy and ffmpeg)"
fi
echo "== rojo build"
rojo build default.project.json -o build/DeadAir.rbxl
echo "== bake map"
lune run scripts/bake.luau build/DeadAir.rbxl
echo "Done: build/DeadAir.rbxl"
```

## scripts/bundle_source.py

````python
"""
Writes docs/SOURCE_BUNDLE.md: every source file of the project in one
Markdown document (path headings + fenced code), for an assistant that can't
read the repository directly. Regenerate after changing code:

    python3 scripts/bundle_source.py

Binary audio banks are left out (they are generated by scripts/audio/); the map
of where each sound sits is included as src/shared/Config/AudioBank.luau.
"""

import os

ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), ".."))
OUT = os.path.join(ROOT, "docs", "SOURCE_BUNDLE.md")

TOP_FILES = ["default.project.json", "rokit.toml", "selene.toml", "stylua.toml", ".gitignore"]
TREES = ["src", "tests", "scripts"]
LANG = {".luau": "lua", ".py": "python", ".sh": "bash", ".json": "json", ".toml": "toml"}


def collect():
    files = [f for f in TOP_FILES if os.path.exists(os.path.join(ROOT, f))]
    for tree in TREES:
        for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(os.path.join(ROOT, tree)):
            dirnames[:] = sorted(d for d in dirnames if d != "__pycache__")
            for name in sorted(filenames):
                if os.path.splitext(name)[1] in LANG:
                    files.append(os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dirpath, name), ROOT))
    return files


def main():
    files = collect()
    sections, toc, total = [], [], 0
    for rel in files:
        with open(os.path.join(ROOT, rel), encoding="utf-8") as f:
            text = f.read()
        lines = text.count("\n") + 1
        total += lines
        anchor = rel.lower().replace("/", "").replace(".", "").replace("_", "")
        toc.append("- [`%s`](#%s) (%d lines)" % (rel, anchor, lines))
        fence = "````" if "```" in text else "```"
        lang = LANG.get(os.path.splitext(rel)[1], "")
        sections.append("## %s\n\n%s%s\n%s\n%s\n" % (rel, fence, lang, text.rstrip("\n"), fence))
    header = [
        "# DEAD AIR: CHANNEL ZERO — complete source bundle",
        "",
        "Generated by `scripts/bundle_source.py`. Every source file in the repository,",
        "in order: project config, `src/` (the game), `tests/` (Lune test suite and",
        "mock engine), `scripts/` (build, bake, audio synthesis). %d files, %d lines." % (len(files), total),
        "Read `docs/AI_HANDOFF.md` first; it explains how these fit together.",
        "",
        "## Files",
        "",
    ]
    with open(OUT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(header + toc) + "\n\n" + "\n".join(sections))
    print("Wrote %s: %d files, %d lines, %.0f KB" % (os.path.relpath(OUT, ROOT), len(files), total, os.path.getsize(OUT) / 1024))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
````

## scripts/mapsvg.luau

```lua
--[[
	Writes docs/station04-map.svg: a top-down plan of Broadcast Station 04 drawn
	from the same MapLayout and Case 01 data the game uses.

		lune run scripts/mapsvg.luau
]]

local fs = require("@lune/fs")

local Harness = require("../tests/lib/Harness")

local h = Harness.new()
local Shared = h.mount("Shared", "src/shared")
local MapLayout = h.require(Shared.Config.MapLayout)
local Case01 = h.require(Shared.Config.Cases.Case01)

local SCALE = 4
local minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ = math.huge, -math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge
for _, id in MapLayout.StationRooms do
	local r = MapLayout.Rooms[id]
	minX, maxX = math.min(minX, r.x0), math.max(maxX, r.x1)
	minZ, maxZ = math.min(minZ, r.z0), math.max(maxZ, r.z1)
end
local pad = 20
local width = (maxX - minX + pad * 2) * SCALE
local height = (maxZ - minZ + pad * 2) * SCALE + 70

local function X(x)
	return (x - minX + pad) * SCALE
end
local function Z(z)
	return (z - minZ + pad) * SCALE + 50
end

local out = {}
local function add(s)
	table.insert(out, s)
end

add(string.format('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="%d" height="%d" viewBox="0 0 %d %d" font-family="monospace">', width, height, width, height))
add(string.format('<rect width="%d" height="%d" fill="#0e1210"/>', width, height))
add('<text x="20" y="32" fill="#c4ba9e" font-size="22">BROADCAST STATION 04 · FLOOR PLAN (north is up)</text>')

local fills = {
	Lobby = "#4a4638", ControlRoom = "#34423a", Archive = "#43382c", EditingSuite = "#323c46",
	Generator = "#3c3a37", Transmitter = "#2c2c2b", StudioA = "#262a2a", LoadingDock = "#2a2c2c",
}
for _, id in MapLayout.StationRooms do
	local r = MapLayout.Rooms[id]
	add(string.format('<rect x="%.1f" y="%.1f" width="%.1f" height="%.1f" fill="%s" stroke="#5a7a62" stroke-width="2"/>',
		X(r.x0), Z(r.z0), (r.x1 - r.x0) * SCALE, (r.z1 - r.z0) * SCALE, fills[id] or "#2f3631"))
end
for _, door in MapLayout.Doors do
	if door.rooms[1] ~= "HQ" then
		local colour = door.kind == "opening" and "#8aa08e" or (Case01.doorLocks[door.id] and "#e2a44c" or "#d6e0d2")
		if door.plane == "z" then
			add(string.format('<line x1="%.1f" y1="%.1f" x2="%.1f" y2="%.1f" stroke="%s" stroke-width="6"/>',
				X(door.center - door.width / 2), Z(door.at), X(door.center + door.width / 2), Z(door.at), colour))
		else
			add(string.format('<line x1="%.1f" y1="%.1f" x2="%.1f" y2="%.1f" stroke="%s" stroke-width="6"/>',
				X(door.at), Z(door.center - door.width / 2), X(door.at), Z(door.center + door.width / 2), colour))
		end
	end
end
for _, win in MapLayout.Windows do
	add(string.format('<line x1="%.1f" y1="%.1f" x2="%.1f" y2="%.1f" stroke="#7fb0c8" stroke-width="4" stroke-dasharray="4 3"/>',
		X(win.center - win.width / 2), Z(win.at), X(win.center + win.width / 2), Z(win.at)))
end
local labelled = {}
for _, id in MapLayout.StationRooms do
	local r = MapLayout.Rooms[id]
	if not labelled[r.label] then
		labelled[r.label] = true
		local big = (r.x1 - r.x0) > 20 and (r.z1 - r.z0) > 20
		add(string.format('<text x="%.1f" y="%.1f" fill="#c4ba9e" font-size="%d" text-anchor="middle">%s</text>',
			X((r.x0 + r.x1) / 2), Z((r.z0 + r.z1) / 2), big and 14 or 10, r.label))
	end
end
for _, camId in MapLayout.CameraOrder do
	local cam = MapLayout.Cameras[camId]
	add(string.format('<circle cx="%.1f" cy="%.1f" r="4" fill="#96d2a0"/><text x="%.1f" y="%.1f" fill="#96d2a0" font-size="10">%s</text>',
		X(cam.pos.x), Z(cam.pos.z), X(cam.pos.x) + 6, Z(cam.pos.z) - 4, camId))
end
local function marker(pos, colour, text)
	add(string.format('<rect x="%.1f" y="%.1f" width="8" height="8" fill="%s"/><text x="%.1f" y="%.1f" fill="%s" font-size="10">%s</text>',
		X(pos.x) - 4, Z(pos.z) - 4, colour, X(pos.x) + 7, Z(pos.z) + 4, colour, text))
end
for id, deck in Case01.decks do
	marker(deck.pos, "#e2a44c", deck.label)
end
for _, id in { "I_GENERATOR", "I_CAGE", "I_RACK", "I_LEVER", "I_VAN" } do
	local def = Case01.interactables[id]
	marker(def.pos, "#d6e0d2", def.object)
end
local legendY = height - 16
add(string.format('<text x="20" y="%d" fill="#8aa08e" font-size="12">white = door · amber = locked until a condition · dashed blue = glass · green dots = CCTV · amber squares = tape decks</text>', legendY))
add("</svg>")

fs.writeDir("docs")
fs.writeFile("docs/station04-map.svg", table.concat(out, "\n"))
print("Wrote docs/station04-map.svg")
```

## scripts/audio/build_banks.py

```python
"""
Renders every sound, packs them into a few "bank" files and writes the map the
game uses to find each sound inside its bank.

    python3 scripts/audio/build_banks.py            # writes assets/audio/*.ogg
                                                     # and src/shared/Config/AudioBank.luau

Why banks: Roblox limits how many audio files an account can upload each month.
Packing ~80 sounds into 4 files keeps the whole game within that limit. The game
plays a slice of a bank with Sound.PlaybackRegion, and loops a slice with
Sound.LoopRegion.

Loops are written as [last 1 s][the loop][first 1 s] so that the region is
surrounded by its own continuation: a loop jump that lands a few samples early
or late still lands on matching audio.
"""

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import wave

import numpy as np

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)

from dsp import SR, circular, highpass, n  # noqa: E402
from loops import LOOPS  # noqa: E402
from music import TRACKS  # noqa: E402
from sfx import LIB  # noqa: E402

ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))
OUT_DIR = os.path.join(ROOT, "assets", "audio")
LUAU_OUT = os.path.join(ROOT, "src", "shared", "Config", "AudioBank.luau")
JSON_OUT = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "banks.json")

GAP = 0.25  # silence between one-shots
MARGIN = 1.0  # loop continuation on each side
MAX_BANK = 300.0  # seconds; Roblox accepts longer, this keeps uploads small

# Loudness targets (RMS dBFS) by category.
TARGET = {"ui": -27, "sfx": -22, "sting": -19, "ambience": -27, "presence": -22, "music": -21}


def level(sig, rms_db, peak_db=-3.0):
    """Match loudness to rms_db, but never push the peak above peak_db (no
    clipping or limiting: punchy one-shots simply come out a little quieter)."""
    rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(sig ** 2)) + 1e-12
    peak = np.max(np.abs(sig)) + 1e-12
    gain = min(10 ** (rms_db / 20) / rms, 10 ** (peak_db / 20) / peak)
    return sig * gain


def check(name, sig):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(sig)):
        raise ValueError(name + " produced NaN/inf")
    if len(sig) == 0:
        raise ValueError(name + " is empty")


def render_oneshots():
    out = []
    for name in sorted(LIB):
        category, fn = LIB[name]
        sig = np.asarray(fn(), dtype=np.float64)
        check(name, sig)
        target = TARGET["ui"] if category == "ui" else TARGET.get(category, TARGET["sfx"])
        if name.startswith("Step_"):
            target = -24
        sig = highpass(sig, 25, 1)  # no DC or sub-sonic rumble
        sig = level(sig, target)
        # Trim trailing near-silence (reverb tails below -60 dB).
        loud = np.nonzero(np.abs(sig) > 10 ** (-60 / 20))[0]
        if len(loud):
            sig = sig[: loud[-1] + n(0.02)]
        out.append((name, category, sig))
    return out


def render_loops(source, kind):
    out = []
    for name in sorted(source):
        if kind == "music":
            period, fn = source[name]
            category = "music"
        else:
            category, period, fn = source[name]
        with circular():
            sig = np.asarray(fn(), dtype=np.float64)
        check(name, sig)
        if len(sig) != n(period):
            raise ValueError("%s is %d samples, expected one period of %d" % (name, len(sig), n(period)))
        sig = sig - np.mean(sig)  # remove DC; keeps the loop periodic
        sig = level(sig, TARGET[category], -2.0)
        seam = abs(sig[-1] - sig[0])
        typical = np.mean(np.abs(np.diff(sig))) * 6 + 1e-4
        if seam > typical:
            raise ValueError("%s does not loop cleanly (seam step %.4f > %.4f)" % (name, seam, typical))
        out.append((name, category, period, sig))
    return out


def pack(entries, bank, loops=False):
    """Concatenate entries; return (audio, regions)."""
    parts, regions, cursor = [], {}, 0.0
    for entry in entries:
        if loops:
            name, category, period, sig = entry
            m = n(MARGIN)
            seg = np.concatenate([sig[-m:], sig, sig[:m]])
            regions[name] = {"bank": bank, "start": round(cursor + MARGIN, 6), "length": round(period, 6), "loop": True, "category": category}
        else:
            name, category, sig = entry
            seg = np.concatenate([np.zeros(n(GAP)), sig, np.zeros(n(GAP))])
            regions[name] = {"bank": bank, "start": round(cursor + GAP, 6), "length": round(len(sig) / SR, 6), "loop": False, "category": category}
        parts.append(seg)
        cursor += len(seg) / SR
    return np.concatenate(parts), regions


def split(entries, loops, prefix):
    """Split entries into banks no longer than MAX_BANK seconds."""
    banks, current, length = [], [], 0.0
    for entry in entries:
        seconds = (entry[2] + 2 * MARGIN) if loops else (len(entry[2]) / SR + 2 * GAP)
        if current and length + seconds > MAX_BANK:
            banks.append(current)
            current, length = [], 0.0
        current.append(entry)
        length += seconds
    if current:
        banks.append(current)
    if len(banks) == 1:
        return [(prefix, banks[0])]
    return [("%s%d" % (prefix, i + 1), b) for i, b in enumerate(banks)]


def write_ogg(path, audio):
    peak = np.max(np.abs(audio))
    if peak > 0.999:
        audio = audio / peak * 0.999
    pcm = (audio * 32767).astype("<i2")
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        wav_path = os.path.join(tmp, "bank.wav")
        with wave.open(wav_path, "wb") as w:
            w.setnchannels(1)
            w.setsampwidth(2)
            w.setframerate(SR)
            w.writeframes(pcm.tobytes())
        subprocess.run(
            ["ffmpeg", "-loglevel", "error", "-y", "-i", wav_path, "-c:a", "libvorbis", "-q:a", "4", "-map_metadata", "-1", "-fflags", "+bitexact", "-flags:a", "+bitexact", "-serial_offset", "1", path],
            check=True,
        )


def lua_number(x):
    return ("%.6f" % x).rstrip("0").rstrip(".")


def write_luau(banks, regions):
    lines = [
        "--[[",
        "\tGENERATED by scripts/audio/build_banks.py - do not edit by hand.",
        "",
        "\tWhere every sound lives inside the audio banks (assets/audio/*.ogg).",
        "\tstart/length are seconds into the bank; loop sounds repeat exactly that",
        "\tregion. Upload the banks and put their asset ids in Config/AudioAssets.",
        "]]",
        "",
        "local AudioBank = {}",
        "",
        "AudioBank.Banks = {",
    ]
    for name, info in sorted(banks.items()):
        lines.append('\t%s = { file = "%s", duration = %s },' % (name, info["file"], lua_number(info["duration"])))
    lines += ["}", "", "AudioBank.Sounds = {"]
    for name in sorted(regions):
        r = regions[name]
        lines.append(
            '\t%s = { bank = "%s", start = %s, length = %s, loop = %s },'
            % (name, r["bank"], lua_number(r["start"]), lua_number(r["length"]), "true" if r["loop"] else "false")
        )
    lines += ["}", "", "return AudioBank", ""]
    with open(LUAU_OUT, "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(lines))


def main():
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    print("rendering one-shots...")
    oneshots = render_oneshots()
    print("rendering loops...")
    ambience = render_loops(LOOPS, "ambience")
    print("rendering music...")
    music = render_loops(TRACKS, "music")

    banks, regions = {}, {}
    groups = split(oneshots, False, "Sfx") + split(ambience, True, "Ambience") + split(music, True, "Music")
    for bank_name, entries in groups:
        is_loop = len(entries[0]) == 4
        audio, regs = pack(entries, bank_name, loops=is_loop)
        file_name = "DeadAir_%s.ogg" % bank_name
        write_ogg(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, file_name), audio)
        banks[bank_name] = {"file": file_name, "duration": len(audio) / SR}
        regions.update(regs)
        print("  %-10s %6.1fs  %3d sounds  -> %s" % (bank_name, len(audio) / SR, len(entries), file_name))

    write_luau(banks, regions)
    with open(JSON_OUT, "w") as f:
        json.dump({"banks": banks, "sounds": regions}, f, indent=1, sort_keys=True)
    print("%d sounds in %d banks" % (len(regions), len(banks)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## scripts/audio/check_banks.py

```python
"""
Checks the encoded banks the way the game will play them: decodes each .ogg,
cuts out every sound's region and fails on clipping, DC offset, silence, a
region outside its bank, or a loop whose end doesn't flow into its start.

    python3 scripts/audio/check_banks.py [--table]
"""

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys

import numpy as np

SR = 44100
ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", ".."))
AUDIO = os.path.join(ROOT, "assets", "audio")


def decode(path):
    raw = subprocess.run(
        ["ffmpeg", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", path, "-f", "s16le", "-ac", "1", "-ar", str(SR), "-"],
        capture_output=True,
        check=True,
    ).stdout
    return np.frombuffer(raw, "<i2").astype(float) / 32768


def main():
    meta = json.load(open(os.path.join(AUDIO, "banks.json")))
    banks = {name: decode(os.path.join(AUDIO, info["file"])) for name, info in meta["banks"].items()}
    problems = []
    rows = []
    for name, r in sorted(meta["sounds"].items()):
        audio = banks[r["bank"]]
        a, b = int(round(r["start"] * SR)), int(round((r["start"] + r["length"]) * SR))
        if a < 0 or b > len(audio):
            problems.append("%s: region outside %s" % (name, r["bank"]))
            continue
        s = audio[a:b]
        peak = np.max(np.abs(s))
        rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(s ** 2))
        dc = np.mean(s)
        if np.sum(np.abs(s) > 0.985) > 0:
            problems.append("%s: clips (peak %.3f)" % (name, peak))
        if abs(dc) > 0.005:
            problems.append("%s: DC offset %.4f" % (name, dc))
        if 20 * np.log10(rms + 1e-12) < -50:
            problems.append("%s: nearly silent" % name)
        if r["loop"]:
            # The sample after the region's end must match the region's start.
            after = audio[b:b + 64]
            start = audio[a:a + 64]
            # Relative to the sound's own level: lossy coding never reproduces
            # noise sample-for-sample, but a real seam error is far louder.
            err = np.sqrt(np.mean((after - start) ** 2)) / (rms + 1e-6) if len(after) == 64 else 9
            if err > 0.6:
                problems.append("%s: loop seam mismatch (%.2f of its level)" % (name, err))
        rows.append((name, r["length"], 20 * np.log10(peak + 1e-12), 20 * np.log10(rms + 1e-12), r["loop"]))
    if "--table" in sys.argv:
        for row in rows:
            print("%-20s %7.2fs  peak %6.1f dB  rms %6.1f dB%s" % (row[0], row[1], row[2], row[3], "  loop" if row[4] else ""))
    total = sum(len(a) for a in banks.values()) / SR
    print("%d sounds, %d banks, %.0f s of audio" % (len(rows), len(banks), total))
    if problems:
        print("PROBLEMS:\n  " + "\n  ".join(problems))
        sys.exit(1)
    print("all sounds OK")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## scripts/audio/dsp.py

```python
"""
Small numpy DSP toolkit for the game's original sound design.

Everything here is deterministic: random sources take an explicit seed so the
generated banks are identical on every run. Signals are mono float64 arrays at
SR samples per second, nominally in [-1, 1].
"""

import numpy as np

SR = 44100


def rng(seed):
    return np.random.default_rng(seed)


def n(seconds):
    return int(round(seconds * SR))


def time(seconds):
    return np.arange(n(seconds)) / SR


def silence(seconds):
    return np.zeros(n(seconds))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Oscillators
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def phase_of(freq, length):
    """Phase (radians) for a constant or per-sample frequency."""
    if np.isscalar(freq):
        return 2 * np.pi * freq * np.arange(length) / SR
    return 2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(freq) / SR


def sine(freq, seconds, phase0=0.0):
    length = n(seconds)
    return np.sin(phase_of(freq, length) + phase0)


def additive(freq, seconds, partials):
    """partials: list of (ratio, amplitude). Band-limited by construction."""
    length = n(seconds)
    ph = phase_of(freq, length)
    out = np.zeros(length)
    for ratio, amp in partials:
        out += amp * np.sin(ph * ratio)
    return out


def saw(freq, seconds, harmonics=24):
    f = freq if np.isscalar(freq) else float(np.max(freq))
    count = max(1, min(harmonics, int(SR / 2 / max(f, 1))))
    return additive(freq, seconds, [(k, 1.0 / k) for k in range(1, count + 1)]) * 0.55


def square(freq, seconds, harmonics=24):
    f = freq if np.isscalar(freq) else float(np.max(freq))
    count = max(1, min(harmonics, int(SR / 2 / max(f, 1))))
    return additive(freq, seconds, [(k, 1.0 / k) for k in range(1, count + 1, 2)]) * 0.75


def triangle(freq, seconds):
    return additive(freq, seconds, [(k, ((-1) ** ((k - 1) // 2)) / (k * k)) for k in range(1, 16, 2)]) * 0.8


def white(seconds, seed):
    return rng(seed).uniform(-1, 1, n(seconds))


def coloured(seconds, seed, slope):
    """Noise with a 1/f^slope power spectrum (slope 1 = pink, 2 = brown)."""
    length = n(seconds)
    spec = np.fft.rfft(rng(seed).normal(0, 1, length))
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(length, 1 / SR)
    freqs[0] = freqs[1] if len(freqs) > 1 else 1
    spec /= freqs ** (slope / 2)
    out = np.fft.irfft(spec, length)
    return out / (np.max(np.abs(out)) + 1e-9)


def pink(seconds, seed):
    return coloured(seconds, seed, 1)


def brown(seconds, seed):
    return coloured(seconds, seed, 2)


def crackle(seconds, seed, density=40):
    """Sparse clicks, like dust on a record or arcing contacts."""
    r = rng(seed)
    out = np.zeros(n(seconds))
    count = int(density * seconds)
    for pos in r.integers(0, len(out), count):
        width = r.integers(2, 30)
        end = min(len(out), pos + width)
        out[pos:end] += r.uniform(-1, 1) * np.exp(-np.arange(end - pos) / max(width / 4, 1))
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Envelopes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def env_ad(seconds, attack, decay_tau):
    t = time(seconds)
    a = np.clip(t / max(attack, 1e-4), 0, 1)
    d = np.exp(-np.maximum(t - attack, 0) / max(decay_tau, 1e-4))
    return a * d


def env_adsr(seconds, attack, decay, sustain, release):
    length = n(seconds)
    t = np.arange(length) / SR
    env = np.ones(length) * sustain
    a = t < attack
    env[a] = t[a] / max(attack, 1e-4)
    d = (t >= attack) & (t < attack + decay)
    env[d] = 1 - (1 - sustain) * (t[d] - attack) / max(decay, 1e-4)
    r_start = seconds - release
    r = t >= r_start
    env[r] *= np.clip(1 - (t[r] - r_start) / max(release, 1e-4), 0, 1)
    return env


def fade(sig, fade_in=0.005, fade_out=0.01):
    sig = sig.copy()
    a, b = n(fade_in), n(fade_out)
    if a > 0:
        sig[:a] *= np.linspace(0, 1, a)
    if b > 0:
        sig[-b:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, b)
    return sig


def ramp(seconds, start, end, curve=1.0):
