```python
    x = np.linspace(0, 1, n(seconds))
    return start + (end - start) * x ** curve


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Filters (zero-phase, frequency domain; fine for offline rendering)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


_MODE = {"circular": False}


class circular:
    """Inside this context, frequency-domain filters wrap around (for loops).
    Outside it, signals are zero-padded so a one-shot's ringing can't wrap."""

    def __enter__(self):
        self.previous = _MODE["circular"]
        _MODE["circular"] = True

    def __exit__(self, *exc):
        _MODE["circular"] = self.previous


def _apply(sig, response):
    pad = 0 if _MODE["circular"] else min(n(0.3), max(len(sig), 1))
    padded = np.concatenate([np.zeros(pad), sig, np.zeros(pad)]) if pad else sig
    length = len(padded)
    spec = np.fft.rfft(padded)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(length, 1 / SR)
    out = np.fft.irfft(spec * response(freqs), length)
    return out[pad:pad + len(sig)] if pad else out


def lowpass(sig, cutoff, order=2):
    return _apply(sig, lambda f: 1 / np.sqrt(1 + (f / cutoff) ** (2 * order)))


def highpass(sig, cutoff, order=2):
    return _apply(sig, lambda f: 1 / np.sqrt(1 + (cutoff / np.maximum(f, 1e-3)) ** (2 * order)))


def bandpass(sig, low, high, order=2):
    return highpass(lowpass(sig, high, order), low, order)


def peak(sig, freq, q, gain):
    """Resonant boost (gain > 1) or cut around freq."""
    def response(f):
        bw = freq / q
        return 1 + (gain - 1) / (1 + ((f - freq) / (bw / 2)) ** 2)
    return _apply(sig, response)


def resonators(sig, modes):
    """Body resonance: modes = [(freq, q, gain)]."""
    out = np.zeros_like(sig)
    for freq, q, gain in modes:
        out += peak(sig, freq, q, gain) - sig
    return sig + out


def sweep_lowpass(sig, start, end, blocks=64):
    """Time-varying low-pass by overlapping blocks (cutoff moves start->end)."""
    length = len(sig)
    out = np.zeros(length)
    size = max(256, length // blocks)
    hop = size // 2
    window = np.hanning(size)
    pos = 0
    while pos < length:
        chunk = np.zeros(size)
        part = sig[pos:pos + size]
        chunk[:len(part)] = part
        centre = min(1.0, (pos + size / 2) / length)
        cutoff = start * (end / start) ** centre
        filt = lowpass(chunk * window, cutoff)
        end_i = min(length, pos + size)
        out[pos:end_i] += filt[:end_i - pos]
        pos += hop
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Effects
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def convolve(sig, ir, circular=False):
    if circular:
        length = len(sig)
        ir_fold = np.zeros(length)
        for start in range(0, len(ir), length):
            seg = ir[start:start + length]
            ir_fold[:len(seg)] += seg
        return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(sig) * np.fft.rfft(ir_fold), length)
    total = len(sig) + len(ir) - 1
    size = 1 << (total - 1).bit_length()
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(sig, size) * np.fft.rfft(ir, size), size)[:total]


def room_ir(seconds, seed, brightness=4000, predelay=0.01):
    """Exponentially decaying filtered noise: a plausible room."""
    t = time(seconds)
    ir = rng(seed).normal(0, 1, len(t)) * np.exp(-t * 6.9 / seconds)
    ir = lowpass(ir, brightness)
    ir = np.concatenate([np.zeros(n(predelay)), ir])
    ir[0] = 1.0
    return ir / np.sqrt(np.sum(ir ** 2))


def reverb(sig, seconds=1.2, mix=0.3, seed=1, brightness=4000, circular=False):
    ir = room_ir(seconds, seed, brightness)
    wet = convolve(sig, ir, circular)
    if not circular:
        sig = np.concatenate([sig, np.zeros(len(wet) - len(sig))])
    wet *= np.max(np.abs(sig)) / (np.max(np.abs(wet)) + 1e-9)
    return sig * (1 - mix) + wet * mix


def wow_flutter(sig, depth=0.0025, rate=0.6, flutter=0.0004, flutter_rate=7.0, seed=3, circular=False):
    """Old tape: pitch wobble by modulating read position."""
    length = len(sig)
    t = np.arange(length) / SR
    r = rng(seed)
    p1, p2 = r.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi, 2)
    if circular:
        # Whole number of cycles over the loop so the wobble is seamless.
        dur = length / SR
        rate = max(1, round(rate * dur)) / dur
        flutter_rate = max(1, round(flutter_rate * dur)) / dur
    offset = depth * SR / (2 * np.pi * rate) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * rate * t + p1)
    offset += flutter * SR / (2 * np.pi * flutter_rate) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * flutter_rate * t + p2)
    idx = np.arange(length) + offset
    if circular:
        return np.interp(idx % length, np.arange(length + 1), np.concatenate([sig, sig[:1]]))
    return np.interp(np.clip(idx, 0, length - 1), np.arange(length), sig)


def tape_stop(sig, seconds):
    """Playback slowing to a halt over the last `seconds`."""
    length = len(sig)
    stop = min(length, n(seconds))
    speed = np.ones(length)
    speed[-stop:] = np.linspace(1, 0, stop) ** 1.5
    idx = np.cumsum(speed)
    idx -= idx[0]
    return np.interp(np.clip(idx, 0, length - 1), np.arange(length), sig)


def saturate(sig, drive=2.0):
    return np.tanh(sig * drive) / np.tanh(drive)


def bitcrush(sig, bits=8, down=2):
    step = 2 ** (bits - 1)
    held = np.repeat(sig[::down], down)[:len(sig)]
    return np.round(held * step) / step


def pitch(sig, ratio):
    """Resample (changes length): ratio > 1 is higher and shorter."""
    length = int(len(sig) / ratio)
    return np.interp(np.arange(length) * ratio, np.arange(len(sig)), sig)


def mix(*parts):
    length = max(len(p) for p in parts)
    out = np.zeros(length)
    for p in parts:
        out[:len(p)] += p
    return out


def place(buf, sig, start, circular=False):
    """Add sig into buf at sample index start (wrapping for loops)."""
    if circular:
        idx = (np.arange(len(sig)) + start) % len(buf)
        np.add.at(buf, idx, sig)
        return buf
    end = min(len(buf), start + len(sig))
    if end > start:
        buf[start:end] += sig[:end - start]
    return buf


def seamless(sig, period_seconds, crossfade_seconds=1.0):
    """Turn a render of (period + crossfade) into an exactly periodic buffer."""
    p = n(period_seconds)
    x = n(crossfade_seconds)
    out = sig[:p].copy()
    tail = sig[p:p + x]
    w = np.linspace(0, 1, len(tail))
    out[:len(tail)] = out[:len(tail)] * w + tail * (1 - w)
    return out


def normalise(sig, peak_db=-1.0):
    m = np.max(np.abs(sig)) + 1e-12
    return sig / m * 10 ** (peak_db / 20)


def rms_normalise(sig, rms_db, peak_db=-0.5):
    rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(sig ** 2)) + 1e-12
    out = sig * (10 ** (rms_db / 20) / rms)
    m = np.max(np.abs(out))
    limit = 10 ** (peak_db / 20)
    if m > limit:
        out = saturate(out / m * 1.2, 1.2) * limit
    return out


def midi(note):
    return 440.0 * 2 ** ((note - 69) / 12)
```

## scripts/audio/loops.py

```python
"""
Looping beds: room tones, machinery and the Presenter's presence.

Every loop is returned exactly periodic (its last sample flows into its first),
so the game can loop it with a LoopRegion with no click.
"""

import numpy as np

from dsp import (
    SR, additive, bandpass, brown, crackle, env_ad, highpass, lowpass, n, pink, place, reverb, rng, saturate,
    seamless, sine, sweep_lowpass, time, white, wow_flutter,
)

LOOPS = {}


def loop(name, period, category="ambience"):
    def wrap(fn):
        LOOPS[name] = (category, period, fn)
        return fn
    return wrap


def noise_bed(period, seed, fn):
    """fn(seconds, seed) -> noise; returns a seamless loop of `period`."""
    return seamless(fn(period + 1.0, seed), period, 1.0)


def events(buf, period, seed, count, make):
    """Scatter one-off details through a loop (wrapping at the seam)."""
    r = rng(seed)
    for i in range(count):
        place(buf, make(i, r), r.integers(0, n(period)), circular=True)
    return buf


def tick(seed, freq):
    s = white(0.03, seed) * env_ad(0.03, 0.0005, 0.005)
    return bandpass(s, freq * 0.7, freq * 1.4)


@loop("AmbStation", 40)
def amb_station():
    p = 40
    rumble = lowpass(noise_bed(p, 300, brown), 380) * 0.6
    vent = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 301, pink), 1500, 6000) * 0.07
    hum = (sine(60, p) + sine(120, p) * 0.4) * 0.03
    out = rumble + vent + hum
    # Building settling: ticks, pipe knocks and a far-off door.
    out = events(out, p, 302, 10, lambda i, r: tick(303 + i, r.uniform(1500, 4000)) * r.uniform(0.05, 0.15))
    knock = lambda i, r: lowpass(white(0.3, 320 + i), 700) * env_ad(0.3, 0.002, 0.04) * r.uniform(0.1, 0.2)
    out = events(out, p, 310, 4, knock)
    return reverb(out, 2.5, 0.35, 330, brightness=2500, circular=True)


@loop("AmbDark", 40)
def amb_dark():
    p = 40
    t = time(p)
    rumble = lowpass(noise_bed(p, 340, brown), 160) * (0.8 + 0.2 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t / p * 2))
    whine = sine(3520, p) * 0.004 * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t / p * 3))
    out = rumble + whine
    thump = lambda i, r: lowpass(white(1.2, 350 + i), 200) * env_ad(1.2, 0.01, 0.3) * r.uniform(0.2, 0.4)
    out = events(out, p, 341, 3, thump)
    return reverb(out, 3.5, 0.45, 360, brightness=1500, circular=True)


@loop("AmbHQ", 30)
def amb_hq():
    p = 30
    aircon = lowpass(noise_bed(p, 370, pink), 1400) * 0.3
    hum = (sine(50, p) + sine(100, p) * 0.5) * 0.015
    out = aircon + hum
    # Distant typing and paper from the next office.
    def typing(i, r):
        burst = np.zeros(n(1.5))
        for k in range(r.integers(4, 9)):
            place(burst, tick(380 + i * 10 + k, 2000) * 0.08, int(r.uniform(0, 1.3) * SR))
        return lowpass(burst, 3000)
    out = events(out, p, 371, 4, typing)
    return reverb(out, 1.2, 0.3, 390, circular=True)


@loop("AmbOutdoor", 40)
def amb_outdoor():
    p = 40
    t = time(p)
    gust = 0.55 + 0.45 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t / p * 3 + 1) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t / p * 2)
    wind = lowpass(noise_bed(p, 400, pink), 900) * gust
    whistle = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 401, white), 700, 900) * gust ** 2 * 0.15
    far = lowpass(noise_bed(p, 402, brown), 120) * 0.3
    return wind * 0.6 + whistle + far


@loop("Fluorescent", 4)
def fluorescent():
    p = 4
    t = time(p)
    hum = additive(120, p, [(1, 1), (2, 0.5), (3, 0.3), (5, 0.15), (7, 0.08)]) * 0.25
    buzz = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 410, white), 2000, 7000) * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.abs(np.sin(2 * np.pi * 120 * t))) * 0.12
    return saturate(hum + buzz, 1.3)


@loop("GeneratorLoop", 4)
def generator_loop():
    p = 4
    t = time(p)
    firing = np.zeros(n(p))
    r = rng(420)
    for k in range(100):  # 25 Hz firing, 100 per loop
        pos = int((k / 25 + r.uniform(-0.002, 0.002)) * SR) % n(p)
        place(firing, lowpass(white(0.03, 421 + k), 500) * env_ad(0.03, 0.001, 0.008), pos, circular=True)
    body = lowpass(firing, 350) * 3
    harmonics = additive(25, p, [(1, 0.6), (2, 0.4), (3, 0.3), (4, 0.15)]) * 0.3
    clatter = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 430, white), 1500, 5000) * (0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 25 * t)) * 0.08
    return saturate(body + harmonics + clatter, 1.5)


@loop("TransmitterLoop", 4)
def transmitter_loop():
    p = 4
    hum = additive(50, p, [(1, 0.5), (2, 0.6), (3, 0.4), (4, 0.3), (6, 0.2), (8, 0.1)]) * 0.35
    whine = sine(2000, p) * 0.03
    arcs = crackle(p, 440, 25) * 0.25
    return saturate(hum + whine + highpass(arcs, 1500), 1.4)


@loop("VanIdle", 4)
def van_idle():
    p = 4
    t = time(p)
    idle = additive(14, p, [(1, 0.5), (2, 0.6), (3, 0.3), (4, 0.2), (6, 0.1)]) * (0.8 + 0.2 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 14 * t))
    rattle = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 450, white), 800, 3000) * 0.05
    return lowpass(idle * 0.5, 500) + rattle


@loop("PresenterNear", 8, "presence")
def presenter_near():
    p = 8
    t = time(p)
    drone = sine(45, p) * 0.5 + sine(47.5, p) * 0.4 + sine(90, p) * 0.15
    flutter_tone = sine(220, p) * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t * 0.75)) * 0.08
    whisper = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 460, pink), 500, 3000) * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t * 0.5 + 2)) * 0.12
    out = wow_flutter(drone + flutter_tone + whisper, 0.006, 0.5, seed=461, circular=True)
    return reverb(out, 2.0, 0.3, 462, brightness=2000, circular=True)


@loop("PresenterStatic", 4, "presence")
def presenter_static():
    p = 4
    snow = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 470, white), 400, 7000) * 0.4
    sizzle = crackle(p, 471, 80) * 0.3
    line = sine(15625 / 2, p) * 0.01
    return snow + sizzle + line


@loop("RadioBed", 4)
def radio_bed():
    p = 4
    return bandpass(noise_bed(p, 480, white), 400, 3200) * 0.3 + crackle(p, 481, 15) * 0.1


@loop("DeckHum", 4)
def deck_hum():
    p = 4
    hum = (sine(60, p) + sine(180, p) * 0.3) * 0.15
    whine = sine(12000, p) * 0.012
    snow = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 490, white), 2000, 8000) * 0.04
    return hum + whine + snow


@loop("TapeScrub", 2)
def tape_scrub():
    p = 2
    t = time(p)
    whine = sine(900, p) * 0.3 + sine(1800, p) * 0.1
    hiss = bandpass(noise_bed(p, 495, white), 2000, 9000) * 0.12
    return wow_flutter(whine + hiss, 0.006, 6, seed=496, circular=True) * (0.85 + 0.15 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 8 * t))
```

## scripts/audio/music.py

```python
"""
The score: eight looping tracks in the voice of a 1960s broadcaster's library
music, played back off worn tape. Organ, music box, vibraphone and strings for
the calm; pulse, bass and metal for the chase.

Each track is rendered into a buffer exactly one loop long, with note tails,
reverb and tape wobble wrapping around the seam, so it loops perfectly.
"""

import numpy as np

from dsp import (
    SR, additive, bandpass, brown, env_ad, env_adsr, highpass, lowpass, midi, n, pink, place, reverb, rng,
    saturate, saw, seamless, sine, sweep_lowpass, time, triangle, white, wow_flutter,
)

TRACKS = {}


def track(name, period):
    def wrap(fn):
        TRACKS[name] = (period, fn)
        return fn
    return wrap


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Instruments
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def organ(note, dur, seed=0, bright=1.0):
    f = midi(note)
    t = time(dur + 0.3)
    drawbars = [(0.5, 0.4), (1, 1.0), (2, 0.6 * bright), (3, 0.35 * bright), (4, 0.25 * bright), (6, 0.12 * bright), (8, 0.08 * bright)]
    tone = additive(f, dur + 0.3, [(r, a) for r, a in drawbars if f * r < 9000])
    leslie = 1 + 0.15 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 5.8 * t + seed)
    return tone * leslie * env_adsr(dur + 0.3, 0.04, 0.1, 0.85, 0.3) * 0.18


def music_box(note, dur=2.0, seed=0):
    f = midi(note)
    t = time(dur)
    tine = np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t) * np.exp(-t * 2.2) + 0.35 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * 3.0 * t) * np.exp(-t * 6)
    tine += 0.15 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * 5.98 * t) * np.exp(-t * 10)
    return tine * np.clip(t / 0.002, 0, 1) * 0.3


def vibes(note, dur=2.4, seed=0):
    f = midi(note)
    t = time(dur)
    bar = np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t) + 0.25 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * 4 * t) * np.exp(-t * 5)
    trem = 1 + 0.3 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 5.5 * t + seed)
    return bar * trem * np.exp(-t * 1.6) * np.clip(t / 0.003, 0, 1) * 0.22


def piano(note, dur=3.0, seed=0):
    f = midi(note)
    t = time(dur)
    out = np.zeros(len(t))
    for k in range(1, 9):
        inharm = k * np.sqrt(1 + 0.0004 * k * k)
        if f * inharm < 10000:
            out += np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * inharm * t) * np.exp(-t * (1.2 + 0.6 * k)) / k
    hammer = lowpass(white(0.02, seed + 900), 3000) * env_ad(0.02, 0.0005, 0.004) * 0.3
    out[: len(hammer)] += hammer
    return out * np.clip(t / 0.002, 0, 1) * 0.3


def pad(notes, dur, seed=0, cutoff=1400, attack=1.5):
    out = np.zeros(n(dur + 1.5))
    r = rng(seed)
    for note in notes:
        for d in (-0.08, 0.0, 0.09):
            f = midi(note + d)
            out[: n(dur + 1.5)] += saw(f * (1 + r.uniform(-0.001, 0.001)), dur + 1.5, 14)[: n(dur + 1.5)]
    out = lowpass(out, cutoff)
    return out * env_adsr(dur + 1.5, attack, 0.5, 0.8, 1.5) * 0.05


def strings(note, dur, seed=0, attack=0.15):
    f = midi(note)
    t = time(dur + 0.4)
    vib = f * (1 + 0.004 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 5.2 * t + seed))
    tone = saw(vib, dur + 0.4, 18) + saw(vib * 1.003, dur + 0.4, 18) * 0.8
    tone = bandpass(tone, 180, 3500)
    return tone * env_adsr(dur + 0.4, attack, 0.1, 0.85, 0.4) * 0.12


def bass(note, dur, seed=0, drive=1.0):
    f = midi(note)
    tone = triangle(f, dur) + sine(f / 2, dur) * 0.2 + saw(f * 2, dur, 8) * 0.25
    tone = saturate(tone * drive, 1 + drive)
    return tone * env_adsr(dur, 0.005, 0.08, 0.7, 0.05) * 0.35


def kick(seed=0):
    t = time(0.5)
    body = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (55 + 90 * np.exp(-t * 35)) * t) * np.exp(-t * 9) * 0.5
    beater = bandpass(white(0.5, seed + 700), 1500, 5000) * env_ad(0.5, 0.0005, 0.008) * 0.25
    return body + beater


def tom(note=40, seed=0):
    t = time(0.8)
    f = midi(note)
    return (np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * (1 + 0.5 * np.exp(-t * 20)) * t) * np.exp(-t * 5) + lowpass(white(0.8, seed), 800) * np.exp(-t * 30) * 0.3) * 0.5


def tick(seed=0, freq=2500):
    s = white(0.04, seed) * env_ad(0.04, 0.0005, 0.006)
    return bandpass(s, freq * 0.8, freq * 1.3) * 0.5


def brush(seed=0):
    return bandpass(white(0.18, seed), 3000, 10000) * env_ad(0.18, 0.01, 0.05) * 0.12


def metal(seed=0, freq=520):
    hit = white(0.02, seed) * env_ad(0.02, 0.0003, 0.004)
    t = time(1.2)
    ring = sum(np.sin(2 * np.pi * freq * r * t) * np.exp(-t * (2 + i * 1.5)) / (1 + i) for i, r in enumerate((1, 1.47, 2.09, 2.76, 3.51)))
    ring[: len(hit)] += hit * 2
    return ring * 0.18


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class Score:
    def __init__(self, period, bpm=60):
        self.period = period
        self.buf = np.zeros(n(period))
        self.beat = 60.0 / bpm

    def at(self, sig, beat_or_seconds, seconds=False):
        start = beat_or_seconds if seconds else beat_or_seconds * self.beat
        place(self.buf, sig, n(start), circular=True)

    def bed(self, sig):
        place(self.buf, sig, 0, circular=True)

    def tape(self, wow=0.004, cutoff=6000, hiss=0.01, seed=0, room=1.8, wet=0.3):
        # Thin out the sub-bass: small speakers can't play it and it masks the rest.
        out = reverb(highpass(self.buf, 45, 2), room, wet, seed + 1, circular=True)
        out = wow_flutter(out, wow, 0.5, 0.0006, 6.5, seed=seed + 2, circular=True)
        out = lowpass(out, cutoff)
        hiss_bed = highpass(seamless(white(self.period + 1, seed + 3), self.period, 1.0), 3000) * hiss
        return saturate(out + hiss_bed, 1.2)


def chord_notes(root, kind):
    shapes = {"m": (0, 3, 7), "M": (0, 4, 7), "m7": (0, 3, 7, 10), "M7": (0, 4, 7, 11), "7": (0, 4, 7, 10), "dim": (0, 3, 6)}
    return [root + i for i in shapes[kind]]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tracks
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@track("MusicMenu", 64)
def music_menu():
    # A broadcaster's sign-off theme, slightly wrong. Am - F - Dm - E, twice.
    s = Score(64, 60)
    progression = [(57, "m"), (53, "M"), (50, "m"), (52, "M")]
    for rep in range(2):
        for i, (root, kind) in enumerate(progression):
            start = rep * 32 + i * 8
            for note in chord_notes(root, kind):
                # Second pass: the organ drifts a little flat.
                s.at(organ(note - (0.15 if rep == 1 else 0), 7.6, seed=i, bright=0.6), start)
            s.at(organ(root - 12, 7.6, seed=i + 5, bright=0.3) * 0.8, start)
    melody = [76, None, 74, 72, 71, None, 72, 74, 72, None, 69, None, 68, None, 71, None,
              76, None, 77, 76, 74, None, 72, 71, 69, None, 72, 71, 68, None, None, None]
    for rep in range(2):
        for i, note in enumerate(melody):
            if note:
                s.at(music_box(note + (0 if rep == 0 else -0.2), 2.4, seed=i), rep * 32 + i)
    s.bed(lowpass(seamless(brown(65, 501), 64, 1), 200) * 0.05)
    return s.tape(wow=0.006, cutoff=5000, hiss=0.012, seed=500, room=2.4, wet=0.35)


@track("MusicLobby", 51.2)
def music_lobby():
    # Field office library music: vibraphone over a soft organ, a touch too slow.
    s = Score(51.2, 75)
    progression = [(60, "M7"), (57, "m7"), (53, "M7"), (55, "7")]
    for cycle in range(4):
        for i, (root, kind) in enumerate(progression):
            bar = cycle * 16 + i * 4
            notes = chord_notes(root, kind)
            for note in notes:
                s.at(organ(note, 3.0, seed=i, bright=0.35) * 0.55, bar)
            s.at(bass(root - 24, 1.4) * 0.6, bar)
            s.at(bass(root - 17, 1.4) * 0.5, bar + 2)
            arp = notes + [notes[1] + 12]
            for k in range(8):
                note = arp[k % len(arp)] + 12
                s.at(vibes(note, 2.0, seed=k) * (0.9 if k % 2 == 0 else 0.6), bar + k * 0.5)
            for k in range(4):
                s.at(brush(510 + cycle * 16 + i * 4 + k), bar + k + 0.5)
    return s.tape(wow=0.004, cutoff=5500, hiss=0.008, seed=510, room=1.6, wet=0.3)


@track("MusicBriefing", 32)
def music_briefing():
    # Low drone under the radio briefing, a slow pulse like a held breath.
    s = Score(32, 60)
    s.bed(pad([38, 45, 50], 32, seed=520, cutoff=700, attack=4) * 1.5)
    for b in range(32):
        s.at(tom(45, 521 + b) * (0.5 if b % 4 == 0 else 0.25), b)
    for k, note in enumerate([62, 63, 62, 60]):
        s.at(strings(note, 6, seed=k, attack=2.5) * 1.0, k * 8 + 1)
    for b in (6.5, 14.25, 22.75, 29.5):
        s.at(sine(1400, 0.08) * env_ad(0.08, 0.002, 0.03) * 0.08, b)
    return s.tape(wow=0.003, cutoff=4000, hiss=0.003, seed=520, room=2.5, wet=0.35)


@track("MusicExplore", 64)
def music_explore():
    # The station at night: a sub drone, a piano in another room, distant swells.
    s = Score(64, 60)
    t = time(64)
    drone = (sine(55, 64) + sine(82.5, 64) * 0.4 + sine(110, 64) * 0.15) * (0.75 + 0.25 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t / 32))
    s.bed(drone * 0.08)
    r = rng(530)
    phrase = [57, 60, 64, 63, 57, 56, 60, 52]
    for i, note in enumerate(phrase):
        s.at(piano(note, 4.0, seed=i) * 1.3, i * 8 + r.uniform(0, 2.5))
    for start, notes in ((4, [69, 70]), (36, [72, 73])):
        for note in notes:
            s.at(strings(note, 10, seed=start, attack=4) * 0.6, start)
    s.bed(bandpass(seamless(pink(65, 531), 64, 1), 200, 1200) * 0.03)
    return s.tape(wow=0.004, cutoff=4500, hiss=0.0025, seed=530, room=3.5, wet=0.45)


@track("MusicTension", 32)
def music_tension():
    # Something is close. A low string ostinato, a clock, a cluster that swells.
    s = Score(32, 120)
    figure = [45, 45, 46, 45, 45, 45, 46, 48]
    for bar in range(16):
        for k, note in enumerate(figure):
            s.at(strings(note, 0.22, seed=k, attack=0.01) * (1.0 if k == 0 else 0.7), bar * 4 + k * 0.5)
    for b in range(64):
        s.at(tick(540 + b, 3000 if b % 2 == 0 else 2400), b)
    for start in (0, 16, 32, 48):
        for note in (69, 70, 71):
            s.at(strings(note, 7, seed=start, attack=5) * 0.35, start)
    return s.tape(wow=0.003, cutoff=6000, hiss=0.003, seed=540, room=2.0, wet=0.3)


@track("MusicHunt", 25.6)
def music_hunt():
    # The chase: 150 bpm, driven bass in A phrygian, metal and sirens.
    s = Score(25.6, 150)
    pattern = [45, 45, 46, 45, 45, 48, 45, 46, 45, 45, 46, 45, 43, 45, 46, 48]
    for bar in range(16):
        for b in range(4):
            s.at(kick(550 + bar * 4 + b), bar * 4 + b)
        for k, note in enumerate(pattern):
            s.at(bass(note + (12 if bar % 4 == 3 and k >= 12 else 0), 0.14, drive=2.5) * 0.9, bar * 4 + k * 0.25)
        s.at(metal(560 + bar, 520 if bar % 2 == 0 else 466) * 1.6, bar * 4 + 1.5)
        s.at(tick(570 + bar, 6000) * 0.6, bar * 4 + 3.5)
    for start in (0, 8, 16, 24, 32, 40, 48, 56):
        t = time(3.2)
        f = midi(69) * (1 + 0.06 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2.5 * t))
        siren = (saw(f, 3.2, 10) + saw(f * 1.059, 3.2, 10) * 0.7) * env_adsr(3.2, 0.4, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0) * 0.14
        s.at(lowpass(siren, 2500), start)
    riser = sweep_lowpass(white(3.2, 580), 300, 9000) * np.linspace(0, 1, n(3.2)) ** 2 * 0.15
    s.at(riser, 28.8)
    s.at(riser, 60.8)
    return s.tape(wow=0.002, cutoff=8000, hiss=0.004, seed=550, room=1.2, wet=0.2)


@track("MusicExtract", 32)
def music_extract():
    # Get out: timpani eighths, rising string chords, a heartbeat kick.
    s = Score(32, 120)
    chords = [(57, "m"), (58, "M"), (60, "M"), (62, "M")]
    for i, (root, kind) in enumerate(chords * 2):
        start = i * 8
        for note in chord_notes(root, kind):
            s.at(strings(note, 3.8, seed=i, attack=0.6) * 0.45, start)
            s.at(strings(note + 12, 3.8, seed=i + 9, attack=0.8) * 0.25, start)
    for b in range(128):
        s.at(tom(45 if b % 4 else 43, 590 + b) * (0.7 if b % 2 == 0 else 0.4), b * 0.5)
    for b in range(0, 64, 2):
        s.at(kick(600 + b) * 0.4, b)
    return s.tape(wow=0.002, cutoff=7500, hiss=0.006, seed=590, room=1.6, wet=0.25)


@track("MusicReport", 48)
def music_report():
    # The case report: a slow organ hymn and a music box, played back too many times.
    s = Score(48, 60)
    progression = [(50, "m"), (46, "M"), (53, "M"), (48, "M")]
    for rep in range(2):
        for i, (root, kind) in enumerate(progression):
            start = rep * 24 + i * 6
            for note in chord_notes(root, kind):
                s.at(organ(note, 5.6, seed=i, bright=0.45) * 0.8, start)
            s.at(organ(root - 12, 5.6, seed=i + 3, bright=0.2) * 0.7, start)
    melody = [74, 72, 70, 69, 70, 72, 74, 77, 76, 74, 72, 69]
    for rep in range(2):
        for i, note in enumerate(melody):
            s.at(music_box(note, 2.6, seed=i) * 0.9, rep * 24 + i * 2)
    return s.tape(wow=0.007, cutoff=4500, hiss=0.012, seed=610, room=2.6, wet=0.4)
```

## scripts/audio/sfx.py

```python
"""
One-shot sound effects for DEAD AIR: CHANNEL ZERO, all synthesised here.

Each function returns a mono array. The bank builder normalises loudness by
category, so absolute levels here only matter within a sound.
"""

import numpy as np

from dsp import (
    SR, additive, bandpass, brown, crackle, env_ad, env_adsr, fade, highpass, lowpass, mix, n, peak, pink,
    pitch, ramp, resonators, reverb, rng, saturate, saw, silence, sine, square, sweep_lowpass, tape_stop,
    time, triangle, white, wow_flutter,
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Building blocks
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def click(seed, freq=3000, length=0.02, q=6):
    """A small mechanical click: a noise tick through a body resonance."""
    s = white(length, seed) * env_ad(length, 0.0005, length / 6)
    return resonators(s, [(freq, q, 6), (freq * 1.7, q, 3)])


def thud(freq=70, length=0.35, seed=1, noise=0.4):
    t = time(length)
    body = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (freq + 40 * np.exp(-t * 30)) * t) * np.exp(-t * 9)
    hit = lowpass(white(length, seed), 900) * np.exp(-t * 40) * noise
    return body + hit


def bell(freq, length, seed=0, ratios=(1, 2.76, 5.4, 8.93), decay=1.6, strike=0.6):
    t = time(length)
    out = np.zeros(len(t))
    r = rng(seed)
    for i, ratio in enumerate(ratios):
        detune = 1 + r.uniform(-0.002, 0.002)
        out += np.sin(2 * np.pi * freq * ratio * detune * t) * np.exp(-t * decay * (1 + i * 0.9)) / (1 + i)
    c = click(seed, freq * 3, 0.01) * strike * 0.2
    out[: len(c)] += c[: len(out)]
    return out


def static_burst(length, seed, band=(300, 7000), crackles=60):
    s = bandpass(white(length, seed), *band) + crackle(length, seed + 1, crackles) * 0.6
    return s


def motor(length, f0, f1, seed=2):
    f = ramp(length, f0, f1)
    s = saw(f, length, 12) * 0.6 + bandpass(white(length, seed), 300, 2500) * 0.2
    return lowpass(s, 1800)


def footstep(kind, variant):
    seed = 100 + variant * 7 + {"tile": 1, "concrete": 2, "carpet": 3, "metal": 4}[kind] * 13
    r = rng(seed)
    if kind == "tile":
        # Hard heel click on ceramic over a short body.
        heel = resonators(white(0.06, seed) * env_ad(0.06, 0.0005, 0.008), [(2600 + r.uniform(-200, 200), 5, 6), (4100, 6, 4)])
        body = lowpass(white(0.12, seed + 1), 900) * env_ad(0.12, 0.002, 0.025) * 0.6
        scuff = bandpass(white(0.09, seed + 2), 2000, 7000) * env_ad(0.09, 0.02, 0.03) * 0.15
        s = mix(heel, body, np.concatenate([silence(0.03), scuff]))
    elif kind == "carpet":
        s = lowpass(white(0.16, seed), 700) * env_ad(0.16, 0.006, 0.04)
        s += bandpass(white(0.16, seed + 1), 900, 2500) * env_ad(0.16, 0.02, 0.05) * 0.12
    elif kind == "metal":
        hit = white(0.03, seed) * env_ad(0.03, 0.0005, 0.006)
        ring = resonators(hit, [(420 + r.uniform(-30, 30), 30, 18), (1130, 40, 12), (2210, 50, 8)])
        ring = np.concatenate([ring, np.zeros(n(0.25))])
        tail = additive(430 + r.uniform(-20, 20), 0.28, [(1, 0.5), (2.63, 0.3), (5.1, 0.15)]) * env_ad(0.28, 0.001, 0.07) * 0.3
        s = mix(ring, tail, lowpass(white(0.1, seed + 2), 600) * env_ad(0.1, 0.001, 0.02) * 0.6)
    else:  # concrete
        s = bandpass(white(0.14, seed), 200, 3200) * env_ad(0.14, 0.001, 0.02)
        s += bandpass(white(0.14, seed + 1), 2500, 8000) * env_ad(0.14, 0.015, 0.035) * 0.25  # grit
        s = mix(s, lowpass(white(0.1, seed + 2), 300) * env_ad(0.1, 0.002, 0.02) * 0.5)
    return fade(pitch(s, r.uniform(0.94, 1.06)), 0.001, 0.02)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Library: name -> (category, function)
# category: ui | sfx | sting | voice
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

LIB = {}


def sound(name, category="sfx"):
    def wrap(fn):
        LIB[name] = (category, fn)
        return fn
    return wrap


# --- Interface -------------------------------------------------------------

@sound("UIHover", "ui")
def ui_hover():
    return fade(click(11, 4200, 0.015) * 0.5)


@sound("UIClick", "ui")
def ui_click():
    # A relay closing: two contacts, a hint of hum.
    a = click(12, 2400, 0.03)
    b = click(13, 1800, 0.025) * 0.6
    hum = sine(120, 0.05) * env_ad(0.05, 0.001, 0.015) * 0.15
    return fade(mix(a, np.concatenate([silence(0.012), b]), hum))


@sound("UIBack", "ui")
def ui_back():
    return fade(mix(click(14, 1400, 0.03), np.concatenate([silence(0.015), click(15, 1100, 0.03) * 0.7])))


@sound("UIToggle", "ui")
def ui_toggle():
    return fade(mix(click(16, 3000, 0.02), np.concatenate([silence(0.05), click(17, 2200, 0.02)])))


@sound("UISlider", "ui")
def ui_slider():
    return fade(click(18, 5200, 0.01) * 0.4)


@sound("UIError", "ui")
def ui_error():
    s = square(150, 0.28, 10) * env_adsr(0.28, 0.005, 0.05, 0.7, 0.06)
    return fade(lowpass(s, 2500) * 0.6)


@sound("UINotify", "ui")
def ui_notify():
    # Teletype tick-tick-tick then a soft blip.
    out = silence(0.32)
    for i in range(3):
        start = n(0.045 * i)
        out[start:start + n(0.02)] += click(20 + i, 2800, 0.02)[: n(0.02)] * 0.8
    blip = sine(1760, 0.12) * env_ad(0.12, 0.003, 0.04) * 0.4
    return fade(mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.16), blip])))


@sound("MenuOpen", "ui")
def menu_open():
    # CRT switching on: thump, rising whine, settling static.
    t = time(0.9)
    thump = thud(55, 0.5, 30, 0.2)
    whine = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (6000 + 3000 * (1 - np.exp(-t * 6))) * t) * env_ad(0.9, 0.05, 0.4) * 0.05
    hiss = bandpass(white(0.9, 31), 1000, 8000) * env_ad(0.9, 0.02, 0.15) * 0.25
    return fade(mix(thump, whine, hiss))


@sound("MenuClose", "ui")
def menu_close():
    t = time(0.4)
    pop = click(33, 900, 0.03) * 1.2
    fall = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (900 * np.exp(-t * 8) + 60) * t) * env_ad(0.4, 0.002, 0.08) * 0.3
    return fade(mix(pop, fall))


@sound("TutorialPage", "ui")
def tutorial_page():
    # Paper sliding across a desk.
    s = bandpass(white(0.35, 40), 1500, 9000) * env_adsr(0.35, 0.08, 0.1, 0.5, 0.15)
    return fade(s * 0.6)


@sound("Countdown", "ui")
def countdown():
    return fade(mix(click(41, 1600, 0.03), sine(880, 0.08) * env_ad(0.08, 0.001, 0.03) * 0.3))


@sound("Ready", "ui")
def ready():
    # A rubber stamp on paper.
    return fade(mix(thud(110, 0.25, 42, 0.8), click(43, 700, 0.04) * 0.5))


@sound("Deploy", "ui")
def deploy():
    # Van door rolling shut and the engine turning over.
    t = time(2.2)
    roll = lowpass(brown(0.6, 44), 900) * env_adsr(0.6, 0.05, 0.1, 0.7, 0.1) * 0.8
    slam = mix(thud(60, 0.5, 45, 0.8), click(46, 500, 0.05) * 0.4)
    crank = np.zeros(len(t))
    for i in range(6):
        pos = n(0.8 + i * 0.11)
        crank[pos:pos + n(0.08)] += lowpass(white(0.08, 47 + i), 500) * env_ad(0.08, 0.004, 0.02)
    engine = saw(ramp(1.0, 18, 32), 1.0, 8) * env_adsr(1.0, 0.2, 0.2, 0.8, 0.3) * 0.5
    out = mix(roll, np.concatenate([silence(0.55), slam]), crank, np.concatenate([silence(1.2), lowpass(engine, 400)]))
    return fade(reverb(out, 0.8, 0.2, 48))


@sound("RewardTick", "ui")
def reward_tick():
    return fade(bell(1568, 0.3, 50, decay=9, strike=0.2) * 0.5)


@sound("Unlock", "ui")
def unlock():
    out = silence(1.4)
    for i, f in enumerate([523, 659, 784, 1047]):
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.09 * i), bell(f, 1.0, 51 + i, decay=3.5)]))
    return fade(reverb(out, 1.0, 0.25, 52))


@sound("ReportKey", "ui")
def report_key():
    # Typewriter type-bar strike.
    return fade(mix(click(53, 1900, 0.025) * 1.2, thud(180, 0.08, 54, 0.3) * 0.5))


@sound("ReportStamp", "ui")
def report_stamp():
    return fade(reverb(mix(thud(90, 0.4, 55, 1.0), click(56, 600, 0.05)), 0.6, 0.2, 57))


@sound("VoteChime", "ui")
def vote_chime():
    return fade(mix(bell(784, 0.9, 58, decay=4), np.concatenate([silence(0.22), bell(1047, 0.9, 59, decay=4)])))


# --- Player ----------------------------------------------------------------

for _kind in ("tile", "concrete", "carpet", "metal"):
    for _v in range(4):
        def _make(kind=_kind, v=_v):
            return footstep(kind, v)
        LIB["Step_%s_%d" % (_kind, _v + 1)] = ("sfx", _make)


@sound("FlashlightOn")
def flashlight_on():
    return fade(mix(click(60, 3400, 0.02), np.concatenate([silence(0.03), click(61, 2100, 0.02) * 0.6])))


@sound("FlashlightOff")
def flashlight_off():
    return fade(mix(click(62, 2900, 0.02), np.concatenate([silence(0.025), click(63, 1700, 0.02) * 0.5])))


@sound("BreathOut")
def breath_out():
    # Winded exhale after running out of stamina.
    s = bandpass(pink(0.9, 64), 350, 2400)
    s = peak(s, 900, 3, 3)
    return fade(s * env_adsr(0.9, 0.08, 0.2, 0.6, 0.4) * 0.7)


@sound("BreathIn")
def breath_in():
    s = bandpass(pink(0.6, 65), 600, 3500)
    return fade(s * env_adsr(0.6, 0.25, 0.1, 0.7, 0.2) * 0.5)


@sound("Heartbeat")
def heartbeat():
    t = time(0.7)
    lub = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (52 + 25 * np.exp(-t * 25)) * t) * np.exp(-t * 18)
    t2 = time(0.5)
    dub = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (44 + 20 * np.exp(-t2 * 25)) * t2) * np.exp(-t2 * 20) * 0.7
    return fade(lowpass(mix(lub, np.concatenate([silence(0.18), dub])), 200))


@sound("ToolEquip")
def tool_equip():
    # Camcorder: lens motor and a latch.
    return fade(mix(motor(0.45, 140, 220, 66) * env_adsr(0.45, 0.03, 0.05, 0.8, 0.1) * 0.5, click(67, 1500, 0.03)))


@sound("ToolUnequip")
def tool_unequip():
    return fade(mix(motor(0.3, 220, 120, 68) * env_adsr(0.3, 0.02, 0.05, 0.8, 0.1) * 0.4, np.concatenate([silence(0.25), click(69, 1300, 0.03)])))


@sound("RecBeep")
def rec_beep():
    beep = sine(2093, 0.07) * env_adsr(0.07, 0.002, 0.01, 0.9, 0.01)
    return fade(mix(beep, np.concatenate([silence(0.11), beep])) * 0.5)


@sound("Inspect")
def inspect():
    return fade(motor(0.25, 300, 420, 70) * env_adsr(0.25, 0.02, 0.05, 0.6, 0.1) * 0.25)


@sound("PromptHold")
def prompt_hold():
    return fade(click(71, 2000, 0.02) * 0.4)


@sound("PickupTape")
def pickup_tape():
    # Plastic VHS shell knocked against a shelf, then lifted.
    a = resonators(white(0.04, 72) * env_ad(0.04, 0.0005, 0.006), [(1800, 8, 8), (3300, 10, 4)])
    b = resonators(white(0.04, 73) * env_ad(0.04, 0.0005, 0.005), [(2100, 8, 6)]) * 0.6
    rattle = bandpass(white(0.25, 74), 2000, 6000) * env_ad(0.25, 0.01, 0.05) * 0.15
    return fade(mix(a, np.concatenate([silence(0.06), b]), np.concatenate([silence(0.08), rattle])))


@sound("PickupMetal")
def pickup_metal():
    hit = white(0.02, 75) * env_ad(0.02, 0.0003, 0.004)
    ring = resonators(np.concatenate([hit, np.zeros(n(0.6))]), [(2150, 80, 30), (3430, 90, 20), (5260, 90, 10)])
    return fade(ring * 0.5)


@sound("PickupKey")
def pickup_key():
    out = silence(0.4)
    for i in range(4):
        hit = white(0.015, 76 + i) * env_ad(0.015, 0.0003, 0.003)
        ring = resonators(np.concatenate([hit, np.zeros(n(0.2))]), [(3600 + i * 300, 60, 20), (5800, 60, 10)])
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.05 * i), ring * 0.4]))
    return fade(out)


# --- Doors and the building -----------------------------------------------

@sound("DoorOpen")
def door_open():
    # Latch, then a hinge creak (stick-slip friction through the door's body).
    r = rng(80)
    length = 1.1
    t = time(length)
    rate = 18 + 30 * np.sin(np.pi * t / length) + r.normal(0, 3, len(t)).cumsum() / 400
    pulses = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(rate) / SR)
    stick = (pulses > 0.97).astype(float)
    creak = resonators(stick * 0.8 + white(length, 81) * 0.02, [(620, 12, 20), (1450, 15, 12), (2700, 20, 6)])
    creak = bandpass(creak, 250, 5000) * env_adsr(length, 0.15, 0.2, 0.8, 0.3) * 0.35
    latch = mix(click(82, 1300, 0.04), np.concatenate([silence(0.05), click(83, 900, 0.04) * 0.6]))
    return fade(reverb(mix(latch, np.concatenate([silence(0.08), creak])), 0.9, 0.2, 84))


@sound("DoorClose")
def door_close():
    s = mix(thud(65, 0.5, 85, 0.6), np.concatenate([silence(0.02), click(86, 1100, 0.04) * 0.7]))
    return fade(reverb(s, 1.0, 0.25, 87))


@sound("DoorLocked")
def door_locked():
    out = silence(0.6)
    for i in range(3):
        rat = mix(click(88 + i, 900, 0.05), thud(140, 0.12, 91 + i, 0.6) * 0.4)
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.13 * i), rat]))
    return fade(out)


@sound("DoorForced")
def door_forced():
    # Something hits the door from the other side.
    bang = mix(thud(48, 0.9, 95, 1.2), lowpass(white(0.4, 96), 2500) * env_ad(0.4, 0.001, 0.05))
    rattle = bandpass(white(0.5, 97), 600, 3000) * env_ad(0.5, 0.01, 0.12) * 0.3
    return fade(reverb(mix(bang, rattle), 1.6, 0.35, 98))


@sound("FreightDoor")
def freight_door():
    length = 3.2
    rumble = lowpass(brown(length, 100), 300) * env_adsr(length, 0.3, 0.3, 0.9, 0.6)
    chain = np.zeros(n(length))
    for i in range(int(length * 9)):
        pos = n(0.1 + i * 0.105)
        c = resonators(white(0.02, 101 + i) * env_ad(0.02, 0.0005, 0.004), [(1900 + (i % 3) * 300, 30, 10)])
        chain = mix(chain, np.concatenate([silence(pos / SR), c * 0.25]))[: n(length)]
    clang = np.concatenate([silence(length - 0.4), thud(55, 0.4, 130, 0.9)])
    return fade(reverb(mix(rumble, chain, clang), 2.0, 0.35, 131))


@sound("KeypadPress")
def keypad_press():
    beep = sine(1209, 0.09) * env_adsr(0.09, 0.002, 0.01, 0.9, 0.02) * 0.5
    return fade(mix(click(132, 2600, 0.015) * 0.6, beep))


@sound("KeypadWrong")
def keypad_wrong():
    s = square(165, 0.5, 12) * env_adsr(0.5, 0.005, 0.05, 0.8, 0.08)
    return fade(lowpass(saturate(s, 2), 3000) * 0.6)


@sound("KeypadRight")
def keypad_right():
    a = sine(880, 0.12) * env_adsr(0.12, 0.002, 0.02, 0.9, 0.02)
    b = sine(1320, 0.18) * env_adsr(0.18, 0.002, 0.02, 0.9, 0.05)
    solenoid = mix(thud(150, 0.2, 133, 0.5), click(134, 800, 0.04))
    return fade(mix(a * 0.5, np.concatenate([silence(0.13), b * 0.5]), np.concatenate([silence(0.35), solenoid])))


@sound("CageOpen")
def cage_open():
    # Wire-mesh door swinging on a dry hinge.
    rattle = bandpass(white(0.9, 135), 1500, 6000) * env_adsr(0.9, 0.02, 0.2, 0.4, 0.4) * 0.3
    creak = door_open()[: n(1.0)] * 0.7
    return fade(mix(rattle, creak))


@sound("Install")
def install():
    # A component seated into its socket: slide, heavy clunk, ratchet.
    slide = bandpass(white(0.3, 136), 800, 4000) * env_adsr(0.3, 0.1, 0.05, 0.6, 0.1) * 0.4
    clunk = mix(thud(80, 0.5, 137, 1.0), click(138, 700, 0.05) * 0.8)
    ratchet = silence(0.5)
    for i in range(5):
        ratchet = mix(ratchet, np.concatenate([silence(0.07 * i), click(139 + i, 2400, 0.02) * 0.5]))
    return fade(reverb(mix(slide, np.concatenate([silence(0.25), clunk]), np.concatenate([silence(0.7), ratchet])), 1.0, 0.2, 145))


@sound("Relay")
def relay():
    # Industrial contactor slamming in, with a brief arc buzz.
    buzz = saturate(sine(100, 0.2) + sine(300, 0.2) * 0.3, 3) * env_ad(0.2, 0.001, 0.05) * 0.4
    return fade(mix(thud(120, 0.3, 146, 1.0), click(147, 1500, 0.04), buzz))


@sound("GeneratorPrime")
def generator_prime():
    # Hand-primed fuel pump strokes then a crank that slows.
    out = silence(2.6)
    for i in range(4):
        stroke = mix(click(148 + i, 700, 0.04), lowpass(white(0.15, 152 + i), 900) * env_ad(0.15, 0.03, 0.04) * 0.6)
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.35 * i), stroke]))
    for i in range(7):
        gap = 1.45 + 0.13 * i + 0.01 * i * i
        chuff = lowpass(white(0.12, 160 + i), 600) * env_ad(0.12, 0.005, 0.03)
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(gap), chuff]))
    return fade(reverb(out, 1.2, 0.25, 170))


@sound("GeneratorStart")
def generator_start():
    length = 3.5
    t = time(length)
    rate = ramp(length, 4, 26, 0.6)
    firing = (np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(rate) / SR) > 0.85).astype(float)
    body = lowpass(firing, 400) * 3 + lowpass(brown(length, 171), 250) * 0.4
    hum = saw(ramp(length, 25, 50), length, 10) * np.clip(t / length, 0, 1) * 0.3
    return fade(reverb(mix(body * env_adsr(length, 0.1, 0.1, 1, 0.3), lowpass(hum, 600)), 1.4, 0.3, 172))


@sound("PowerOn")
def power_on():
    # Breakers thrown, then the tubes strike one by one.
    out = mix(thud(70, 0.5, 173, 1.0), click(174, 900, 0.05))
    hum = (sine(120, 1.6) + sine(240, 1.6) * 0.4) * env_adsr(1.6, 0.4, 0.2, 0.6, 0.5) * 0.25
    out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.2), hum]))
    for i in range(5):
        ping = mix(click(175 + i, 3500, 0.02) * 0.5, sine(4800 + i * 200, 0.15) * env_ad(0.15, 0.001, 0.04) * 0.08)
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.35 + 0.17 * i + (i % 2) * 0.05), ping]))
    return fade(reverb(out, 1.5, 0.3, 180))


@sound("LightsFail")
def lights_fail():
    # Mains collapsing: a dropping whine and a final clunk.
    length = 1.8
    t = time(length)
    f = 120 * np.exp(-t * 1.6) + 20
    whine = (np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) + 0.4 * np.sin(4 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR)) * env_adsr(length, 0.005, 0.2, 0.8, 0.6)
    fizz = bandpass(white(0.4, 181), 2000, 9000) * env_ad(0.4, 0.001, 0.08) * 0.3
    clunk = np.concatenate([silence(1.2), thud(60, 0.5, 182, 0.8)])
    return fade(reverb(mix(whine * 0.5, fizz, clunk), 1.6, 0.3, 183))


@sound("Alarm")
def alarm():
    # Electric fire bell: a hammer at 18 strikes per second.
    length = 3.0
    out = silence(length + 1.0)
    for i in range(int(length * 18)):
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(i / 18), bell(1150, 0.5, 184, decay=7, strike=0.4) * 0.3]))
    out = out * np.concatenate([np.ones(n(length)), np.linspace(1, 0.3, len(out) - n(length))])
    return fade(reverb(saturate(out, 1.5), 1.8, 0.3, 185))


@sound("PhoneRing")
def phone_ring():
    # Two-bell 1960s ringer, one double ring.
    out = silence(2.6)
    for burst in (0.0, 0.55):
        for i in range(int(0.4 * 20)):
            t0 = burst + i / 20
            b = mix(bell(1040, 0.35, 186, decay=8, strike=0.3), bell(1300, 0.35, 187, decay=8, strike=0.3)) * 0.25
            out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(t0), b]))
    return fade(reverb(out, 1.2, 0.25, 188))


@sound("TransmitterArmed")
def transmitter_armed():
    length = 3.2
    t = time(length)
    f = 70 * (6 ** (t / length))
    s = saw(f, length, 20) * (0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 9 * t))
    s = sweep_lowpass(s, 300, 5000)
    zap = crackle(length, 189, 30) * np.clip(t / length, 0, 1)
    return fade(reverb(mix(s * env_adsr(length, 0.3, 0.2, 0.9, 0.4) * 0.6, zap * 0.4), 2.0, 0.3, 190))


@sound("OnAir")
def on_air():
    # The broadcast going out: 1 kHz line-up tone over the carrier.
    tone = sine(1000, 2.4) * env_adsr(2.4, 0.01, 0.1, 0.9, 0.2) * 0.4
    carrier = bandpass(white(2.4, 191), 200, 5000) * 0.12
    thump = thud(50, 0.6, 192, 0.5)
    return fade(mix(thump, tone + carrier))


@sound("EndOfTransmission")
def end_of_transmission():
    tone = sine(1000, 3.0) * 0.45
    return fade(tone, 0.005, 0.002)


@sound("VanDoorSlam")
def van_door_slam():
    s = mix(thud(70, 0.6, 193, 1.0), resonators(white(0.05, 194) * env_ad(0.05, 0.0005, 0.01), [(900, 15, 10), (1700, 20, 6)]) * 0.5)
    return fade(reverb(s, 0.9, 0.2, 195))


# --- The deck --------------------------------------------------------------

@sound("DeckOpen")
def deck_open():
    return menu_open() * 1.0


@sound("DeckClose")
def deck_close():
    return menu_close()


@sound("TapeInsert")
def tape_insert():
    # Cassette pushed in, the carriage drops, the heads thread.
    push = resonators(white(0.06, 196) * env_ad(0.06, 0.001, 0.01), [(1700, 8, 6)])
    drop = mix(thud(130, 0.25, 197, 0.6), click(198, 800, 0.04))
    thread = motor(0.9, 60, 90, 199) * env_adsr(0.9, 0.1, 0.1, 0.7, 0.3) * 0.5
    clunk = mix(click(200, 1100, 0.04), thud(160, 0.15, 201, 0.4) * 0.5)
    return fade(mix(push, np.concatenate([silence(0.2), drop]), np.concatenate([silence(0.45), thread]), np.concatenate([silence(1.35), clunk])))


@sound("TapeEject")
def tape_eject():
    unthread = motor(0.6, 90, 60, 202) * env_adsr(0.6, 0.05, 0.1, 0.7, 0.2) * 0.45
    pop = mix(thud(140, 0.25, 203, 0.6), click(204, 900, 0.04))
    return fade(mix(unthread, np.concatenate([silence(0.55), pop])))


@sound("DeckPlay")
def deck_play():
    return fade(mix(click(205, 1300, 0.03), thud(170, 0.12, 206, 0.3) * 0.4, np.concatenate([silence(0.05), motor(0.3, 70, 80, 207) * env_ad(0.3, 0.02, 0.1) * 0.3])))


@sound("DeckPause")
def deck_pause():
    return fade(mix(click(208, 1100, 0.03), thud(150, 0.12, 209, 0.3) * 0.4))


@sound("FrameStep")
def frame_step():
    return fade(click(210, 2200, 0.02) * 0.6)


@sound("ChannelSwitch")
def channel_switch():
    # Rotary channel selector detent and a flash of snow.
    det = mix(click(211, 1600, 0.03), click(212, 2500, 0.02) * 0.5)
    snow = static_burst(0.22, 213) * env_ad(0.22, 0.005, 0.07) * 0.6
    return fade(mix(det, np.concatenate([silence(0.03), snow])))


@sound("MarkFrame")
def mark_frame():
    # Leaf shutter: two clicks and a spring.
    s = mix(click(214, 2600, 0.02), np.concatenate([silence(0.045), click(215, 3100, 0.02) * 0.8]))
    spring = sine(1800, 0.12) * env_ad(0.12, 0.001, 0.03) * 0.1
    return fade(mix(s, spring))


@sound("EditCommit")
def edit_commit():
    # Heavy transport clunk, a reversed swell, and the picture warping as the
    # future is overwritten.
    swell = (bandpass(white(1.2, 216), 400, 6000) * env_ad(1.2, 1.1, 0.05))
    clunk = mix(thud(60, 0.8, 217, 1.0), click(218, 700, 0.06))
    t = time(1.6)
    warp = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(220 * np.exp(-t * 1.2) + 30) / SR) * env_ad(1.6, 0.01, 0.6) * 0.35
    out = mix(swell * 0.5, np.concatenate([silence(1.15), clunk, ]), np.concatenate([silence(1.2), wow_flutter(warp, 0.01, 3, seed=219)]))
    return fade(reverb(out, 1.8, 0.3, 220))


@sound("EditRejected")
def edit_rejected():
    return ui_error()


@sound("Rewind")
def rewind():
    length = 2.0
    t = time(length)
    f = 500 + 1400 * (1 - np.exp(-t * 2))
    whine = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) * 0.3 + np.sin(4 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) * 0.1
    hiss = bandpass(white(length, 221), 2000, 9000) * 0.15
    stop = np.concatenate([silence(length - 0.08), click(222, 1000, 0.08)])
    return fade(mix(wow_flutter((whine + hiss) * env_adsr(length, 0.05, 0.1, 0.9, 0.1), 0.004, 5, seed=223), stop))


# --- Static and signal -------------------------------------------------------

@sound("StaticShort")
def static_short():
    return fade(static_burst(0.35, 224) * env_adsr(0.35, 0.002, 0.05, 0.7, 0.1))


@sound("StaticLong")
def static_long():
    s = static_burst(2.6, 225, crackles=120) * env_adsr(2.6, 0.002, 0.2, 0.8, 0.8)
    return fade(saturate(s, 1.5))


@sound("SignalLost")
def signal_lost():
    # The feed cutting out: static, then a dead carrier tone dropping away.
    snow = static_burst(1.6, 226) * env_adsr(1.6, 0.002, 0.1, 0.8, 0.5)
    t = time(2.2)
    tone = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (1000 - 300 * t / 2.2) * t) * env_adsr(2.2, 0.01, 0.1, 0.6, 1.0) * 0.25
    return fade(mix(snow, np.concatenate([silence(0.9), tone])))


@sound("RadioIn")
def radio_in():
    sq = highpass(white(0.18, 227), 1500) * env_ad(0.18, 0.001, 0.05) * 0.8
    return fade(mix(click(228, 1900, 0.02), sq))


@sound("RadioOut")
def radio_out():
    sq = highpass(white(0.12, 229), 1800) * env_ad(0.12, 0.001, 0.03) * 0.6
    return fade(mix(sq, np.concatenate([silence(0.1), click(230, 1500, 0.02) * 0.7])))


@sound("Reentry")
def reentry():
    # Tuning back in: a sweep through the noise that resolves.
    length = 2.2
    t = time(length)
    noise = static_burst(length, 231) * np.linspace(1, 0.05, n(length)) * 0.6
    f = 200 + 1600 * (t / length) ** 2
    sweep = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) * np.linspace(0.1, 0.35, n(length))
    chord = (sine(523, 1.2) + sine(659, 1.2) * 0.7 + sine(784, 1.2) * 0.5) * env_ad(1.2, 0.1, 0.4) * 0.25
    return fade(reverb(mix(noise, sweep, np.concatenate([silence(1.6), chord])), 1.5, 0.3, 232))


# --- The Presenter -----------------------------------------------------------

@sound("PresenterStep")
def presenter_step():
    # Heavy, slightly too slow, with a tape-dragged tail.
    t = time(0.9)
    body = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (38 + 30 * np.exp(-t * 20)) * t) * np.exp(-t * 7)
    hit = lowpass(white(0.9, 233), 1200) * np.exp(-t * 30) * 0.6
    s = wow_flutter(mix(body, hit), 0.01, 2.5, seed=234)
    return fade(reverb(s, 1.4, 0.35, 235, brightness=2500))


@sound("PresenterAppear")
def presenter_appear():
    rev = static_burst(1.0, 236)[::-1] * np.linspace(0, 1, n(1.0)) ** 2
    boom = thud(40, 1.2, 237, 0.5)
    return fade(reverb(mix(rev * 0.7, np.concatenate([silence(0.95), boom])), 2.0, 0.35, 238))


@sound("PresenterVanish")
def presenter_vanish():
    burst = static_burst(0.8, 239) * env_ad(0.8, 0.002, 0.2)
    return fade(tape_stop(burst, 0.6) * 0.8)


@sound("HuntStart", "sting")
def hunt_start():
    # A dissonant chord grinding to a halt, a slam, and a scream of static.
    length = 2.4
    chord = mix(*(saw(f, length, 16) * 0.3 for f in (98, 104, 147, 156, 233)))
    chord = lowpass(chord, 2500) * env_adsr(length, 0.005, 0.2, 0.8, 0.3)
    chord = tape_stop(chord, 1.4)
    slam = thud(42, 1.2, 240, 1.0) * 1.2
    snow = static_burst(0.7, 241) * env_ad(0.7, 0.001, 0.15) * 0.7
    return fade(reverb(saturate(mix(chord, slam, snow), 2), 2.2, 0.35, 242))


@sound("HuntEnd", "sting")
def hunt_end():
    # The signal clears: noise filtering up and away.
    length = 2.4
    s = sweep_lowpass(pink(length, 243), 300, 9000) * env_adsr(length, 0.2, 0.3, 0.6, 1.2) * 0.5
    tone = sine(392, 1.6) * env_ad(1.6, 0.4, 0.5) * 0.15
    return fade(reverb(mix(s, np.concatenate([silence(0.8), tone])), 1.8, 0.3, 244))


@sound("Eliminated", "sting")
def eliminated():
    return mix(static_long(), signal_lost() * 0.6)


@sound("EditorShadow", "sting")
def editor_shadow():
    # A soft, wrong chord swelling behind you.
    length = 2.5
    pad = mix(*(sine(f, length) for f in (233.1, 246.9, 349.2))) * env_adsr(length, 1.2, 0.2, 0.6, 0.9) * 0.3
    breath = bandpass(pink(length, 245), 500, 2500) * env_adsr(length, 1.5, 0.1, 0.5, 0.8) * 0.2
    return fade(reverb(wow_flutter(mix(pad, breath), 0.006, 0.8, seed=246), 2.5, 0.4, 247))


# --- The Loop ----------------------------------------------------------------

@sound("LoopStart", "sting")
def loop_start():
    # The building rewinds: a long rewind whine and a hard reset.
    return mix(rewind(), np.concatenate([silence(1.9), static_short()]))


@sound("LoopReset")
def loop_reset():
    return mix(rewind()[: n(1.0)] * np.linspace(1, 0, n(1.0)), static_short())


@sound("LoopBreak", "sting")
def loop_break():
    # Tape snapping.
    crack = highpass(white(0.05, 248), 1500) * env_ad(0.05, 0.0002, 0.006) * 1.5
    t = time(1.6)
    fall = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(300 * np.exp(-t * 2.5) + 20) / SR) * env_ad(1.6, 0.005, 0.5) * 0.4
    flap = silence(0.8)
    for i in range(10):
        flap = mix(flap, np.concatenate([silence(0.03 * i + 0.1), click(249 + i, 900, 0.02) * (1 - i / 10) * 0.5]))
    return fade(reverb(mix(crack, fall, flap), 1.5, 0.3, 260))


# --- Stingers ------------------------------------------------------------------

@sound("StingDiscovery", "sting")
def sting_discovery():
    # Finding a tape: a music-box figure with a sour last note.
    out = silence(2.4)
    for i, f in enumerate([659.3, 784, 880, 830.6]):
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(0.22 * i), bell(f, 1.6, 261 + i, ratios=(1, 4.0, 9.2), decay=2.5)]))
    return fade(reverb(wow_flutter(out, 0.004, 1.1, seed=265), 2.0, 0.35, 266))


@sound("StingScare", "sting")
def sting_scare():
    hit = mix(thud(45, 1.4, 267, 1.0), static_burst(0.4, 268) * env_ad(0.4, 0.001, 0.08))
    cluster = mix(*(saw(f, 1.6, 12) for f in (311, 330, 349, 370))) * env_ad(1.6, 0.002, 0.5) * 0.25
    return fade(reverb(saturate(mix(hit, lowpass(cluster, 3500)), 1.8), 2.0, 0.35, 269))


@sound("StingSuccess", "sting")
def sting_success():
    out = silence(4.0)
    for i, (f, d) in enumerate([(523.3, 0), (659.3, 0.25), (784, 0.5), (1046.5, 0.8)]):
        tone = additive(f, 3.0, [(1, 1), (2, 0.4), (3, 0.2), (4, 0.1)]) * env_adsr(3.0, 0.01, 0.4, 0.4, 2.0) * 0.2
        out = mix(out, np.concatenate([silence(d), tone]))
    return fade(reverb(wow_flutter(out, 0.004, 0.7, seed=270), 2.5, 0.35, 271))


@sound("StingFail", "sting")
def sting_fail():
    length = 4.0
    chord = mix(*(additive(f, length, [(1, 1), (2, 0.4), (3, 0.2)]) for f in (110, 130.8, 155.6, 185))) * env_adsr(length, 0.05, 0.5, 0.6, 2.5) * 0.25
    return fade(reverb(tape_stop(wow_flutter(chord, 0.008, 0.9, seed=272), 2.5), 2.5, 0.35, 273))


# --- Sounds named by the case's own subtitles ---------------------------------

def stick_slip(length, seed, rate_lo, rate_hi, modes, threshold=0.96):
    """Friction: a stuttering series of tiny impulses through resonant modes."""
    r = rng(seed)
    t = time(length)
    rate = rate_lo + (rate_hi - rate_lo) * np.sin(np.pi * t / length) + r.normal(0, 2, len(t)).cumsum() / 300
    impulses = (np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(np.maximum(rate, 1)) / SR) > threshold).astype(float)
    return resonators(impulses + white(length, seed + 1) * 0.01, modes)


@sound("ChairScrape")
def chair_scrape():
    s = stick_slip(0.9, 280, 60, 140, [(380, 6, 10), (900, 8, 8), (2100, 10, 4)], 0.9)
    s = bandpass(s, 200, 6000) * env_adsr(0.9, 0.05, 0.1, 0.8, 0.2)
    return fade(reverb(s * 0.5, 1.4, 0.35, 281))


@sound("ShelfCreak")
def shelf_creak():
    s = stick_slip(1.6, 282, 8, 26, [(240, 14, 20), (610, 16, 12), (1300, 18, 6)])
    s = bandpass(s, 120, 4000) * env_adsr(1.6, 0.3, 0.2, 0.8, 0.5)
    return fade(reverb(s * 0.4, 1.8, 0.4, 283, brightness=3000))


@sound("MetalCreak")
def metal_creak():
    s = stick_slip(2.2, 284, 5, 14, [(150, 40, 25), (410, 50, 18), (1270, 60, 10), (2980, 60, 5)])
    s = bandpass(s, 80, 6000) * env_adsr(2.2, 0.4, 0.3, 0.8, 0.8)
    return fade(reverb(s * 0.4, 2.6, 0.45, 285, brightness=3500))


@sound("TapeFlutter")
def tape_flutter():
    length = 2.2
    tone = (sine(311, length) + sine(466, length) * 0.5 + sine(622, length) * 0.2) * env_adsr(length, 0.4, 0.2, 0.7, 0.8)
    hiss = bandpass(white(length, 286), 1500, 7000) * env_adsr(length, 0.3, 0.2, 0.6, 0.8) * 0.1
    return fade(reverb(wow_flutter(tone * 0.3 + hiss, 0.02, 4, 0.004, 11, seed=287), 1.8, 0.35, 288))
```

## scripts/audio/upload.py

```python
"""
Uploads the four audio banks to Roblox and writes their ids into
src/shared/Config/AudioAssets.luau.

  Upload with Open Cloud (needs an API key with the Assets API's read and
  write permissions, created on the Creator Dashboard):

    ROBLOX_API_KEY=... python3 scripts/audio/upload.py --user 123456
    ROBLOX_API_KEY=... python3 scripts/audio/upload.py --group 7890

  Use the user or group that owns the experience; Roblox only lets an
  experience play audio its owner is allowed to use.

  Or upload assets/audio/DeadAir_*.ogg by hand on the Creator Dashboard and
  just write the ids:

    python3 scripts/audio/upload.py --ids Sfx=111 Ambience=222 Music1=333 Music2=444

Only the Python standard library is used.
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
import uuid

ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", ".."))
AUDIO = os.path.join(ROOT, "assets", "audio")
ASSETS_LUAU = os.path.join(ROOT, "src", "shared", "Config", "AudioAssets.luau")
API = "https://apis.roblox.com/assets/v1"


def banks():
    with open(os.path.join(AUDIO, "banks.json")) as f:
        return json.load(f)["banks"]


def request(method, url, key, body=None, content_type=None):
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, method=method)
    req.add_header("x-api-key", key)
    if content_type:
        req.add_header("Content-Type", content_type)
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
            return json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8") or "{}")
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err:
        detail = err.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
        raise SystemExit("%s %s failed: HTTP %d\n%s" % (method, url, err.code, detail))


def upload(path, name, key, creator):
    boundary = uuid.uuid4().hex
    meta = {
        "assetType": "Audio",
        "displayName": name,
        "description": "DEAD AIR: CHANNEL ZERO original audio bank (%s)." % name,
        "creationContext": {"creator": creator},
    }
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        data = f.read()
    body = b"".join(
        [
            ("--%s\r\n" % boundary).encode(),
            b'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="request"\r\n',
            b"Content-Type: application/json\r\n\r\n",
            json.dumps(meta).encode(),
            ("\r\n--%s\r\n" % boundary).encode(),
            ('Content-Disposition: form-data; name="fileContent"; filename="%s"\r\n' % os.path.basename(path)).encode(),
            b"Content-Type: audio/ogg\r\n\r\n",
            data,
            ("\r\n--%s--\r\n" % boundary).encode(),
        ]
    )
    op = request("POST", API + "/assets", key, body, "multipart/form-data; boundary=" + boundary)
    # Uploads are processed asynchronously: poll the operation until done.
    path_id = op.get("path", "")
    for _ in range(60):
        if op.get("done"):
            break
        time.sleep(2)
        op = request("GET", API + "/" + path_id, key)
    if not op.get("done"):
        raise SystemExit("Upload of %s did not finish; check the Creator Dashboard." % name)
    response = op.get("response", {})
    asset_id = response.get("assetId")
    if not asset_id:
        raise SystemExit("Upload of %s returned no asset id:\n%s" % (name, json.dumps(op, indent=1)))
    return str(asset_id)


def write_ids(ids):
    with open(ASSETS_LUAU) as f:
        text = f.read()
    for bank, asset_id in ids.items():
        value = asset_id if asset_id.startswith("rbxassetid://") else "rbxassetid://" + asset_id
        pattern = r'(\n\t%s = )"[^"]*",' % re.escape(bank)
        if not re.search(pattern, text):
            raise SystemExit("No entry for bank %s in AudioAssets.luau" % bank)
        text = re.sub(pattern, lambda m: '%s"%s",' % (m.group(1), value), text)
    with open(ASSETS_LUAU, "w") as f:
        f.write(text)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    who = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    who.add_argument("--user", help="upload as this Roblox user id")
    who.add_argument("--group", help="upload as this Roblox group id")
    parser.add_argument("--ids", nargs="*", help="skip uploading; write Bank=assetId pairs")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    known = banks()
    if args.ids:
        ids = {}
        for pair in args.ids:
            bank, _, asset_id = pair.partition("=")
            if bank not in known or not asset_id.strip():
                raise SystemExit("Expected Bank=assetId with Bank one of: " + ", ".join(sorted(known)))
            ids[bank] = asset_id.strip()
        write_ids(ids)
        print("Wrote %d id(s) to %s" % (len(ids), os.path.relpath(ASSETS_LUAU, ROOT)))
        return

    key = os.environ.get("ROBLOX_API_KEY")
    if not key or not (args.user or args.group):
        parser.print_help()
        sys.exit(2)
    creator = {"userId": str(args.user)} if args.user else {"groupId": str(args.group)}
    ids = {}
    for bank, info in sorted(known.items()):
        path = os.path.join(AUDIO, info["file"])
        print("Uploading %s (%.0f s)..." % (info["file"], info["duration"]))
        ids[bank] = upload(path, "DeadAir " + bank, key, creator)
        print("  -> rbxassetid://%s" % ids[bank])
    write_ids(ids)
    print("Wrote ids to %s. Rebuild the place (scripts/build.sh) to use them." % os.path.relpath(ASSETS_LUAU, ROOT))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
